# Rashi on Job

רש"י על איוב

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job)

הפירוש הנפוץ והמוכר ביותר לתורה, המבאר את פשטי המקראות בתוספת הרחבות פרשניות; חובר במאה ה-11.

Most widely-read biblical commentary, compiled in the 11th-century, explaining the simple meaning of the text with interpretive elaborations.

Sections: Chapter-Verse-Comment

Location: Tanakh/Rishonim on Tanakh/Rashi/Writings

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Sefaria vocalized edition (Segments: 1511, License: unknown)

Version (En): The Judaica Press complete Tanach with Rashi, translated by A. J. Rosenberg (Segments: 1487)


## Chapter 1


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Job 1:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:1:1)


**אִישׁ הָיָה בְאֶרֶץ עוּץ.** פֵּירוּשׁוֹ: בְּאֶרֶץ אֲרָם, כְּמָה דִכְתִיב ״אֶת עוּץ בְּכֹרוֹ״ מִבְּנֵי נָחוֹר (בראשית כב:כא):


**There was a man in the land of Uz** This means in the land of Aram, as it is written (Gen. 22: 21): “Uz, his firstborn,” of the sons of Nahor.


###### Rashi on Job 1:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:1:2)


**וְהָיָה הָאִישׁ.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא לְשׁוֹן עָתִיד, כֵּן דֶּרֶךְ לָשׁוֹן עִבְרִית; וּלְשׁוֹן מְלִיצָתוֹ: וְהוֹוֶה הָאִישׁ הַהוּא גָּדוֹל מִכָּל בְּנֵי קֶדֶם – בְּנֵי אֶרֶץ הַמִּזְרָחִי; שֶׁאֲרָם בְּמִזְרָחוֹ שֶׁל עוֹלָם הוּא, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״מִן אֲרָם יַנְחֵנִי בָלָק מֵהַרְרֵי קֶדֶם״ (במדבר כג:ז). דָּבָר אַחֵר: עוּץ – זֶה אִיּוֹב; בּוּז – זֶה אֱלִיהוּא בֶּן בַּרַכְאֵל הַבּוּזִי; וַהֲרֵי הוּא שֶׁאָמַר אִיּוֹב: ״וּבוּז מִשְׁפָּחוֹת יְחִתֵּנִי״ (איוב לא:לד) – זֶה הוּא מִמִּשְׁפַּחְתִּי שֶׁמִּחְתָּה דְבָרַי; וְהַיְנוּ דְּאָמְרֵי אִינְשֵׁי (סנהדרין לט:): מִינֵּהּ וּבֵהּ אַבָּא לֵיזִיל בֵּהּ נַרְגָּא. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **בְּאֶרֶץ עוּץ.** בְּאֶרֶץ שֶׁנּוֹטְלִין מִמֶּנּוּ עֵצוֹת רָעוֹת עַל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״מִן הָאָרֶץ הַהִיא יָצָא אַשּׁוּר״ (בראשית י:יא); וּמָה עֵצָה נָטְלוּ? שֶׁהָיוּ אוֹמְרִים: לֹא כָּל הֵימֶנּוּ לִטּוֹל אֶת הָעֶלְיוֹנִים וְלִתֵּן לָנוּ אֶת הַתַּחְתּוֹנִים! אֶלָּא בּוֹאוּ לְאֶרֶץ שִׁנְעָר שֶׁשָּׁם אֱלוֹהוֹת הַרְבֵּה, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בָּהּ ״אֶרֶץ פְּסִילִים הִיא״ (ירמיהו נ:לח). ״וְנַעֲשֶׂה מִגְדָּל וְרֹאשׁוֹ בַשָּׁמַיִם וְנַעֲשֶׂה לָּנוּ שֵׁם״ (בראשית יא:ד), וְאֵין שֵׁם הָאָמוּר כָּאן אֶלָּא עֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וְשֵׁם אֱלֹהִים אֲחֵרִים לֹא תַזְכִּירוּ״ (שמות כג:יג). מֶה עָשָׂה הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא? יָשַׁב עֲלֵיהֶם בְּדִין וְהֶעֱמִיד עֲלֵיהֶם עֵדִים, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וַיֵּרֶד ה׳ לִרְאֹת אֶת הָעִיר וְאֶת הַמִּגְדָּל״ (בראשית יא:ה) – וְכִי צָרִיךְ יְרִידָה לִפְנֵי הַמָּקוֹם לִרְאוֹת? וְהַכְּתִיב ״עֵינֵי ה׳ מְשׁוֹטְטוֹת בְּכָל הָאָרֶץ״ (זכריה ד:י)! אֶלָּא לְלַמֵּד הֲלָכָה לְדוֹרוֹת, שֶׁאֵין דָּנִין אֶלָּא עַל פִּי עֵדֵי רְאִיָּה, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״אוֹ רָאָה אוֹ יָדָע״ (ויקרא ה:א):


**and that man was** Although that is a future expression, that is the style of the Hebrew language, and the proper expression for it is “Now that man was greater than all the children of the land of the East”; the children of the land of the East, for Aram is in the east of the world, as it is stated (Num. 23:7): “From Aram has Balak, king of Moab, brought me, from the mountains of the East.” Another explanation: Uz is Job. Buz is Elihu the son of Barachel the Buzite. This is what Job said (31:34): “and the most contemptible of families shatters me.” This one (the Buzite) who shatters my words is of my family. This is what people say: “From the very forest, the ax handle is formed.” Another explanation: **in the land of Uz** In the land whence they take evil counsels against the Holy One, blessed be He. As it is stated (Gen. 10:11): “Out of that land Asshur went forth.” Now what counsel did they take? “He has no right to take the upper worlds and to give us the lower world.” Instead, come to the land of Shinar, where there are many gods, concerning which it is stated: “for it is a land of graven images” (Jer. 50:38): “And let us make a tower whose top shall reach to the heavens, and let us make a name for ourselves” (Gen. 11:4). The word “name” stated here refers only to idolatry, as it is stated (Exod. 23:13): “You shall not make mention of the name of other gods.” What did the Holy One, blessed be He, do? He sat in judgment over them and stood up witnesses against them, as it is stated (Gen. 11:5): “And the Lord came down to see the city and the tower.” Now does the Omnipresent have to come down to see? Is it not written: “The eyes of the Lord are roving to and fro throughout the entire earth”? Rather, it is to teach a ruling to future generations, that judges may not pass judgment except with eyewitnesses, as it is stated (Lev.5:1): “Having seen or known.”


###### Rashi on Job 1:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:1:3)


**וְהָיָה.** פְּשׁוּטוֹ כְּמוֹ וַיְהִי, וּמַשְׁמָעוֹ לְעָבָר וּלְהַבָּא:


**was** Heb. והיה. According to its simple meaning, it is like ויהי, and its meaning is both past and future.


###### Rashi on Job 1:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:1:4)


**תָּם וְיָשָׁר.** אֲבָל בִּדְבָרִים שֶׁבֵּין קוֹנוֹ וּבֵינוֹ לֹא הָיָה צַדִּיק? לְכָךְ נֶאֱמַר: **וִירֵא אֱלֹהִים וְסָר מֵרָע**:


**sincere and upright** But [I could think] that as regards matters between him and his Maker, he was not righteous. Therefore, it is stated: God- fearing and shunning evil.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 1:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:3:1)


**שִׁבְעַת אַלְפֵי צֹאן.** הָרוֹב מָנָה תְּחִלָּה:


**seven thousand sheep** He counted the largest number first.


###### Rashi on Job 1:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:3:2)


**גָּדוֹל מִכָּל בְּנֵי קֶדֶם.** מִדּוֹר הַפְּלַגָּה, דִּכְתִיב ״וַיְהִי בְּנָסְעָם מִקֶּדֶם״ (בראשית יא:ב). אֲבָל מֵאַבְרָהָם אֵינוֹ כֵּן, שֶׁהֲרֵי בְּאַבְרָהָם כְּתִיב בּוֹ ׳הַגָּדוֹל׳, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וְשֵׁם חֶבְרוֹן לְפָנִים קִרְיַת אַרְבַּע הָאָדָם הַגָּדוֹל בָּעֲנָקִים הוּא״ (יהושע יד:טו). הָיָה לוֹ לִכְתֹּב ׳אָדָם גָּדוֹל׳, וְלָמָּה כָּתַב ״הָאָדָם הַגָּדוֹל״? אֶלָּא רֶמֶז שֶׁהוּא אָדָם גָּדוֹל, שֶׁעָמַד בְּעֶשֶׂר נִסְיוֹנוֹת. לְפִיכָךְ כּוֹתְבִין אוֹתוֹ שְׁנֵי הֵהִי״ן. וְאִיּוֹב שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בּוֹ גָּדוֹל – לֹא עָמַד אֶלָּא בְּאַחַת, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״תָּם וְיָשָׁר וְגוֹ׳״ – מִכָּל אוֹתוֹ הַדּוֹר, שֶׁלֹּא נִתְפַּס עַל אוֹתוֹ עָוֹן (סא״א):


**greater than all the children of the East** Than the generation of the division, as it is written (Gen. 11:2): “And it came to pass as they migrated from the east.” But concerning Abraham, it is not so, for concerning Abraham, it is written הגדול, the greatest, as it is stated (Josh. 14:15): “And the name of Hebron before was Kirjath-Arba; he was the greatest man among the “Anakim.” It should have said אדם גדול, a great man. Why did he write האדם הגדול, the greatest man? By this, Scripture hinted that he was a great man who passed ten tests. Therefore, his title is written with two “heys”, the numerical value of which is ten. Job, however, described by the word גדול, greater, passed only one [test], as it is stated: sincere and upright, etc., that out of that entire generation, he was not apprehended for that iniquity.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 1:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:4:1)


**וְהָלְכוּ בָנָיו וְעָשׂוּ מִשְׁתֶּה בֵּית אִישׁ יוֹמוֹ.** בְּבֵית כָּל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד יוֹמוֹ:


**Now his sons would go and make a feast in each one’s house on his day** In the house of each one on his day.


###### Rashi on Job 1:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:4:2)


**וְשָׁלְחוּ וְקָרְאוּ.** כְּמוֹ וְשׁוֹלְחִים וְקוֹרְאִים, שֶׁהָיוּ עוֹשִׂין כֵּן תָּמִיד:


**and they would send and invite** Heb. ושלחו וקראו, like ושולחים וקוראים, that they would do so constantly.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 1:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:5:1)


**וַיְהִי כִּי הִקִּיפוּ.** כַּאֲשֶׁר כָּלוּ יְמֵי הַמִּשְׁתֶּה, נוֹהֵג הָיָה אִיּוֹב בְּכָל סוֹף שִׁבְעַת הַיָּמִים, שֶׁחָזְרוּ יְמֵי חֲלִילָתָם, הָיָה שׁוֹלֵחַ וּמְקַדְּשָׁם, כְּלוֹמַר: מְזַמְּנָם וְקוֹרְאָם; כְּמוֹ ״הִתְקַדְּשׁוּ לְמָחָר״ (במדבר יא:יח):


**Now it would come about when the cycle of the feasting days would be over** When the days of the feasting would be over, Job would act in this manner; upon every termination of the seven days on which the days of feasting rotated, he would send and summon them (ויקדשם) ; i.e., he would prepare them and summon them, as in (Num. 11:18). “Prepare yourselves for tommorow.”


###### Rashi on Job 1:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:5:2)


**וְהִשְׁכִּים בַּבֹּקֶר וְהֶעֱלָה.** מְלִיצָתוֹ, שֶׁהָיָה מַשְׁכִּים וּמַעֲלֶה עוֹלוֹת. לָכֵן כָּתַב בִּלְשׁוֹן עָתִיד, שֶׁהָיָה נוֹהֵג לַעֲשׂוֹת כֵּן תָּמִיד, לְסוֹף כָּל שִׁבְעַת הַיָּמִים:


**and offer up... early in the morning** The meaning is that he would offer up burnt-offerings early. Therefore, Scripture wrote in the future tense, that he was wont to do so always at the end of every seven days.


###### Rashi on Job 1:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:5:3)


**מִסְפַּר כֻּלָּם.** הַבָּנִים וְהַבָּנוֹת:


**the number of all of them** The sons and the daughters.


###### Rashi on Job 1:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:5:4)


**וּבֵרֲכוּ.** לְשׁוֹן כִּנּוּי כְּלַפֵּי מַעְלָה:


**and blasphemed** Heb. וברכו, lit. will do. A euphemism [used in] referring to the Most High.


###### Rashi on Job 1:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:5:5)


**כָּכָה יַעֲשֶׂה אִיּוֹב.** כְּמוֹ הָיָה עוֹשֶׂה מָחָר; כְּשֶׁכָּתַב בְּדָבָר הַהוֹוֶה וְתָדִיר, פְּעָמִים שֶׁכָּתַב בּוֹ לְשׁוֹן עָבָר וּפְעָמִים לְשׁוֹן עָתִיד:


**So would Job do** Heb. יעשה, lit. will do. He would always do in this manner. When Scripture writes about a constant occurrence, sometimes it writes in the past tense, and sometimes in the future tense.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 1:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:6:1)


**וַיְהִי הַיּוֹם.** אוֹתוֹ יוֹם שֶׁהָיָה רֹאשׁ הַשָּׁנָה (ראש השנה טז.), שֶׁהוּא יוֹם תְּרוּעָה, וְצִוָּה הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לַשָּׂטָן לְהָבִיא זְכוּת וְחוֹבָה שֶׁל כָּל הַבְּרִיּוֹת. הֲדָא הוּא דִּכְתִיב **מִשּׁוּט בָּאָרֶץ**:


**Now the day came about** That day which was Rosh Hashanah, (known as a day of sounding the shofar, and the Holy One, blessed be He, commanded the Adversary to bring the merit and the guilt of all creatures. This is the meaning of “from going to and fro on the earth.”)


###### Rashi on Job 1:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:6:2)


**וַיָּבֹאוּ בְּנֵי הָאֱלֹהִים לְהִתְיַצֵּב עַל־ה׳.** לָרִיב עִמּוֹ, שֶׁאֵין לְהִתְיַצֵּב אֶלָּא עַל־דִּין, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: ״נִצָּב לָרִיב ה׳״ (ישעיהו ג:יג):


**and the angels of God came to stand beside the Lord** to contend with Him, because the expression of standing refers only to judgment, as it is stated (Isa. 3:13): “The Lord stands to plead.”


###### Rashi on Job 1:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:6:3)


**וַיָּבֹא גַם הַשָּׂטָן בְּתוֹכָם.** לְקַטְרֵג הַבְּרִיּוֹת:


**and the Adversary, too, came among them** to accuse the people.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 1:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:7:1)


**מֵאַיִן תָּבֹא.** דִּבֵּר הַכָּתוּב דֶּרֶךְ אֶרֶץ, שֶׁהַגָּדוֹל מְדַבֵּר תְּחִלָּה וּמַרְשֶׁה הַקָּטָן לַעֲנוֹת:


**“Where are you coming from?”** Scripture speaks of etiquette, that the greater one speaks first and permits the smaller one to reply.


###### Rashi on Job 1:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:7:2)


**מִשּׁוּט בָּאָרֶץ.** כַּאֲשֶׁר מְפֹרָשׁ לְמַעְלָה, וּבַהַגָּדָה דְּהַשּׁוּתָּפִין הוּא (בבא בתרא טז.):


**“From going to and fro on the earth”** As is explained above, and it is in the homiletic section of the chapter [that begins], “The partners.”


###### Rashi on Job 1:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:7:3)


**מִשּׁוּט בָּאָרֶץ וּמֵהִתְהַלֵּךְ בָּהּ.** כֵּן דַּרְכִּי, לָשׁוּט וְלִרְאוֹת רָעִים וְטוֹבִים; וְשׁוֹטַטְתִּי בְּכָל הָאָרֶץ וְלֹא מָצָאתִי כְּאַבְרָהָם, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בּוֹ ״קוּם הִתְהַלֵּךְ בָּאָרֶץ״ (בראשית יג:יז). וְאָמְרוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה (בבא בתרא טז.): שָׂטָן לְשֵׁם שָׁמַיִם נִתְכַּוֵּן, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא נִשְׁכָּח לִפְנֵי אֱלֹהֵינוּ זְכוּתוֹ שֶׁל אַבְרָהָם:


**“From going to and fro on the earth and from walking in it.”** So is my wont to go to and fro, to see the evil ones and the good ones. Now I went to and fro throughout the entire earth and I did not find anyone like Abraham, about whom it is said (Gen. 13:17): “Rise, go to and fro in the land.” The Sages of blessed memory (Baba Bathra 16a) said that the Adversary meant this for the sake of Heaven, in order that Abraham’s merit not be forgotten by our God.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 1:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:8:1)


**הֲשַׂמְתָּ לִבְּךָ עַל עַבְדִּי אִיּוֹב.** שֶׁתִּרְצֶה לְקַטְרְגוֹ:


**Have you paid attention to My servant Job** that you should desire to denounce him?


###### Rashi on Job 1:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:8:2)


**הֲשַׂמְתָּ.** לְשׁוֹן תֵּימַהּ הוּא:


**“Have you paid, etc.”** An expression of a wonder.


###### Rashi on Job 1:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:8:3)


**תָּם וְיָשָׁר.** שָׁלֵם בְּמַעֲשָׂיו:


**sincere and upright** Perfect in his deeds.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Job 1:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:9:1)


**הֶחִנָּם יָרֵא אִיּוֹב.** בִּתְמִיהַּ:


**“Does Job fear God for nothing?”** This is a question.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 1:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:10:1)


**הֲלֹא.** אַתָּה שַׂכְתָּ בַּעֲדוֹ לְפִי שֶׁהוּא יוֹדֵעַ שֶׁאַתָּה רָגִיל אֶצְלוֹ לַעֲשׂוֹת לוֹ נִסִּים, וּפָרֶה וְרָבֶה, לְפִיכָךְ עוֹשֶׂה צְדָקוֹת הַלָּלוּ; אֲבָל לֹא נִסִּיתָ אוֹתוֹ:


**Haven’t You made a hedge around him** Because he knows that You visit him frequently to perform miracles for him, and he is fruitful and multiplies; therefore he performs all these righteous deeds—but You have not tested him.


###### Rashi on Job 1:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:10:2)


**שַׂכְתָּ בַעֲדוֹ.** הִגַּנְתָּ בַּעֲדוֹ, כִּמְחִיצָּה זוֹ; כְּמוֹ ״הָסֵר מְשׂוּכָתוֹ״ (ישעיהו ה:ה) – מְחִיצָתוֹ. וְכָל ׳בְּעַד׳ הָאָמוּר בִּלְשׁוֹן עִבְרִי אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא לְשׁוֹן ׳כְּנֶגְדּוֹ וּבְפָנָיו׳; כְּמוֹ ״וְכַפֵּר בַּעַדְךָ״ (ויקרא ט:ז) – קַנַּח הֶעָוֹן כְּנֶגְדְּךָ וּבְפָנֶיךָ, לִהְיוֹת הַכַּפָּרָה מָגֵן בַּעַדְךָ; וְכֵן ״ה׳ מָגֵן בַּעֲדִי״ (תהלים ג:ד):


**made a hedge around him** You protected him like a wall, as in (Isa. 5:5) “I will remove its hedge (משוכתו),” its wall. Every expression of בעד stated in the Hebrew language is only an expression of “opposite and before him,” as in (Lev.16:6), “and he shall effect atonement for himself (וכפר בעדו),” he shall wipe away the sin from opposite you and before you (sic), so that the atonement shall be a shield about you. Similarly (Ps. 3:4), “[But You,] O Lord, are a shield about me (בעדי) .”


###### Rashi on Job 1:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:10:3)


**פָּרַץ בָּאָרֶץ.** נִתְחַזְּקוּ, כְּמוֹ ״וְכֵן יִפְרֹץ״ (שמות א:יב), וּמְתַרְגְּמִינָן: ״יִתְקוֹף״. זֶהוּ פְּשׁוּטוֹ. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה אָמְרוּ בַּהַגָּדָה (בבא בתרא טו:; טז.): פָּרַץ גְּדֵרוֹ שֶׁל עוֹלָם, שֶׁהָעִזִּים הוֹרְגִים הַזְּאֵבִים:


**spread out in the land** Heb. פרץ, gained strength, as in (Exod. 1:12), “So would they gain strength (יפרץ), which the Targum renders: And so would they become strong. This is its simple meaning, but our Sages of blessed memory stated in the Aggadah (Baba Bathra 15b, 16a) that they broke the fence of the world, for the goats would slay the wolves.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 1:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:11:1)


**וְאוּלָם.** לוּ הָיִיתָ נוֹגֵעַ בְּכָל אֲשֶׁר לוֹ, **אִם לֹא עַל פָּנֶיךָ יְבָרֲכֶךָּ.** מִיָּד; בִּתְמִיהַּ. וְאָז:


**But** were you to touch all that he has, would he not blaspheme You to Your face immediately? This is a question. And then, the Lord said to the Adversary, “Behold, all that he has is in your hands.”



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 1:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:12:1)


**וַיֹּאמֶר ה׳ אֶל הַשָּׂטָן הִנֵּה כָל אֲשֶׁר לוֹ בְּיָדֶךָ.** (דָּבָר אַחֵר:) **וַיְהִי הַיּוֹם**, אוֹתוֹ הַיּוֹם שֶׁחָזְרוּ חֲלִילָה לֶאֱכוֹל בְּבֵית אֲחִיהֶם הַבְּכוֹר:


**“Behold, all that he has etc.** Another explanation: **Now the day came about** That day when they recommenced the cycle [and were] to eat at the house of their eldest brother.


###### Rashi on Job 1:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:12:2)


**בְּנֵי הָאֱלֹהִים.** צְבָא הַשָּׁמַיִם הַקְּרוֹבִים לַשְּׁכִינָה, לִהְיוֹת בְּנֵי בֵיתוֹ; וְעַל שֵׁם כָּךְ נִקְרְאוּ בָּנָיו:


**the angels of God** The heavenly hosts, which are near the Divine Presence, to be the members of His household, and were therefore called His children.


###### Rashi on Job 1:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:12:3)


**לְהִתְיַצֵּב עַל ה׳.** סְבִיבוֹתָיו, כְּמוֹ ״לְכָל הַנִּצָּבִים עָלָיו״ (בראשית מה:א):


**to stand beside the Lord** Heb. Around Him, as (Gen. 45:1) “all who stood around him (עליו).”



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 1:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:14:1)


**עַל יְדֵיהֶם.** כְּמוֹ ״אִישׁ עַל יָדוֹ״ (במדבר ב:יז). וְאֵינוֹ לְשׁוֹן ׳מָקוֹם׳, אֶלָּא עַל שֵׁם שֶׁהוּא מוּכָן וּמְזֻמָּן לְיָדוֹ. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה אָמְרוּ בַּהַגָּדָה (בבא בתרא טו:; טז.) שֶׁהִטְעִימָם הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מֵעֵין עוֹלָם הַבָּא, שֶׁלְּאַחַר חֲרִישַׁת הָאֲתוֹנוֹת הָיוּ זוֹרְעִים עִם הַחֲרִישָׁה וְרוֹעוֹת הָאֲתוֹנוֹת מִיָּד חָזִיז מִן הַתֶּלֶם:


**beside them** Heb. על ידיהם lit. on their hands. This is an expression of a place only because it is prepared and available at his hand. Our Sages of blessed memory said in the Aggadah (Baba Bathra 15b, 16a), that the Holy One, blessed be He, gave them a taste of the world to come; for after the plowing by the she-donkeys, they would sow with the plowing, and the she-donkeys would immediately graze on the young greens from the furrow.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 1:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:15:1)


**וַתִּפֹּל שְׁבָא.** נִתְנַפְּלָה וְחָנְתָה מַלְכוּת שְׁבָא עֲלֵיהֶם, כְּמוֹ (בראשית כ״ה:י״ח) ״עַל פְּנֵי כָל אֶחָיו נָפָל״:


**And Sheba** The kingdom of Sheba fell upon them and encamped beside them, as in (Gen. 25: 18), “before all his brethren he settled.”


###### Rashi on Job 1:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:15:2)


**וְאֶת־הַנְּעָרִים.** מְשָׁרְתֶיךָ, הַשּׁוֹמְרִים אוֹתָם:


**the youths** Your servants who were guarding them.


###### Rashi on Job 1:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:15:3)


**וָאִמָּלְטָה.** אֵין מְלִיטָה אֶלָּא לְשׁוֹן הַשְׁמָטָה, שֶׁנִּשְׁמַט מִן הַצָּרָה, (וּבָאתִי לְהַגִּיד לָךְ. וְכֵיוָן שֶׁהִגִּיד, מִיָּד מֵת כן הוא בס״א):


**and I... escaped** Heb. ואמלטה. The word מליטה is only an expression of withdrawal, that one withdrew from trouble.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 1:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:16:1)


**וּבַנְּעָרִים.** בַּמְשָׁרְתִים שׁוֹמְרֵי הַצֹּאן מְדַבֵּר הַכָּתוּב:


**and the youths** Scripture speaks of the guards of the flocks.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Job 1:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:17:1)


**וַיִּפְשְׁטוּ עַל הַגְּמַלִּים.** זֶה לְשׁוֹן ״בָּזְזוּ בַּז״ (ישעיה לג:כג) – לְפִי מִנְהָגָם. כָּל זְמַן שֶׁהֵם מְהַלְּכִים, נִדְבָּקִים יַחַד וְהוֹלְכִים בְּמַאֲרָב, שֶׁלֹּא יָבִינוּ בָּהֶם וְיַבְרִיחוּ אֶת הַמִּקְנֶה; וּכְשֶׁמַּגִּיעִים לִמְקוֹם הַמִּקְנֶה, פּוֹשְׁטִים בְּמֶרְחַב הַמִּקְנֶה זֶה לְכָאן וְזֶה לְכָאן, לֶאֱסֹף הַמִּקְנֶה. וְהַמַּלְאָכִים הַלָּלוּ – שְׁלוּחֵי שָׂטָן הָיוּ; וּכְדֵי לְהַקְנִיטוֹ וּלְהַחֲטִיאוֹ בִּשְּׂרוּהוּ תְּחִלָּה בְּהֶפְסֵד מוּעָט, וְאַחַר כָּךְ בְּגָדוֹל הֵימֶנּוּ, וְכֵן כֻּלָּם:


**spread out on the camels** This is an expression of plundering plunder according to their custom that as long as they are walking, they stick together and walk stealthily lest people recognize them and drive the cattle away. When they reach the place of the cattle, they spread out in the width of the herd, one on each side, in order to gather the cattle. Now these messengers were messengers of the Adversary, and in order to provoke him [Job] and cause him to sin, they first informed him of a small loss and later of a larger one. So is the sequence of all of them.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 1:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:19:1)


**מֵעֵבֶר הַמִּדְבָּר.** מִצַּד שֵׁנִי שֶׁל מִדְבָּר, וְהָיָה הַבַּיִת מִצַּד זֶה; וְזֶהוּ לְשׁוֹן ׳עֵבֶר׳ בַּמִּקְרָא:


**from the other side of the desert** From the other side of the desert, for the house was on this side. This is the meaning of עֵבֶר, as it is found in Scriptures.


###### Rashi on Job 1:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:19:2)


**עַל הַנְּעָרִים.** בָּנִים, אֲבָל אֵין צָרִיךְ לְהַזְכִּיר הַבָּנוֹת:


**upon the youths** The sons, but it is unnecessary to mention the daughters.


###### Rashi on Job 1:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:19:3)


(**לְהַגִּיד לָךְ.** שֶׁהָיָה לוֹ רְשׁוּת לְהַגִּיד, וְהָיָה מֵת בְּאוֹתוֹ רֶגַע ס״א אינו):


(**to tell you** He was permitted only to tell, and at that moment, he died. Not found in certain editions.)



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 1:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:20:1)


**וַיָּגָז.** תָּלַשׁ, כְּמוֹ ״גָּזִּי נִזְרֵךְ״ (ירמיה ז:כט), וּמְתַרְגְּמִינָן: ״תְּלִישָׁה״, וּכְמוֹ ״מִמְּעֵי אִמִּי אַתָּה גוֹזִי״ (תהלים עא:ו) – מוֹשְׁכִי:


**tore** Heb. ויגז, tore out, as in (Jer. 7:29), “Tear out (גזי) your hair for your great men,” which Jonathan renders: תלישה tear out, and like (Ps. 71:6) “From my mother’s womb You pull me (גוזי),” pull me.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Job 1:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:21:1)


**מִבֶּטֶן אִמִּי.** הָאֲדָמָה אֲשֶׁר לוּקַחְתִּי מִשָּׁם (בראשית ג:כג), אוֹ אִמִּי מַמָּשׁ:


**From my mother’s womb** the earth, “whence I was taken,” or actually, “my mother.”


###### Rashi on Job 1:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:21:2)


**וְעָרוֹם אָשׁוּב שָׁמָּה.** לֹא בַּבֶּטֶן הוּא מְדַבֵּר. וּמַה שָׁמָּה? אֶל מְקוֹם תְּשׁוּבָתוֹ. וְעַל שֶׁהוּא מוּכָן לְכָךְ, וְלֹא יַחֲלִיף הַחֹק לָשׁוּב כִּי אִם אֶל הֶעָפָר, לְפִיכָךְ לֹא הֻצְרַךְ לְהַזְכִּירוֹ:


**and I will return there naked** He is not referring to the womb. What then is the meaning of “there”? To the place of his return, for which he is destined, and he will not change the law, to return anywhere but to the earth. Therefore, it was not necessary to mention it.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Job 1:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:22:1)


**תִּפְלָה.** לֹא שׁוּם שֶׁמֶץ וְתִפְלוּת נָתַן עַל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, כְּמוֹ (ירמיהו כג:יג) ״וּבִנְבִיאֵי שׁוֹמְרוֹן רָאִיתִי תִפְלָה״:


**unseemliness** Heb. תפלה. He did not ascribe any blemish or unseemliness to the Holy One, Blessed be He. Similarly (Jer. 23:13), “And in the prophets of Samaria I saw unseemliness (תפלה).”


###### Rashi on Job 1:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/1:22:2)


**וְלֹא נָתַן.** לְהַטִּיל תִּפְלָה וְעוֹל אֶל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, כְּמוֹ ״נָתְנוּ לְדָוִד רְבָבוֹת וְלִי נָתְנוּ הָאֲלָפִים״ (שמואל א יח:ח):


**neither did he ascribe** Heb. נתן lit. gave, to ascribe unseemliness and injustice to the Holy One, blessed be He, like (I Sam. 18:8) “They ascribed (נתנו) to David ten thousands, and to me they ascribed (נתנו) the thousands.”



## Chapter 2


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 2:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:2:1)


**אֵי מִזֶּה תָּבוֹא.** אַיֵּה מָקוֹם שֶׁתּוּכַל לוֹמַר: מִזֶּה אֲנִי בָּא?


**“Where are you coming from?”** Where is the place about which you may say, “From this place I come”?



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 2:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:3:1)


**מַחֲזִיק.** אוֹחֵז, כְּמוֹ ״וַיִּשְׁלַח יָדוֹ וַיַּחֲזֶק בּוֹ״ (שמות ד:ד), ״מַחֲזִיק בְּאָזְנֵי כֶלֶב״ (משלי כו:יז):


**maintains** Heb. מחזיק, holds, as in (Exod. 4:4) “and he held (ויחזק) onto it”; (Prov. 26:17), “One who holds (מחזיק) onto a dog’s ears.”


###### Rashi on Job 2:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:3:2)


**וַתְּסִיתֵנִי בוֹ.** הֲסִיתַנִי לְבָלְעוֹ. כָּל לְשׁוֹן הֲסָתָה אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא לְשׁוֹן הַמְשָׁכָה, שֶׁמַּמְשִׁיךְ אֶת הָאָדָם לַעֲצָתוֹ:


**Yet you enticed Me against him** You enticed Me to destroy him. Every expression of הֲסָתָה is only an expression of attracting a person to his counsel.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 2:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:4:1)


**עוֹר בְּעַד עוֹר.** אֵבֶר בִּפְנֵי אֵבֶר. כֵּן דֶּרֶךְ בְּנֵי אָדָם: כְּשֶׁרוֹאֶה הַחֶרֶב בָּאָה עַל רֹאשׁוֹ, מֵגִין בִּזְרוֹעוֹ לְפָנָיו. וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן – **כֹּל אֲשֶׁר לָאִישׁ בְּמָמוֹן יִתֵּן**:


**Skin for skin** One limb for another limb. It is human nature that, when one sees a sword coming toward his head, he protects it with his arm in front of it. Surely a person will give all the money he has.


###### Rashi on Job 2:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:4:2)


**בְּעַד נַפְשׁוֹ.** בִּפְנֵי נַפְשׁוֹ, לְהָגִין. לְפִי שֶׁזֶּה יוֹדֵעַ בְּנַפְשׁוֹ שֶׁנִּתְחַיֵּב, לְפִיכָךְ אֵינוֹ מִצְטַעֵר עַל מָמוֹנוֹ:


**for his life** For his life to protect it. Because this person knows that he is liable to lose his life, he does not worry about his money.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 2:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:5:1)


**אוּלָם.** אֲבָל:


**But** Heb. אולם, but.


###### Rashi on Job 2:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:5:2)


**אִם לֹא.** תִּרְאֶה בּוֹ מִיָּד, שֶׁעַל פָּנֶיךָ יְבָרֲכֶךָּ:


**will he not** Will You not see immediately that he will blaspheme You to Your face?



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 2:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:6:1)


**אַךְ אֶת נַפְשׁוֹ שְׁמֹר.** שֶׁלֹּא תֵצֵא. מִכָּאן אָמְרוּ חֲכָמִים (בבא בתרא טז.): קָשֶׁה הָיָה צַעֲרוֹ שֶׁל שָׂטָן יוֹתֵר מִשֶּׁל אִיּוֹב; כְּאוֹמֵר לַחֲבֵירוֹ: שְׁבֹר חָבִית זוֹ וּשְׁמֹר יֵינָהּ:


**but preserve his life** that it does not go out. From here the Sages said, “The Adversary’s pain was greater than Job’s. This is analogous to one saying to his friend, ‘Break this barrel but preserve its wine.’”



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 2:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:8:1)


**לְהִתְגָּרֵד בּוֹ.** בְּדָלֶ״ת כָּתוּב, וּלְשׁוֹן מִשְׁנָה הִיא: גּוֹרְדוֹ וּמַעֲמִידוֹ עַל גִּלְדּוֹ (ראש השנה כז:). אֲבָל גּוֹרֵר בְּרֵי״שׁ אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא לְשׁוֹן סְחִיבָה, כְּמוֹ גּוֹרֵר אָדָם מִטָּה וְסַפְסָל (שבת כב.):


**to scratch himself with** Heb. להתגרד. This word is spelled with a “dalet.” It is a Mishnaic expression (Rosh Hashanah 27b): “If he scraped it (גרדו) and reduced it to its normal size,” but גורר with a “reish” is only an expression of dragging, like (Shabbath 29b), “A person may drag a bed or a bench.”



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Job 2:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:9:1)


**מַחֲזִיק.** אוֹחֵז:


**maintain** Heb. מחזיק, hold.


###### Rashi on Job 2:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:9:2)


**וָמֻת:** לְשׁוֹן צִוּוּי, כְּמוֹ ״וּמֻת בָּהָר״ (דברים לב:נ):


**and die** Heb. ומת. This is an imperative expression, as (Deut. 32: 50), “And die on the mountain.”



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 2:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:10:1)


**גַּם.** לֹא דַי שֶׁבְּרָאָנוּ, אֶלָּא **גַּם אֶת הַטּוֹב נְקַבֵּל וְאֶת הָרָע לֹא נְקַבֵּל**?! בִּתְמִיהַּ:


**also** Is it not enough that He created us? Should we also accept the good, but not accept the evil? This is a wonder.


###### Rashi on Job 2:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:10:2)


**בִּשְׂפָתָיו.** אֲבָל בְּלִבּוֹ חָטָא:


**with his lips** but in his heart he did sin.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 2:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:11:1)


**רֵעֵי אִיּוֹב.** אוֹהֲבָיו:


**Job’s three friends** Heb. רעי, those who loved him.


###### Rashi on Job 2:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:11:2)


**לָנוּד.** כְּמוֹ ״מִי יָנוּד לָךְ״ (ישעיהו נא:יט), ״לֹא תָנֻדוּ לוֹ״ (ירמיהו כב:י):


**to bemoan** Heb. לנוד, as in (Isa. 51:19): “who will lament (ינוד) for you?”; (Jer. 22:10), “neither bemoan (תנדו) him.”



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 2:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:12:1)


**וְלֹא הִכִּירוּהוּ.** כִּי נִשְׁתַּנּוּ פָנָיו מֵחֲמַת הַיִּסּוּרִין:


**but they did not recognize him** because his face had changed as a result of the agonies.


###### Rashi on Job 2:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:12:2)


**הַשָּׁמָיְמָה.** כְּלוֹמַר: לְמַעְלָה מֵרָאשֵׁיהֶם (הַרְבֵּה), וְהוּא הָיָה לָהֶם מִנְהַג אֵבֶל:


**towards heaven** i.e., high over their heads. That was their mourning custom.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 2:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/2:13:1)


**לָאָרֶץ.** עַל הָאָרֶץ, לְצַעֵר בְּצַעֲרוֹ:


**on the ground** On the ground, to share his pain.



## Chapter 3


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 3:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:2:1)


**(מענה איוב)** 
**וַיַּעַן.** וַיִּצְעַק, שֶׁכָּל עֲנִיָּה הָאֲמוּרָה בַּתּוֹרָה אֵינָהּ אֶלָּא לְשׁוֹן הֲרָמַת קוֹל, וְאָב לְכֻלָּם: ״וְעָנוּ הַלְוִיִּם קוֹל רָם״ (דברים כז:יד):


**Job’s Address**
**Now Job raised his voice** Heb. ויען, he cried out, for every expression of עניה in the Torah is only an expression of raising the voice, and the “father” of them all is (Deut. 27:14): “The Levites shall raise their voices (וענו) etc. with a loud voice.”



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 3:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:3:1)


**אִוָּלֶד בּוֹ.** לְשׁוֹן עָתִיד, שֶׁהָיָה אוֹמֵר: הַלְוַאי וְיֹאבַד הַיּוֹם שֶׁהָיִיתִי עָתִיד לְהִוָּלֵד בּוֹ, וְאָז לֹא הָיִיתִי נוֹלָד:


**in which I was to be born** This is a future expression, that he was saying, “Would that the day in which I was destined to be born be lost, and then I would not have been born.”


###### Rashi on Job 3:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:3:2)


**וְהַלַּיְלָה.** אַף הִיא תֹּאבַד:


**and the night** That too should have been lost.


###### Rashi on Job 3:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:3:3)


**אָמַר.** שֶׁיֹּאמַר הַמְבַשֵּׂר אֲשֶׁר עָלָיו הַדָּבָר מֻטָּל:


**when one said** that the reporter, upon whom it was incumbent [to report] would say.


###### Rashi on Job 3:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:3:4)


**הוֹרָה גָבֶר.** שֶׁעִיבֵּר אָבִי אֶת אִמִּי. בִּנְקֵבָה נוֹהֵג לוֹמַר ׳הוֹרָתָה׳, וּבְזָכָר נוֹהֵג לוֹמַר ׳הוֹרָה אֶת הַנְּקֵבָה׳:


**“A man has impregnated.”** That my father impregnated my mother. Concerning a female, it is customary to say הרתה, she conceived, and concerning a male, it is customary to say, הורה, he impregnated the female.


###### Rashi on Job 3:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:3:5)


**וְהַלַּיְלָה אָמַר.** זֶה אֶחָד מִן הַמְּקוֹמוֹת שֶׁחִסֵּר הַמִּקְרָא אֶת דְּבָרָיו, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁלֹּא הֻצְרַךְ לְפָרֵשׁ מִי הוּא; וּמְלִיצָתוֹ: אָמַר הָאוֹמֵר. (וְדוֹמֶה לוֹ ״הֲיָרֻצוּן בַּסֶּלַע סוּסִים אִם יַחֲרוֹשׁ בַּבְּקָרִים״ (עמוס ו:יב)) – הָיָה לוֹ לוֹמַר: אִם יַחֲרוֹשׁ אָדָם בַּבְּקָרִים. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה אָמְרוּ (נדה טז:): מַלְאָךְ הַמְמֻנֶּה עַל הַהֵרָיוֹן, ׳לַיְלָה׳ שְׁמוֹ:


**and the night when one said** This is one of the places where Scripture abbreviates its words because it is unnecessary to explain who [the subject] is, and the expression means, “the sayer said.” [Similar to this is (Amos. 6:12), “Will horses run on the rock, or will one plow with cattle?” He should have said, “Will a man plow with cattle?”] However, our Sages said, “The angel appointed over conception is named Laylah.”



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 3:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:4:1)


**הַיּוֹם הַהוּא יְהִי חֹשֶׁךְ.** תָּמִיד בְּכָל שָׁנָה, כְּשֶׁיַּגִּיעַ אוֹתוֹ יוֹם:


**May that day be dark** constantly; every year, when that day arrives.


###### Rashi on Job 3:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:4:2)


**אַל יִדְרְשֵׁהוּ אֱלוֹהַּ מִמָּעַל.** לְטוֹבָה:


**let God not seek it from above** for good.


###### Rashi on Job 3:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:4:3)


**וְאַל תּוֹפַע עָלָיו נְהָרָה.** אוֹרָה, כְּמוֹ ״צֹהַר״ (בראשית ו:טז), וּמְתַרְגְּמִינָן ״נִיהוֹר״ (תרגום אונקלוס):


**and let no light shine upon it** Heb. נהרה, light, like צהר, which is translated נהור.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 3:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:5:1)


**יִגְאָלֻהוּ.** כְּמוֹ ״לֶחֶם מְגֹאָל״ (מלאכי א:ז), ״בַּמֶּה גֵאַלְנוּךָ״ (מלאכי א:ז), וְהוּא לְשׁוֹן לִכְלוּךְ:


**defile it** Heb. יגאלהו, like (Malachi 1:7), “defiled (מגאל) bread”; “How have we defiled You (גאלנוך)?” an expression of contamination.


###### Rashi on Job 3:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:5:2)


**צַלְמָוֶת.** צֵל שֶׁל מָוֶת (חֹשֶׁךְ), שֶׁאֵינוֹ מֵאִיר לְעוֹלָם:


**the shadow of death** Heb. צלמות, the shadow of death, darkness that never lights up.


###### Rashi on Job 3:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:5:3)


**עֲנָנָה.** אֲפֵלָה:


###### Rashi on Job 3:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:5:4)


**כְּמְרִירֵי יוֹם.** שֵׁדִים הַמּוֹשְׁלִים בַּצָּהֳרַיִם, כְּמוֹ ״קֶטֶב מְרִירִי״ (דברים לב:כד), וְהוּא מוֹשֵׁל בַּצָּהֳרַיִם, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וּמִקֶּטֶב יָשׁוּד צָהֳרָיִם״ (תהלים צא:ו):


**like demons of the day** Heb. כמרירי, demons who rule at noon, like (Deut. 32: 34), “Ketev Meriri,” who rules at noon; as it is stated (Ps. 91:6): “from Ketev, who rules at noontime.”



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 3:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:6:1)


**אַל יִחַדְּ.** אַל יִשְׂמַח, כְּמוֹ ״וַיִּחַדְּ יִתְרוֹ״ (שמות יח:ט). פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר: אַל יִתְאַחֵד (סא״א):


**it shall not rejoice among the days** Heb. אל יחד, it shall not rejoice, as in (Exod. 18:9), “And Jethro rejoiced (וַיִחַד).” (Another explanation is: It shall not join. This does not appear in certain editions.)



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 3:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:7:1)


**גַּלְמוּד.** יָחִיד, וְחָסֵר מֵאָדָם וּמִכָּל בְּרִיָּה:


**lonely** Alone and lacking any man or creature.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 3:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:8:1)


**יִקְּבֻהוּ.** יְקַלְלוּהוּ, כְּמוֹ ״קָבָה לִי״ (במדבר כב:יז):


**curse it** Heb. יקבהו, curse it, as in (Num. 22:17), “curse (קבה) for me.”


###### Rashi on Job 3:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:8:2)


**אוֹרְרֵי יוֹם.** הַמְקַלְּלִים אֶת יְמֵיהֶם, לְפִי שֶׁהֵם מְכַוְּנִים לְקַלֵּל קְלָלָה מִתּוֹךְ צַעֲרָם:


**those who curse the day** Those who curse their days, since they intend to pronounce a curse because of their anguish.


###### Rashi on Job 3:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:8:3)


**הָעֲתִידִים עוֹרֵר לִוְיָתָן.** לִהְיוֹת עֲרִירִים בְּמַחְבַּרְתָּם, לְיַחֵד לִוּוּי שֶׁלָּהֶם מֵחֶבְרַת אִישׁ וְאִשְׁתּוֹ, מֵאֵין בָּנִים. וּבַגְּמָרָא יְרוּשַׁלְמִי רָאִיתִי (מועד קטן א,ה): **עוֹרֵר לִוְיָתָן.** לְקוֹנֵן עַל לִוּוּי שֶׁלָּהֶם כְּשֶׁיָּמוּת, כְּמוֹ (מועד קטן ח.): לֹא יְעַרְעֵר עַל מֵתוֹ:


**those destined to be childless in their union** Heb. ערר לויתן, to be childless (ערירים) in their union; to join their mate in the union of man and wife, without children. And I saw in the Jerusalem Talmud (Moed Katan 1:5) that עֹרֵר means, to lament their mate when he dies, as in (Moed Katan 1:6): A person may not inspire lamentations (יערער) for his dead relative.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Job 3:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:9:1)


**נִשְׁפּוֹ.** חֹשֶׁךְ אִישׁוֹנוֹ:


**its evening** Heb. נשפו, the darkness of its night.


###### Rashi on Job 3:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:9:2)


**בְּעַפְעַפֵּי.** בְּזָהֳרוּרֵי, כְּמוֹ ״וְעַפְעַפֵּינוּ יִזְּלוּ מָיִם״ (ירמיהו ט:יז):


**the rays of** Heb. עפעפי, rays of, like (Jer. 9:17), “and our eyelids (or pupils) (ועפעפינו) gush water.”



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 3:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:10:1)


**כִּי לֹא סָגַר.** מִי שֶׁיְּכֹלֶת בְּיָדוֹ לִסְגּוֹר. וְגַם זֶה מִן הַמִּקְרָאוֹת דּוֹמִין לְ״הַלַּיְלָה אָמַר״ (לעיל ג:ג):


**For He did not shut** He Who was able to shut. This too is one of the verses that resemble “the night when one said” (verse 2).


###### Rashi on Job 3:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:10:2)


**דַּלְתֵי בִטְנִי.** שֶׁיָּצָאתִי מִמֶּנּוּ, שֶׁאִם עָשָׂה כֵּן, **וַיַּסְתֵּר עָמָל מֵעֵינָי**:


**the doors of my womb** from which I emerged, for had He done so, He would have hidden trouble from my eyes.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 3:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:11:1)


**לָמָּה לֹא מֵרֶחֶם אָמוּת.** מִיצִיאָתִי מֵרֶחֶם (הָיִיתִי) מוּכָן לָמוּת מִיָּד. וּמְדַבֵּר בִּלְשׁוֹן עָתִיד, כְּאִלּוּ עוֹמֵד עַכְשָׁיו בְּיוֹם יְצִיאַת הָרֶחֶם, וְאוֹמֵר: (לָמָּה) לֹא אָמוּת עַתָּה, וְלָמָּה לֹא מִבֶּטֶן יָצָאתִי וְאֶגְוָע. לָמָּה לֹא הָיְתָה לִי גְּוִיעָה מוּכֶנֶת:


**Why did I not die from the womb?** why was I not ready to die immediately upon my emergence from the womb? He speaks in the future tense, as though he were standing now on the day of his emergence from the womb and saying, “Why will I not die now, and why did I not... **emerge from the belly and perish?”** Why was my death not imminent?



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 3:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:12:1)


**מַדּוּעַ קִדְּמוּנִי.** בִּרְכֵּי אִמִּי קוֹדֶם לְמִיתָה:


**Why did knees receive me** Why did the knees of my mother receive me before my death?


###### Rashi on Job 3:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:12:2)


**כִּי אִינָק.** נִגְזַר עָלַי לִינֹק מֵהֶם. לְפִי שֶׁהוּא מְדַבֵּר בְּיוֹם לֵידָה, הוּא מַזְכִּיר דְּבָרִים שֶׁל אַחֲרָיו בִּלְשׁוֹן עָתִיד:


**that I should suck** [Why] was it decreed upon me to suck them? As he is speaking on the day of his birth, he mentions those subsequent events in the future tense.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 3:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:13:1)


**שָׁכַבְתִּי.** הָיִיתִי שׁוֹכֵב בַּקֶּבֶר:


**I would be lying** Heb. ושכבתי, I would be lying in the grave.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 3:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:14:1)


**הַבּוֹנִים חֳרָבוֹת לָמוֹ.** זֶהוּ דֶּרֶךְ לְמַעֲמִידֵי שֵׁם, לִבְנוֹת עָרִים נֶחֱרָבוֹת לְזִכָּרוֹן לָהֶם; כְּמוֹ ״וּבָנוּ מִמְּךָ חָרְבוֹת עוֹלָם וְקֹרֵא לְךָ גֹּדֵר פֶּרֶץ״ (ישעיה נח:יב). כְּלוֹמַר: עִם שָׂרִים וּבַעֲלֵי שֵׁם. אוֹ הַלְוַאי שֶׁלֹּא נוֹדַעְתִּי וְאֶהְיֶה כְּנֵפֶל. וּמַה שֶּׁכָּתַב לֹא אֶהְיֶה **כְנֵפֶל טָמוּן** – מִפְּנֵי שֶׁעוֹמֵד עַל לָשׁוֹן שֶׁהִתְחִיל לְדַבֵּר, בִּלְשׁוֹן תֵּימַהּ; וּמוּסָב עַל **לָמָּה לֹּא, מַדּוּעַ קִדְּמוּנִי בִרְכָּיִם**, אוֹ מַדּוּעַ לֹא אֶהְיֶה **כְנֵפֶל טָמוּן**:


**who build ruins for themselves** It is customary for those who seek fame to rebuild ruined cities as a memorial for themselves, as in (Isa. 58: 12), “And those coming from you shall be called the repairer of the breaches.” That is to say, with princes and famous people. Or, would that I was unknown, and I would be like a stillborn. What Scripture states: I was not like a hidden stillborn, is because it continues with the language with which it started to speak, viz. in the interrogative, and it refers to “Why did knees receive me...or why was I not like a hidden stillborn child?”



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 3:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:16:1)


**כְּעוֹלְלִים.** תִּינוֹקוֹת. וּלְכָךְ קוֹרֵא אוֹתָם עוֹלְלִים, שֶׁכֻּלָּם שְׂחוֹק וְלִכְלוּךְ; כְּמוֹ ״וְעֹלַלְתִּי בֶעָפָר״ (איוב טז:טו), וּכְמוֹ ״וַיִּתְעַלְּלוּ בָהּ כָּל הַלָּיְלָה״ (שופטים יט:כה):


**like infants** Heb. כעוללים, infants. He calls them עוללים because they are all play and dirt, like (below 16:15), “And I sullied (ועללתי) with dust,” and like (Jud. 19:25), “and abused (ויתעללו) her the entire night.”



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Job 3:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:17:1)


**חָדְלוּ רֹגֶז.** נִמְנָעִים מֵרוּגְזָם, שֶׁהָיוּ נוֹהֲגִים וּרְגִילִים לְהַרְגִּיז בָּאָרֶץ:


**cease from anger** They restrain their anger, with which they were accustomed and wont to cause anger in the earth.


###### Rashi on Job 3:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:17:2)


**וְשָׁם יָנוּחוּ.** וְשָׁם נָחוּ, דָּבָר הַהוֹוֶה כְּתָבוֹ בִּלְשׁוֹן עָתִיד:


**and there...rest** Heb. ינוחו, and there rested. Something that occurs regularly is written in the future tense.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 3:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:19:1)


**קָטֹן וְגָדוֹל שָׁם.** שָׁם נִכָּר מִי חָשׁוּב וּמִי שָׁפָל, וּלְפִי שֶׁאוֹתָהּ גְּדֻלָּה גְּדֻלַּת עוֹלָם הוּא, וְאוֹתוֹ שִׁפְלוּת שִׁפְלוּת עוֹלָם הִיא:


**Small and great are there** There it is obvious who is esteemed and who is humble, and since that greatness is perpetual greatness, and that humility is perpetual humility...



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 3:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:20:1)


**לָמָּה יִתֵּן.** הַנּוֹתֵן, וְהוּא הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**Why does He give** (the giver, i.e., the Holy One, blessed be He.)


###### Rashi on Job 3:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:20:2)


**לְעָמָל אוֹר.** לְמִי שֶׁגָּדַל בְּעָמָל וְצָרָה, לָמָּה נוֹתֵן לוֹ אוֹר עוֹלָם, וְלֹא הֱמִיתוֹ בְּהִוָּלְדוֹ?


**the toiler light** He who grew up with toil and trouble—why did He give him perpetual light and not slay him at birth?



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Job 3:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:21:1)


**הַמְחַכִּים לַמָּוֶת.** לְמָרֵי נֶפֶשׁ הַתְּאֵבִים וּמִתְאַוִּים לַמָּוֶת, וְקוֹבְלִים לֵאמֹר: **אֵינֶנּוּ**:


**Who await death** to those of bitter spirit, who long for and desire death and complain, saying, “it is not here.”


###### Rashi on Job 3:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:21:2)


**וַיַּחְפְּרֻהוּ.** מְרַגְּלִים אַחֲרָיו:


**and they spy after it** Heb. ויתפרהו, and they spy after it.


###### Rashi on Job 3:21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:21:3)


**מִמַּטְמוֹנִים.** יוֹתֵר מִשֶּׁמְּרַגְּלִים אַחַר מַטְמוֹנֵי אוֹצָרוֹת. וְהָיוּ:


**more than after hidden treasures** More than people spy after hidden treasures.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Job 3:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:22:1)


**שְׂמֵחִים אֱלֵי גִיל.** וְיָשִׂישׂוּ אִם הָיוּ מוֹצְאִים קָבֶר:


**Those who are happy over joyous occurrences** and rejoice if they find a grave.


###### Rashi on Job 3:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:22:2)


**אֱלֵי גִיל.** בִּשְׁבִיל הַגִּילָה וְהַשָּׂשׂוֹן שֶׁל **מְצִיאַת קָבֶר**:


**over joyous occurrences** Because of the joy and the happiness of finding a grave.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Job 3:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:23:1)


**לְגֶבֶר אֲשֶׁר דַּרְכּוֹ נִסְתָּרָה.** כָּל טוֹבוֹת אֲשֶׁר פָּעַל, נִסְתְּרוּ מֵאֵת בַּעַל הַגְּמוּל, וְלֹא הִבִּיט בָּהֶם:


**for a man whose way is hidden** All the good deeds that he performed were hidden from the Great Recompenser, and He did not look at them.


###### Rashi on Job 3:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:23:2)


**וַיָּסֶךְ אֱלוֹהַּ בַּעֲדוֹ.** עָשָׂה מְחִיצָּה לְפָנָיו, לְסוֹגְרוֹ בְּמַסְגֵּר. כְּמוֹ ״הִנְנִי שָׂךְ אֶת דַּרְכֵּךְ״ (הושע ב:ח):


**and God hedged him in** He made a wall in front of him to shut him up in a prison, as in (Hos. 2:8) “Behold I will close off your way.”



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Job 3:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:24:1)


**תָּבֹא.** בָּאָה:


**comes** Heb. תבא, lit. shall come.


###### Rashi on Job 3:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:24:2)


**וַיִּתְּכוּ.** לְשׁוֹן ״נִתַּךְ אַרְצָה״ (שמות ט:לג), נִגָּרִים וְיוֹרְדִים כַּמַּיִם. וְיֵשׁ לְפָרֵשׁ ׳נְתִיכָה׳ לְשׁוֹן ׳רִבְּיָה׳:


**pour** An expression of (Exod. 9:33) “pour (נתך) to the ground.” They run and descend like water. The word ויתכו may also be interpreted as an expression denoting abundance.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Job 3:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:25:1)


**פַּחַד פָּחַדְתִּי.** כָּל יָמַי עָמַדְתִּי בְּפַחַד עַל זֶה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַרְנוּ ״וַיְהִי כִּי הִקִּיפוּ וְגוֹ׳״ (איוב א:ה):


**the thing I feared** All my life, I stood in fear of this, as I said (1:5): “Now it would come about, when the cycle of the feasting would days be over, etc.”


###### Rashi on Job 3:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:25:2)


**וַאֲשֶׁר יָגֹרְתִּי.** הֵן עַתָּה הוּעְתַּד וְנִגְזַר שֶׁיָּבֹא לִי:


What I dreaded—Behold!



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Job 3:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/3:26:1)


**לֹא שָׁלַוְתִּי.** מִדְּאָגָה זוֹ, וְהִנֵּה **וַיָּבֹא רֹגֶז**:


**I was not at ease** from this worry, and behold, trouble has come.



## Chapter 4


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Job 4:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:1:1)


**(מענה אליפז)** אֱלִיפַז בֶּן עֵשָׂו הָיָה (בראשית לו:ד), וְעַל יְדֵי שֶׁנִּתְגַּדֵּל בְּחֵיקוֹ שֶׁל יִצְחָק, זָכָה שֶׁתִּשְׁרֶה עָלָיו שְׁכִינָה:


**Eliphaz’s Address**
**Eliphaz** He was Esau’s son, and because he was nurtured in Isaac’s bosom, he merited that the Shechinah rest upon him.


###### Rashi on Job 4:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:1:2)


**הַתֵּימָנִי.** מֵאֶרֶץ תֵּימָן, מֵאַרְצוֹ שֶׁל עֵשָׂו (בראשית לו:לד) (סא״א):


**the Temanite** From the land of Tema, Esau’s country. (Not found in some editions.)



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 4:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:2:1)


**הֲנִסָּה.** לְשׁוֹן תֵּימַהּ: הֲבַּעֲבוּר שֶׁנִּסָּה דָבָר אֵלֶיךָ תִּלְאֶה? בְּנִסָּיוֹן אֶחָד שֶׁנִּסְּךָ בּוֹרַאֲךָ וּבָא אֵלֶיךָ, אַתָּה נִלְאֶה?! וּמֵעַתָּה, **עֲצֹר בְּמִלִּין מִי יוּכַל.** מִלְּהַשִׁיבְךָ?


**Because He tested** This is an expression of wonder. Because He tested you with one thing, should you weary? With one rest that the Holy One, blessed be He, tested you, you have become weary. From now on, who will be able to withhold his words from answering you?



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 4:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:3:1)


**הִנֵּה יִסַּרְתָּ רַבִּים.** שֶׁהִרְבּוּ דְבָרִים, הוֹכַחְתָּם וְיִסַּרְתָּם:


**Behold, you have chastised many** who multiplied words; you admonished them and chastised them.


###### Rashi on Job 4:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:3:2)


**וְיָדַיִם רָפוֹת.** וִיגוּרוֹת מִפּוּרְעָנוּת הַבָּא עֲלֵיהֶם, אַתָּה הָיִיתָ רָגִיל לְחַזְּקָם, וְלוֹמַר: אַל תִּירָא, שֶׁכָּךְ הוּא מִדַּת הַדִּין:


**and...weak hands** of those who feared the retribution that came upon them. You used to strengthen [them] and say, “Fear not, for that is the Divine Standard of Justice.”



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 4:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:4:1)


**יְקִימוּן.** רְגִילוֹת הֵם מִלֶּיךָ לְהָקִים אֶת הַכּוֹשֵׁל:


**would pick up** Your words were wont to pick up the stumbler.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 4:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:6:1)


**הֲלֹא.** עַתָּה סוֹפְךָ מוֹכִיחַ עַל תְּחִלָּתְךָ, שֶׁיִּרְאָתְךָ, שֶׁהָיִיתָ יְרֵא שָׁמַיִם:


**Surely** now, your end reveals your beginning, that your fear, that you feared heaven...


###### Rashi on Job 4:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:6:2)


**כִּסְלָתֶךָ.** הִיא (בבא מציעא נח.) מֵחֲמַת כְּסִילוּת הִיא, וְלֹא מִדַּעַת שְׁלֵימָה. וְכֵן **תִּקְוָתְךָ וְתֹם דְּרָכֶיךָ** הַכֹּל כְּסִילוּת:


**your foolishness** is due to foolishness and not to full understanding, and so are your hope and the sincerity of your ways all foolishness.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 4:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:7:1)


**נִכְחָדוּ.** נֶעֶלְמוּ וְנֶאֶבְדוּ, כְּמוֹ ״וַתִּכָּחֵד מִן הָאָרֶץ״ (שמות ט:טו):


**destroyed** Heb. נכחדו, disappeared and perished, like (Exod. 9:15), “and you would vanish (ותכחד) from the earth.”



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 4:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:8:1)


**כַּאֲשֶׁר רָאִיתִי.** אֲשֶׁר (כֵּן) נִכְחֲדוּ חוֹרְשֵׁי אָוֶן וּמְכִינִים אֶת הָאָוֶן בְּמַחֲשַׁבְתָּם, כְּחוֹרֵשׁ זֶה שֶׁמֵּכִין לִפְנֵי הַזְּרִיעָה, וְאַחֲרֵי כֵן זוֹרְעִים בְּמַעֲשֵׂיהֶם אֶת הֶעָמָל; וְרָאִיתִי, שֶׁהֵם **יִקְצְרֻהוּ.** כְּלוֹמַר: רָעָתָם שֶׁבָּאָה עֲלֵיהֶם. וְרָאִיתִי, כִּי:


**As I have seen** that those who plow violence and prepare the iniquity in their thoughts, like a plower who prepares [the field] before sowing, afterwards they sow mischief with their deeds. And I saw that they will reap it—i.e., their evil that comes upon them, and I saw that.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Job 4:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:9:1)


**מִנִּשְׁמַת אֱלוֹהַּ יֹאבֵדוּ.** מִנְּשִׁיבַת סַעֲרָתוֹ וְכַעֲסוֹ:


**From the breath of God they will perish** From the blowing of His stormwind and His anger.


###### Rashi on Job 4:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:9:2)


**וּמֵרוּחַ אַפּוֹ יִכְלוּ.** כָּלִים תָּמִיד, וְעַל דּוֹר הַמַּבּוּל דִּבֵּר (בראשית ו:יג):


**and from the wind of His nostrils** they are always destroyed, and he spoke of the Generation of the Flood.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 4:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:10:1)


**שַׁאֲגַת אַרְיֵה וְקוֹל שָׁחַל וְגוֹ׳.** הָאֲרִי גָּדוֹל, וְהַשַּׁחַל בֵּינוֹנִי, וְהַכְּפִיר קָטָן; כֻּלָּם נִתָּעוּ. כְּלוֹמַר: הַמְּלָכִים וְהַשָּׂרִים וְהָעֲבָדִים. וְכָל שֵׁמוֹת הַלָּלוּ שְׁמוֹת אַרְיֵה הֵם:


**The roar of an old lion, the voice of a young lion** The אריה is the old one, the שַׁחַל the middle one, and the כפיר the cub; all of them were misled, i.e., the kings, the princes and the slaves; all these names are names of the lion.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 4:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:11:1)


**לַיִשׁ אוֹבֵד מִבְּלִי טָרֶף.** הַשָּׂרִים אָבְדוּ כְּלַיִשׁ זֶה הָאוֹבֵד מִבְּלִי מָזוֹן, כָּךְ אָבַד כֹּחָם מֵעֲשׂוֹת חָמָס. אוֹבֵד אֵינוֹ לְשׁוֹן אָדָם הָאוֹבֵד דָּבָר מִמֶּנּוּ, אֶלָּא הוּא עַצְמוֹ הוֹלֵךְ לְאִבּוּד, כְּמוֹ ״תָּעִיתִי כְּשֶׂה אֹבֵד״ (תהילים קיט:קעו), וּכְמוֹ ״הָיִיתִי כִּכְלִי אֹבֵד״ (תהילים לא:יג):


**A lion perishes without prey** The princes perished like a lion who perishes without food; so did their strength to commit violence dissipate. The word אֹבֵד is not an expression of a man who loses something, but [of a man who has] himself become lost, like (Ps. 119.176), “I have gone astray like a lost (אבד) sheep.” and like (ibid. 31:13), “I was like a lost (אבד) vessel.”


###### Rashi on Job 4:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:11:2)


**וּבְנֵי לָבִיא יִתְפָּרָדוּ.** מְדַבֵּר בְּדוֹר הַפְּלַגָּה, אֲשֶׁר נָפוֹצוּ מִשָּׁם עַל פְּנֵי כָל הָאָרֶץ (בראשית יא:ט):


**and the young of the lion will scatter** He is speaking of the Generation of the Dispersion, who were scattered from there [Babel] over the face of the entire earth.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 4:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:12:1)


**וְאֵלַי דָּבָר יְגֻנָּב.** חוֹזֵר עַל תּוֹכַחְתּוֹ שֶׁהוֹכִיחוֹ עַד הֵנָּה: מַה תִּזְעַק? **זְכָר נָא מִי הוּא נָקִי אָבַד וְגוֹ׳כַּאֲשֶׁר רָאִיתִי וְגוֹ׳**, וְעַל אוֹדוֹתֶיךָ **אֵלַי דָּבָר** נְבוּאָה **יְגֻנָּב.** מִן הַסִּגְנוֹן, כְּדָבָר גָּנוּב; לְפִי שֶׁאֵין רוּחַ הַקֹּדֶשׁ נִגְלֶה עַל נְבִיאֵי עוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים בְּפַרְהֶסְיָא. מָשָׁל לְמֶלֶךְ, שֶׁהָיְתָה לוֹ אִשָּׁה וּפִילֶגֶשׁ; כְּשֶׁבָּא אֵצֶל אִשְׁתּוֹ בָּא בְּגָלוּי, וְאֵצֶל פִּילַגְשׁוֹ בָּא בְּסֵתֶר וּבִגְנֵבָה. כָּךְ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לִנְבִיאֵי עוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים: ״וַיָּבֹא אֱלֹהִים אֶל אֲבִימֶלֶךְ בַּחֲלוֹם הַלָּיְלָה״ (בראשית כ:ג); וְכֵן ״וַיָּבֹא אֱלֹהִים אֶל לָבָן וְגוֹ׳״ (בראשית לא:כד); לְבִלְעָם: ״נֹפֵל וּגְלוּי עֵינָיִם״ (במדבר כד:ד); לֶאֱלִיפַז: בַּחֶזְיוֹנוֹת הַלַּיְלָה. אֲבָל בִּנְבִיאֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל כְּתִיב ״פֶּה אֶל פֶּה אֲדַבֶּר בּוֹ בְּמַרְאֶה וְלֹא בְחִידוֹת״ (במדבר יב:ח). בְּמִדְרַשׁ תַּנְחוּמָא מָצָאתִי מֻגֶּה וְל״שׁ:


**Now a word was conveyed secretly to me** He returns to his rebuke with which he has been chastising him till now: Why are you crying? “Remember now who was innocent that perished...As I have seen, etc.” And concerning you a word of prophecy was secretly conveyed to me, [lit. was stolen to me], from the expression like a stolen thing, because the Holy Spirit does not reveal itself to the prophets of the heathens in public. This is analogous to a king who had a wife and a mistress. When he would go to his wife, he would go in public, but to his mistress, he would go clandestinely and stealthily. So it is with the Holy One, blessed be He. Regarding the prophets of the heathens [Scripture states:] (Gen. 20:3), “And God came to Abimelech in the dream of the night,” and similarly (ibid. 31: 24), “And God came to Laban the Aramean in the dream of the night”; to Balaam (Num. 24:4), “who falls, but his eyes are open” [in Gen. Rabbah 52:5 (Num. 22:20), “And God came to Balaam at night”]; to Eliphaz, “in the visions of the night.” But concerning the prophets of Israel it is written (ibid. 12:8): “With him I speak mouth to mouth manifestly and not with riddles.” In Midrash Tanchuma, I found it emended [See Tanchuma Balak 8, Tanchuma Buber Vayishlach 24.]


###### Rashi on Job 4:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:12:2)


**וַתִּקַּח אָזְנִי שֵׁמֶץ מֶנְהוּ.** שֶׁמֶץ מִקְצָת רוּחַ הֲבָנָה:


Now [in] the expression, “And my ear grasped a bit of it,” שֶׁמֶץ, means a bit of the spirit of understanding.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 4:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:13:1)


**בִּשְׂעִפִּים.** בְּמַחֲשָׁבוֹת:



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 4:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:14:1)


**פַּחַד קְרָאַנִי.** מִן הָרוּחַ שֶׁבָּא אֵלַי. וְרוּחַ הוּא מַלְאָךְ, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (תהילים קד:ד) ״עֹשֶׂה מַלְאָכָיו רוּחוֹת״:


**terror befell me** from the spirit that came to me. and the spirit was an angel. as it is stated (Ps. 104:4): “He makes his angels spirits.”



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 4:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:15:1)


**תְּסַמֵּר.** הָרוּחַ שַׂעֲרַת בְּשָׂרִי:


**it made stand on end** The spirit made the hair of my flesh (stand on end].


###### Rashi on Job 4:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:15:2)


**תְּסַמֵּר.** הוּא לְשׁוֹן אָדָם שֶׁעָמְדוּ שַׂעֲרוֹתָיו:


תסמר. That is an expression of a man whose hair stood on end.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 4:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:16:1)


**יַעֲמֹד.** עוֹמֵד הָיָה בְּפָנַי, וְלֹא הָיִיתִי מַכִּיר מַרְאֵהוּ:


**It was standing** It was standing before me, but I did not recognize its appearance.


###### Rashi on Job 4:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:16:2)


**דְּמָמָה וָקוֹל.** קוֹל דָּבָר חֲשַׁאי הָיִיתִי שׁוֹמֵעַ. דָּבָר אַחֵר: דְּמָמָה וָקוֹל אֶשְׁמַע – קוֹל שָׁמַעְתִּי מִדְּמָמָה, אֲבָל דְּמָמָה לֹא שָׁמַעְתִּי. דְּמָמָה – אֵלּוּ הַמַּלְאָכִים שֶׁאוֹמְרִים שִׁירָה, כְּדִמְתַרְגֵּם ״וְאַחַר הָרַעַשׁ קוֹל דְּמָמָה דַקָּה״ (מלכים א יט:יב): ״קָל דִּמְשַׁבְּחִין בַּחֲשַׁאי״. מֹשֶׁה – דְּמָמָה שָׁמַע; מִכָּאן, שֶׁהָרִאשׁוֹנִים נִשְׁתַּמְּשׁוּ בְּקוֹל, וְהָאַחֲרוֹנִים – בְּבַת קוֹל. כְּאָדָם הַמַּכֶּה בְּפַטִּישׁ, וְקוֹל הַהֲבָרָה נִשְׁמַע לְמֵרָחוֹק; כָּךְ הוּא אוֹמֵר:


**a faint voice** I heard the sound of faint words. Another explanation: I heard silence and a voice. I heard the voice of the silence, but the silence I did not hear. “Silence” denotes angels reciting praise silently, as [Jonathan] renders (I Kings 19:12): “a still small voice” as: a voice of those praising quietly. Moses heard the silence [i.e., the voice of the angels praising]—from here we deduce that the early prophets heard [lit. used] the voice, whereas the later ones heard an echo, like a man who hits with a hammer, and the echo resounds far away. From here... far away. Here it is written: silence and a voice, whereas concerning Elijah it is written: a still small voice; because Eliphaz heard only the silencing of the voice, like a person who shouts into a barrel, that the voice of the barrel shouts toward him. That is an echo, not the voice itself, but concerning Elijah if is written: a still, small voice, for he heard the voice itself] And this is what it said.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Job 4:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:17:1)


**הַאֱנוֹשׁ מֵאֱלוֹהַ יִצְדָּק.** בִּתְמִיהַּ:


**Can a mortal be more righteous than God?** This is a wonder.


###### Rashi on Job 4:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:17:2)


**וְאִם מֵעֹשֵׂהוּ יִטְהַר גָּבֶר.** וְאִם יוֹתֵר מֵעוֹשֵׂהוּ יִטְהַר גָּבֶר? וּמָה זֶּה חֲבֵירְךָ יִצְעַק?!


**or can a man be purer than his Maker?** Or can a man be more pure than his Maker?



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Job 4:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:18:1)


**הֵן בַּעֲבָדָיו.** הַצַּדִּיקִים לֹא יַאֲמִין שֶׁלֹּא יֶחֶטְאוּ לוֹ, וּמְסַלְּקָם מִן הָעוֹלָם לִפְנֵי זְמַנָּם:


**Behold...His servants** the righteous. He does not trust that they will not sin against Him, and He takes them out of the world before their time.


###### Rashi on Job 4:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:18:2)


**תָּהֳלָה.** לְשׁוֹן ״הוֹלְלוּת״ (קהלת י:יג):


**reproach** Heb. תהלה, an expression of folly.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 4:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:19:1)


**אַף.** כָּל שֶׁכֵּן:


**Surely** Surely.


###### Rashi on Job 4:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:19:2)


**שׁוֹכְנֵי בָתֵּי חֹמֶר אֲשֶׁר בֶּעָפָר יְסוֹדָם.** קִבְרָם:


**those who dwell in mud houses, whose foundation is in dust,** meaning their graves.


###### Rashi on Job 4:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:19:3)


**יְדַכְּאוּם לִפְנֵי עָשׁ.** מִן הַשָּׁמַיִם מְתִישִׁין אֶת כֹּחָם, עַד שֶׁהֵן שְׁפָלִים וְנִדְכָּאִים לִפְנֵי הָרִמָּה הָאוֹכַלְתָּן בְּמוֹתָן:


**they crush them before the worms** From heaven, their strength is sapped until they are humble and crushed before the worms that eat them in death.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 4:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:20:1)


**מִבֹּקֶר לָעֶרֶב יֻכַּתּוּ.** בְּיוֹם אֶחָד הֵם כְּתוּתִים וְנִדְכָּאִים:


**From morning to evening they are crushed** in one day, they are crushed and subdued.


###### Rashi on Job 4:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:20:2)


**מִבְּלִי מֵשִׂים.** עַל לֵב לָשׁוּב לְקוֹנָם:


**without giving [a thought]** lit. without putting it upon their heart to return to their Maker.


###### Rashi on Job 4:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:20:3)


**לָנֶ֥צַח יֹאבֵֽדוּ.** וְגַם זֶה הוּא מִקְרָא חָסֵר:


**they perish to eternity** This too is an elliptical verse.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Job 4:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/4:21:1)


**הֲלֹא נִסַּע יִתְרָם בָּם.** כְּמוֹ ״יִתְרָה עָשָׂה וְגוֹ׳״ (ישעיהו טו:ז): גַּאֲוָתָם וְחָכְמָתָם תִּתְבַּלַּע בְּגוּפָן (תהלים קז:כז), וְהִנֵּה נוֹסַעַת וְנִסַּחַת, כְּמוֹ ״בַּל יִסַּע יְתֵדוֹתָיו״ (ישעיהו לג:כ):


**Their haughtiness, which is absorbed within them does it not leave [them]?** Heb. יתרם בם, like (Isa. 15:7), “Because of haughty things (יתרה) they did.” Their haughtiness and their wisdom, which is absorbed into their body—behold it will move away and be uprooted from them, like (ibid. 33: 20), “whose pegs shall never be moved (יסע).”



## Chapter 5


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Job 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:1:1)


**קְרָא נָא.** עַד כָּאן דִּבֵּר הַנְּבוּאָה, מִכָּאן וָאֵילָךְ חוֹזֵר לְתוֹכַחְתּוֹ: צְעַק הַרְבֵּה, מִי יַעַנְךָ?


**Now call** Up to here are words of prophecy. From here on, he returns to his rebuke: Cry out to your utmost, who will answer you?


###### Rashi on Job 5:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:1:2)


**וְאֶל מִי מִקְּדֹשִׁים.** הַגּוֹזְרִים זֹאת עָלֶיךָ **תִּפְנֶה.** לְהִלָּחֵם אִתּוֹ:


**To which of the holy ones [i.e., of the angels]** who decrees this upon you will you turn, to strive with him?



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:2:1)


**כִּי לֶאֱוִיל וְגוֹ׳.** לֶאֱוִיל כְּמוֹתְךָ יַהֲרֹג כַּעֲסוֹ, שֶׁאִלּוּ הָיִיתָ שׁוֹתֵק, שֶׁמָּא תָּשׁוּב מִדַּת הָרַחֲמִים עָלֶיךָ:


**a fool** like you, anger will kill him, for had you remained silent, perhaps the Divine standard of clemency would return upon you.


###### Rashi on Job 5:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:2:2)


**קִנְאָה.** הִיא כַּעַס וְחֵמָה, כְּמוֹ ״הֵם קִנְאוּנִי״ (דברים לב:כא):


**wrath** That is anger and fury, as in (Deut. 32:21), “They have provoked Me (קנאוני) to wrath.”



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 5:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:4:1)


**יִרְחֲקוּ בָנָיו.** זוֹ הִיא קְלָלָה שֶׁקִּלַּלְתִּיו:


**May his sons distance themselves from salvation** This is the curse with which I cursed him.


###### Rashi on Job 5:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:4:2)


**וְיִדַּכְּאוּ.** בְּדָגֵשׁ הוּא יְדֻכְּאוּ, וְאִם הוּא נָקוּד רָפֵי מְשַׁמֵּשׁ יְדַכְּאוּ אֲחֵרִים:


**and may they be crushed** It is punctuated with a dagesh: וְיִדַּכְּאוּ, and may they be crushed, but if it was punctuated without a dagesh (וִידַכְּאוּ), it would mean that they should crush others.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:5:1)


**אֲשֶׁר קְצִירוֹ.** שֶׁל רָשָׁע:


**his harvest** of the wicked man.


###### Rashi on Job 5:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:5:2)


**רָעָב יֹאכֵל.** כִּי יָמוּת רָשָׁע, יָבוֹאוּ הָרְעֵבִים שֶׁקִּצֵּץ בְּחַיָּיו, וְיִשְׁתַּלְּמוּ גְזֵלוֹתָיו מִנְּכָסָיו:


**The hungry shall eat** When the wicked man dies, the hungry whom he cut off during his lifetime shall come and take payment from his property.


###### Rashi on Job 5:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:5:3)


**וְאֶל מִצִּנִּים יִקָּחֵהוּ.** כְּמוֹ ״וְיִקְחוּ אֵלֶיךָ״ (שמות כז:כ), כְּלוֹמַר: לְצֹרֶךְ הֶעָנִי הַנּוֹשָׁע מִצִּנִּים וּכְלֵי זַיִן שֶׁל רָשָׁע זֶה, יִקָּחֵהוּ הַשּׁוֹפֵט הַמְּמֻנֶּה לְכָךְ:


**and he will take it for the one saved from his weapons** Like (Exod. 27:20), “and they shall take to you”; for the benefit of the poor man who was saved from the weapons of this wicked man, the judge who is appointed for that purpose shall take it [the estate of the wicked man].


###### Rashi on Job 5:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:5:4)


**וְשָׁאַף.** וּבָלַע אִישׁ אִישׁ מִן הַצַּמִּים שֶׁגָּזַל בְּחַיָּיו:


**shall swallow** One of the thirsty ones whom he robbed during his lifetime shall swallow.


###### Rashi on Job 5:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:5:5)


**חֵילָם.** מָמוֹנָם, שֶׁגָּזַל זֶה בְּחַיָּיו:


**their riches** Their money that this one stole during his lifetime.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 5:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:6:1)


**כִּי לֹא יֵצֵא מֵעָפָר.** מַכָּה הַבָּאָה עַל הָאָדָם, לֹא לְחִנָּם הוּא, וְלֹא מִן הֶעָפָר תִּצְמַח לוֹ:


**For not from the dust does ruin emerge** A plague that comes upon a person is not for nothing, nor does it spring from the ground.


###### Rashi on Job 5:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:6:2)


**אָוֶן.** הוּא לְשׁוֹן שֶׁבֶר:


**ruin** Heb. און. It is an expression of ruin.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 5:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:7:1)


**כִּי אָדָם לְעָמָל יוּלָּד.** כִּי אִי אֶפְשָׁר שֶׁלֹּא יֶחֱטָא וִיקַבֵּל עָמָל בְּחֶטְאוֹ, לְקַבֵּל פּוּרְעָנוּתוֹ. וְאֵינוֹ כִּבְנֵי רֶשֶׁף, שֶׁהֵם מַלְאָכִים וְרוּחוֹת, שֶׁהֵם **יַגְבִּיהוּ עוּף**, וְאֵינָם מִן הַתַּחְתּוֹנִים (יַגְבִּיהוּ לָעוּף וְלִהְיוֹת מִן הַתַּחְתּוֹנִים (סא״א)), לִשְׁלוֹט בָּם שָׂטָן וְיֵצֶר הָרָע:


**Because man is born for trouble** For it is impossible that he should not sin and receive trouble as punishment for sin. He is not like the flying creatures—the angels and spirits—who fly upward, and are not of the earthly creatures (other editions read: fly upward so as not to be among the earthly creatures), over whom the Adversary and temptation rule.


###### Rashi on Job 5:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:7:2)


**רֶשֶׁף.** כְּמוֹ ״רִשְׁפֵּי קֶשֶׁת״ (תהילים עו:ד), כְּשֶׁהַחֵץ מְעוֹפֶפֶת נִקְרָא רִשְׁפֵּי, לְשׁוֹן עוּף (סא״א):


**flying** Heb. רשף, as in (Ps. 76:4), “arrows of (רשפי) a bow.” When the arrow flies, it is called רשפי, an expression of flying. This is what I heard.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 5:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:8:1)


**אוּלָם אֲנִי אֶדְרֹשׁ.** אִם הָיוּ בָּאִים עָלַי יִסּוּרִים הַלָּלוּ, הָיִיתִי דּוֹרֵשׁ אֶל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא בִּתְפִלָּה וּבִתְחִנָּה; וְאִלּוּ הָיִיתִי שָׂם, **דִּבְרָתִי** בְּבַקָּשָׁה:


**But I would seek of God** If these pains came over me, I would appeal to the Holy One, blessed be He, with prayer and supplication, and if I would commit my cause to a request.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 5:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:10:1)


**הַנּוֹתֵן מָטָר עַל פְּנֵי אָרֶץ.** אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**Who gives rain upon the face of the land** The land of Israel.


###### Rashi on Job 5:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:10:2)


**וְשׁוֹלֵחַ מַיִם עַל פְּנֵי חוּצוֹת.** עַל יְדֵי שָׁלִיחַ, לִשְׁאָר אֲרָצוֹת:


**and sends water upon the face of outside places** through a messenger to other countries.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 5:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:11:1)


**לָשׂוּם שְׁפָלִים לְמָרוֹם.** הוּא נוֹתֵן אֶת הַמָּטָר, וְהוּא מֵפֵר מַחֲשְׁבוֹת עֲרוּמִים, שֶׁמְּדַמִּין לְהַפְקִיעַ שְׁעָרִים וְלִקְנוֹת שְׂדוֹת הָעֲנִיִּים בִּמְעַט תְּבוּאָה:


**to place the humble on high** He gives the rain, and He frustrates the plans of the crafty, who intend to raise the prices and buy the fields of the poor for little grain.


###### Rashi on Job 5:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:11:2)


**קוֹדְרִים.** שֶׁקּוֹמְטִים פָּנִים בָּרָעָב:


**and those blackened** whose fares are shriveled from hunger.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 5:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:12:1)


**וְלֹא תַעֲשֶׂינָה יְדֵיהֶם תּוּשִׁיָּה.** אוֹתָהּ עֵצָה שֶׁדִּמּוּ לַעֲשׂוֹת:


**so that their hands do not carry out their plans** The counsel that they planned to perform.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 5:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:13:1)


**נִמְהָרָה.** כָּל הָעֵצָה הָעֲשׂוּיָה בִּמְהִירוּת הִיא סִכְלוּת:


**is hasty** Any counsel that is planned hastily is foolishness.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 5:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:14:1)


**יוֹמָם יִפְגְּשׁוּ חֹשֶׁךְ.** שֶׁאֵין עֲצָתָם מִתְקַיֶּמֶת וְנַעֲשֵׂית, וְאוֹר חָכְמָתָם נֶהְפַּךְ לְחֹשֶׁךְ:


**By day, they meet darkness** For their counsel is not executed nor accomplished, and the light of their wisdom is converted to darkness.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 5:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:15:1)


**וַיּוֹשַׁע.** אֶת הַנִּכְשָׁלִים:


**And He saved** the stumblers.


###### Rashi on Job 5:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:15:2)


**מֵחֶרֶב.** וּמֵאֵיזֶה חֶרֶב? **מִפִּיהֶם**; שֶׁהָיוּ סְבוּרִים לְבוֹלְעָם, וּמִתְאַוִּים לְהַשְׁמִידָם:


**from the sword.** From what sword? From their mouth, for they thought to swallow them and longed to destroy them.


###### Rashi on Job 5:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:15:3)


**וּמִיַּד חָזָק.** יוֹשִׁיעַ אֶבְיוֹן:


**and... from the hand of the mighty** He saves the needy.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 5:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:16:1)


**וְעוֹלָתָהּ.** הָעֲלִילָה וְהָרִשְׁעָה, כְּמוֹ (תהילים סד:ז) ״יַחְפְּשׂוּ עוֹלוֹת״ – עֲלִילוֹת:


**and libel** Heb. ועלתה. The libel (עלילה) and the wickedness, as (Ps. 64:7), “They search for false charges,” equivalent to עלילות, libels.


###### Rashi on Job 5:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:16:2)


**קָפְצָה.** סָגְרָה, כְּמוֹ ״לֹא תִקְפֹּץ״ (דברים טו:ז):


**shut** Heb. קפצה, shut, as (Deut. 15:7), “nor shut (תקפץ).”



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Job 5:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:17:1)
