**הִנֵּה אַשְׁרֵי אֱנוֹשׁ וְגוֹ׳.** בְּיִסּוּרִין. וּכְשֵׁם שֶׁהוּא (מְרַפֵּא) מַכְאוֹבִים שֶׁל אֵלּוּ, כָּךְ אֲנִי אֶדְרוֹשׁ אֵלָיו עַל יִסּוּרִין אֵלּוּ שֶׁיָּבוֹאוּנִי; וְהָיָה לְךָ לְקַבְּלָם וְלֹא לְמָאֳסָם:


**Behold, fortunate is the man etc.** with pains, and just as He heals the pain of these [people], so would I [myself] appeal to Him about these pains that would come upon me. So should you accept them and not despise them.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 5:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:19:1)


**בְּשֵׁשׁ צָרוֹת.** כְּשֶׁיָּבֹאוּ לָעוֹלָם יַצִּילְךָ עַל יְדֵי יִסּוּרִין שֶׁנִּתְיַסַּרְתָּ בָּהֶם, וְגַם בַּשְּׁבִיעִית כְּשֶׁתָּבֹא **לֹא יִגַּע בְּךָ.** וְעַכְשָׁיו הוּא מוֹנֶה אוֹתָן:


**In six troubles** When they come to the world, he will save you through the pains with which you wee tormented, and also with the seventh one, which when it comes will not touch you.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Job 5:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:21:1)


**בְּשׁוֹט לָשׁוֹן.** שֶׁל שָׂטָן תֵּחָבֵא אוֹתְךָ. אֵלּוּ הֵן הַשֶּׁבַע צָרוֹת: רָעָב וְחֶרֶב, **לָשׁוֹן וְשׁוֹד וְגוֹ׳**, וְחַיַּת הָאָרֶץ – הֵם לִיסְטִים:


**from the scourging tongue** of the Adversary you shall be hidden. These are the seven troubles: 1 famine, 2) the sword, 3) the tongue, 4) plunder etc., and 5) the beasts of the land, which are brigands.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Job 5:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:23:1)


**אַבְנֵי הַשָּׂדֶה.** מִין אָדָם הֵן:


**the stones of the field** They are: 6) a type of man.


###### Rashi on Job 5:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:23:2)


**וְחַיַּת הַשָּׂדֶה.** הוּא שֶׁנִּקְרָא גְּרוּשׁ״ה בְּלַעַ״ז, וְזוֹ הִיא חַיַּת הַשָּׂדֶה מַמָּשׁ. וּבִלְשׁוֹן מִשְׁנָה תּוֹרַת כֹּהֲנִים נִקְרָאִים אַדְנֵי הַשָּׂדֶה (כלאים ח,ה):


**and the beasts of the field** That is what is known as grouse(?) in Old French, and this is actually a beast of the field. In the language of the Mishnah in Torath Kohanim, they are called “adnei hasadeh.”


###### Rashi on Job 5:23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:23:3)


**הָשְׁלְמָה לָךְ.** יִהְיֶה לָהּ שָׁלוֹם עִמָּךְ:


**made peace with you** will have peace with you.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Job 5:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:24:1)


**וְיָדַעְתָּ כִּי שָׁלוֹם אָהֳלֶךָ.** וְאָז בְּכָל מָקוֹם תִּהְיֶה בָּטוּחַ, **כִּי שָׁלוֹם אָהֳלֶךָ**:


**Then you shall know that there is peace in your tent** Then, wherever you are, you will be confident that all is well in your tent.


###### Rashi on Job 5:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:24:2)


**וּפָקַדְתָּ נָוְךָ וְלֹא תֶחֱטָא.** כְּמוֹ ״אֶל הַשַּׂעֲרָה וְלֹא יַחֲטִא״ (שופטים כ:טז), וְזוֹ הִיא הַשְּׁבִיעִית שֶׁמָּנִינוּ לְמַעְלָה (איוב ה:יט):


**and you shall visit your habitation and miss nothing** Heb. תחטא, like (Jud. 20:16), “at a hairbreadth and not miss (יחטא).” This is the seventh [trouble] that we counted above.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Job 5:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:26:1)


**תָּבוֹא בְכֶלַח אֱלֵי קָבֶר.** שֶׁתִּנָּצֵל מִן הַדֶּבֶר. וּבְכֶלַח – אֵין סֶמֶךְ בַּמִּקְרָא לְהָבִין מְלִיצָתוֹ. וּלְפִי עִנְיַן הַמְּלִיצָה יִפָּתֵר וְיִלָּמֵד מֵעִנְיָנוֹ, שֶׁהוּא לְשׁוֹן גְּמַר בִּשּׁוּל הַתְּבוּאָה, כְּשֶׁבִּשְּׁלָה כָּל צָרְכָּהּ. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה פֵּרְשׁוּ (מועד קטן כח.) בְּכֶלַח – בְּגִימַטְרִיָּא: שִׁשִּׁים שָׁנָה:


**You shall come to the grave at a ripe old age** Heb. בְכֶלַח. [In other words,] you will be saved from pestilence. As for the word בְכֶלַח: there is no Biblical support to understand its meaning, but according to the context, it can be interpreted as an expression of the completion of the ripening of the grain, when it is completely ripe. Our Rabbis, however, interpret בְכֶלַח as “sixty years,” according to its numerical value.


###### Rashi on Job 5:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/5:26:2)


**כַּעֲלוֹת גָּדִישׁ.** כַּאֲשֶׁר נִסְתַּלֵּק הַגָּדִישׁ מִן הַקַּרְקַע בְּעִתּוֹ:


**as the grain stock is taken away** As the grain stack is taken off the ground in its time.



## Chapter 6


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Job 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:1:1)


**(מענה איוב):**



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:2:1)


**וְהַוָּתִי.** שִׁבְרִי:


**and my calamity** my ruin.


###### Rashi on Job 6:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:2:2)


**יִשְׂאוּ יָחַד.** עִם מִשְׁקָל שֶׁכְּנֶגְדָּם, וַאֲפִלּוּ הֵם חוֹל יַמִּים, יִכְבַּד מִמֶּנּוּ:


**placed together on a scale** with a weight opposite it. even if [this weight] were the sand of the seas, it would outweigh it.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:3:1)


**לָעוּ.** מְגֻמְגָּמִין, כְּאָדָם שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ כֹּחַ לְהוֹצִיא דָּבָר מִפִּיו כְּתִקּוּנוֹ; וְכֵן ״וְשָׁתוּ וְלָעוּ״ (עובדיה א:טז), שֶׁכֵּן דֶּרֶךְ שִׁכּוֹר לְגַמְגֵּם בִּדְבָרָיו:


**stammer** Heb. לעו. They are uncertain, like a person who has no strength to pronounce a word properly. Likewise (Obad. 16), “And they shall drink and stammer (ולעו),” because it is usual for a drunkard to stammer in his speech.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 6:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:4:1)


**חֲמָתָם.** אֶרֶס שֶׁלָּהֶם. דֶּרֶךְ פַּרְסִיִּים לָתֵת אֶרֶס שֶׁל נָחָשׁ בְּחִצֵּיהֶם:


**venom** Heb. חמתם, their poison. It was the custom of the Persians to apply snake venom to their arrows.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 6:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:5:1)


**הֲיִנְהַק פֶּרֶא וְגוֹ׳.** כְּלוֹמַר: וְכִי לְחִנָּם אֲנִי צוֹעֵק? וַהֲלֹא בְּהֵמָה שׁוֹטָה אֵינָהּ נוֹהֶקֶת בִּזְמַן שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהּ דֶּשֶׁא, וְכֵן **שׁוֹר לֹא יִגְעֶה** אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן אֵין לוֹ **בְּלִיל** תְּבוּאָה. **בְּפֶרֶא** נוֹהֵג לְשׁוֹן ׳נְהַק׳, וּבְשׁוֹר – לְשׁוֹן ׳גְּעִיָּה׳:


**Does a wild donkey bray** That is to say, do I cry out for nothing? Even a stupid animal does not bray when it has food, neither does an ox low unless it does not have grain fodder. With a wild donkey, the expression used is נַהַק, braying, and with an ox, the expression of געיה, lowing.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 6:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:6:1)


**הֲיֵאָכֵל תָּפֵל.** דָּבָר שֶׁהוּא בְּלִי מֶלַח וְצָרִיךְ לְהַמְלִיחוֹ, וְלֹא נִמְלַח, קָרוּי תָּפֵל בִּלְשׁוֹן מִשְׁנָה (שבת קכח.; חלין קיג.; נדרים נא.):


**Can bland food be eaten** A thing that has no salt and must be salted, but was not salted, is called תָּפֵל in Mishnaic Hebrew (Shabbath 128a, Hullin 113a, Nedarim 51a).


###### Rashi on Job 6:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:6:2)


**אִם יֵשׁ טַעַם בְּרִיר.** הַבָּא מֵחֲמַת הַחֲלָמוֹת כְּשֶׁאוֹכֵל דָּבָר חָזָק שֶׁמַּרְבֶּה רִיר וְרוֹק, כְּגוֹן שׁוּם. כְּלוֹמַר: וְכִי סְבוּרִים אַתֶּם שֶׁיִּהְיוּ מִתְקַבְּלוֹת תְּשׁוּבוֹת שֶׁאֵין בָּהֶם מַמָּשׁ? **רִיר**, כְּמוֹ ״וַיּוֹרֶד רִירוֹ עַל זְקָנוֹ״ (שמואל א כא:יד):


**or is there taste in the saliva** that comes from strong tasting foods, for the one who eats a strong-tasting food that increases the saliva, e.g. garlic. That is to say, Do you think their replies that have no substance are acceptable? רִיר [means saliva] as in (I Sam. 2 1:14), “and let his saliva (ריר) run down upon his beard.”


###### Rashi on Job 6:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:6:3)


**חַלָּמוּת.** כְּמוֹ ״יֶחֶלְמוּ בְנֵיהֶם״ (איוב לט:ד), לְשׁוֹן חוֹזֶק. וְיֵשׁ פּוֹתְרִים לְשׁוֹן חֲלוֹם, וְאֵין נִרְאֶה בְּעֵינַי, מִפְּנֵי דַּגְשׁוּת הַלָּמֶ״ד:


חַלָמוּת is like (below 39:4), “Their sons wax strong (יחלמו),” an expression of strength. Some interpret it as an expression of a dream (חלום), but this does not appeal to me because of the dagesh in the “lammed.”



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 6:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:7:1)


**מֵאֲנָה לִנְגּוֹעַ נַפְשִׁי.** כְּלוֹמַר: הַרְבֵּה יֵשׁ לִי לִזְעוֹק, כִּי דְבָרִים שֶׁהָיְתָה נַפְשִׁי מְמָאֶנֶת וְקָצָה לִנְגּוֹעַ בָּהֶן, לְשׁוֹן נְגִיעָה:


**What my body refused to touch** That is to say, I have reason to cry increasingly, because things that my body refused [to touch] and was disgusted to touch... [לנגוֹע] is an expression of touching.


###### Rashi on Job 6:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:7:2)


**הֵמָּה כִּדְוֵי לַחְמִי.** הֵמָּה לִי עַתָּה נְכוֹנִים לְמַשְׁמֵשׁ בָּהֶם כִּמְפוֹת שֻׁלְחָנִי, שֶׁלַּחְמִי נָתוּן בָּהֶם. כִּדְוֵי לְשׁוֹן ״וַיִּכְרֹת אֶת מִדְוֵיהֶם״ (שמואל ב י:ד). כָּךְ שָׁמַעְתִּי מִפִּי רַבִּי מְשֻׁלָּם רוֹפֵא. עִנְיָן אַחֵר: כִּמְפוֹת שֶׁמְּסַנְּנִין בָּהֶן אֶת הַתַּבְשִׁיל וּמַאֲכָל זָב מֵהֶם, דְּוֵי לְשׁוֹן דָּוָה זָבָה (ויקרא טו:לג, יט):


**as cloths for my food** Now they are prepared for me to touch them like my tablecloths upon which my food is placed. כִּדְוֵי is an expression like (II Sam. 10:4), “and he cut off their garments (מדויהם).” I heard this from Rabbi Meshullam the physician. Another interpretation: Like cloths with which they strain the cooked foods and the food runs out of them. דְוֵי is an expression of דָוֶה, running.


###### Rashi on Job 6:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:7:3)


**לַחְמִי.** כָּל מַאֲכָל קָרוּי לֶחֶם, כְּמוֹ ״עֲבַד לְחֶם רַב״ (דניאל ה:א), וּכְמוֹ ״נַשְׁחִיתָה עֵץ בְּלַחְמוֹ״ (ירמיהו יא:יט), וְתִרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״סַמָּא דְמוֹתָא בְּמֵיכְלֵיהּ״. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: הֵמָּה כְּמַבָּטֵי מַאֲכָלִי, כְּלוֹמַר: גַּס לִבִּי לְהִסְתַּכֵּל בָּהֶם בְּרִמָּה, כַּאֲשֶׁר אֲנִי מִסְתַּכֵּל בְּמַאֲכָלִי. וּלְשׁוֹן מִשְׁנָה הוּא: ״וּמְסַגּוֹ כִּי דָווּ״, בְּמַסֶּכֶת שַׁבָּת (שבת נג:):


**my food** Heb. לחמי, lit. my bread. All food is called לחם, as in (Dan. 5:1), “made a great feast (לחם).” and as in (Jer. 11:19), “Let us destroy his food (בלחמו) with wood.” which Jonathan renders: poison into his food. Another explanation: They are as the sights of my food. That is to say: I am accustomed to looking at them, at the worms, as I look at my food. This is Mishnaic Hebrew: “and they walk when they look (דוי)” in Tractate Shabbath (53b).



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Job 6:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:9:1)


**וְיוֹאֵל.** וְיִרְצֶה:


**desire** and may He want.


###### Rashi on Job 6:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:9:2)


**וִידַכְּאֵנִי.** לְשׁוֹן מִיתָה:


**and crush me** [This is] an expression of death.


###### Rashi on Job 6:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:9:3)


**יַתֵּר יָדוֹ.** יְגַדֵּל יָדוֹ בְּמַכָּתוֹ, כְּמוֹ ״הִנֵּה יַד ה׳ הוֹיָה״ (שמות ט:ג):


**enlarge his hand** May He enlarge His hand with His plague like (Exod. 9:3), “Behold God’s hand is.”


###### Rashi on Job 6:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:9:4)


**וְיִבַצְּעֵנִי.** וִיכַלֵּנִי, כְּמוֹ ״בִּצַּע אֶמְרָתוֹ״ (איכה ב:יז), ״יָדָיו תְּבַצַּעְנָה״ (זכריה ד:ט):


**and finish me off** Heb. ויבצעני, and finish me off, as in (Lam. 2:17), “He has carried out (בצע) His word,” and (Zech. 4:9), “and his hands shall finish it (תבצענה).”



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 6:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:10:1)


**וּתְהִי עוֹד נֶחָמָתִי.** וּתְהִי עוֹד זֹאת לִי לְנֶחָמָה:


**Then should I yet have comfort** And this shall yet be to me for comfort.


###### Rashi on Job 6:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:10:2)


**וַאֲסַלְּדָה בְחִילָה וְלֹא יַחְמוֹל.** וַאֲבַקֵּשׁ מִמֶּנּוּ סְלִיחָה שֶׁלֹּא יַחְמוֹל מִלְּבַצְּעֵנִי, וַאֲנִי אֵינִי מוֹצֵא לוֹ דִּמְיוֹן בַּמִּקְרָא, זוּלָתִי שֶׁבִּלְשׁוֹן מִשְׁנָה הוּא לְשׁוֹן יָרֵא וְדוֹאֵג (חולין קה:): שֶׁהַיָּד סוֹלֶדֶת בָּהֶן מִלִּכָּווֹת בָּרוֹתְחִין; כְּלוֹמַר: אֶתְחוֹלֵל בִּדְאָגָה וּבְחִילָה בַּקָּשָׁה זוֹ:


**and I will beg, shuddering, that He should have no pity** And I will beg of Him with shuddering (Shem Ephraim) that He not refrain from finishing me off: I cannot find any similar word in Scriptures, except that in Mishnaic Hebrew it is an expression of fear and concern, that the hand fears them lest it be scalded in boiling water. That is to say, I will shudder with concern and with shuddering request this.


###### Rashi on Job 6:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:10:3)


**כִּי לֹא כִחַדְתִּי.** וְלֹא חָסַרְתִּי אֲמָרָיו מִלְּקַיְּמָן:


**for I have not denied** I did not fail to fulfill His words.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 6:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:11:1)


**מַה כֹּחִי.** חָזָק לִסְבּוֹל:


**What is my strength** How strong is it to bear [my pains]?


###### Rashi on Job 6:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:11:2)


**כִּי אֲיַחֵל.** כִּי אֲמְתִּין עַד יוֹם רְפוּאָה אוֹ עַד יוֹם מִיתָה:


**that I should wait** That I should wait until the day of recovery or until the day of death?


###### Rashi on Job 6:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:11:3)


**אֲיַחֵל.** כְּמוֹ ״וַיָּחֶל עוֹד״ דְּנֹחַ (בראשית ח:י):


**I should wait** Heb. איחל, like (Gen. 8:10), “And he waited (ויחל) again,” concerning Noah.


###### Rashi on Job 6:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:11:4)


**וּמַה קִּצִּי.** וּמָה חָשׁוּב קִצִּי וְחָזָק כִּי אֲיַחֵל, כִּי לֹא אוּכַל לְהַאֲרִיךְ קִצִּי מִלִּסְבּוֹל:


**and what** Of what importance is my end that I should be able to restrain my desire to bear?


###### Rashi on Job 6:11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:11:5)


**נַפְשִׁי.** הוּא יֵצֶר תַּאֲוַת אָדָם, בְּכַמָּה מְקוֹמוֹת, כְּמוֹ ״אִם יֵשׁ אֶת נַפְשְׁכֶם״ (בראשית כג:ח):


**my desire** Heb. נפשי. This [word denoting] a person’s temptation toward desire [is used] in many places, as (Gen. 23:8), “If it is truly your will (נפשכם).”



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 6:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:13:1)


**הַאִם אֵין עֶזְרָתִי בִי.** לְשׁוֹן תֵּימָא: הֲגַם זֹאת תָּבֹא עָלַי, שֶׁאֵין עֶזְרָתִי בִּי – אוֹתָם רֵיעִים שֶׁהָיוּ לִי לְעָזְרֵנִי, אֵינָם בְּעֶזְרָתִי?!


**Have I no help** This is an expression of wonder. Will this too come upon me, that I have no help? Those friends that I had to help me are not helping me.


###### Rashi on Job 6:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:13:2)


**וְתוּשִׁיָּה נִדְּחָה.** עֵצָה שֶׁל יוֹעֲצִים נִדְּחָה מִמֶּנִּי, שֶׁקָּמָה עָלַי לְהַקְנִיט וְלִדְחוֹת:


**or is counsel lost** The counsel of counselors is lost from me, because you have risen against me to provoke and to reject.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 6:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:14:1)


**לַמָּס מֵרֵעֵהוּ חָסֶד.** לְמִי שֶׁמּוֹנֵעַ חֶסֶד מֵחֲבֵירוֹ. וְהַלָּמֶ״ד – מְשַׁמֶּשֶׁת, כְּמוֹ ״לָרָב״ (במדבר כו:נד), ״לָצָו״ (ישעיהו כח:י), לָשָׁב. **מָס** גַּם הוּא לְשׁוֹן פּוֹעֵל, כְּמוֹ בָּא, שָׁב, נָד, גַּם אֵלֶּה לְשׁוֹן עוֹשֶׂה:


**By one who withholds kindness from his friend** Heb. למס, by one who withholds kindness. The “lammed” is a prefix, as in (Num. 26:54), “to the numerous one (לָרַב)”; “to the one who returns (?) (לָשָב),” and (Isa. 28:10, 13) “for a precept (לצו).” מָס too is an expression of a verb, like בָּא, comes: שָב, returns; גָר, dwells; these also are an expression of doing [i.e., the present tense]:


###### Rashi on Job 6:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:14:2)


**לַמָּס** הַחֶסֶד. מְכַלֶּה אוֹתוֹ, כְּמוֹ ״וְחַם הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ וְנָמָס״ (שמות טז:כא):


**who withholds kindness**, who destroys it, like (Exod. 16:21), “when the sun grew hot, it melted (ונמס).”



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 6:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:15:1)


**אַחַי.** בְּנֵי מְלִיצָתִי:


**My brethren** Those who defend me.


###### Rashi on Job 6:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:15:2)


**בָּגְדוּ.** בִּי, כְּמוֹ הַנַּחַל הַבּוֹגֵד בְּגִידוֹת כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפָרֵשׁ בָּעִנְיָן:


**betrayed** me like the stream that betrays [many] betrayals, as is explained in this chapter.


###### Rashi on Job 6:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:15:3)


**כַּאֲפִיק נְחָלִים.** מוֹצָא נְחָלִים יַעַבְרוּ מִמִּדָּה נְכוֹחָה וְרֵיעוּת. וּמַה הִיא בְּגִידוּתוֹ שֶׁל נַחַל?


**like the source of streams** the source of streams, they pass from the proper trait of friendship. Now what is the betrayal of a stream?



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 6:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:16:1)


**הַקּוֹדְרִים מִנִּי קָרַח.** אִם בָּא עֲלֵיהֶם קֶרַח נִקְמָטִים וְנַעֲשִׂים קְרִים (קרשים קרים):


**which wrinkle from the ice** if ice comes upon them, they wrinkle and become like boards (Shem Ephraim),


###### Rashi on Job 6:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:16:2)


**וְעָלֵימוֹ יִתְעַלֶּם.** מִתְכַּסֶּה הַשֶּׁלֶג שֶׁנּוֹפֵל עַל הַקֶּרַח. הֲרֵי בְּגִידָה אַחַת: שֶׁנִּתְכַּסָּה מִן הָעַיִן, וְאֵין הַצָּמֵא מוֹצֵא מַיִם לִשְׁתּוֹת:


**and upon them the snow disappears**—it is covered, for it falls with the ice. This is one betrayal, for it [the stream] is hidden from the eye, and a thirsty man does not find water to drink.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Job 6:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:17:1)


**בְּעֵת יְזֹרְבוּ.** לְשׁוֹן יְצֹרְבוּ, כְּחוֹם הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ:


**At the time they become warm** Heb. יזרבו, an expression of יְצֹרָבוּ, they become warm from the heat of the sun.


###### Rashi on Job 6:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:17:2)


**נִצְמָתוּ.** רֶטְרִי״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז, כְּמוֹ ״וְחֹמֶץ צוֹמְתָן״ בִּלְשׁוֹן גְּמָרָא (פסחים מ:). צָדִ״י מִתְחַלֶּפֶת בְּזַיִ״ן, כְּמוֹ צְעָקָה זְעָקָה (בראשית כז:לד; ישעיהו סה:יט):


**they retreat** Heb. נצמתו, retrait in Old French, shrink, contract, as “and the vinegar shrinks them (צומתן), (Pesachim 41a) in Talmudic Hebrew. [In the word יְזֹרָבוּ], the “zaddi” is converted to a “zayin,” like צעקה and זעקה, a cry.


###### Rashi on Job 6:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:17:3)


**יְזֹרְבוּ.** לְשׁוֹן ״וְנִצְרְבוּ בָהּ כָּל פָּנִים״ (יחזקאל כא:ג), וּכְמוֹ ״אֵשׁ צָרֶבֶת״ (משלי טז:כז):


[Therefore,] יְזֹרָבוּ is an expression of (Ezekiel 21:3), “and all faces shall be scorched (ונצרבו),” and like (Prov. 16:27), “searing (צרבת) fire.”


###### Rashi on Job 6:17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:17:4)


**נִדְעֲכוּ.** נִקְפְּצוּ מִמְּקוֹמָם:


**they jump** they jump from their place.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Job 6:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:18:1)


**יִלָּפְתוּ.** יֵאָחֲזוּ אָרְחוֹת דַּרְכָּם, כְּמוֹ ״וַיִּלְפֹּת שִׁמְשׁוֹן״ (שופטים טז:כט), וְכֵן ״וַיֶּחֱרַד הָאִישׁ וַיִּלָּפֵת״ (רות ג:ח) – שֶׁחִבְּקַתּוּ הָאִשָּׁה:


**are held** Heb. ילפתו. They hold the paths of their way, as in (Jud. 16:29), “And Samson grasped (וילפת),” and similarly (Ruth 3:8), “and the man quaked and was taken around (וילפת),” for the woman embraced him.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 6:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:19:1)


**תֵּימָא.** אֶרֶץ יִשְׁמָעֵאל שֶׁהִיא נְמוּכָה, וְהַמַּיִם נִגָּרִים שָׁם:


**They look at the paths of Tema** The land of Ishmael, which is low, and the water flows there.


###### Rashi on Job 6:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:19:2)


**קִוּוּ.** לְשׁוֹן קַו נָטוּי (ישעיהו לד:יא). דָּבָר אַחֵר: לְשׁוֹן ״יִקָּווּ הַמַּיִם״ (בראשית א:ט):


**they make a line** Heb. קוו, an expression of an extended line. Another explanation: it is an expression of (Gen. 1:9) “Let the waters...gather (יקוו).”



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 6:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:20:1)


**בֹּשׁוּ כִּי בָטָח בָּאוּ עָדֶיהָ.** יֵבוֹשׁוּ שׁוֹתֶיהָ, כִּי בָטַח כָּל אִישׁ וְאִישׁ לִשְׁתּוֹת מֵהֶם:


**They are ashamed because they had hoped; they came to it** Those who drink it are ashamed because everyone had hoped to drink thereof.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Job 6:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:21:1)


**כִּי עַתָּה הֱיִיתֶם לוֹ.** הֱיִיתֶם נִמְשָׁלִים לְאוֹתוֹ נַחַל:


**For now you are like it** You are compared to that stream


###### Rashi on Job 6:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:21:2)


**תִּירְאוּ חֲתַת.** וּפַחַד הַמַּכָּה, וַתִּירְאוּ לוֹמַר הָאֱמֶת; וּמַחֲנִיפִים אֶת בַּעַל רִיבִי:


**you fear ruin** The fear of the plague, and you are afraid to say the truth, thereby flattering my opponent.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Job 6:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:22:1)


**וּמִכֹּחֲכֶם שִׁחֲדוּ בַעֲדִי.** וּמִמָּמוֹנְכֶם:


**or “Offer a bribe for me from your wealth”** [Heb. מכחכם, lit. from your strength.] And from your wealth.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Job 6:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:25:1)


**מַה נִּמְרְצוּ.** כְּמוֹ ״קְלָלָה נִמְרֶצֶת״ (מלכים א ב:ח), לְשׁוֹן ״דָּבָר דָּבוּר עַל אָפְנָיו״ (משלי כה:יא). וְכֵן ״נִמְלְצוּ לְחִכִּי״ (תהלים קיט:קג) – כֻּלָּן לְשׁוֹן מְלִיצָה הֵם, וְלָמֶ״ד מִתְחַלֶּפֶת בְּרֵי״שׁ:


**How clear** Heb. נמרצו, like (I Kings 2:8), “a clear curse (נמרצת),” an expression of (Prov. 25:11) “a word spoken with proper basis.” and similarly (Ps. 119:103), “How clear (נמלצו) are Your words to my palate.” All of them are expressions of clarification (מליצה), and the “lammed” is converted to a “resh.”


###### Rashi on Job 6:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:25:2)


**אִמְרֵי יֹשֶׁר.** אִם אִמְרֵי יֹשֶׁר הֱיִיתֶם אוֹמְרִים, הָיוּ מְקֻבָּלִים; אֲבָל עַתָּה, **מַה יּוֹכִיחַ הוֹכֵחַ מִכֶּם**? כָּל תּוֹכָחָה שֶׁבְּאִיּוֹב – בֵּרוּר הַדְּבָרִים הֵם, וּמַעֲמִידִים עַל הָאֱמֶת:


**words of uprightness** If you were saying words of uprightness, they would be accepted, but now, what does your proof prove? Every expression of תוֹכָחָה in Job means the clarification and verification of matters.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Job 6:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:26:1)


**הֲלְהוֹכַח מִלִּים תַּחְשֹׁבוּ.** הֲלְבָרֵר דְּבָרִים תַּחְשֹׁבוּ?


**Do you think to clarify words** Heb. הלהוכח, Do you think to clarify words?


###### Rashi on Job 6:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:26:2)


**וּלְרוּחַ.** הֵם מְשׁוּלִים:


**They are like wind** They are likened [to wind].


###### Rashi on Job 6:26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:26:3)


**אִמְרֵי נוֹאָשׁ.** שֶׁאֵין בָּהֶם מַמָּשׁ, כְּמוֹ ״וַתֹּאמְרִי נוֹאָשׁ״ (ירמיה ב:כה) – אֵינִי חוֹשֵׁשׁ לְדִבְרֵי הַנְּבִיאִים:


**words of futility** which have no substance, like (Jer. 2:25), “but you said. I despair (נואש).” I am not concerned with the words of the prophets.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Job 6:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:27:1)


**תַּפִּילוּ.** בְּדִינְכֶם אַתֶּם מַפִּילִים. הַאַף עַל עָנִי כָּמוֹנִי בְּקַו מִשְׁפָּט? **וְתִכְרוּ** שׁוּחָה **עַל רֵיעֲכֶם** לְהַפִּילוֹ?


**You cast** with your judgment, you cast wrath upon a poor man like me, with the line of judgment; and you dig a pit for your friend into which to cast him.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Job 6:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:28:1)


**הוֹאִילוּ.** הִתְרַצּוּ לִפְנוֹת אֵלַי וְלִשְׁמֹעַ דְּבָרַי, וְתִרְאוּ **אִם עַל פְּנֵיכֶם אֲכַזֵּב**:


**agree** Be willing to turn to me and to hear my words. Then you will see whether I lie to your face.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Job 6:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:29:1)


**שׁוּבוּ נָא.** לִבְחוֹן, וְתִבְחֲנוּ, שֶׁלֹּא תְהֵא עוֹד עַוְלָה **וְשׁוּבוּ** לַחֲקוֹר, וַהֲרֵי צִדְקִי יִמָּצֵא בָהּ:


**Return now** to test, and you will determine that there shall be no more injustice; return to investigate, and behold, my righteousness will be found in it.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Job 6:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/6:30:1)


**אִם חִכִּי לֹא יָבִין הַוּוֹת.** לֹא יָבִין כְּשֶׁאֲנִי מְדַבֵּר הַוּוֹת?


**or will my palate not understand wickedness?** Will it not understand when I speak words of wickedness?



## Chapter 7


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Job 7:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:1:1)


**הֲלֹא צָבָא וְגוֹ׳.** זֶה הַדָּבָר אֲשֶׁר אָמַרְתִּי לָכֶם הוֹאִילוּ פְּנוּ בִי, כִּי אֵיךְ אַחֲרִישׁ מִלִּזְעוֹק עַל הַוָּתִי? הֲלֹא יְדַעְתֶּם כִּי יֵשׁ צָבָא וּזְמַן לֶאֱנוֹשׁ, כַּמָּה יִחְיֶה:


**Is not man on earth for a limited time** This is what I said to you. ‘Agree and turn to me.’ because how can I be silent from crying our about my misfortune? Do you not know that the time a man has to live is limited?


###### Rashi on Job 7:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:1:2)


**וְכִימֵי שָׂכִיר.** שֶׁנִּשְׂכַּר לְשָׁנָה, וְיוֹדֵעַ שֶׁיִּכְלוּ יְמֵי שְׂכִירוּתוֹ, כֵּן זֶה יוֹדֵעַ שֶׁיִּכְלוּ שָׁנִים הַקְּצוּבוֹת לוֹ. וַאֲנִי – אוֹתוֹ צָבָא הַנִּתָּן לִי:


**and...like the days of a hireling** and I—that time that is allotted to me...



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 7:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:2:1)


**כְּעֶבֶד יִשְׁאַף צֵל.** כְּעֶבֶד שֶׁהוּא יָגֵעַ כָּל הַיּוֹם, וְיִשְׁאַף וְיִתְאַוֶּה מָתַי יִהְיֶה **צֵל** הָעֶרֶב; **וּכְשָׂכִיר אֲשֶׁר יְקַוֶּה פָעֳלוֹ** לָעֶרֶב, לְפִי שֶׁהָיָה לוֹ כָּל הַיּוֹם לְעָמָל, וְיִתְאַוֶּה לְבִיאַת הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ:


**As a slave who longs for the shadow** As a slave, who toils all day through and longs and yearns, “When will the shadow of evening come?” And as a hireling, who hopes for his wages at eventide, because the whole day was to him for toil, and he yearns for the sunset.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 7:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:3:1)


**כֵּן הָנְחַלְתִּי לִי.** מִשָּׁמַיִם **יַרְחֵי שָׁוְא** וְיִסּוּרִין, אוֹתוֹ ״צָבָא הַנִּתָּן לֶאֱנוֹשׁ עֲלֵי אָרֶץ״ (איוב ז:א):


**So was I made to possess** from Heaven **months of futility** and torments, that limited time that is given to man upon earth.


###### Rashi on Job 7:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:3:2)


**וְלֵילוֹת עָמָל מִנּוּ לִי.** (מִן הַשָּׁמַיִם):


**and they appointed wearisome nights for me** from Heaven



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 7:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:4:1)


**אִם שָׁכַבְתִּי.** בַּלַּיְלָה, צָפִיתִי **וְאָמַרְתִּי וְגוֹ׳**:


**If I would lie down** at night, I would hope and say, etc.


###### Rashi on Job 7:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:4:2)


**וּמִדַּד עֶרֶב.** מָתַי יָאִיר, וְיָבֹא עֵת קִימָה וְעֵת מִידוּד הַנֶּשֶׁף – לְשׁוֹן ״וַתִּדַּד שְׁנָתִי״ (בראשית לא:מ), וְהַמֵּ״ם הוּא עִקָּר נוֹפֵל, כְּמוֹ ״מַאֲמַר״ (אסתר א:טו), ״מִשְׁמָר״ (בראשית מב:יז):


**and the evening depart** When will it become light and when will the time of arising come, and when will the evening depart? [The word מדד ] is an expression of (Gen. 31:40) “and sleep was banished (ותדד).” The “mem” is a defective radical, as in מאמר, statement; משמר, watch.


###### Rashi on Job 7:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:4:3)


**וְשָׂבַעְתִּי נְדוּדִים.** וְאֶשְׂבַּע נְדוּדִים עַל מִטָּתִי בַּיּוֹם עַד נָשֶׁף, לְפִי שֶׁלֹּא הָיִיתִי יָכוֹל לִישֹׁן מִפְּנֵי יִסּוּרִים עַד נֶשֶׁף שֶׁל יוֹם, לְפִי שֶׁלָּבַשׁ בְּשָׂרִי רִמָּה:


**And I was sated with restlessness** I was sated with restlessness on my bed by day until twilight, because, due to the torments I could not sleep until the twilight of day, since my flesh was clothed with worms.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 7:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:5:1)


**וְגוּשׁ עָפָר.** גּוּשַׁת עָפָר, וְהִיא הֶעֶלְתָה חֲלוּדָּה; שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ גּוּשׁ עָפָר לְלַחְלֵחַ וּלְהַעֲלוֹת חֲלוּדָּה:


**and clods of earth** Clods of earth (?) Now this is the development of rust, because it is the custom of clods of earth to moisten and cause rust.


###### Rashi on Job 7:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:5:2)


**רָגַע.** נִקְמַט, כְּמוֹ ״רֹגַע הַיָּם״ (ישעיה נא:טו). פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר: **עוֹרִי רָגַע.** לְשׁוֹן נוֹחַ, גַּל אַחַר גַּל, כַּקְּמָטִים הַלָּלוּ:


**wrinkled** Heb. רגע wrinkled, as in (Isa. 51:15), “Who wrinkles (רגע) the sea.” Another explanation: עוֹרִי רָגַע is an expression of resting a wave after a wave, like wrinkles (?).



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 7:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:6:1)


**יָמַי קַלּוּ.** יְמֵי טוֹבָתִי מִהֲרוּ לָלֶכֶת:


**My days are swifter** The days of my prosperity hastened to go away.


###### Rashi on Job 7:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:6:2)


**מִנִּי אָרֶג.** מִנִּי אֲרִיגָה הַנַּעֲשֵׂית מְהֵרָה, וְכֵן אָמַר חִזְקִיָּה: ״קִפַּדְתִּי כָאֹרֵג חַיַּי״ (ישעיה לח:יב):


**than a weaver’s shuttle** Than weaving that is done swiftly. So said Hezekiah (Isa. 38:12), “I severed, like a weaver, my life.”


###### Rashi on Job 7:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:6:3)


**בְּאֶפֶס תִּקְוָה.** אֵינִי מְקַוֶּה עוֹד לְטוֹבָה:


**without hope** I no longer hope for good.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 7:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:7:1)


**לֹא תָשׁוּב עֵינִי.** לְאַחַר מִיתָה. וְכָאן כָּפַר אִיּוֹב בִּתְחִיַּת הַמֵּתִים:


**my eye will not return** after death. Here Job denies the dogma of the Resurrection of the Dead.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 7:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:8:1)


**לֹא תְשׁוּרֵנִי.** לֹא יִרְאֵנִי עוֹד עַיִן שֶׁתַּחְפֹּץ לִרְאוֹתִי אַחַר שֶׁאָמוּת:


**shall see me no more** The eye that wishes to see me shall see me no more after I die.


###### Rashi on Job 7:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:8:2)


**עֵינֶיךָ בִּי וְאֵינֶנִּי.** לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מְדַבֵּר: לָמָּה הֻצְרַכְתָּ לְפַצְּעֵנִי וּלְדַכְּאֵנִי בְּיִסּוּרִים? בִּרְאִיָּה אַחַת, שֶׁתָּשִׂים עֵינֶיךָ עָלַי, וְאֵינֶנִּי בָּעוֹלָם!


**set Your eyes upon me and I will be here no longer** He is addressing the Holy One, blessed be He: “Why did You have to finish me off and crush me with pains? With one look that you set Your eyes on me, I am no longer in the world.”



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 7:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:11:1)


**גַּם אֲנִי לֹא אֶחֱשָׂךְ פִּי.** הוֹאִיל וְאֵינְךָ חָדֵל מִמֶּנִּי, גַּם אֲנִי לֹא אֶמְנַע דִּבּוּר מִלִּצְעֹק עַל מִדּוֹתֶיךָ:


**Neither will I restrain my mouth** Since You do not desist from me, neither will I restrain my speech from complaining about Your ways.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 7:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:12:1)


**הֲיָם אָנִי.** שֶׁשַּׂמְתָּ עָלַי חוֹל לְמִשְׁמָר?!


**Am I a sea** that You placed sand over me as a guard?


###### Rashi on Job 7:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:12:2)


**אִם תַּנִּין.** דָּג גָּדוֹל שֶׁחֲבַשְׁתּוֹ בְּנִבְכֵי יָם, **כִּי תָשִׂים עָלַי מִשְׁמָר.** הַשָּׂטָן הַזֶּה, לְשׁוֹמְרֵנִי, שֶׁלֹּא תֵצֵא נַפְשִׁי?!


**or a sea monster** A huge fish that You imprisoned in the depths of the sea, that You place this Adversary as a guard over me, to watch me lest my soul depart?



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 7:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:13:1)


**יִשָּׂא בְשִׂיחִי מִשְׁכָּבִי.** מִשְׁכַּב הַלַּיְלָה יִסְבֹּל בְּצָרָתִי מְעַט, וְאוּכַל לִמְנֹעַ שִׂיחִי:


**that my couch shall bear my speech** The couch upon which I sleep at night will bear my trouble somewhat, and I will be able to restrain my speech.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 7:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:15:1)


**מָוֶת מֵעַצְמוֹתָי.** מָוֶת אֲנִי בּוֹחֵר יוֹתֵר מֵאֵיבָרִים הַלָּלוּ שֶׁבִּי:


**death rather than these my bones** I choose death rather than these limbs in my body.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 7:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:16:1)


**מָאַסְתִּי.** בְּחַיַּי, כִּי סוֹף סוֹף **לֹא לְעוֹלָם אֶחְיֶה**:


**I despised** my life, because ultimately I will not live forever.


###### Rashi on Job 7:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:16:2)


**חֲדַל מִמֶּנִּי.** מִלְּהָרַע לִי, כִּי הֶבֶל וּמְעַט יָמָי:


**desist from me** From harming me, because my days are futile and few.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Job 7:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:17:1)


**כִּי תְגַדְּלֶנּוּ.** לָשִׂית אֵלָיו לִבְּךָ, לִפְקֹד מַעֲשָׂיו בְּכָל בֹּקֶר, וְלִבְחֹן לְכָל רֶגַע:


**that You should give him importance** to pay attention to him, to visit his deeds every morning and test him every moment?



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 7:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:19:1)


**כַּמָּה.** עֵת אָרֹךְ אֲשֶׁר לֹא תִשְׁעֶה מִמֶּנִּי, כְּמוֹ (שמות ה:ט) ״וְאַל יִשְׁעוּ בְּדִבְרֵי שָׁקֶר״, וְכֵן (איוב יד:ו) ״שְׁעֵה מֵעָלַי וַחֲדָל״:


**How long** For what length of time will You not desist from me, like (Exod. 5:9), “and let them not turn (ישעו) to false words.” Similarly (below 14:6), “Turn away (שעה) from him and desist.”


###### Rashi on Job 7:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:19:2)


**בִּלְעִי רֻקִּי.** כְּדֵי בְּלִיעַת רֻקִּי:


**until I swallow my spit** long enough to swallow my spit.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 7:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:20:1)


**לְמִפְגָּע לָךְ.** כְּפוֹגֵעַ הָאָדָם בְּעֵת חֲמָתוֹ, וּמְעוֹרֵר עָלָיו כָּל מַעֲשֵׂה אֵיבָתוֹ (ס״א חוֹבָתוֹ):


**as a mark for You** As an object that a man strikes when he is angry and vents upon it the entire act of his hatred (other editions: his sin).


###### Rashi on Job 7:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:20:2)


**וָאֶהְיֶה עָלַי לְמַשָּׂא.** זֶה אֶחָד מִן תֵּיבוֹת שֶׁכִּינָּה הַכָּתוּב, וְהֵן תִּיקּוּן סוֹפְרִים:


**so that I have become a burden to myself** [Etz Chayim ms: Like “and I have become a burden to You.”] This is one of the words in which Scripture euphemized, and they are known as the emendation of the scribes.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Job 7:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:21:1)


**וּמֶה לֹא תִשָּׂא פִשְׁעִי.** וּמָה הַדָּבָר הַזֶּה, שֶׁאֵינְךָ נוֹשֵׂא לְפִשְׁעִי?


**Now why do You not forgive my transgression** What is this matter that You do not forgive my transgression?


###### Rashi on Job 7:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/7:21:2)


**וְשִׁחַרְתַּנִי.** תְּבַקְּשֵׁנִי וְלֹא תִמְצָאֵנִי:


**and You shall seek me** You shall seek me and not find me.



## Chapter 8


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:2:1)


**(מענה בלדד):** 
**וְרוּחַ כַּבִּיר.** רוּחַ דְּבָרִים:


**Bildad’s Address**
**a mighty wind** Many words.


###### Rashi on Job 8:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:2:2)


**כַּבִּיר.** לְשׁוֹן רוֹב:


**mighty** Heb. כביר, an expression of multiplicity.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:3:1)


**הַאֵל יְעַוֵּת מִשְׁפָּט.** הַהֵ״א נָקוּד פַּתָּח, וּמְלִיצָתוֹ לְשׁוֹן תֵּימַהּ:


**Does God pervert judgment** Heb. הא-ל. The “hey” is vowelized with a “patach,” and it means an expression of wonder.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 8:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:4:1)


**וַיְשַׁלְּחֵם בְּיַד פִּשְׁעָם.** עַל יְדֵי פִּשְׁעָם, (הַפֶּשַׁע) הוּא נַעֲשָׂה שָׁלִיחַ לְהוֹבִילָם:


**He sent them away in the hands of their transgression** Through their transgression. (The transgression) became the agent to carry them off.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 8:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:5:1)


**אִם אַתָּה תְשַׁחֵר וְגוֹ׳.** יָעִיר עָלֶיךָ אֶת זְכוּתְךָ:


**If you seek God etc.,** He will arouse your merit over you.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 8:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:6:1)


**וְשִׁלַּם נְוַת צִדְקֶךָ.** וְיַעֲשֶׂה אֶת נְוַת צִדְקְךָ שָׁלֵם וְתָמִים:


**and will complete your righteous home** And He will make your righteous home complete and perfect.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 8:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:7:1)


**וְהָיָה רֵאשִׁיתְךָ מִצְעָר.** רֵאשִׁיתְךָ שֶׁעָבְרָה מִצְעָר, לְפִי אַחֲרִיתְךָ, שֶׁיִּשְׂגֶּה מְאֹד:


**And your beginning shall be small** Your beginning, which has passed, shall be small as compared to your end, which shall increase exceedingly.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 8:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:8:1)


**לְדוֹר רִישׁוֹן.** עַל דּוֹר רִאשׁוֹן, כְּמוֹ ״כִּי שְׁאַל נָא לְיָמִים רִאשׁוֹנִים״ (דברים ד:לב):


**about the past generation** About the past generation, like (Deut. 4:32), “For inquire now about the earlier times.”


###### Rashi on Job 8:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:8:2)


**וְכוֹנֵן.** הָכֵן וְהִזְדַּמֵּן לַעֲמֹד עַל חֶקְרֵי אֲבוֹתָם:


**and apply yourself** Apply and prepare yourself to understand what their fathers have searched out.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 8:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:11:1)


**הֲיִגְאֶה גֹּמֶא.** זֹאת יֹאמְרוּ: אֵין הַגֹּמֶא גָּדֵל אֶלָּא בְּעוֹד שֶׁהַבִּצָּה לַחָה בְּמֵימֶיהָ וּבְלִחְלוּחֶיהָ. **בִּצָּה** מָרִישְׁ״ק בְּלַעַ״ז:


**Can papyrus shoot up** They will say this: Papyrus cannot grow except when the marsh is wet with its water and its moisture. בִצָה is marese in Old French, a marsh.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 8:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:12:1)


**בְּאִבּוֹ.** בְּלִחְלוּחוֹ וּבְפִרְיוֹ:


**in its greenness** In its moisture and with its produce.


###### Rashi on Job 8:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:12:2)


**לֹא יִקָּטֵף.** לֹא יִשָּׁבֵר וְלֹא יִנָּתֵק, כְּמוֹ ״וְקָטַפְתָּ מְלִילֹת״ (דברים כג:כו):


**will not be plucked** Heb. יקטף, will not be broken or pulled off; like (Deut. 23:26), “you may pluck (וקטפת) ears.”


###### Rashi on Job 8:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:12:3)


**וְלִפְנֵי כָל חָצִיר יִיבָשׁ.** קוֹדֶם כָּל חָצִיר יִיבַשׁ, כְּשֶׁהַמַּיִם כָּלִים וְהַבִּיצָּה חֲרֵבָה:


**but before any other grass it will dry up** Before any other grass [does], it will dry up when the water evaporates and the marsh dries up.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 8:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:13:1)


**כֵּן אָרְחוֹת כָּל שֹׁכְחֵי אֵל.** לְהַצְלִיחַ בְּעֵת שֶׁשָּׁעָתוֹ מְשַׂחֶקֶת לוֹ, עַד שֶׁתִּתְמַלֵּא סְאָתוֹ:


**So are the ways of all those who forget God** to prosper when it goes well with him until his measure [of sin] is full.


###### Rashi on Job 8:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:13:2)


**וְתִקְוַת.** סוֹפוֹ שֶׁל חָנֵף יֹאבַד:


**and the hope** the end of the flatterer will be lost.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 8:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:14:1)


**אֲשֶׁר יָקוֹט.** יִקְצוֹר וְיִכְרוֹת כִּסְלָתוֹ וְהַבְטָחָתוֹ:


**whose confidence shall be cut off** His confidence and his trust Will be cut off


###### Rashi on Job 8:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:14:2)


**כִּסְלוֹ.** הִיא מַחֲשֶׁבֶת מִבְטַחוֹ, כְּמוֹ (איוב לא:כד) ״אִם שַׂמְתִּי זָהָב כִּסְלִי״. יָקוֹט, לְשׁוֹן קָצִיר, כְּמוֹ (יחזקאל טז:מז) ״כִּמְעַט קָט״:


**confidence** Heb. כסלו. That is the thought of his confidence. like (below 31:24), “If I put my confidence (כסלי) in gold. יקוט is an expression of cutting off; as (Ezek. 16:47): “for after a very little while (קט).”


###### Rashi on Job 8:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:14:3)


**וּבֵית עַכָּבִישׁ.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ קַיָּם, יִהְיֶה מִבְטַחוֹ:


**a spider’s web** which does not last, is his trust.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 8:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:16:1)


**רָטֹב הוּא.** לַח וְחָזָק מַזָּלוֹ טֶרֶם בָּא אֵלָיו עֵת פְּקֻדַּת מַכַּת חוּמוֹ, הַמְיַיבַּשְׁתּוֹ. וּלְפִי שֶׁדִּמָּהוּ בְּגֹמֶא (לעיל ח:יא), מְדַבֵּר לְפִי הָעִנְיָן:


**It is fresh** His fortune is moist and strong before the time of the visitation of the heat plague—which dries him out—comes to him. And because he [Bildad] compares him [the wicked] to the papyrus, he speaks according to that metaphor.


###### Rashi on Job 8:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:16:2)


**וְעַל גַּנָּתוֹ יוֹנַקְתּוֹ תֵצֵא.** יוֹנַקְתּוֹ שֹׁרֶשׁ הַיּוֹנֵק, אוֹ עָנָף הַיַּלְדָּה הַגָּדֵל בְּשָׁנָה זוֹ, כְּמוֹ ״מֵרֹאשׁ יֹנְקוֹתָיו קָטֹף״ (יחזקאל יז:כב):


**and on its garden its tender branch emerges** Heb. יונקתו, its root that draws sustenance, or a young branch that grows this year, like (Ezek. 17: 22), “I will pluck from the top of his young twigs (ינקותיו).”



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Job 8:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:17:1)


**יְסֻבָּכוּ.** יִתְאַחֲזוּ בֶּעָנָף וּבְסוֹבֶךְ:


**are entwined** Will be entwined with the branch and with the thicket.


###### Rashi on Job 8:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:17:2)


**בֵּית אֲבָנִים.** יֶחֱזֶה מָקוֹם אֵיתָן רוֹאֶה וּבוֹנֶה לוֹ לִמְצוּדָה. וּמְנַחֵם פֵּרֵשׁ יֶחֱזֶה לְשׁוֹן גְּבוּל: יִגְבּוֹל אוֹתוֹ, יִבְנֶנּוּ עַד גְּבוּלוֹ, וְכֵן ״מְחוֹז חֶפְצָם״ (תהילים קז:ל), וְכֵן ״מֶחֱזָה אֶל מֶחֱזָה״ (מלכים א ז:ד). כָּל אֵלֶּה לִפְנֵי מַכָּתוֹ:


**a stone house** He sees a strong place and builds it for himself as a stronghold. Menachem, however explains יֶחֶזֶה as an expression of a border: will border him; he will build it up to his border. [Perlow ed, reads: on his border.] Likewise (Ps. 107:30), “their desired haven.” Likewise (I Kings 7:4), “an edge to an edge (מחזה אל מחזה)” (Machbereth Menachem p. 86). All these are before his plague.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Job 8:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:18:1)


**וּכְשֶׁיְּבַלְּעֶנּוּ.** מִמְּקוֹמוֹ – הַנִּפְרָע מִמֶּנּוּ. וּמִקְרָא חָסֵר הוּא זֶה:


**If one destroys him from his place** the one who requites him, and this is an elliptical verse.


###### Rashi on Job 8:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:18:2)


**וְכִחֶשׁ בּוֹ.** מְקוֹמוֹ:


**then it shall deny him** his place [shall deny him].


###### Rashi on Job 8:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:18:3)


**לֹא רְאִיתִיךָ.** שֶׁאֵין נִשְׁאָר מִמֶּנּוּ לֹא שֹׁרֶשׁ וְלֹא עָנָף, וַהֲרֵי הוּא כְּלֹא הָיָה. שֶׁאֵין נְפִילָתוֹ כְּמַפֶּלֶת צַדִּיקִים; כִּי הַצַּדִּיק יִפּוֹל וְיָקוּם (משלי כד:טז), וְזֶה יִפּוֹל בְּלֹא תְקוּמָה:


**‘I have not seen you,’** For neither root nor branch shall remain of him, and it will be as though he had never existed, for his fall shall not ascend like the downfall of the righteous—for the righteous will fall and rise, but this one will fall without rising.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 8:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:19:1)


**הֵן הוּא מְשׂוֹשׂ דַּרְכּוֹ.** שֶׁל רָשָׁע, שֶׁכָּל מְשׂוֹשׂוֹ וְגַם הַצְלָחָתוֹ, לְלֹא תִּקְוָה תָּשׁוּב:


**Behold this is the joy of his way** Of the wicked man, for all his joy and also his success will end up without hope.


###### Rashi on Job 8:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:19:2)


**וּמֵעָפָר אַחֵר יִצְמָחוּ.** בְּנֵי אָדָם שֶׁהָיוּ עַד הֵנָּה שָׁחִים עַד עָפָר, יִצְמָחוּ:


**and from the earth, others will spring forth** And people who were until now bent down to the ground will spring forth.


###### Rashi on Job 8:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:19:3)


**אַחֵר.** אָדָם אַחֵר יִצְמַח לְקַבֵּל גְּדֻלָּתוֹ שֶׁל זֶה, כְּשֵׁם שֶׁמָּצִינוּ ״וְלַחוֹטֵא נָתַן עִנְיָן וְגוֹ׳״ (קהלת ב:כו). עַל כֵּן דַּע עַל עַצְמְךָ: אִם רָשָׁע הָיִיתָ, אֵין תְּקוּמָה לְמַפַּלְתְּךָ, וְאִם תָּם הָיִיתָ וְכוּ׳:


**others** Another man will spring forth to receive the greatness of this one, as we find (Eccles. 2:26): “and to the sinner He has given the task...” Therefore, you should know about yourself, if you were wicked, you will not rise after your downfall. If you were innocent...



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 8:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/8:20:1)


**הֵן אֵל לֹא יִמְאַס תָּם.** וְלֹא יַחֲזִיק בְּיַד מְשַׂנְאֶיךָ הַמְּרֵעִים לְךָ, עַד אֲשֶׁר יָשׁוּב לְרַחֵם עָלֶיךָ וִימַלֵּא שְׂחוֹק פִּיךָ:


**Behold God will not reject the innocent** and He will not uphold your enemies who are harming you, until He returns to grant you clemency, and [then] He will fill your mouth with laughter.



## Chapter 9


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 9:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:3:1)


**(מענה איוב):** 
**לָרִיב עִמּוֹ.** לְהִתְוַכֵּחַ עִמּוֹ:


**Job’s Reply**
**to contend with Him** To debate with Him.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 9:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:4:1)


**חֲכַם לֵבָב.** הוּא לָרִיב:


**He is wise in heart** to contend.


###### Rashi on Job 9:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:4:2)


**וְאַמִּיץ כֹּחַ.** לְהִפָּרַע:


**and mighty in strength** to requite.


###### Rashi on Job 9:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:4:3)


**מִי הִקְשָׁה.** לִבּוֹ וְהָלַךְ שָׁלֵם?! פַּרְעֹה הִקְשָׁה וְנֶחְסַר (שמות ז:יג, ח:טו, ט:יב), וְכֵן כָּל הַמַּקְשִׁים:


**who hardened** his heart and went away whole? Pharaoh hardened [his heart] and was destroyed, and so it is with all who hardened [their hearts].



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 9:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:5:1)


**הַמַּעְתִּיק הָרִים.** הַמְּסַלְּקָם, כְּהָפְכוֹ סְדוֹם (בראשית יט:כה):


**He Who moved mountains** He moved them away when He overturned Sodom.


###### Rashi on Job 9:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:5:2)


**וְלֹא יָדְעוּ.** עַד שֶׁנֶּהֶפְכוּ:


**and they knew not** until He turned them over.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 9:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:6:1)


**הַמַּרְגִּיז אֶרֶץ.** בְּהַבִּיטוֹ אֵלֶיהָ וְתִרְעָד (תהלים קד:לב):


**He Who causes the earth to quake** when He looks at it, and it trembles.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 9:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:7:1)


**הָאוֹמֵר לַחֶרֶס.** לַשֶּׁמֶשׁ: דּוֹם (יהושע י:יב)! וְהוּא שָׁמַר אִמְרַת מֶלֶךְ **וְלֹא יִזְרָח**:


**He Who spoke to the sun** Heb. לחרס, to the sun, “Stand still.” and it kept the command of the King and did not shine.


###### Rashi on Job 9:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:7:2)


**וּבְעַד.** וּכְנֶגֶד הַכּוֹכָבִים יִסְגֹּר מְחִיצָה, וְלֹא יָאִירוּ:


**and He settled up the stars** He closed off the stars with a partition and they did not shine.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 9:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:8:1)


**נוֹטֶה שָׁמַיִם לְבַדּוֹ.** וּמַלְאָכִים נִבְרְאוּ בַּיּוֹם שֵׁנִי, שֶׁלֹּא תֹּאמַר: מִיכָאֵל מָתַח בַּצָּפוֹן, וְגַבְרִיאֵל בַּדָּרוֹם:


**He Who clone stretched out the heavens** and the angels were created on the second day, lest you say that Michael stretched it out in the north and Gabriel in the south.


###### Rashi on Job 9:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:8:2)


**עַל בָּמֳתֵי יָם.** עַל גָּבְהֵי יָם. וְעַל מַעֲשֵׂה בְרֵאשִׁית נֶאֱמַר, שֶׁגָּבְהוּ הַמַּיִם הַתַּחְתּוֹנִים לָרוּם לְמַעְלָה, וּדְרָכָם:


**on the high places of the sea** On the heights of the sea. This was stated regarding the Creation, that the lower waters raised themselves up on high, and He trod them.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Job 9:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:9:1)


**כְּסִיל וְכִימָה.** מַזָּלוֹת הֵם:


**Orion, and the Pleiades** they are constellations.


###### Rashi on Job 9:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:9:2)


**וְחַדְרֵי תֵימָן.** לְהַכְנִיס שָׁם סוּפָה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַר ״מִן הַחֶדֶר תָּבוֹא סוּפָה״ (איוב לז:ט):


**and the chambers of the south** into which to bring the tempest, as he states (below 37:9): “From the chamber shall come the tempest.”



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 9:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:11:1)


**הֵן יַעֲבֹר וְגוֹ׳.** יָדַעְתִּי כִּי כָּל אֲשֶׁר יַחְפֹּץ יַעֲשֶׂה:


**Behold, He goes by me** I know that all He wishes, He does.


###### Rashi on Job 9:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:11:2)


**יַעֲבֹר עָלַי.** לְפָנַי, וְלֹא אֶרְאֶנּוּ:


**He goes by me** Before me, but I do not see Him.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 9:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:12:1)


**יַחְתֹּף.** יַכֶּה פִּתְאוֹם, כְּמוֹ ״הִיא כְחָתֶף תֶּאֱרֹב״ (משלי כג:כח). לְשׁוֹן ׳פִּתְאוֹם׳ הוּא:


**Behold, He strikes suddenly** Heb. יחתף, He strikes suddenly, like (Prov. 23:28), “She, too, will suddenly (כחתף) lurk,” an expression of suddenness.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 9:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:13:1)


**לֹא יָשִׁיב אַפּוֹ.** מִפְּנֵי יִרְאַת (וְצִדְקַת ס״א) אִישׁ:


**will not hold back His wrath** because of fear (other editions: righteousness) of a man.


###### Rashi on Job 9:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:13:2)


**תַּחְתָּיו שָׁחֲחוּ.** צְבָא מָרוֹם שֶׁבָּאוּ לַעֲזוֹר אֶת מִצְרַיִם. מִצְרַיִם נִקְרָא רַהַב, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״לָכֵן קָרָאתִי לָזֹאת רַהַב הֵם שָׁבֶת״ (ישעיהו ל:ז) – עַל שֵׁם גַּאֲוָתָם, אֲשֶׁר אָמְרוּ ״מִי ה׳ וְגוֹ׳״ (שמות ה:ב):


**[all the helpers of Rahab] stoop under Him** The celestial host that came to aid the Egyptians. Egypt is called Rahab, as it is said (Isa. 30:7): “Therefore, I called this, ‘They are haughty (רהב) idlers,’” by dint of their haughtiness, for they said, “Who is the Lord? etc.” (Exod. 5:2).



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 9:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:14:1)


**אַף.** כָּל שֶׁכֵּן:


**Surely** Heb. אַף.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 9:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:15:1)


**לֹא אֶעֱנֶה.** אִירָא לְהָרִים קוֹל לְפָנָיו:


**I will not reply** I will fear to raise my voice before Him.


###### Rashi on Job 9:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:15:2)


**לִמְשׁוֹפְטִי.** כְּמוֹ לְשׁוֹפְטִי:


**my Judge** Heb. למשפטי, like לשופטי.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 9:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:16:1)


**לֹא אַאֲמִין.** לֹא אוּכַל לְהַאֲמִין מִיִּרְאָתִי אוֹתוֹ, כִּי אֵיךְ לֹא אִירָאֶנּוּ?


**I will not believe** I will be unable to believe out of my fear for Him, for how do I not see Him?



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Job 9:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:17:1)


**אֲשֶׁר בִּשְׂעָרָה.** נִתְחַלְּפָה צוּרָתִי (ס״א שׁוּרָתִי) לְפָנָיו:


**He Who would crush me with a tempest** My form was changed (other editions: my status) before Him.


###### Rashi on Job 9:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:17:2)


**יְשׁוּפֵנִי.** יְכַתְּתֵנִי, וְ״אָכֹת אֹתוֹ טָחוֹן״ (דברים ט:כא) מְתַרְגְּמִינָן ״וְשָׁפֵית יָתֵיהּ״ (תרגום אונקלוס):


**would crush me** Heb. ישופני, would crush me. “I crushed it and ground it” (Deut. 9:21) is translated into Aramaic as: ושפית יתֵה.


###### Rashi on Job 9:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:17:3)


**פְּצָעַי.** מַכָּה הַמּוֹצִיאָה דָּם וְלֵחָה:


**my wounds** Heb. פצעי, a wound that oozes blood and pus.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Job 9:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:18:1)


**לֹא יִתְּנֵנִי.** אֵינוֹ מַנִּיחֵנִי לְהָשִׁיב רוּחִי אֵלַי:


**He Who would not let me** He does not let me bring back my breath to myself.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 9:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:19:1)


**אִם לְכֹחַ.** אֵיךְ אָרִיב עִמּוֹ? אִם לְכֹחַ הוּא בָּא – **אַמִּיץ הִנֵּה הוּא**:


**If it is a trial of strength** How can I contend with Him? If He comes with strength, behold He is mighty.


###### Rashi on Job 9:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:19:2)


**וְאִם לְמִשְׁפָּט.** מִי יוּכַל לְהוֹעִידֵנִי לְפָנָיו וּלְהַחֲזִיק דְּבַר מִשְׁפָּטִי?


**and if of judgment** who will be able to summon me before Him and confirm my verdict? Is it not so that...



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 9:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:20:1)


**הֲלֹא אִם אֶצְדָּק פִּי יַרְשִׁיעֵנִי.** כִּי יִסָּתְמוּ דְבָרַי מִיִּרְאָה, וְיַעְקְשֵׁנִי פִּי:


**If I am innocent, my mouth will condemn me** because my words will be silenced out of fear, and my mouth will make me crooked.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Job 9:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:21:1)


**תָּם אָנִי.** יוֹדֵעַ אֲנִי בְּעַצְמִי שֶׁאֲנִי תָּם, וְאַף עַל פִּי כֵן **לֹא אֵדַע נַפְשִׁי.** בַּמֶּה אֶמְצָא נוֹפֶשׁ:


**I am blameless** I know of myself that I am blameless; nonetheless, I do not know my rest, how I will find rest.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Job 9:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:22:1)


**אַחַת הִיא.** בָּעוֹלָם, וְעָלֶיהָ אָמַרְתִּי **שֶׁתָּם וְרָשָׁע הוּא מְכַלֶּה** בַּעֲבוּרָהּ; וּמַה הִיא?


**It is all one** in the world, concerning which I said that He destroys both the innocent and the wicked. Now, what is it?...



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Job 9:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:23:1)


**אִם שׁוֹט.** לָשׁוֹן שֶׁל שָׂטָן יָמִית פִּתְאוֹם אֶת הַצַּדִּיק:


**If the scourge** of the Adversary’s tongue suddenly puts the righteous to death.


###### Rashi on Job 9:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:23:2)


**לְמַסַּת נְקִיִּים.** שֶׁהֵמִית, יִלְעָג:


**at the calamity of the innocent** that he put to death he will mock.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Job 9:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:24:1)


**בְּיַד רָשָׁע.** כְּלַפֵּי שָׂטָן:


**into the hands of a wicked one** Referring to the Adversary.


###### Rashi on Job 9:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:24:2)


**פְּנֵי שׁוֹפְטֶיהָ יְכַסֶּה.** מֵהַבְחִין בְּדָבָר אֱמֶת:


**he covers the faces of its judges** from discerning and comprehending the truth of the matter.


###### Rashi on Job 9:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:24:3)


**וְאִם לֹא אֵפוֹא.** דָּבָר אֱמֶת דָּבָר זֶה, מִי הוּא הַמְכַלֶּה אֶת הַתְּמִימִים?


**If not, then** If this [interpretation] is not the truth of the matter, who is it that destroys the innocent?



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Job 9:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:25:1)


**וְיָמַי.** עִם כִּלְיוֹן שְׁאָר הַתְּמִימִים, **קַלּוּ מִנִּי רָץ**:


**My days** with the destruction of the other innocent people are swifter than a runner.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Job 9:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:26:1)


**אֵבֶה.** שֵׁם נָהָר שׁוֹטֵף:


**Ebeh** The name of a rushing river.


###### Rashi on Job 9:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:26:2)


**יָטוּשׂ.** יִפְרַח:


**swoops** Heb. יטוש, flies.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Job 9:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:27:1)


**שִׂיחִי.** דִּבְרֵי צַעֲרִי:


**my speech** The words of my pain.


###### Rashi on Job 9:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:27:2)


**פָּנַי.** חֲמָתִי:


**my anger** Heb. פני, lit. my face.


###### Rashi on Job 9:27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:27:3)


**וְאַבְלִיגָה.** אֶתְחַזֵּק, כְּמוֹ ״הַמַּבְלִיג שֹׁד עַל עָז״ (עמוס ה:ט):


**and [I will] restrain myself** Heb. ואבליגה, I will strengthen myself, like (Amos 5:9), “Who strengthens (המבליג) the robbed upon the strong.”



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Job 9:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:28:1)


**יָגֹרְתִּי כָל עַצְּבוֹתָי.** שֶׁלֹּא יַנִּיחוּנִי מִלִּצְעוֹק:


**I will gather in all my sadness** that it not let me cry out.


###### Rashi on Job 9:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:28:2)


**יָדַעְתִּי כִּי לֹא תְנַקֵּנִי.** מֵעֲוֹנִי, מִלְּהִפָּרַע מִמֶּנִּי:


**I know that You will not declare me innocent** of my iniquity, so as not to requite me.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Job 9:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:29:1)


**לָמָּה זֶּה הֶבֶל אִיגָע.** לָבוֹא בְּמִשְׁפָּט:


**why should I toil in vain** to come to judgment?



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Job 9:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:30:1)


**בְּבוֹר.** נִקָּיוֹן:


**with purity** Heb. בבור, with cleanliness.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Job 9:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:31:1)


**בַּשַּׁחַת תִּטְבְּלֵנִי.** לִהְיוֹת צָחוֹן וּמְגֹאָל, כְּלוֹמַר: אָז תִּמְצָא לִי עָוֹן רַב:


**You will plunge me into the ditch** to be sullied and contaminated; i.e., You will find great iniquity in me.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Job 9:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:32:1)


**בַּמִּשְׁפָּט.** בְּדִבְרֵי תוֹכָחוֹת. מִשְׁפָּט מְשַׁמֵּשׁ שָׁלֹשׁ לְשׁוֹנוֹת: תְּחִלָּתוֹ הוֹכָחַת דְּבָרִים, אֶמְצָעִיתוֹ פְּסַק דִּין, וְסוֹפוֹ מִשְׁטַר יִסּוּרִין וְגִבּוּי חוֹב. וּפְעָמִים שֶׁהַמִּקְרָא כּוֹתְבוֹ עַל שֵׁם תְּחִלָּתוֹ, וּפְעָמִים עַל שֵׁם אֶמְצָעִיתוֹ, וּפְעָמִים עַל שֵׁם אַחֲרִיתוֹ:


**in debate** Heb. במשפט, in words of debate. [The word] משפט is used in three expressions: 1) its beginning, viz. the clarification of the matters, 2) its middle, viz. the verdict, and 3) its end, viz. the meting out of the tortures and the collection of the debt. Sometimes Scripture writes it as referring to the beginning, sometimes to the middle, and sometimes to the end.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Job 9:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:33:1)


**מוֹכִיחַ.** דְּרַשְׁנִי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז, מַרְאֶה לְכָל אֶחָד חוֹבָתוֹ וּזְכוּתוֹ:


**arbiter** discerner in Old French. He shows each one his guilt and his innocence.


###### Rashi on Job 9:33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:33:2)


**יָשֶׁת יָדוֹ.** מִשְׁטַר אֵימָתוֹ וְנִיגוּשׁוֹ:


**who will place his hand** The ruling of his fear and his power.


###### Rashi on Job 9:33:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:33:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:33:3)


**עַל שְׁנֵינוּ.** שֶׁלֹּא יְחַזֵּק הֶחָזָק אֶת הָרָפֶה:


**on both of us** so that the stronger does not overpower the weaker.



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on Job 9:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/9:35:1)


**כִּי לֹא כֵן אָנֹכִי.** עַל כֵּן שֶׁאָנֹכִי יָרֵא, אֲנִי מֻחְזָק בִּי שֶׁלֹּא אֶתְחַיֵּב בַּדִּין:


**for I am not so with myself** Because I fear [God], I am certain with myself that I will not be found guilty in the trial.



## Chapter 10


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Job 10:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:1:1)


**נָקְטָה נַפְשִׁי.** נַפְשִׁי מִתְקוֹטֶטֶת עַל שֶׁאֲנִי חַי, וְהוּא לְשׁוֹן ״וּנְקֹטֹתֶם בִּפְנֵיכֶם״ (יחזקאל כ:מג):


**My soul quarrels** Heb. נקטה. My soul quarrels about the fact that I am alive. That is an expression of (Ezek. 20:43), “and you shall quarrel (ונקטתם) with yourselves.


###### Rashi on Job 10:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:1:2)


**אֶעֶזְבָה עָלַי שִׂיחִי.** צָרָתִי, לִזְעוֹק וְלִקְבּוֹל, וְלֹא אוּכַל לְשַׁבְּחוֹ וּלְהַבְלִיגוֹ:


**I will leave my speech** i.e., my trouble, to cry and lament, and I cannot forget it or restrain it.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 10:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:3:1)


**הֲטוֹב.** בִּתְמִיהַּ, כִּי תַעֲשֹׁק וְתִגְזֹל מִן הַצַּדִּיק אֶת צִדְקוֹ, **וְעַל עֲצַת רְשָׁעִים** וּמַקְנִיטִים **הוֹפַעְתָּ** זִיוְךָ, לְהַרְאוֹת לָהֶם פָּנִים שׂוֹחֲקוֹת?


**Is it good** Heb. הטוב, an interrogative form. [Is it good to You] that You should oppress and rob the righteous man of his righteousness, [while] upon the council of the wicked, who vex You, You shine Your splendor to show them a friendly countenance?



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 10:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:5:1)


**הֲכִימֵי אֱנוֹשׁ יָמֶיךָ.** לְהִתְגָּרוֹת בּוֹ וְלִרְדֹּף?


**Are Your days like the days of a mortal** to provoke him and pursue [him]?



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 10:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:7:1)


**עַל דַּעְתְּךָ.** יוֹדֵעַ אַתָּה:


**It is in Your knowledge** You know.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 10:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:8:1)


**עִצְּבוּנִי.** עֲשָׂאוּנִי, עֶצֶב דְּפוּס כְּמוֹ אֵין מְעַצְּבִין אֶת הַקָּטָן (שבת קמו:). וְעַתָּה חֵילוֹתֶיךָ יַחַד סָבִיב:


**shaped me** Heb. עצבוני, They made me a shape, a mold, like “nor straighten (מעצבין) an infant’s limbs,” in Tractate Shabbath (22:6). But now Your hosts are together round about.


###### Rashi on Job 10:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:8:2)


**וַתְּבַלְּעֵנִי.** הֵם הַתּוֹלָעִים:


**and You destroy me** These are the worms.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Job 10:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:9:1)


**תְּשִׁיבֵנִי.** לְבַסּוֹף:


**You will return me** in the end.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 10:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:10:1)


**כֶּחָלָב תַּתִּיכֵנִי.** כְּטִפַּת תּוֹלְדוֹתַי:


**Did you not pour me like milk** Concerning the drop from which I was born.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 10:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:12:1)


**וּפְקֻדָּתְךָ.** פְּקֻדַּת מִשְׁמַרְתְּךָ, שֶׁמִּנִּיתָ לִי שׁוֹמְרִים:


**and Your providence** The providence of Your watch, for You appointed watchers for me.


###### Rashi on Job 10:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:12:2)


**שָׁמְרָה רוּחִי.** בְּבֶטֶן אִמִּי, וְאַחַר כָּךְ:


**watched my spirit** in my mother’s womb, and afterwards...



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 10:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:13:1)


**וְאֵלֶּה צָפַנְתָּ בִלְבָבֶךָ.** לְבַלְּעֵנִי, כְּאִלּוּ אֵין (אַתָּה) זוֹכְרָם:


**But You hid these in Your heart** to destroy me as though You did not remember them, but...


###### Rashi on Job 10:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:13:2)


**יָדַעְתִּי כִּי זֹאת עִמָּךְ.** הַכֹּל כַּמְפֹרָשׁ, וְלֹא שָׁכַחְתָּ:


**I knew that this was with You**, everything as it was delineated, and You did not forget.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 10:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:14:1)


**אִם חָטָאתִי וּשְׁמַרְתָּנִי.** נָתַתָּ עֵינֶיךָ בִּי מִלְּהַעְלִים וְלַעֲבוֹר עַל מִדּוֹתֶיךָ. (וּמֵעַתָּה) **וּמֵעֲוֺנִי אֵין אַתָּה מְנַקֵּנִי**, אֶלָּא עַל הַכֹּל אַתָּה פּוֹרֵעַ:


**If I sinned, You watched me** You laid Your eyes on me so as not to ignore [my sins] nor to forbear retaliation, and [from now on] You are not cleansing me of my iniquity but are requiting me for everything.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 10:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:15:1)


**שְׂבַע קָלוֹן.** אֲנִי:


**sated with disgrace** am I.


###### Rashi on Job 10:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:15:2)


**וּרְאֵה עָנְיִי.** אֵין זֶה צִוּוּי, אֶלָּא שֵׁם דָּבָר: **רְאֵה עָנְיִי.** בֶּדְנַ״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז; שֶׁכָּל שָׁעָה אֲנִי רוֹאֵהוּ. כְּמוֹ שֶׁאַתָּה אוֹמֵר ״וּשְׂבַע יָמִים״ (איוב מב:יז) – כְּמוֹ וְשָׂבֵעַ יָמִים, כֵּן תֹּאמַר וּרְאֵה – כְּמוֹ ׳וְרוֹאֶה עָנְיִי׳:


**and seeing my affliction** Heb. וראה. This is not an expression of the imperative, but is an adjective. וראה עניי is badlace (?) in Old French, “that I see it all the time.” As you say (below, at the end of the Book), “וּשְׂבַע יָמִים, and I am sated with days,” like “וְשָׂבֵעַ יָמִים” (with a “kamatz” under the “sin”) so do you say; וּרְאֵה, like וְרוֹאֶה, “and seeing my affliction.”



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 10:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:16:1)


**וְיִגְאֶה כַּשַּׁחַל תְּצוּדֵנִי.** וְיִגְדַּל בְּעֵינֶיךָ לִהְיוֹת לְךָ לְמַשָּׂא וּלְפַח נֶפֶשׁ, אֶת אֲשֶׁר **כַּשַּׁחַל תְּצוּדֵנִי** אֲשֶׁר תִּפְרֹשׂ לִי מְצוּדָה, כְּאִלּוּ אֲנִי גִּבּוֹר כַּשַּׁחַל:


**And it is so great that You hunt me like a lion** And it is so great in Your eyes to be to You as a burden and a disappointment, that You hunt me like a lion; You spread out a net for me as though I was as mighty as a lion.


###### Rashi on Job 10:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:16:2)


**וְתָשׁוּב.** מִיּוֹם אֶל יוֹם:


**and You repeatedly** from day to day.


###### Rashi on Job 10:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:16:3)


**תִּתְפַּלָּא בִי.** לְדַקְדֵּק וּלְהַפְלִיא בַּמִּשְׁפָּט, לְשׁוֹן ״כִּי יַפְלִא״ (במדבר ו:ב):


**pronounce a verdict upon me** Heb. תתפלא, to exact and to pronounce judgment, an expression of (Num. 6:2). “shall pronounce (יפלא).”



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Job 10:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:17:1)


**עֵדֶיךָ.** לְהָעִיד עַל חֲלִיפַת הֶחֳלָאִים וּמַכְאוֹבִים:


**Your witnesses** to testify about the changes of the ailments and the pains.


###### Rashi on Job 10:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:17:2)


**וְצָבָא עִמִּי.** זְמַנִּים קְצוּבִים לְיִסּוּרַי; אֵלּוּ מְשַׁמְּשִׁים (צבא א׳), וְחוֹזְרִים וּמִתְחַלְּפִים שִׁנּוּיִים מְשֻׁנִּים מֵאֵלֶּה, וּמְשַׁמְּשִׁין זְמַן אַחֵר:


**with set times are with me** Times set for these my tortures, which serve (once) and [then] repeatedly shift with changes different from these, and serve again.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Job 10:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:18:1)


**אֶגְוַע וְעַיִן לֹא תִרְאֵנִי.** הַלְוַאי אֶגְוַע מֵרֶחֶם:


**Would that I had died and no eye had seen me** If only I had died [upon emerging] from the womb.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 10:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:19:1)


**אוּבָל.** הַלְוַאי הָיִיתִי מוּבָל:


**would that I was brought from the womb to the grave** Would that I was brought.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 10:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:20:1)


**וְשִׁית מִמֶּנִּי.** תָּשִׁית עַצְמְךָ מִמֶּנִּי וּמֵעָלַי, כְּמוֹ ״קְרַב אֵלֶיךָ״ (ישעיה סה:ה) – קְרַב לְעַצְמְךָ וּרְחַק מִמֶּנִּי:


**withdraw from me** Withdraw Yourself from me and from upon me, like (Isa. 65:5), “Keep to yourself” Remain by yourself and distance yourself from me.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Job 10:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:21:1)


**בְּטֶרֶם אֵלֵךְ.** בְּעוֹדֶנִּי חַי וְלֹא הִגִּיעַ עֵת מוֹתִי, הַנַּח לִי וְאַרְגִּיעַ:


**before I go** While I am still alive, and the time for me to die has not yet arrived, desist from me and I will rest.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Job 10:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:22:1)


**עֵיפָתָה.** שֵׁם דָּבָר, חֲשֵׁיכָה, כְּמוֹ ״שַׁחַר וְעֵיפָה״ (עמוס ד:יג):


**darkness** Heb. עפתה. This is a noun meaning darkness, like (Amos 4: 13) “dawn and darkness (ועיפתה).”


###### Rashi on Job 10:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:22:2)


**צַלְמָוֶת.** הִיא וְלֹא סְדָרִים בָּהּ (שֶׁאֵין שָׁם סִדְרֵי אָדָם כְּלוֹמַר מִנְהָגִים שֶׁאֵין שָׁם יִשּׁוּב וְאוֹר שֶׁלָּהּ כְּמוֹ אֹפֶל הוּא):


**the shadow of death** it is, without orders in it (that there are no orders of man; i.e., there are no customs, for there is no civilization, and its light is like darkness).


###### Rashi on Job 10:22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/10:22:3)


**וַתֹּפַע כְּמוֹ אֹפֶל.** מָקוֹם מוּפָע שֶׁבָּהּ, כְּמוֹ אֹפֶל הוּא:


**and where the light is as darkness** The illuminated place [therein] is like darkness.



## Chapter 11


### Verse 2
