###### Rashi on Job 11:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:2:1)


**(מענה צופר:)** 
**לֹא יֵעָנֶה.** מֵאֲחֵרִים, מִפְּנֵי רֹב דְּבָרָיו:


**Zophar’s Answer**
**Should not...be answered** by others because of the multitude of his words?



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 11:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:3:1)


**בַּדֶּיךָ.** שֶׁאַתָּה בּוֹדֶה מִלִּבְּךָ:


**Your fabrications** that you invent from your heart.


###### Rashi on Job 11:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:3:2)


**מְתִים יַחֲרִישׁוּ.** בְּנֵי אָדָם:


**may silence men** Heb. מתים, people.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 11:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:4:1)


**וַתֹּאמֶר.** לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא: **זַךְ לִקְחִי וּבַר הָיִיתִי בְעֵינֶיךָ**:


**You said** to the Holy One, blessed be He, ‘My doctrine is pure, and I was clean in Your eyes.’



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 11:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:6:1)


**תַּעֲלוּמוֹת חָכְמָה.** וְתֵדַע, כִּי **כִפְלַיִם לְתוּשִׁיָּה** יֵשׁ לַתּוֹרָה, שֶׁלֹּא קִיַּמְתָּ:


**mysteries of wisdom** and you would know that the wisdom is double, i.e., the Torah that you did not fulfill.


###### Rashi on Job 11:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:6:2)


**כִּי יַשֶּׁה.** כְּמוֹ ״כִּי תַשֶּׁה בְרֵעֲךָ״ (דברים כד:י), (כְּלוֹמַר) הַרְבֵּה יֵשׁ לוֹ עוֹד חוֹב עָלֶיךָ בְּדִבְרֵי עֲוֹנֶיךָ:


**that [God] lends** Heb. ישה, like (Deut.24: 10), “If you lend (תשה) your friend.” He has yet a large debt over you in the matters of your iniquity.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 11:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:7:1)


**הַחֵקֶר אֱלוֹהַּ תִּמְצָא.** שֶׁאַתָּה סָבוּר שֶׁקִּיַּמְתָּ הַכֹּל?


**Can you find out the mystery of God** that you think that you have fulfilled everything?



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 11:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:8:1)


**גָּבְהֵי שָׁמַיִם.** דָּבָר שֶׁהוּא גָּבוֹהַּ כְּגָבְהֵי שָׁמַיִם, **מַה תִּפְעַל** עַתָּה? וַעֲמֻקָּה הִיא הַחָכְמָה **מִשְּׁאוֹל**:


**[In] the heights of heaven** [About] something that is as high as the heights of heaven, what can you do? And the wisdom is deeper than the grave.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Job 11:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:9:1)


**מִדָּה.** מִדּוּד שֶׁלָּהּ (ס״א מִדָּה שֶׁלָּהּ), וְכָאן הִזְכִּיר מִדּוּד בְּלָשׁוֹן זָכָר (שֶׁלֹּא אָמַר מִדָּתָהּ):


**its measure** Heb. מדה, its measure, but here he mentions measure with a masculine noun (since he did not say, מדתה).



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 11:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:10:1)


**אִם יַחֲלֹף וְיַסְגִּיר.** בְּיִסּוּרִין אֶת מִי שֶׁיִּרְצֶה:


**If He passes and confines** with pains the one He wishes,


###### Rashi on Job 11:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:10:2)


**וְיַקְהִיל.** כָּל פַּמַלְיָאוֹת שֶׁלּוֹ, לְוַכֵּחַ עַל שֶׁהִסְגִּיר:


and He assembles all His divine ministers to justify His act in having confined.


###### Rashi on Job 11:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:10:3)


**וּמִי.** בָּהֶן יְשִׁיבֶנּוּ בִּדְבָרִים, לִמְצֹא עָוֶל בְּפָעֳלוֹ? וְאִם מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהַנִּדּוֹן שָׁלֵו וְשָׁקֵט יָמִים רַבִּים, כֵּן דַּרְכּוֹ:


**who** among them **can hinder Him** with words to find injustice in His act? And if because the One being judged is tranquil and silent for many days, that is His way.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 11:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:11:1)


**כִּי הוּא יָדַע מְתֵי שָׁוְא וַיַּרְא אָוֶן וְלֹא יִתְבּוֹנָן.** שֶׁהֵם עוֹשִׂים יָמִים וְשָׁנִים, וְדוֹמֶה כְּמִי שֶׁלֹּא יִתְבּוֹנֵן (יִתְבּוֹנְנוּ), כִּי מַאֲרִיךְ אַפּוֹ:


**For He knows deceitful people; when He sees iniquity, He does not consider it** [He sees the iniquity] that they commit for days and years, and it seems as though He does not consider it because He is slow to anger.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 11:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:12:1)


**וְאִישׁ נָבוּב.** חָלוּל, בְּלֹא לֵב, אֲשֶׁר לֹא הֵבִין דַּרְכּוֹ:


**But an empty man** a hollow man, without a heart [i.e., understanding], who did not understand His way.


###### Rashi on Job 11:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:12:2)


**יִלָּבֵב.** יִקְנֶה לוֹ לֵב, לָשׁוּב לְבוֹרְאוֹ וּלְפַשְׁפֵּשׁ בְּמַעֲשָׂיו. אוֹ אָדָם אֲשֶׁר הָיָה כְּעַיִר פֶּרֶא ״לִמֻּד מִדְבָּר״ (ירמיה ב:כד), קַל בְּלֹא דַעַת:


**will gain understanding** Heb. ילבב, he will gain for himself a heart [i.e., understanding] to return to his Creator and to search through his deeds—or [he will remain] a man who was like a wild donkey, accustomed to the desert, hasty, without sense.


###### Rashi on Job 11:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:12:3)


**אָדָם יִוָּלֵד.** יְלַמֵּד עַצְמוֹ לִהְיוֹת אָדָם חָדָשׁ, כְּסֵדֶר בְּנֵי אָדָם, וִיכַוֵּן דַּרְכּוֹ:


**a man will be born** He will teach himself to be a new man according to the order of people, and he will determine his way.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 11:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:13:1)


**אִם אַתָּה.** הָיִיתָ מְכַוֵּן לִבְּךָ אַחַר יִסּוּרִין:


**If you** would aim your heart after your pains.


###### Rashi on Job 11:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:13:2)


**וּפָרַשְׂתָּ אֵלָיו כַּפֶּיךָ.** בִּתְחִנָּה:


**and spread out your hands to Him** in supplication.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 11:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:14:1)


**וְתַרְחֵק אָוֶן** מִיָּדְךָ, **וְאַל תַּשְׁכֵּן בְּאָהָלֶיךָ עַוְלָה**:


**Distance iniquity** from your hand, and do not allow injustice to dwell in your tents.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 11:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:15:1)


**כִּי אָז.** אִם אַתָּה הָיִיתָ בָּטוּחַ שֶׁתִּשָּׂא פָנֶיךָ מִמּוּם שֶׁתִּגְרֹם לְעַצְמְךָ לָשֵׂאת פָנֶיךָ מִכָּל מוּם (מִתּוֹךְ כָּךְ):


**For then** you would be sure that you could lift your face unblemished, for you would cause yourself to lift your face without any blemish. Because of this...


###### Rashi on Job 11:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:15:2)


**וְהָיִיתָ מֻצָּק.** חָזָק, כְּמוֹ ״מְצוּקֵי אֶרֶץ״ (שמואל א ב:ח):


**and you shall be strong** Heb. מצק, strong, like (I Sam. 2:8), “the pillars of (מצקי) the earth,”



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 11:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:16:1)


**עָמָל תִּשְׁכָּח.** וְכָל עָמָל הָיִיתָ שׁוֹכֵחַ:


and you shall forget all your trouble.


###### Rashi on Job 11:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:16:2)


**וּכְמַיִם עָבְרוּ.** וְחָלְפוּ, כֵּן יִהְיֶה לְךָ כָּל זִכְרוֹן עָמָל:


**like water that has passed** and gone by, so shall be the entire memory of your trouble.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Job 11:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:17:1)


**וּמִצָּהֳרַיִם יָקוּם חָלֶד.** יוֹתֵר מֵאוֹר צָהֳרַיִם יָקוּם לְךָ חֶלֶד – מַזָּל וּזְמַן, כְּמוֹ ״וְחֶלְדִּי כְאַיִן נֶגְדֶּךָ״ (תהילים לט:ו). דָּבָר אַחֵר: **יָקוּם חָלֶד** כְּמוֹ יָקוּם אוֹר חֲלוּדָה שֶׁלְּךָ, כְּלוֹמַר: שֶׁיִּהְיֶה מְקוֹם אֲפֵלָה מֵאִיר יוֹתֵר מֵאוֹר צָהֳרַיִם. זוֹ שָׁמַעְתִּי, אֲבָל הָרִאשׁוֹן לֹא נִרְאֶה (סא״א):


**And your luck shall rise more than midday** Heb. חלד. More than the light of midday, חֶלֶד shall rise for you, i.e., luck and time, like (Ps. 39:6), “and my age (חלדי) is as nothing before You.” Another explanation: יקום חָלֶד: means, The light of your rust shall rise; i.e., your darkest place shall illuminate more than the light of midday. I heard this, but the first [explanation] does not please me (not found in some editions).


###### Rashi on Job 11:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:17:2)


**תָּעֻפָה.** אֲפֵלָה שֶׁלְּךָ כַּבֹּקֶר תִּהְיֶה. עִנְיָן אַחֵר: **תָּעֻפָה** לְשׁוֹן ״עַפְעַפֵּי שַׁחַר״ (איוב ג:ט), שֶׁאִם הוּא לְשׁוֹן אֹפֶל, הָיָה לוֹ לוֹמַר: ׳תְּעוּפָה׳, נָקוּד בִּשְׁוָ״א, כְּמוֹ ״תְּנוּפָה״ (שמות כט:כד), ״תְּקוּמָה״ (ויקרא כו:לז), ״תְּרוּמָה״ (שמות כה:ב), ״תְּנוּמָה״ (תהלים קלב:ד):


**your darkness** Heb. תעפה. Your darkness shall be like the morning. Another explanation: תָּעֻפָה is an expression of (3:8) “the rays of (עפעפי) dawn.” Because, if it is an expression of darkness, he should have said, “תעוּפה” like תנוּפה, waving, תקוּמה rising, תרוּמה, raising, תנוּמה, slumber.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 11:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:20:1)


**וְעֵינֵי רְשָׁעִים.** שׂוֹנְאֶיךָ:


**And the eyes of the wicked** your enemies,


###### Rashi on Job 11:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:20:2)


**תִּכְלֶינָה.** בִּפְנוֹתָם בְּרָעָתְךָ לִרְאוֹת, וְלֹא יַשִּׂיגוּ (תַּאֲוָתָם). וְכָל ׳כִּלְיוֹן עֵינַיִם׳ שֶׁבִּלְשׁוֹן קֹדֶשׁ, זֶהוּ הַמְּצַפֶּה לִרְאוֹת וְאֵינוֹ מַשִּׂיג, וּכְמוֹ ״וְעֵינֶיךָ רֹאוֹת וְכָלוֹת אֲלֵיהֶם וְגוֹ׳״ (דברים כח:לב):


**will fail** when they turn to your troubles to see, but will not achieve their desire. Every expression of כליון עינים failure of the eyes, in the holy tongue means one who looks forward to see something but does not achieve [his wish], and as (Deut. 28:32) “and languish (כלות) for them etc.”


###### Rashi on Job 11:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:20:3)


**וּמָנוֹס.** הוּא מִבְטָח:


**way to flee** Heb. מנוס. That is a refuge


###### Rashi on Job 11:20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/11:20:4)


**וְתִקְוָתָם.** מַה שֶּׁהֵם מְקַוִּים לִרְאוֹת בְּךָ, לְמַפַּח נֶפֶשׁ יִהְיֶה לָהֶם:


**and their hope** What they hope to see in you will be intense grief for them.



## Chapter 12


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 12:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:2:1)


**(מענה איוב:)** 
**תָּמוּת חָכְמָה.** כְּשֶׁתָּמוּתוּ, תָּמוּת עִמָּכֶם כָּל חָכְמָה, כִּי כָל חָכְמָה בָּכֶם. אֲבָל:


**wisdom shall perish** When you die, all wisdom shall perish with you because all wisdom is in you, but I too have intelligence like you.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 12:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:3:1)


**גַּם לִי לֵבָב כְּמוֹכֶם.** וְאֶת מִי אֵין כְּמוֹ אֵלֶּה, שֶׁהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא שַׁלִּיט לַעֲשׂוֹת רְצוֹנוֹ וּלְהַאֲרִיךְ שַׁלְוַת רְשָׁעִים? אֵינְכֶם אֶלָּא כִּמְשַׂחֲקִים בִּי, וַאֲנִי הוּא (הַהוֹוֶה) שְׂחוֹק לְרֵעֵהוּ:


**and with whom are there none comparable to these?** (Who is it who does not know like these) that the Holy One, blessed be He, has power to perform His will and to allow the tranquility of the wicked to last? You are only mocking me, and I am...



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 12:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:4:1)


**אֶהְיֶה קוֹרֵא** לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, וַיַּעֲנֵהוּ מִצַּעַר הַקְנָטַתְכֶם. וּמָה יַעֲנֵהוּ? לָתֵת לוֹ **שְׂחוֹק לְצַדִּיק וְתָמִים.** שָׁלֵם:


**a laughingstock to his friend** He calls to the Holy One, blessed be He, and He answers him concerning the pain of their provocation. Now what does He answer him? To give the righteous laughter. **completely** Heb. תָּמִים, perfectly.


###### Rashi on Job 12:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:4:2)


**שְׂחוֹק צַדִּיק תָּמִים.** הִיא הָעֲנִיָּה אֲשֶׁר יַעֲנֵהוּ. יָדַעְתִּי אֲנִי כַּאֲשֶׁר אֲמַרְתֶּם, כִּי:


**that the completely righteous will have laughter** That is the answer that He will reply with, and I know that it is as you said (8:21), (“He will yet fill your mouth with laughter,”) for...



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 12:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:5:1)


**לַפִּיד בּוּז.** אֵשׁ שֶׁל גֵּיהִנֹּם עוֹמֵד לְמִי שֶׁשַּׁאֲנָן בְּעַשְׁתּוּתוֹ, לוֹמַר: ״שָׁלוֹם יִהְיֶה לִי״ (דברים כט:יח):


**A brand of contempt** The fire of Gehinnom stands for one who is tranquil in his thoughts, saying, “I will have peace.”


###### Rashi on Job 12:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:5:2)


**נָכוֹן.** מְזֻמָּן הוּא הָעַשְׁתּוֹת שַׁאֲנָן, לִהְיוֹת בְּמוֹעֲדֵי רֶגֶל – שֶׁרַגְלָם מוֹעֶדֶת:


**prepared** The one who is tranquil in his thoughts is destined to be among those who stumble, whose foot slips.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 12:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:6:1)


**יִשְׁלָיוּ.** יָדַעְתִּי אֲנִי, כִּי יְמֵי יִשְׁלָיוּ אֹהָלִים לִבְנֵי קֵדָר הַשּׁוֹכְנִים בָּאֹהָלִים הַמִּדְבָּרָה, וְהֵם שׁוֹדְדִים, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״פֶּרֶא אָדָם יָדוֹ בַכֹּל״ (בראשית טז:יב):


**prosper** I know that at times tents will prosper, namely those of the Kedarites who dwell in tents in the deserts, and who are robbers, as it is stated (Gen. 16:12): “a free man among men; his hand shall be against every man.”


###### Rashi on Job 12:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:6:2)


**וּבַטֻּחוֹת.** שְׁלֵמוּת יֵשׁ לְמַרְגִּיזֵי אֵל, לְכָל אֲשֶׁר הֵבִיא לוֹ הַצּוּר הַשָּׁלוֹם בְּיָדוֹ, אִם טוֹב וְאִם רַע:


**are secure** Those who provoke God have perfection, anyone to whom the Rock brought peace with His hand, whether he is good or bad.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 12:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:11:1)


**הֲלֹא אֹזֶן מִלִּין תִּבְחָן.** וְלָמָּה לֹא יָדְעוּ זֹאת הַשּׁוֹמְעִים פָּעֳלוֹ, **כַּאֲשֶׁר הַחֵךְ יִטְעַם לֶאֱכֹל**?


**Does not the ear discern words** So why did the listeners not know this, as the palate tastes to eat?



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 12:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:12:1)


**בִּישִׁישִׁים חָכְמָה וְגוֹ׳.** לָדַעַת כִּי עִמּוֹ חָכְמָה וְגוֹ׳:


**In elders there is wisdom etc.** to know that with Him is wisdom.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 12:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:15:1)


**וִישַׁלְּחֵם וְיַהַפְכוּ אָרֶץ.** בִּשְׁלִיחוּתוֹ, כַּאֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה לְדוֹר אֱנוֹשׁ, שֶׁהֵצִיף שְׁלִישׁוֹ שֶׁל עוֹלָם (מכילתא דרבי ישמעאל יתרו ו):


**He sends it away and it overturns the earth** As His agents, as He did to the generation of Enosh, when He inundated a third of the world.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 12:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:16:1)


**לוֹ שׁוֹגֵג.** הָאָדָם הַשּׁוֹגֵג:


**His are the misled** The man who is misled.


###### Rashi on Job 12:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:16:2)


**וּמַשְׁגֶּה.** שָׂטָן הַמַּשְׁגֵּהוּ:


**and the misleader** The Adversary, who misleads.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Job 12:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:17:1)


**שׁוֹלָל.** שֵׁם דָּבָר הוּא שְׁטוּת, מוֹלִיכָם בִּשְׁטוּת כְּשֶׁהוּא רוֹצֶה לְעַרְבֵּב וּלְבַלֵּעַ חָכְמָתָם, כְּמוֹ ״וְסָר מֵרָע מִשְׁתּוֹלֵל״ (ישעיהו נט:טו), ״אֶשְׁתּוֹלְלוּ אַבִּירֵי לֵב״ (תהלים עו:ו). וְתָ״ו אֲשֶׁר בְּ״מִשְׁתּוֹלֵל״ וְ״אֶשְׁתּוֹלְלוּ״ – כֵּן דֶּרֶךְ לְשׁוֹן עִבְרִית בְּתֵיבָה שֶׁיְּסוֹדָהּ שִׁי״ן אוֹ סָמֶ״ךְ, וְהוּא בָּא לְדַבֵּר בִּלְשׁוֹן נַעֲשָׂה וְנִפְעַל אוֹ מִתְפַּעֵל, נוֹתֵן תָּ״ו אַחַר אוֹת רִאשׁוֹנָה שֶׁל יְסוֹד, כְּמוֹ ״וְיִשְׁתַּכְּחוּ בָעִיר״ (קהלת ח:י), ״וְיִשְׁתַּמֵּר חֻקּוֹת עָמְרִי״ (מיכה ו:טז), ״מִסְתּוֹלֵל בְּעַמִּי״ (שמות ט:יז), ״וְיִסְתַּבֵּל הֶחָגָב״ (קהלת יב:ה):


**madness** Heb. שולל. It is a noun, ‘madness.’ He leads them away with madness when He wishes to confuse and destroy their wisdom, as in (Isa. 59: 15), “and he who turns away from evil is considered mad (משתולל)”; (Ps. 76:6), “The stouthearted became mad אשתוללוּ.” The “tav” in משתולל and in אשתוללוּ [is inserted because] so is the structure of the Hebrew language: in a word that has a radical “shin” or “sammech,” and which is used in passive or reflexive conjugation, a “tav” is placed after the first radical, like (Ecc. 8:10), “and they will be forgotten (וישתכחו) in the city”; (Micah 6: 16), “For the statutes of Omri are kept (וישתמר)”; (Exod. 9:17), “You still exalt yourself very high (מסתולל) above My people”; (Ecc. 12:5), “and the grasshopper shall drag himself along (ויסתבל).”


###### Rashi on Job 12:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:17:2)


**יְהוֹלֵל.** יְשׁוּטֶה, לְשׁוֹן ״הוֹלְלוּת וְסִכְלוּת״ (קהלת ב:יב):


**Me makes...fools** Heb. יהולל, He makes mad, an expression of folly and foolishness.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Job 12:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:18:1)


**מוּסַר מְלָכִים פִּתֵּחַ.** לְהָסִיר מוּסַר סִבְלָם מֵעַל אֲחֵרִים, וּמַעֲבִירָם מִמְּלוּכָה:


**He loosens the bond of kings** He removed the bond of the yoke from Nebuchadnezzar and dethroned him.


###### Rashi on Job 12:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:18:2)


**וַיֶּאֱסֹר אֵזוֹר בְּמָתְנֵיהֶם.** בִּתְחִלָּתָם, כְּשֶׁהוּא חָפֵץ בָּהֶם, חוֹגֵר חֲלָצֵיהֶם וּמְזָרְזָם כֹּחַ לַמְּלוּכָה:


**and He binds a girdle to their loins** At his start, when He desired him, He girded his loins, and imbued him with strength for kingship.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 12:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:19:1)


**כֹּהֲנִים.** שָׂרִים, כְּמוֹ ״כֹּהֵן מִדְיָן״ (שמות ג:א), ״כֹּהֵן אוֹן״ (בראשית מא:מה):


**princes** Heb. כהנים, like (Exod. 3:1), “the prince (כהן) of Midian”; (Gen. 41:45), “the prince (כהן) of On.”



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 12:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:20:1)


**לְנֶאֱמָנִים.** אַף לְנֶאֱמָנִים, כִּי פְּעָמִים שְׂפָתָיו נִבְלָלִים לְדַבֵּר סָרָה, כְּגוֹן אַבְרָהָם: ״וְהֶאֱמִין בַּה׳״ (בראשית טו:ו), וְסוֹף נִכְשַׁל: ״בַּמָּה אֵדַע״ (בראשית טו:ח):


**from trusty men** Even from trusty men. (Even to those who believe in Him. Etz Chayim ms.) Sometimes his lips become confused, speaking improper words, e.g. Abraham (about whom it is written:] (Gen. 15:6), And he believed in the Lord.” And later, he stumbled [by saying,] “How will I know?”



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Job 12:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:21:1)


**וּמְזִיחַ אֲפִיקִים רִפָּה.** וַחֲגוֹר הַחֲזָקִים מְרַפֶּה, וּמְפַתֵּחַ חֲגוֹרָם לְהָתִישׁ כֹּחָם, כְּמוֹ (תהלים קט:יט) ״וּלְמֵזַח תָּמִיד יַחְגְּרֶהָ״:


**and loosens the belt of the strong** And He loosens the belt of the strong. He opens their belt to weaken them, like (Ps. 109:19), “and for the girdle (ולמזח) wherewith he is girded continually.”


###### Rashi on Job 12:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:21:2)


**אֲפִיקִים.** חֲזָקִים, כְּמוֹ ״אֲפִיקֵי נְחוּשָׁה״ (איוב מ:יח); ״אֲפִיקֵי מָגִנִּים״ (איוב מא:ז):


**the strong** Heb. אפיקים, like (below 40:18), “as strong as (אפיקי) copper; (ibid. 41:7), “He is proud of the strength of (אפיקי) His scales.”



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Job 12:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:23:1)


**מַשְׂגִּיא לַגּוֹיִם וַיְאַבְּדֵם.** יֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים: כַּאֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה לְמִצְרַיִם, בְּהַשִּׂיגָם אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל ״לִפְנֵי בַּעַל צְפוֹן״ (שמות יד:ט), שָׁגוּ וְאָמְרוּ: תֵּדְעוּ, שֶׁהִסְכִּים עִמָּנוּ לְטַבְּעָם בַּיָּם, כַּאֲשֶׁר גָּזַרְנוּ ״כָּל הַבֵּן הַיִּלּוֹד הַיְאֹרָה תַּשְׁלִיכֻהוּ״ (שמות א:כב); וְקָשָׁה יִרְאָתוֹ שֶׁל בַּעַל צְפוֹן, שֶׁלֹּא נַעֲשׂוּ בּוֹ שְׁפָטִים כְּכָל אֱלֹהֵי מִצְרַיִם. וְגָרַם הַמָּקוֹם לְהַשְׁגּוֹתָם, כְּדֵי לְטַבְּעָם. וְזֶה הַלָּשׁוֹן אֵינוֹ עִקָּר; דְּאִם כֵּן הָיָה לוֹ לוֹמַר בְּצַד יָמִין ׳מַשְׁגִּיא׳, כְּמוֹ ״שׁוֹגֶה״ (משלי כ:א). אֲבָל זֶה עִקָּר ׳מַגְדִּיל׳, לְשׁוֹן ״שַׂגִּיא כֹחַ״ (איוב לז:כג): מַשְׂגִּיא אֶת הַגּוֹיִם וּמַצְלִיחָם, וְלִבָּם מִתְגָּאֶה בְּהַצְלָחָתָם, וְהוּא הָיָה אָבְדָן שֶׁלָּהֶן – מַה שֶּׁהִצְלִיחוּ:


**He makes nations great and destroys them** Heb. משׂגיא. Some say, as He did to the Egyptians when they overtook Israel before Baal-zephon (Exod. 14:9). They erred and said, “You should know that He agreed with us, to drown them in the sea as we decreed, ‘Every son that is born you shall cast into the river’ (ibid. 1:22), and [they also said] that their deity Baal-zephon is mighty, seeing that no acts of judgment were performed upon it, as [they were upon) all the [other] gods of Egypt, and the Omnipresent caused them to be misled in order to drown them. But this explanation is not the main one, for were it so, it should have been dotted on the right, מַשְׁגִיא like שׁוגֶה, the misled. This is the main explanation: It means making great, an expression of שׂגיא כֹח, having great strength. He makes the nations great and causes them to prosper, and their heart becomes haughty with their prosperity, so that [the fact] that they prosper is their destruction.


###### Rashi on Job 12:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:23:2)


**שׁוֹטֵחַ.** מַרְבֶּה אוֹתָם, לְהַשְׁטִיחַ בָּאָרֶץ וּלְכַסּוֹתָם:


**He spreads out** He multiplies them to spread them out over the earth and to cover them.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Job 12:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/12:24:1)


**בְּתֹהוּ לֹא דָרֶךְ.** בְּתֹהוּ שֶׁאֵינוֹ דֶרֶךְ:


**in a wasteland not a road** In a wasteland that is not a road.



## Chapter 13


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 13:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:3:1)


**אוּלָם אֲנִי.** אֵינִי מְבַקֵּשׁ אֶלָּא לְדַבֵּר אֵלָיו, וּלְהִתְוַכֵּחַ עִמּוֹ **אֶחְפָּץ**:


**But I would speak** I wish only to speak to Him and I wish to debate with Him.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 13:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:4:1)


**טוֹפְלֵי שָׁקֶר.** מְחַבְּרֵי דִּבְרֵי שֶׁקֶר:


**combine lies** Heb. טפלי שקר,combine false words of falsehood.


###### Rashi on Job 13:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:4:2)


**רוֹפְאֵי אֱלִיל כֻּלְּכֶם.** כָּל אֱלִיל שֶׁבַּמִּקְרָא לְשׁוֹן אַל. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּ (חולין קכא.): זֶה גִּיד הַצַּוָּאר, שֶׁאֵין לוֹ רְפוּאָה, וּשְׁמוֹ אֱלָל:


**all of you are quacks** Heb. רפאי אלל. Every אֱלִיל in Scripture is an expression of אַל, nought. Our Sages, however, explained that this is the cervical ligament, which [if damaged] has no cure, and it is called אֱלָל.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 13:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:6:1)


**וְרִיבוֹת.** הוֹכָחוֹת:


**the contentions** The proofs.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 13:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:7:1)


**הֲלָאֵל תְּדַבְּרוּ עַוְלָה.** אַחֲרֵי אֲשֶׁר תַּחַת אֵל בָּאתֶם לָרִיב, לֹא טוֹב שֶׁתְּדַבְּרוּ עַוְלָה:


**Will you speak unjustly for God** Since you come to contend in the place of God, it is not good that you should speak unjustly.


###### Rashi on Job 13:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:7:2)


**וְלוֹ תְּדַבְּרוּ רְמִיָּה.** בַּעֲבוּרוֹ תְּדַבְּרוּ רְמִיָּה, כְּמוֹ ״הֵן אֲנִי כְפִיךָ לָאֵל״ (איוב לג:ו), וּכְמוֹ ״אֶל הָאָרֶץ אֲשֶׁר דִּבֶּר לָהֶם״ (דברים ט:כח) – לְצָרְכָם, וּכְמוֹ ״יִלָּחֵם לָכֶם״ (שמות יד:יד); ״הַאַתֶּם תְּרִיבוּן לַבַּעַל״ (שופטים ו:לא). הֻקְשָׁה בְּעֵינַי לְדַמּוֹתוֹ כָּאן, וְהוּא דֻּגְמַת ״בְּיַד מֹשֶׁה לוֹ״ – עַל קֹרַח (במדבר יז:ה):


**and will you speak deceitfully for Him?** Heb. ולו [usually “to him”]. For His sake will you speak deceitfully? As in (below 33:6), “Behold I am like your mouth for God (לאל),” and like (Deut. 9:28), “to the land that He spoke for them (להם),” for their benefit, and like (Exod. 14:14), “will fight for you (לכם)”; (Jud. 6:31), “Will you contend for the Baal (לבעל)?” (I find it difficult to compare these instances to here [this verse]. Rather, this is analogous to [Num. 17: 5], “through Moses concerning him [לו],” concerning Korah.)



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 13:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:8:1)


**הֲפָנָיו תִּשָּׂאוּן.** וְכִי לְהַחֲנִיף לוֹ בִּשְׂאֵת פָּנִים בָּאתֶם?


**Will you show Him favor?** Have you come to flatter Him by being partial to Him?


###### Rashi on Job 13:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:8:2)


**אִם לָאֵל.** בִּשְׁבִילוֹ תִּתְוַוּכְחוּן עַל רְמִיָּה?


**for God** will you debate deceitfully?



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Job 13:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:9:1)


**הֲטוֹב כִּי יַחְקֹר אֶתְכֶם.** וְתִמָּצְאוּ שְׁקָרִים?


**Will it be good when He searches you out** and you are found to be liars?


###### Rashi on Job 13:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:9:2)


**אִם כְּהָתֵל בֶּאֱנוֹשׁ תְּהָתֵלּוּ בוֹ.** לוֹמַר: לִכְבוֹדְךָ עָשִׂינוּ עַצְמֵינוּ שְׁקָרִים?


**Will you mock him as one mocks a mortal** by saying, “We made liars of ourselves for Your honor”?



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 13:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:10:1)


**הוֹכֵחַ יוֹכִיחַ אֶתְכֶם.** הֲלֹא יוֹדִיעַ לָכֶם דִּבַּתְכֶם?


**He will surely reprove you** Will He not let you know your speech?


###### Rashi on Job 13:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:10:2)


**אִם בַּסֵּתֶר.** וְכִי בַּסֵּתֶר שֶׁתַּעַמְדוּ לְפָנָיו בַּדִּין, **פָּנִים תִּשָּׂאוּ לוֹ**, לוֹמַר: שָׁם נָשָׂאנוּ פָנֶיךָ?!


When you stand before Him in judgment, will you secretly show Him partiality, saying there. “We showed You partiality”?



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 13:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:11:1)


**הֲלֹא שְׂאֵתוֹ.** גָּבְהוֹ וְאֵימָתוֹ תְּבַעֵת אֶתְכֶם. וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים: **שְׂאֵתוֹ** שְׂרֵפַת אֵשׁוֹ, כְּמוֹ ״וַיִּשָּׂאֵם דָּוִד וַאֲנָשָׁיו״ (שמואל ב ה:כא), וּכְמוֹ ״מַשְׂאַת הָעִיר״ (שופטים כ:לח):


**Will not His majesty** Heb. שאתו. [Will not] His exaltation and His awesomeness terrify you? Others define שְׂאֵתוֹ as “His burning fire.” like (II Sam. 5:21), “and David and his men burned them (וישאם),” and like (Jud. 20:38), “a great pillar of (משאת) smoke.”



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 13:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:12:1)


**זִכְרֹנֵיכֶם.** נִמְשַׁל מִשְׁלֵי אֵפֶר זִכְרֹנֵיכֶם נִמְשַׁל לְאֵפֶר, אַתֶּם סְבוּרִים לְהִתְמַשֵּׁל לְאַבְרָהָם שֶׁאָמַר ״אָנֹכִי עָפָר וָאֵפֶר״ (בראשית יח:כז):


**your memorials are compared to ashes** Your memorials are compared to ashes. You expect to be compared to Abraham, who said (Genesis 18:27), “I am only dust and ashes.”


###### Rashi on Job 13:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:12:2)


**לְגַבֵּי חֹמֶר.** שָׁוֶה לֶעָפָר יְהִי גַבֵּיכֶם אֶצְלוֹ. גַּבֵּיכֶם. גֹּבַהּ שֶׁלָּכֶם, כְּמוֹ ״וַתִּבְנִי לָךְ גָּב״ (יחזקאל טז:כד):


**are like eminences of clay** Your eminences ore equal to eminences of clay, compared to Him. The word גביכם means, your height, like (Ezek. 16:24), “and you built for yourself a platform (גב).”



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 13:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:13:1)


**וְיַעֲבֹר עָלַי מָה.** מַה שֶּׁיַּעֲבֹר עָלַי בַּעֲוֹן צַעֲקָתִי יַעֲבֹר עָלַי, וְאַל תָּחוּשׁוּ עַל מָה אֲנִי צָרִיךְ לָשֵׂאת וְלֶאֱחֹז:


**and let come upon me what may** Whatever comes upon me because of the sin of my outcry, let it come upon me and do not care. For what reason must I bring up and hold...



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 13:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:14:1)


**בְּשָׂרִי בְשִׁנָּי.** לְיַסְּרֵנִי וּלְכוֹפֵנִי אֶל הַשְּׁתִיקָה:


**my flesh up into my teeth** to afflict myself and force myself to silence?


###### Rashi on Job 13:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:14:2)


**וְנַפְשִׁי אָשִׂים בְּכַפִּי.** לְהַגִּישׁ נַפְשִׁי לַמָּוֶת בְּיִסּוּרֵי שְׁתִיקָה:


**and take my life into my hand** to bring my life close to death with the agonies of silence.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 13:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:15:1)


**הֵן יִקְטְלֵנִי.** הֲלֹא אִם יִקְטְלֵנִי, אֵינִי נִפְרָד מִמֶּנּוּ, וְתָמִיד לוֹ אֲיַיחֵל; עַל כֵּן אֵין מֶרֶד וָפֶשַׁע בִּדְבָרַי:


**Behold, let Him kill me** I will not separate from Him, and I will always hope for Him. Therefore, there is no rebellion or transgression in my words,


###### Rashi on Job 13:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:15:2)


**אַךְ דְּרָכַי.** וּמִדּוֹתַי שֶׁנָּהַגְתִּי בָּהֶם:


but I will reason to His face about my way and my character traits ...


###### Rashi on Job 13:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:15:3)


**אֶל פָּנָיו אוֹכִיחַ.** לָדַעַת מַה יַּפְשִׁיעֵנִי:


with which I have conducted myself, to know of what He will find me guilty.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 13:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:16:1)


**גַּם הוּא.** וְכַאֲשֶׁר אֲנִי שָׁלֵם עִמּוֹ, כֵּן הוּא **גַּם הוּא לִי לִישׁוּעָה.** וְלֹא תִמְצְאוּ אַתֶּם חֵן בְּעֵינָיו:


**He is also** Just as I am wholehearted with Him, so is He also my salvation, but you will not find favor in His eyes.


###### Rashi on Job 13:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:16:2)


**כִּי לֹא לְפָנָיו חָנֵף יָבוֹא.** וְזֶה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר לָהֶם: ״כִּי לֹא דִבַּרְתֶּם אֵלַי נְכוֹנָה״ (איוב מב:ז):


**because a hypocrite cannot come before Him** This is what was said to them (below 42:7): “because you did not speak to Me properly.”



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Job 13:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:18:1)


**עָרַכְתִּי מִשְׁפָּט.** הוֹכָחַת דְּבָרִים; סִדַּרְתִּי טַעֲנוֹתַי בְּלִבִּי, מַה לְהָשִׁיב:


**I have set up judgement** [I have prepared] my case. I have arranged my arguments in my heart viz. what to answer.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 13:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:19:1)


**אֶחֱרִישׁ וְאֶגְוָע.** אִם לֹא אָרִיב, **אֶחֱרִישׁ וְאֶגְוָע**:


**I will be silent and perish** If I will not contend, I will be silent and perish.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 13:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:20:1)


**אַךְ שְׁתַּיִם אַל תַּעַשׂ עִמָּדִי.** דִּין שֶׁל מַעְלָה וְדִין שֶׁל מַטָּה. וְאִם בְּאַחַת תָּבוֹא לְמִשְׁפָּט, **אָז מִפָּנֶיךָ לֹא אֶסָּתֵר.** אִם בְּדִין שֶׁל מַעְלָה, אֲנִי אוֹמֵר: חָטָאתִי, ״מְכַסֶּה פְשָׁעָיו לֹא יַצְלִיחַ וְגוֹ׳״ (משלי כח:יג); וְאִם בְּדִין שֶׁל מַטָּה, אַף אָנֹכִי לֹא אוֹדֶה: כִּסּוּי חֲטָאָה לָשׁוֹן שֶׁקֶר (תהלים לב:א; קט:ב) (סא״א):


**But do not do two things to me** viz. Heavenly judgment and earthly judgment. If You come to judgment with one [of these], I will not hide from before You. If with Heavenly judgment, I will say, “I have sinned.” [because] (Prov. 28:13), “He who conceals his sins will not succeed.” If with earthly judgment, even I will not confess to concealing sin. I did not hear this (absent in some editions).



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Job 13:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:21:1)


**כַּפְּךָ מֵעָלַי הַרְחַק.** כְּפִיָּתְךָ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהֵשִׁיבוֹ אֱלִיהוּא ״וְאַכְפִּי עָלֶיךָ לֹא יִכְבָּד״ (איוב לג:ז). וְיֵשׁ פּוֹתְרִים כַּפְּךָ – כְּלוֹמַר: יָדְךָ, וְקָשֶׁה לִי לְאוֹמְרוֹ, כִּי לֹא מָצִינוּ ׳כַּף׳ לְרָעָה, כִּי אִם לְהָגֵן, כְּמוֹ ״וְשַׂכֹּתִי כַפִּי״ (שמות לג:כב):


**Distance Your compulsion from upon me** Heb. כפי, Your compulsion, as Elihu answered him (below 33:7), “and my compulsion (ואכפי) shall not weigh heavily upon you.” Some interpret כפך as “your hand.” However, it is difficult for me to say that, because we do not find כַּף in the sense of evil, but [meaning] to protect, as in (Exod. 33:22), “And I will cover you with My hand (כפי).”



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Job 13:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:24:1)


**פָּנֶיךָ תַסְתִּיר.** לִרְאוֹת אֶת דַּרְכֵי יָשְׁרִי:


**Why do You hide Your face** from seeing the ways of my uprightness?



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Job 13:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:25:1)


**תַּעֲרוֹץ.** תְּפַחִיד:


**Will You frighten** Heb. תערוץ, will You scare?



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Job 13:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:26:1)


**מְרוֹרוֹת.** סַרְבָנִיּוֹת אֲשֶׁר מָרִיתִי פִּיךָ אַתָּה כּוֹתֵב, וְלֹא הַטּוֹבוֹת שֶׁפָּעַלְתִּי:


**disobediences** Heb. מררות. The cases of obstinacy in which I obeyed Your orders, You write [down] but not the good deeds that I performed.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Job 13:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:27:1)


**בַּסַּד.** בִּלְשׁוֹן אֲרַמִּי ״סַדְנָא בְּסַדְנֵיהּ יָתִיב״ (פסחים כח.) – עֵץ גָּדוֹל שֶׁנּוֹתְנִין בּוֹ רַגְלֵי הָאֲסוּרִים, וּבִלְשׁוֹן יְוָנִי ״אַשְׁתּוֹ״ק״:


**in the stocks** Heb. בסד. In Aramaic (Pesachim 28a), “The one who makes the stocks (סדנא) sits in his own stocks (בסדניה).” [This is] a large [block of] wood in which the prisoners’ feet are inserted, and in Greek it is astock(?).


###### Rashi on Job 13:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:27:2)


**תִּתְחַקֶּה.** אַתָּה עַצְמְךָ מִתְחַקֶּה עַל פְּסִיעוֹתַי: כֵּן פָּסַע זֶה, וְכֹה פָּעַל. וּכְמוֹ ״מֵחֵיק הָאָרֶץ״ (יחזקאל מג:יד); ״וְחֵיק הָאַמָּה״ (יחזקאל מג:יג). זֶה הַיְסוֹד אפיקיו״ל בְּלַעַ״ז; ״הֵן עַל כַּפַּיִם חַקֹּתִיךְ״ (ישעיהו מט:טז):


**You engrave** Heb. תתחקה. You Yourself engrave my footsteps. So did this one transgress (step—Ed. Furth]), and so he did. Like (Ezek. 43:14), “from the bottom (ומחיק) upon the ground”; [and] (verse 13), “the bottom (וחיק) shall be a cubit.” This is the foundation, affishe in Old French, to affix. (Isa. 49:16): “Behold on [My] hands have I engraved you (חקותיך).”



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Job 13:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/13:28:1)


**וְהוּא כְּרָקָב יִבְלֶה.** הַגּוּף הַזֶּה אֲשֶׁר תִּרְדֹּף, יֵרָקֵב וְיִבְלֶה, וְאֵין כְּבוֹדְךָ בִּרְדִיפָתְךָ:


**And it is like rot that decays** The body You pursue will decay like rot, and it is beneath Your dignity to pursue it.



## Chapter 14


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 14:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:3:1)


**אַף עַל זֶה.** הַהֶבֶל וְהַנִּרְקָב:


**also on this one** [creature of] futility that decays


###### Rashi on Job 14:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:3:2)


**פָּקַחְתָּ עֵינֶיךָ.** לְדַקְדֵּק בַּחֲטָאָיו:


have You opened Your eyes to be exact with his sins,


###### Rashi on Job 14:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:3:3)


**וְאוֹתִי.** אַתָּה מֵבִיא בְמִשְׁפָּט עַל עֲווֹנוֹתַי:


and You bring me into judgment for my iniquities?



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 14:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:4:1)


**מִי יִתֵּן טָהוֹר.** מִדְּבַר טִפָּה סְרוּחָה וְשִׁכְבַת זֶרַע שֶׁהוּא טָמֵא. **לֹא אֶחָד** בָּהֶם טָהוֹר, שֶׁלֹּא יֶחֱטָא:


**Who can bring a clean thing** from a putrid drop, and semen, which is unclean? Not one of them is clean, that he should not sin.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 14:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:5:1)


**אִם חֲרוּצִים יָמָיו.** אִם גְּמוּל זֶה הוּא מִשְׁתַּלֵּם, שֶׁחֲרוּצִים וּקְצוּבִים לְיוֹם קָבוּעַ וּמְיֻעָד לְמִיתָה:


**If his days are limited** If he is requited with this recompense—that his days are limited to a day set for death,


###### Rashi on Job 14:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:5:2)


**וּמִסְפַּר חֳדָשָׁיו אִתָּךְ.** קְצוּבִים, וְחֹק זֶה עָשִׂיתָ לוֹ וְלֹא יִתְקַיֵּם עוֹד יוֹתֵר בָּעוֹלָם – דַּי לְךָ בַּתַּשְׁלוּמִין הַלָּלוּ:


and the number of months is set with You, and [that] You set this boundary—and he will no longer exist in the world, [then] this payment is sufficient for You.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 14:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:6:1)


**שְׁעֵה מֵעָלָיו.** בְּאוֹתוֹ מִעוּט חָרַץ הַיָּמִים, וְיֶחְדַּל לוֹ מִמַּכְאוֹב, עַד יִרְצֶה בְּזִקְנוּתוֹ וְתַשּׁוּת כֹּחוֹ **אֶת יוֹם מוֹתוֹ כְּשָׂכִיר** הָרוֹצֶה לְהַשְׁלָמַת עֲבוֹדַת יוֹמוֹ. כִּי עֲבִירָתוֹ מִן הָעוֹלָם חֲלוּטָה וְנִצְמֶתֶת מִשְּׁאָר כָּל הָעוֹבְרִים:


**Turn away from him** during those few limited days and let him rest from pain until he desires, in his old age and the weakness of his strength, the day of his death like a hireling who desires the completion of his day’s work; for his passing from the world is decided and more definite than all passings.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 14:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:7:1)


**כִּי יֵשׁ לָעֵץ תִּקְוָה.** שֶׁיַּחֲלֹף:


**a tree has** This [tree] has hope that it will renew itself


###### Rashi on Job 14:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:7:2)


**וְיֹנַקְתּוֹ.** אֲשֶׁר מִגִּזְעוֹ **לֹא תֶחְדָּל** מִהְיוֹת עָנָף:


and its bough will not cease from being a branch.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Job 14:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:9:1)


**וְעָשָׂה קָצִיר.** הִיא יוֹנֶקֶת, כְּמוֹ ״תְּשַׁלַּח קְצִירֶיהָ עַד יָם״ (תהילים פ:יב):


**and it will produce a branch** Heb. קציר. This is a branch, as (Ps. 80:12), “She sent out her boughs (קצירה) to the sea.”


###### Rashi on Job 14:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:9:2)


**נָטַע.** שֵׁם דָּבָר הוּא, וְטַעַם הֲבָרָתוֹ לְמַעְלָה בַּנּוּ״ן, וְנִנְקַד כֻּלּוֹ קָמַץ מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא סוֹף פָּסוּק. אַף מִגִּזְרַת ״נָטַע״ (ישעיה מד:יד) הוּא:


**a sapling** Heb. נטע. This is a noun. It is accented on the first syllable, and is punctuated completely with a “kamatz” because it is the end of the verse, but it is derived from נֶטַע, a sapling.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 14:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:11:1)


**אָזְלוּ מַיִם מִנִּי יָם.** מִמָּקוֹם שֶׁנָּהָר בָּא וְיוֹצֵא מִמְּקוֹרוֹ, הַבָּא מֵאֵלָיו מִן הַיָּם:


**As the waters fail from the sea** From the place whence the river comes and emerges, from its source that comes to it from the sea. [Alternative explanation of Rashi: From the place whence the river comes, and they went away from its source (from) which (the water) comes to it.]


###### Rashi on Job 14:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:11:2)


**וְהַנָּהָר.** הַבָּא מִשָּׁם יֶחֱרַב וְיִיבַשׁ לְעוֹלָם, כֵּן אִישׁ שָׁכַב וְלֹא יָקוּם:


**and the river** that comes from there is drained dry forever. So does a man lie down and not rise.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 14:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:14:1)


**כָּל־יְמֵי צְבָאִי אֲיַחֵל.** לִתְחִיָּה:


**All the days of my lifespan, I will hope** for life.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 14:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:15:1)


**תִּקְרָא.** תְּחִנָּה הִיא: קְרָא אֵלַי **וְאָנֹכִי אֶעֱנֶךָּ** לְהוֹכִיחַ דְּבָרַי:


**Call** This is a supplication. Call to me and I will answer you to prove my case.


###### Rashi on Job 14:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:15:2)


**תִּכְסֹף.** תַּחְמֹד:


**You desire** Heb. תִכְסֹף, You desire.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 14:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:16:1)


**כִּי עַתָּה.** אַתָּה מֵרֵיעַ לִי:


**But now** You are harming me.


###### Rashi on Job 14:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:16:2)


**צְעָדַי.** אַתָּה סוֹפֵר:


**my steps** You are counting.


###### Rashi on Job 14:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:16:3)


**לֹא תִשְׁמוֹר.** אֵינְךָ מַמְתִּין עַל חַטָּאתִי מִלְּהִפָּרַע, כְּמוֹ ״וְאָבִיו שָׁמַר אֶת הַדָּבָר״ (בראשית לז:יא) – הִמְתִּין שֶׁתִּתְקַיֵּם. וּבִלְשׁוֹן מִשְׁנָה (סנהדרין סג:): לֹא יֹאמַר אָדָם לַחֲבֵרוֹ שְׁמוֹר לִי בְּצַד עֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים פְּלוֹנִית – לְשׁוֹן ״הַמְתֵּן״:


**You do not wait** Heb. תשמר. You do not wait for my sin to requite me, as (Gen. 37:11), “but his father awaited (שמר) the matter,” and in the language of the Mishnah (Sanh. 63b): “A person may not say to his companion, ‘Wait (שמר) for me beside such and such a pagan deity.’” [It is] an expression of waiting.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Job 14:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:17:1)


**חָתוּם בִּצְרוֹר.** חָתוּם וּמְשֻׁמָּר בִּצְרוֹר בֶּגֶד, כְּכֶסֶף וּמַרְגָּלִיּוֹת, שֶׁלֹּא תֹּאבַד:


**sealed up in a bundle** Sealed and preserved in a cloth bundle like silver and pearls, lest it be lost.


###### Rashi on Job 14:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:17:2)


**וַתִּטְפֹּל עַל עֲוֺנִי.** נִתְחַבַּרְתָּ עַל עֲוֺנִי:


**and You have attached Yourself to my iniquity** Heb. ותטפל, You have attached Yourself to my iniquity.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Job 14:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:18:1)


**וְאוּלָם הַר נוֹפֵל.** הַר הַנּוֹפֵל גָּבְהוֹ יִבּוֹל וְיֵעָשֶׂה עָפָר, וּתְהֵא לוֹ תִּקְוָה לֵיהָנוֹת מִמֶּנּוּ. **וְצוּר** אֲשֶׁר נֶעְתַּק מִמְּקוֹמוֹ גַּם הוּא, יִבּוֹל לְשׁוֹן תְּבוּאָה. אוֹ:


**And surely the falling mountain gives forth produce** The height of a falling mountain will give forth produce. It will produce dust, and there will be hope of deriving benefit from it. **and the rock** that moves from its place will also give forth produce. This is an expression of grain.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 14:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:19:1)


**אֲבָנִים.** אֲשֶׁר **שָׁחֲקוּ מַיִם** בְּעָבְרָם עֲלֵיהֶם תָּמִיד:


**Stones** which **the water eroded** by constantly passing over them.


###### Rashi on Job 14:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:19:2)


**תִּשְׁטֹף.** הִשְׁטִיפוּ סְפִיחֶיהָ שֶׁל אֶבֶן לֵיהָפֵךְ לְעָפָר וְלִצְמֹחַ בּוֹ דָּבָר, כַּאֲשֶׁר יִצְמַח סְפִיחַ הַקָּצִיר שֶׁעָבַר – כֵּן יִתְהַפֵּךְ עָפָר זֶה סְפִיחֶיהָ לְאֶבֶן:


**it washes away** i.e., the flood. **its aftergrowth** of the stone, to be transformed into dust so that something should grow in it. As the aftergrowths of the harvest grow, so will the aftergrowths of this earth be transformed to stone.


###### Rashi on Job 14:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:19:3)


**וְתִקְוַת אֱנוֹשׁ.** אֵינָהּ כֵּן, אֲשֶׁר מִשֶּׁיָּמוּת – אָבְדָה סֶלָה:


**but the hope of man** is not so, because as soon as he dies, it is lost forever.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 14:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:20:1)


**תִּתְקְפֵהוּ.** תֶּחֱזַק מִמֶּנּוּ וְתֶאֱנְסֵהוּ בְּתָקְפְּךָ, **וַיַּהֲלֹךְ** מִן הָעוֹלָם:


**You overpower him** You are stronger than he; You overcome him with Your power, and he goes away from the world.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Job 14:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:21:1)


**יִכְבְּדוּ.** יִהְיוּ כְבֵדִים בְּכֶסֶף וּבְזָהָב, וְהוּא לֹא יֵדָע:


**become wealthy** Heb. יכבדו, lit. they will become heavy. They will become heavy with silver and gold, but he will not know.


###### Rashi on Job 14:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:21:2)


**וְיִצְעֲרוּ.** וְיִמְעֲטוּ מִכָּל טוֹב, וְהוּא לֹא יָבִין:


**and they become poor** And they have little of any good, but he will not understand it.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Job 14:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/14:22:1)


**אַךְ בְּשָׂרוֹ עָלָיו יִכְאָב.** קָשָׁה רִמָּה לַמֵּת כְּמַחַט בַּבָּשָׂר הֶחָי (שבת יג:):


**But his flesh causes him pain** A worm is as painful to the dead as a needle in the flesh of the living.



## Chapter 15


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 15:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:2:1)


**(מענה אליפז)**: 
**הֶחָכָם יַעֲנֶה דַעַת רוּחַ.** תָּמוּהַּ הוּא, וְכִי מִי שֶׁהוּא חָכָם יַעֲנֶה וְיָרִים קוֹל בְּדַעַת שֶׁל רוּחַ וְהֶבֶל?


**Eliphaz’s Answer**
**Shall a wise man raise his voice with opinions of wind** It is puzzling. Should one who is wise answer and raise his voice with an opinion of wind and futility?



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 15:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:3:1)


**הוֹכֵחַ בְּדָבָר לֹא יִסְכּוֹן.** לְהִתְוַכֵּחַ בְּדָבָר שֶׁלֹּא תְּהֵא לוֹ מֵהֶן הֲנָאָה, כְּמוֹ ״וַתְּהִי לוֹ סֹכֶנֶת״ (מלכים א א:ב), וּכְמוֹ ״יִסָּכֵן בָּם״ (קהלת י:ט) – יֵהָנֶה בָּם:


**To debate over a matter from which he derives no benefit** Heb. יסכון. To debate over a matter from which he will derive no benefit, like (I Kings 1:2), “and she shall be to him a warmer (סכנת),” and like (Ecc. 10:9), “is warmed (יסכן) by it,” i.e., he will derive benefit from it.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 15:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:4:1)


**אַף אַתָּה.** כָּל שֶׁכֵּן אַתָּה, שֶׁחָכָם מְאֹד, **אַתָּה תָּפֵר יִרְאָה** בְּמַרְבִּית דְּבָרֶיךָ:


**Surely you** Surely you, who are very wise, will do away with fear by the multitude of your words.


###### Rashi on Job 15:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:4:2)


**וְתִגְרַע שִׂיחָה.** כְּמוֹ ״כִּי יְגָרַע נִטְפֵי מָיִם״ (איוב לו:כז), וּכְמוֹ ״וְתִגְרַע אֵלֶיךָ חָכְמָה״ (איוב טו:ח):


**and increase speech** Heb. ותגרע, like (below 36:27) “He will increase (יגרע) the drops of water,” and like (ibid. 15:8), “and [did] you increase (ותגרע) wisdom to yourself.”



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 15:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:5:1)


**כִּי יְאַלֵּף עֲוֹנְךָ.** יִצְרְךָ הָרָע אֶת פִּיךָ לְדַבֵּר כַּדְּבָרִים הָאֵלֶּה, וְהָיָה לְךָ לִבְחֹר **לְשׁוֹן עֲרוּמִים**:


**For your iniquity teaches** Your evil inclination [teaches] your mouth to speak such words, but you should have chosen the tongue of the crafty.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 15:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:6:1)


**יַרְשִׁיעֲךָ פִיךָ וְלֹא אָנִי.** כְּלוֹמַר: הֲשִׁיבֵנִי עַל שְׁאֵלָתִי, וְנִמְצָא פִיךָ הִרְשִׁיעֲךָ; כִּי טוֹב לִי **שֶׁיַּרְשִׁיעֲךָ פִיךָ וְלֹא אָנִי.** וְזוֹ הִיא שְׁאֵלָתִי:


**Let your own mouth condemn you and not I** That is to say: Give me an answer to my question; the result will be that your own mouth will condemn you, for it better for me that your mouth should condemn you and not I. Now this is my question:



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 15:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:7:1)


**הֲרִאשׁוֹן אָדָם תִּוָּלֵד.** הֲבְטֶרֶם אָדָם תִּוָּלֵד? הֵ״א הַזֹּאת מְשַׁמֶּשֶׁת לְשׁוֹן תֵּימַהּ, לְפִיכָךְ נְקוּדָה בַּחֲטַף פַּתָּח. וּמְלִיצָתוֹ: הֲרִאשׁוֹן לְאָדָם הַנּוֹצָר מֵאֲדָמָה נוֹלַדְתָּ, שֶׁיָּדַעְתָּ לְקַיֵּם כָּל חָכְמַת מִצְווֹת הַבּוֹרֵא?!


**Were you born before Adam** Heb. הראישון. Were you born before Adam? This “hey” serves as the interrogative expression. Therefore, it is vowelized with a “hattaf pattah” and its meaning is: Were you born the first one, before Adam who was formed from the earth, so that you knew to fulfill all the wisdom of the commandments of the Creator?



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 15:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:8:1)


**וְתִגְרַע.** וְתַרְבֶּה מִלְמַעְלָה לְמַטָּה לָרֶדֶת עָלֶיךָ:


**and increase** And bring down much [wisdom] from above to descend upon you?



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 15:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:10:1)


**כַּבִּיר.** מְרֻבֶּה יָמִים מֵאָבִיךָ:


**more** of more days than you father.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 15:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:11:1)


**הַמְעַט מִמְּךָ תַּנְחוּמוֹת אֵל.** אֲשֶׁר הֵיטִיב לְךָ הַצּוּר עַד עַתָּה יָמִים רַבִּים, וּדְבָרוֹ הַטּוֹב אֲשֶׁר לָאַט עִמָּךְ בְּלַאט וְנַחַת דִּשְּׁנוֹ:


**Are God’s consolations little for you** with which the Rock benefited you until now for many days, and His good thing that He gave you with pleasantness and gentleness, viz. His fatness [i.e., plenty].


###### Rashi on Job 15:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:11:2)


**לָאַט.** הַנָּעִים. וּלְשׁוֹן פָּעַל הוּא, וְלֹא שֵׁם דָּבָר; וְלָכֵן הוּא נָקוּד חֶצְיוֹ קָמַץ וְחֶצְיוֹ פַּתַּח:


**He gave...gently** Heb. לאט, he gave gently. This is a verb, not a noun. Therefore, it is vowelized half with a “kamatz” and half with a “pattah.”



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 15:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:12:1)


**יִקָּחֲךָ.** יְלַמֶּדְךָ, כְּמוֹ ״יַעֲרֹף כַּמָּטָר לִקְחִי״ (דברים לב:ב):


**teach you** Heb. יקחך, teach you, as in (Deut. 32:2), “so that my teaching (לקחי) may drip like rain.”


###### Rashi on Job 15:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:12:2)


**יִרְזְמוּן.** כְּמוֹ יִרְמְזוּן, כְּמוֹ שִׂמְלָה שַׂלְמָה (שמות כב:ח, כו):


**hint** Heb. ירזמון, like יִרְמְזוּן ; like שַׂלְמָה שִׂמְלָה (Exod. 22:8, 26).



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 15:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:14:1)


**מָה אֱנוֹשׁ כִּי יִזְכֶּה.** מַה כֹּחוֹ, שֶׁיּוּכַל לִהְיוֹת זַכַּאי נֶגֶד הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**What is man that he should be innocent** What is his strength, that he can be innocent against the Holy One, blessed be He?



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 15:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:15:1)


**וְשָׁמַיִם.** צְבָא הַשָּׁמַיִם:


**and the heavens** the host of the heavens.


###### Rashi on Job 15:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:15:2)


**זַכּוּ.** יִצְהָרוּ. כָּל לְשׁוֹן זַךְ הוּא צֹהַר:


**pure** Heb. זכו, shine. Every expression of זַךְ is splendor.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 15:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:16:1)


**אַף.** כָּל שֶׁכֵּן:


**Surely** Heb. אף, how much more.


###### Rashi on Job 15:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:16:2)


**וְנֶאֱלָח.** נִתְעָב וּמְעֹרְבָּב, וְכֵן ״כֻּלּוֹ סָג יַחְדָּו נֶאֱלָחוּ״ (תהילים נג:ד):


**and impure** Heb. ונאלח, abominable and mixed. Similarly (Ps. 53: 4), “Are they all dross, altogether filthy (נאלחו)?”



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Job 15:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:18:1)


**אֲשֶׁר חֲכָמִים יַגִּידוּ.** וְיִתְוַדּוּ פִּשְׁעָם **וְלֹא כִחֲדוּ** עֲוֹנָם **מֵאֲבוֹתָם.** זֶהוּ אֲשֶׁר רָאִיתִי, וְהִנְנִי אֲסַפֵּר לְךָ. וְהֵיכָן רָאִיתִי? יְהוּדָה הוֹדָה וְלֹא בוֹשׁ בְּמַעֲשֵׂה תָמָר (בראשית לח), רְאוּבֵן הוֹדָה וְלֹא בוֹשׁ בְּמַעֲשֵׂה בִּלְהָה (בראשית לה:כב). וּמֶה הָיָה שְׂכָרָם?


**That the wise men tell** and confess their transgression. **and do not hide** their iniquity from their fathers. This is what I saw, and behold, I will tell you. Now where did I see it? Judah confessed and was not ashamed in the incident of Tamar. Reuben confessed and was not ashamed in the incident of Bilhah. And what was their reward?



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 15:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:19:1)


**לָהֶם לְבַדָּם נִתְּנָה הָאָרֶץ.** בִּשְׂכַר זֶה זָכָה יְהוּדָה לְמַלְכוּת נֶצַח, וְנָטְלוּ שְׁנֵיהֶם מִן הַנַּחֲלָה חֶלְקָם, וְלֹא גֹרְשׁוּ מִן נַחֲלָתָם בַּעֲוֹנָם, מֵאַחַר שֶׁהוֹדוּ:


**To them alone the land was given** As a reward for this, Judah merited eternal kingship, and both took their share of the heritage, and they were not driven out of their heritage because of their iniquity, since they confessed.


###### Rashi on Job 15:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:19:2)


**וְלֹא עָבַר זָר בְּתוֹכָם.** כְּשֶׁבָּא מֹשֶׁה לְבָרְכָם שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (דברים לג:ו-ז) ״יְחִי רְאוּבֵן וְאַל יָמֹת וְזֹאת לִיהוּדָה״. דָּבָר אַחֵר **וְלֹא עָבַר זָר בְּתוֹכָם** בְּנַחֲלָה שֶׁל עָתִיד לָבֹא הַחֲלוּקָה בְּסֵפֶר (יחזקאל מח):


**and no stranger passed in their midst** when Moses came to bless them, as it is stated (Deut. 33:6f): “Reuben shall live and not die etc. And is this for Judah?” **And no stranger passed in their midst** in the heritage of the future that is apportioned in the Book of Ezekiel (48). But...



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 15:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:20:1)


**כָּל יְמֵי.** הָרָשָׁע שֶׁאֵינוֹ שָׁב מֵחֶטְאוֹ וְאֵינוֹ מוֹדֶה פְּשָׁעָיו כָּל יָמָיו:


**All the days of a wicked man** who does not repent of his sin and does not confess his transgressions, all his days...


###### Rashi on Job 15:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:20:2)


**הוּא מִתְחוֹלֵל.** וּמִתְאוֹנֵן עַל הַבְטָחָתוֹ, שֶׁיְּשַׁלֵּם רָעָתוֹ אֲשֶׁר פָּעַל. כָּל מִסְפַּר שָׁנִים אֲשֶׁר **נִצְפְּנוּ לֶעָרִיץ.** הוּא מִתְחוֹלֵל:


**he mourns** and grieves over his certainty of being requited for his evil that he committed. All the number of years that were laid up for the oppressor, he mourns.


###### Rashi on Job 15:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:20:3)


**מִתְחוֹלֵל.** לְשׁוֹן אֲנִינוּת, וְלֹא שָׁמַעְתִּי:


The word מתחולל is an expression of mourning, but I did not hear this.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Job 15:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:21:1)


**קוֹל פְּחָדִים בְּאָזְנָיו.** לֵאמֹר: הִגִּיעַ עֵת תַּשְׁלוּמֵי:


**The voice of terrors is in his ears** saying that the time of his retribution has arrived.


###### Rashi on Job 15:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:21:2)


**בַּשָּׁלוֹם.** וּכְשֶׁהוּא שָׁרוּי בְּשָׁלוֹם, אָז **הַשּׁוֹדֵד יְבוֹאֶנּוּ**:


**in peace** And when he lives in peace, then the robber will come upon him.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Job 15:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:22:1)


**וְצָפוּ הוּא אֱלֵי חָרֶב.** וְעָתִיד הוּא אֱלֵי חֶרֶב וְעֹנִי, לָנוּד לַלֶּחֶם אַיֵּה יִמְצָאֶנּוּ:


**and he is destined to the sword** Heb. וצפוי. He is destined to the sword, and his eye to wander for bread—where should he find it?



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Job 15:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:23:1)


**נוֹדֵד הוּא.** כְּלוֹמַר: מֻבְטָח הוּא לָנוּד:


**He wanders** i.e., he is assured of wandering.


###### Rashi on Job 15:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:23:2)


**יָדַע.** לוֹ בֶּאֱמֶת:


**He knows** in fact.


###### Rashi on Job 15:23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:23:3)


**כִּי נָכוֹן.** וּמְזֻמָּן לָבוֹא **בְּיָדוֹ** כְּלוֹמַר: אֵלָיו וְעִמּוֹ **יוֹם חֹשֶׁךְ.** כְּמוֹ ״מִן הַבָּא בְיָדוֹ״ (בראשית לב:יד):


**that...is ready** and prepared to come to his hand, i.e., to him, and with him is the day of darkness. **at his hand** Like (Gen. 32: 14), “from what came into his hand.”



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Job 15:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:24:1)


**תִּתְקְפֵהוּ.** תֶּחֱזַק הַמְּצוּקָה מִמֶּנּוּ וְתֶאֱנְסֵהוּ:


**it overpowers him** The anguish is stronger than him and it will prevail over him.


###### Rashi on Job 15:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:24:2)


**כְּמֶלֶךְ עָתִיד לַכִּידוֹר.** לֹא מָצָאתִי דִּמְיוֹנוֹ. וְיֵשׁ לְפוֹתְרוֹ בָּאוֹתִיּוֹת הַמִּתְחַלְּפוֹת רֵי״שׁ בְּדָלֵי״ת כְּמוֹ אַשְׁכְּנַז וְרִיפַת שֶׁבַּתּוֹרָה (בראשית י:ג) – אַשְׁכְּנַז וְדִיפַת (דברי הימים א א:ו); וְכֵן כִּתִּים וְדוֹדָנִים (בראשית י:ד). אַף כָּאן לַכִּידוֹר – כְּמוֹ לַכִּידוֹד, כְּאוֹתוֹ הַמֶּלֶךְ שֶׁהוּא עָתִיד לִיקוֹד בָּאֵשׁ שֶׁל גֵּיהִנֹּם; וְהוּא סַנְחֵרִיב, שֶׁנִּתְנַבֵּא הַנָּבִיא עָלָיו ״כִּי עָרוּךְ מֵאֶתְמוּל תָּפְתֶּה גַּם הִיא לַמֶּלֶךְ הוּכָן״ (ישעיהו ל:לג) – לִשְׂרֹף חֵילָיו יְקַד יְקוֹד (ישעיהו י:טז); וְדוֹמֶה לוֹ ״כִּידוּדֵי אֵשׁ יִתְמַלָּטוּ״ (איוב מא:יא) – אִם (ס״א אֵשׁ) ״לַפִּידִים״ (איוב מא:יא), אִם לֶהָבוֹת (איוב מא:יג):


**like a king destined for the inferno** Heb. לכידור. I have not found anything similar to it, but it may be interpreted by means of the interchangeable letters, “resh” with “dalet,” like (Gen. 10:3), “Ashkenaz and Riphath” in the Torah, and in Chronicles (I, 1:6): “Ashkenaz and Diphath;” and similarly (Gen. 10: 4). “Kittim and Dodanim” Here too, לַכִּידוֹר is equivalent to לַכִּידוֹד, to the king who is destined to burn (ליקוד) in the fire of Gehinnom; i.e., Sennacherib, about whom the prophet prophesied (Isa. 30: 33): “For Tophteh has been set up from yesterday, that too has been prepared for the king,” to burn his legions; (Isa. 10:16), “a burning shall burn.” Similar to this (below 41:11), “sparks of (כידודי) fire go forth”; either brands or flames.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Job 15:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:26:1)


**יָרוּץ אֵלָיו.** הָיָה רָץ אֶל הַבּוֹרֵא בְּגֹבַהּ וּזְקִיפַת צַוָּאר לְהַכְעִיסוֹ:


**He runs towards Him** He would run toward the Creator with his neck high and upright, in order to provoke Him.


###### Rashi on Job 15:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:26:2)


**בַּעֲבִי גַּבֵּי מָגִנָּיו.** בְּחָזְקַת כֹּחוֹ, **גַּבֵּי** לְשׁוֹן גּוֹבַהּ, כְּמוֹ ״וַתִּבְנִי לָךְ גָּב״ (יחזקאל טז:כד):


**the thickness of the body of his shields** With the power of his strength. **the body of** an expression of height, as (Ezek 16:24), “and you built yourself a platform (גב).”



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Job 15:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:27:1)


**פִּימָה עֲלֵי כָסֶל.** עָשָׂה פֶה עַל חֲלָצַיִם, שֶׁחֶלְבּוֹ וְעֹבִי שׁוּמָנוֹ כָּפוּל עַל כְּסָלָיו, וְנִרְאִין כְּמִין פֶּה:


**collops of fat on his loins** He made a mouth on his loins, for his fat and the thickness of his blubber is folded on his loins, and they look like a sort of mouth.


###### Rashi on Job 15:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:27:2)


**פִּימָה.** מִגִּזְרַת ״וְהָיְתָה הַפְּצִירָה פִים״ (שמואל א יג:כא), זוֹ הִיא לִימָ״א שֶׁל נַפָּחִים, שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ פִּיּוֹת הַרְבֵּה, וְנִקְרֵאת הֶפְצֵר פִּיּוֹת:



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Job 15:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:28:1)


**וַיִּשְׁכּוֹן עָרִים נִכְחָדוֹת.** כֵּן דֶּרֶךְ בַּעֲלֵי גַּאֲוָה: בּוֹנִים חֳרָבוֹת לְשֵׁם:


**And he dwelt in ruined cities** So is the custom of haughty people, to build ruins for a name.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Job 15:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:29:1)


**וְלֹא יָקוּם חֵילוֹ.** לֹא יִתְקַיֵּם לְיָמִים רַבִּים:


**and his possessions will not remain** They will not be preserved for long.


###### Rashi on Job 15:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:29:2)


**וְלֹא יִטֶּה.** לֹא יִנְטֶה מְלִיצָתוֹ; כְּמוֹ ״וַיִּטּוּ אַחֲרֵי הַבָּצַע״ (שמואל א ח:ג), שֶׁפֵּירוּשׁוֹ: ׳וַיִּנְטוּ׳ הֵם עַצְמָם; אַף כָּאן לֹא יִנְטֶה מִן הַשָּׁמַיִם לָאָרֶץ. **מִנְלָם.** מַתַּת תַּאֲוָתָם וְזִמּוּן מַחֲשַׁבְתָּם, וְהוּא כִּשְׁתֵּי תֵיבוֹת: מִן לָהֶם:


**neither will...bend** Heb. יטה, [like] ינטה, like (I Sam. 8:3), “and they turned (ויטו) after gain.” They themselves turned (וינטו). It means that they did not bend from heaven to earth. **the gratification of their desire** Heb. מנלם, the granting of their desire and the fulfillment of their plans. It is like two words, מן להם.


###### Rashi on Job 15:29:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:29:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:29:3)


**מִן.** הֲכָנָתָם, כְּמוֹ ״וַיְמַן לָהֶם הַמֶּלֶךְ״ (דניאל א:ה). וְדוֹמֶה לוֹ ״הַפְּצִירָה פִים״ בִּשְׁתֵּי תֵיבוֹת (שמואל א יג:כא), וְכֵן ״אֶרְאֶלָּם״ (ישעיה לג:ז) – תַּרְגּוּם יוֹנָתָן: ״אִתְגְּלִי לְהוֹן״. זֶה נִרְאֶה בְּעֵינַי, וּמְנַחֵם מְסַיְּעֵנִי. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **מִנְלָם.** לְשׁוֹן ״כַּנְּלֹתְךָ לִבְגֹּד״ (ישעיה לג:א), וְהַנּוּ״ן שֶׁבּוֹ יְסוֹד נוֹפֵל. לְשׁוֹן תַּכְלִית: **לֹא יִטֶּה לָאָרֶץ.** לִהְיוֹת בְּטֵלָה, גְּזֵרַת חָרוּץ:


מן means their preparation, like (Dan. 1:5), “And the king prepared (וימן) for them.” Similar to it is (I Sam. 13:21), “a file (הפצירה פים),” in two words. [The analogy is obscure.] (Isa. 33:7), “Behold I will appear to them (אראלם),” which Jonathan renders: I will appear to them. So it appears to me, and Menachem supports me. Another explanation: מִנְלָם is an expression of (Isa. 33:1), “when you finish (כנלתך) dealing treacherously,” in which the “nun” is a radical that sometimes is omitted, an expression of completion. It will not bend to the ground to be freed from a decree of destruction.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Job 15:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:30:1)


**וְיָסוּר.** מִן הָעוֹלָם:


**and he will turn away** from the world.


###### Rashi on Job 15:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:30:2)


**בְּרוּחַ פִּיו.** שֶׁל שַׁלִּיט בָּעוֹלָם, וְזֶה מִקְרָא חָסֵר. וְיֵשׁ פּוֹתְרִים: בִּשְׁבִיל רוּחַ פִּיו – שֶׁהֵרַע לְדַבֵּר סָרָה:


**by the breath of His mouth** Of the Ruler of the world. This is an ellipsis, Others interpret this to mean: because of the breath of his mouth that was guilty of speaking lies.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Job 15:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:31:1)


**אַל יַאֲמֵן.** אוֹתוֹ אֲשֶׁר בְּשָׁוְא נִתְעָה, כִּי שָׁוְא יִהְיֶה תְמוּרָתוֹ וִיגִיעוֹ, כְּמוֹ ״וְעַל הַתְּמוּרָה״ (רות ד:ז):


**will not believe** He who was misled by futility will not believe that his recompense and his toil shall be futility, like (Ruth 4:7), “and concerning exchange (התמורה).”



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Job 15:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:32:1)


**בְּלֹא יוֹמוֹ.** תַּעֲמֹד תְּמוּרָתוֹ עַל מְלִיאָתָהּ מִלִּגְדֹּל וּמִלְּהַצְלִיחַ עוֹד, כְּמוֹ ״וַיִּמָּלְאוּ לוֹ אַרְבָּעִים יוֹם״ (בראשית נ:ג). פֵּירוּשׁוֹ: ״וְאַשְׁלִימוּ״ (תרגום אונקלוס):


**Before his time** his recompense will be complete [and will cease] from growing and prospering anymore, like (Gen. 50:3), “were fulfilled for him,” i.e., they were completed.


###### Rashi on Job 15:32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:32:2)


**וְכִפָּתוֹ.** כְּפִיַּת עֲנָפָיו, כָּל עַנְפֵי אִילָן דּוֹמִין לְכִפָּה:


**and his branches** His bent-over branches. All branches of a tree resemble a dome.



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on Job 15:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:35:1)


**הָרֹה עָמָל וְיָלֹד אָוֶן.** עָשָׂה רָעָה, וְיִשְׁתַּלְּמוּ שְׂכַר הַלֵּידָה לְפִי הַהֵרָיוֹן:


**They conceive trouble and bear iniquity** They commit evil and are recompensed. The recompense of the birth is in accordance with the conception.


###### Rashi on Job 15:35:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:35:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:35:2)


**תָּכִין.** תַּזְמִין:


**prepares** makes ready.


###### Rashi on Job 15:35:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:35:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/15:35:3)


**מִרְמָה.** לְמִרְמָה תֵּהָפֵךְ לָהֶם תַּאֲוָתָם:


**deceit** Their desire shall be converted for them into deceit.



## Chapter 16


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 16:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:3:1)


**(מענה איוב):** 
**יַמְרִיצְךָ.** יְשַׁעֶרְךָ פִּיךָ לַחֲשֹׁב דְּבָרִים נִמְרָצִים עַל אוֹפְנֵיהֶם:


**Job’s Reply**
**will bring clarity to you** Heb. ימרצך. Your mouth will make you evaluate to think clear words with proper basis.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 16:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:4:1)


**כָּכֶם אֲדַבֵּרָה.** כָּכֶם אֲנִי יוֹדֵעַ לְקַנְתֵּר:


**could speak like you** I know how to chide as you do.


###### Rashi on Job 16:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:4:2)


**לוּ יֵשׁ נַפְשְׁכֶם.** בְּיִסּוּרִין תַּחַת נַפְשִׁי, גַּם אֲנִי הָיִיתִי מְחַבֵּר עֲלֵיכֶם בְּמִלִּין וּמֵנִיעַ עֲלֵיכֶם רֹאשִׁי בְּצַעֲקַתְכֶם, וּמְאַמֵּץ אֶתְכֶם בְּמוֹ פִי לֵאמֹר: הַחֲרִישׁוּ, הִתְאַמְּצוּ וְעִמְדוּ בְּיִסּוּרֵיכֶם, כַּאֲשֶׁר אַתֶּם תֹּאמְרוּ אֵלַי:


**if your soul were** in pain instead of my soul, I too would join words together against you and shake my head at you in your cry, and encourage you with my mouth, saying, “Be still, have courage, and endure your sufferings,” just as you are saying to me.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 16:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:5:1)


**וְנִיד שְׂפָתַי.** שֶׁאֲנִי נָד וְקוֹבֵל עַתָּה וְצוֹעֵק, אָז יַחְשׂוֹךְ וְלֹא אֶזְעוֹק וְלֹא אָנוּד עוֹד, אֲבָל עַתָּה:


**and the movement of my lips** which I move and [with which I] complain now and cry, would then stop, and I would no longer cry or lament, but now...



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 16:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:6:1)


**אִם אֲדַבְּרָה וְגוֹ׳**:


**Should I speak etc.**



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 16:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:7:1)


**הֲשִׁמּוֹתָ.** לְשׁוֹן שְׁתִיקָה, כְּמוֹ ״אֶשְׁתּוֹמֵם״ (ישעיהו סג:ה), וּכְמוֹ ״שֹׁמּוּ שָׁמַיִם״ (ירמיהו ב:יב) – שָׁתְקוּ וְהִתְמָהוּ:


**you have silenced** Heb. השמות, you have silenced, like (Dan. 4:16), “was appalled (אשתומם),” and like (Jer. 2:12), “O heavens, be astonished (שמו).” Be silent and wonder.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 16:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:8:1)


**וַתִּקְמְטֵנִי לְעֵד הָיָה.** הִקְמַטְתַּנִי וְהִקְדַּרְתַּנִי בִּשְׁבִיל עֵד, שֶׁהָיָה לְעֵד עַל עֲוֹנִי לְהַשְׂטִינֵנִי:


**You have shriveled me up; it has become a witness** You have shriveled me up and blackened me for a witness, for it has become a witness to my iniquity, to accuse me.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Job 16:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:9:1)


**צָרִי.** הַשָּׂטָן הוּא הַצָּר:


**My Adversary** Satan is the adversary.


###### Rashi on Job 16:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:9:2)


**יִלְטוֹשׁ.** יְחַדֵּד, כְּמוֹ ״לוֹטֵשׁ כָּל חֹרֵשׁ וְגוֹמֵר״ (בראשית ד:כב):


**sharpens** Heb. ילטוש, sharpens, like (Gen. 4:22), “who sharpened (לטש) every instrument etc.”



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 16:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:10:1)


**פָּעֲרוּ עָלַי בְּפִיהֶם.** פָּתְחוּ, כְּמוֹ ״וּפָעֲרָה פִיהָ״ (ישעיה ה:יד):


**They open their mouth at me** Heb. פערו, they open, like (Isa. 5:14), “and opened (ופערה) its mouth.”


###### Rashi on Job 16:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:10:2)


**יִתְמַלָּאוּן.** יִתְאַסְּפוּן, וְהַרְבֵּה לוֹ דּוֹמִין בַּמִּקְרָא: ״קָרְאוּ אַחֲרֶיךָ מָלֵא״ (ירמיה יב:ו), ״אֲשֶׁר יִקָּרֵא עָלָיו מְלֹא רֹעִים״ (ישעיה לא:ד). וְאֵינוֹ מִשְׁתַּנֶּה מִלְּשׁוֹנוֹ, כִּי כָל ׳מִלּוּי׳ – ׳נֶאֱסָף׳ הוּא:


**they assemble** Heb. יתמלאון, they gather, and there are many similar words in Scripture, e.g. (Jer. 12:6), “have called a gang (מלא) after you”; (Isa. 31:4), “although a band (מלא) of shepherds gather against him,” but it does not deviate from its original meaning because every filling (מלוי) is a gathering together.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 16:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:11:1)


**יַסְגִּירֵנִי אֵל אֶל עֲוִיל.** הִסְגִּירַנִי הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא אֶל אֱוִיל לֵץ עוֹלֵל, לָלוּץ בִּי:


**God delivers me to a fool** The Holy One, blessed be He, delivered me to a fool, a scorner, a mocker, to be scornful of me.


###### Rashi on Job 16:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:11:2)


**וְעַל יְדֵי רְשָׁעִים יִרְטֵנִי.** רְטִיַּת תַּנְחוּמָיו וּרְפוּאָתִי מָסַר לָרְשָׁעִים, שֶׁאֵינָן אֶלָּא מַקְנִיטִים. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **יִרְטֵנִי** כְּמוֹ יְרַצֵּנִי, לְשׁוֹן רִצּוּי וְתַנְחוּמִים:


**and heals me through the hands of the wicked** Heb. ירטני. The bandage of His consolations and my healing He delivered to the wicked, who only provoke. Another explanation: יַרְטֵנִי is like יַרְצֵנִי, He placates me, an expression of placating and consolation



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 16:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:12:1)


**וַיְפַרְפְּרֵנִי.** אוֹתוֹ עֲוִיל, לְשׁוֹן פֵּרוּרִין:


**but he crumbled me to pieces** Heb. ויפרפני. That fool [crumbled me]. [It is] an expression of crumbs (פרורין).


###### Rashi on Job 16:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:12:2)


**וַיְפַצְפְּצֵנִי.** לְשׁוֹן נִשְׁבָּר לִשְׁבָרִים הַרְבֵּה בַּחֲבָטָה רַבָּה, כְּמוֹ ״וְנִפַּצְתִּים אִישׁ אֶל אָחִיו״ (ירמיה יג:יד), ״וְנִפֵּץ עֹלָלַיִךְ אֶל הַסָּלַע״ (תהלים קלז:ט):


**and shattered me** Heb. ויפצפצני, an expression of something being broken into many fragments with a hard blow, like (Jer. 13:14), “And I will dash them (ונפצתים), one against the other”; (Ps. 137:90), “and dash (ונפץ) your little ones against the rock. “



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 16:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:13:1)


**רַבָּיו.** מוֹרֶה חִצָּיו, כְּמוֹ ״רֹבֶה קֶשֶׁת״ (בראשית כא:כ):


**His archers** רביו, the one who shoots his arrows, as in (Gen. 21:20), “and he became an archer (רבה קשת).”


###### Rashi on Job 16:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:13:2)


**מְרֵרָתִי.** מָרָה שֶׁלִּי:


**my gall** Heb. מְרֵירָתִי, my gall.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 16:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:15:1)


**גִּלְדִּי.** מַכָּה יְבֵשָׁה וְהַעֲלָאַת גֶּלֶד, וּלְשׁוֹן מִשְׁנָה (ראש השנה כז:): וְהֶעֱמִידוֹ עַל גִּלְדּוֹ:


**my scab** Heb. גלדי, a dry wound that forms a crust, and [in] the language of the Mishnah (Rosh Hashanah 27b): He scraped it (the shofar) down to a thin crust (גלדו).


###### Rashi on Job 16:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:15:2)


**וְעוֹלַלְתִּי.** וְלִכְלַכְתִּי וְגִנִּיתִי, כְּמוֹ ״וַיִּתְעַלְּלוּ בָהּ״ (שופטים יט:כה) – לְשׁוֹן בִּזָּיוֹן וּגְנַאי, וּכְמוֹ ״עוֹלְלָה לְנַפְשִׁי״ (איכה ג:נא):
