**and sullied** Heb. ועללתי, and I sullied, like (Jud. 19:25), “and abused (ויתעללו) her,” an expression of disgrace and abasement, and like (Lam. 3:51), “sullies (עוללה) my soul.”


###### Rashi on Job 16:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:15:3)


**קַרְנִי.** זִיוִי, לָשׁוֹן ״וְקַרְנַיִם מִיָּדוֹ לוֹ״ (חבקוק ג:ד):


**my radiance** Heb. קרני, my radiance, an expression of (Habakkuk 3:4): “rays of light (קרנים) came to them from his hand.”



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 16:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:16:1)


**חֳמַרְמְרוּ.** נִקְמְטוּ, כְּמוֹ ״נֶחְמְרוּ בְּנֵי מֵעֶיהָ״ בִּלְשׁוֹן מִשְׁנָה (חולין נו:); וְכֵן ״מֵעַי חֳמַרְמָרוּ״ (איכה א:כ):


**became shriveled** Heb. חמרמרו, like (Chullin 3:3), “and its internal organs became shriveled (ונחמרו),” in the language of the Mishnah. Similarly (Lam. 1:20), “my intestines have become shriveled (חמרמרו).”


###### Rashi on Job 16:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:16:2)


**צַלְמָוֶת.** שֶׁחָשְׁכוּ מֵחֲמַת בֶּכִי וְדֶמְעָה:


**the shadow of death** it became darkened from weeping and tears.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Job 16:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:17:1)


**וּתְפִלָּתִי זַכָּה.** לֹא קִלַּלְתִּי חֲבֵרִי, וְלֹא פִלַּלְתִּי לוֹ רָעָה:


**and my prayer is pure** I did not curse my friend nor did I think any evil against him.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Job 16:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:18:1)


**וְאַל יְהִי מָקוֹם.** בָּאָרֶץ לְהַבְלִיעַ בּוֹ צַעֲקָתִי, כִּי לַשָּׁמַיִם תַּעֲלֶה:


**and let there be no place** in the earth to absorb my cry, but it should ascend to heaven.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 16:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:19:1)


**הִנֵּה בַשָּׁמַיִם עֵדִי.** יוֹצְרִי הַיּוֹדֵעַ דְּרָכִי:


**behold my Witness is in heaven** My Creator, Who knows my ways.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 16:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:20:1)


**דָּלְפָה.** דּוֹמַעַת וְזוֹלֶפֶת דִּמְעָה, לְשׁוֹן דִּלּוּף:


**pours out** Heb. דלפה, tears and sprays tears, an expression of dripping (דלוף).



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Job 16:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:21:1)


**וְיוֹכַח לְגֶבֶר.** וְהַלְוַאי יַעֲשֶׂה לִי זֹאת, שֶׁיִּתֵּן לִי מָקוֹם לְהִתְוַכֵּחַ גֶּבֶר עִם אֱלוֹהַּ, לְהַשְׁווֹת שְׁנֵי הָרִיבִים: רִיב גֶּבֶר עִם קוֹנוֹ וְרִיב בֵּין אָדָם לְרֵעֵהוּ:


**Would that a man could reason** If only He would do this for me, that He would give me a place so that a man could reason with God, to liken the two contentions: the contention of man with his Maker, and the contention of man with his fellow.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Job 16:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:22:1)


**כִּי שְׁנוֹת וְגוֹ׳.** הַקְּצוּבִים מִסְפָּר לִימֵי חַיַּי:


**For numbered [years]** The limited number of the days of my life.


###### Rashi on Job 16:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/16:22:2)


**יֶאֱתָיוּ.** יַגִּיעוּ, וְאָז **אֹרַח לֹא אָשׁוּב אֶהֱלֹךְ**:


**will come** Will arrive, and then I will go on a way from which I will not return.



## Chapter 17


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Job 17:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:1:1)


**נִזְעָכוּ.** נִקְפְּצוּ, כְּמוֹ ״נִדְעָכוּ״ (איוב ו:יז):


**flicker** Heb. נזעכו have sprung away, like (above 6:17) “they jump (נדעכו).”


###### Rashi on Job 17:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:1:2)


**קְבָרִים לִי.** מוּכָן אֲנִי לַקֶּבֶר:


**graves are ready for me** I am ready for the grave.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 17:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:2:1)


**אִם לֹא.** אִם לֹא זֹאת בָּאַתְנִי, שֶׁבְּנֵי אָדָם הַתּוּלִים נוֹעֲדוּ עִמִּי בְּתַנְחוּמַי, לְהָתֵל בִּי:


**even if there were no** [Even if] this had not befallen me, that scornful people had gathered with me in my consolations to mock me.


###### Rashi on Job 17:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:2:2)


**וּבְהַמְּרוֹתָם.** כְּמוֹ ״וַיַּמְרוּ בִי״ (יחזקאל כ:ח), ״מַמְרִים הֱיִיתֶם״ (דברים ט:ז) – לְשׁוֹן בְּהַקְנָטַתְכֶם:


**in their provocation** Heb. ובהמרותם, like (Ezek. 20:8, 13, 21), “And they provoked (וימרו) me”; (Deut. 9:7), “you have been provoking (ממרים).” [This is] an expression meaning: in their provocation.


###### Rashi on Job 17:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:2:3)


**תָּלַן עֵינִי.** עֵינִי וְלִבִּי וּמַחֲשַׁבְתִּי לָנוּחַ בָּהֶן:


**my eye...abide** My eye, my heart, and my thought abide in them.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 17:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:3:1)


**שִׂימָה נָּא.** הַבּוֹרֵא:


**Pay heed now** O Creator.


###### Rashi on Job 17:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:3:2)


**עָרְבֵנִי.** בְּתֵקַע כַּף, לְהִתְוַכֵּחַ יַחַד. כְּמוֹ **מִי הוּא מֵאֵלֶּה לְיָדִי יִתָּקֵעַ** וְיִיטַב לוֹ:


**give me surety** by shaking the hands to debate together, for who is it of these who will shake my hand that it should be good for me?



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 17:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:4:1)


**כִּי לִבָּם צָפַנְתָּ מִשָּׂכֶל.** וְעַל כֵּן לֹא תְרוֹמֵם תִּפְאַרְתְּךָ עַל יְדֵיהֶם:


**For** Behold. **You have hidden their hearts from understanding** Therefore, Your glory will not be exalted through them.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 17:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:5:1)


**לְחֵלֶק יַגִּיד.** לַחֲלַקְלַקּוּת **יַגִּיד** כָּל אֶחָד מֵהֶם אֶת רֵעוֹ, וְזֹאת תָּבֹא לָהֶם: **עֵינֵי בָנָיו תִּכְלֶינָה**:


**flattery** Heb. לְחֵלֶק, flattery (חלקלקות). **speaks** Each one of them [speaks] to his friend, and this will come to them: their children’s eyes will fail.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 17:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:6:1)


**וְתֹפֶת.** כְּמוֹ ״תֹּף״ (ישעיהו ה:יב):


**and...a drum** Heb. תפת, like תּוֹף, a drum.


###### Rashi on Job 17:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:6:2)


**לְפָנִים.** שֶׁל בְּרִיּוֹת אֲנִי:


**before them** lit. to the face; [i.e., to the face] of the people, I am [a drum].



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 17:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:7:1)


**וִיצוּרַי.** אֵיבָרִים שֶׁאֲנִי נוֹצָר בָּהֶם:


**and my limbs** namely, the limbs with which I was formed.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 17:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:8:1)


**יִשֹּׁמּוּ.** יִתְמְהוּ:


**will be appalled** they will wonder.


###### Rashi on Job 17:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:8:2)


**וְנָקִי.** אִם יֵשׁ צַדִּיק שׁוֹמֵעַ בְּהַתּוּלוֹת הַחֲנֵפִים הָאֵלֶּה:


**and an innocent one** If there is a righteous [man] who hears the mockery of these hypocrites.


###### Rashi on Job 17:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:8:3)


**יִתְעוֹרָר.** לָרִיב בָּכֶם:


**will be aroused** to quarrel with you.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Job 17:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:9:1)


**וְיֹאחֵז.** כָּל צַדִּיק דַּרְכּוֹ לַעֲמֹד כְּנֶגֶד כַּת חֲנֵפִים:


**Yet the righteous holds on his way** i.e., every righteous man holds on his way to stand up against the company of the hypocrites.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 17:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:10:1)


**וְאוּלָם כֻּלָּם.** אֲנִי אוֹמֵר לָכֶם **תָּשׁוּבוּ נָא וּבֹאוּ נָא**:


**But all of them** I say to you, “Return now and come now.”


###### Rashi on Job 17:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:10:2)


**וְלֹא אֶמְצָא בָכֶם חָכָם.** בְּמַה שֶּׁאַתֶּם מַלְעִיגִים עַל קַבָּלָתִי:


**and I will not find any wise man among you** insofar as you mock my lamentation.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 17:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:11:1)


**יָמַי וְגוֹ׳.** הִנֵּה יְמֵי טוֹבָתִי שֶׁהֻרְגַּלְתִּי בָּהֶם, **עָבְרוּ**:


**My days etc.** Behold, the days of my prosperity, to which I became accustomed, have passed.


###### Rashi on Job 17:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:11:2)


**זִמּוֹתַי.** שֶׁזָּמוֹתִי לְהַאֲרִיךְ בְּטוֹבָה:


**my purposes** Which I had expected to last with prosperity.


###### Rashi on Job 17:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:11:3)


**נִתְּקוּ מוֹרָשֵׁי לְבָבִי.** מַחֲשָׁבוֹת שֶׁלִּבִּי מוֹרִישַׁנִי, שֶׁהָיָה לִבִּי אוֹמֵר לִי לִירַשׁ טוֹבָה לְפִי דַרְכִּי:


**the thoughts of my heart have been broken off** Heb. מורשי, lit. the inheritances of. The thoughts which my heart gave me to possess, for my heart said that I would inherit good because of my [righteous] way.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 17:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:12:1)


**לַיְלָה לְיוֹם יָשִׂימוּ.** הַתּוֹלִים אֵלֶּה הוֹפְכִים לִי לַיְלָה לְיוֹם, כִּי מִצָּרַת הַתּוֹלִים לֹא אִישַׁן בַּלַּיְלָה:


**They turn night into day** These mockers turn night into day for me, for I cannot sleep at night because of the trouble of the mockings.


###### Rashi on Job 17:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:12:2)


**אוֹר קָרוֹב.** וְקָצַר לִי, מִפְּנֵי צָרוֹת חֹשֶׁךְ לַיְלָה. אוֹר קָרוֹב – כַּאֲשֶׁר יָבֹא הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ נִרְאֶה לִי שֶׁעַתָּה מִקָּרוֹב הֵאִיר הַיּוֹם, וְנִקְצַר וְחָלַף לוֹ; כְּמוֹ ״הָעִיר הַזֹּאת קְרֹבָה״ (בראשית יט:כ) – נִתְיַשְּׁבָה מִקָּרוֹב מֵחָדָשׁ; וּכְמוֹ ״כִּי רִנְנַת רְשָׁעִים מִקָּרֹב״ (איוב כ:ה), וּכְמוֹ ״חֲדָשִׁים מִקָּרֹב בָּאוּ״ (דברים לב:יז):


**the light is short** lit. קרוב, near, and short for me because of the trouble of the darkness of the night. The light is short; when the sun sets, it appears to me as though a short time ago the day dawned, was shortened, and left. This is like (Gen. 19:20), “this city is recent (קרובה)” it was newly settled recently and like (below 20:5), “That the triumph of the wicked is short (מקרוב),” and like (Deut. 32:17), “new ones that came up of late (מקרוב).”



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 17:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:13:1)


**אִם אֲקַוֶּה שְׁאוֹל.** כְּמוֹ ״אִם חֲרוּצִים יָמָיו״ (איוב יד:ה), כְּלוֹמַר: מֵאַחַר שֶׁאֲנִי מְקַוֶּה **שְׁאוֹל בֵּיתִי**:


**Since I hope for the grave** Heb. אם, like (above 14:5), “If (אם) his days are limited” [See Rashi ad loc.], i.e., since I hope for the grave as my house.


###### Rashi on Job 17:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:13:2)


**וּבַחֹשֶׁךְ רִפַּדְתִּי.** שִׁטַּחְתִּי יְצוּעִי בַּקֶּבֶר:


**in the darkness I have spread out my couch** I have spread out my couch in the grave.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 17:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:14:1)


**לַשַּׁחַת קָרָאתִי אָבִי אָתָּה.** לְהִתְלוֹנֵן בּוֹ יָמִים רַבִּים, כְּאִלּוּ הוּא אָבִי, מֵאַחַר שֶׁתִּקְוָתִי זֹאת הִיא:


**To the pit I have called, ‘You are my father,’** to lodge there many days as though it was my father, since this is my hope.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 17:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:15:1)


**וְאַיֵּה אֵפוֹ תִקְוָתִי.** וְאַתְּ, לָמָּה נִמְשַׁכְתְּ? וּמִי יְשׁוּרֶנָּה לְהָרִיצָהּ עָדַי?


**Where then is my hope** [Where then is] this [my hope]? Why is it deferred, and who will see it to hasten it to me?



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 17:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:16:1)


**בַּדֵּי שְׁאוֹל תֵּרַדְנָה.** אוֹתָם אֵבָרִים שֶׁסּוֹפָן לֵירֵד שְׁאוֹל, שֶׁתְּהֵא **יַחַד עַל עָפָר נָחַת**:


**The limbs of the grave shall descend** Those limbs [of my body] that will ultimately descend to the grave, for indeed they will all rest on the dust.


###### Rashi on Job 17:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/17:16:2)


**נָחַת.** מְלוֹנָן, לְשׁוֹן ״וַתָּנַח עֲלֵיהֶם הָרוּחַ״ (במדבר יא:כו):


**they rest on the dust** Heb. נחת, will lodge, an expression of (Num. 11:26), “the spirit rested (ותנח) upon them.”



## Chapter 18


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 18:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:2:1)


**(מענה בלדד)**:
**קִנְצֵי.** סוֹף:


**Bildad’s reply**
**“How long?”** will you prolong the discussion?


###### Rashi on Job 18:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:2:2)


**תָּבִינוּ.** תִּשְׁתְּקוּ לְהָבִין וְלִשְׁמֹעַ אֶת אֲשֶׁר נְדַבֵּר:


**Consider** Be silent to understand and to hear what we will speak.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 18:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:3:1)


**נִטְמִינוּ בְעֵינֵיכֶם.** נֶחְשַׁבְנוּ בְעֵינֵיכֶם טְמוּנִין. וְאֵין יְסוֹד בַּתֵּיבָה זוֹ זוּלָתִי טי״ת מ״ם, כְּמוֹ ״טְמוֹנִין פְּלִשְׁתָּאֵי״, תַּרְגּוּם שֶׁל ״סִתְּמוּם פְּלִשְׁתִּים״ (בראשית כו:טו). יֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים לְשׁוֹן טְמִינָה, וְטָעוּת הוּא בְּיָדָם, שֶׁאִם כֵּן צְרִיכָה הַתֵּיבָה שְׁנֵי נוּנִי״ן, אֶחָד בַּיְּסוֹד וְאֶחָד בְּשִׁמּוּשׁ שֶׁל רַבִּים:


**and reputed dull in your sight** Heb. נטמינו. We are considered in your eyes as stopped up. The word has no radical besides the “teth” “mem,” like טַמוֹנוּן פְלִשְתָּאֵי, which is the Aramaic translation of סִתְּמוּם פלשתים “the Philistines stopped them up” (Gen. 26:15). Some explain [this] as an expression of hiding (טמינה), but they are in error, because were that the case, the word would require two “nun”s, one for the radical and one for the plural suffix.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 18:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:4:1)


**טוֹרֵף נַפְשׁוֹ.** חוֹזֵר עַל אִיּוֹב. לְךָ אֲנִי אוֹמֵר, הַטּוֹרֵף וּמְחַבֵּל נַפְשׁוֹ **בְּאַפּוֹ** וּבְכַעֲסוֹ:


**One who destroys his soul** He turns to Job, “To you I say, the one who destroys his soul in his anger and in his wrath.”


###### Rashi on Job 18:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:4:2)


**הֲלְמַעַנְךָ.** הַלְמַעַן צִדְקָתְךָ תֵּעָזֵב הָאָרֶץ מִמִּשְׁפַּט מִדָּתָהּ?


**for your sake** For the sake of your innocence shall the earth be abandoned by its standard of justice?


###### Rashi on Job 18:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:4:3)


**וְיֶעְתַּק צוּר.** הַיּוֹצֵר (מִמְּקוֹמוֹ) מִדַּעְתּוֹ וּמִמִּדָּתוֹ:


**and shall the Rock be removed** The Creator. **from His place** From His knowledge and from His nature.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 18:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:5:1)


**גַּם.** עַתָּה, לְפִי צַעֲקָתְךָ, זֹאת תִּנְהַג לְדוֹרוֹת:


**Yea** Even now, according to your cry, this will be in force for generations.


###### Rashi on Job 18:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:5:2)


**אוֹר רְשָׁעִים יִדְעָךְ.** יִקְפֹּץ מִמְּקוֹמוֹ. **שְׁבִיב.** אֶשְׁטַנְצִיל״אָ:


**the light of the wicked shall be put out** it shall spring from its place. **spark** Etinceler in French.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 18:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:7:1)


**יֵצְרוּ.** יִהְיוּ קְצָרִים **צַעֲדֵי** כֹּחוֹ:


**shall be straitened** They shall be short. **The steps of** The steps of his strength.


###### Rashi on Job 18:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:7:2)


**וְתַשְׁלִיכֵהוּ.** מֵעָלָיו עֲצָתוֹ שֶׁיָּעַץ, אֲשֶׁר לֹא יִתְקַיֵּם:


**shall cast him down** His counsel that he advised, which will not be fulfilled [will cast him down].



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 18:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:8:1)


**כִּי וְגוֹ׳.** סוֹפוֹ אֲשֶׁר שֻׁלַּח בְּרֶשֶׁת רַגְלָיו, (שֻׁלְּחוּ) לִצְעוֹד עַל הָרֶשֶׁת וְלִיתָּפֵשׂ:


**For** his end will be that... **he shall be sent into a net** His feet will be sent to step on a net and be caught.


###### Rashi on Job 18:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:8:2)


**וְעַל שְׂבָכָה יִתְהַלָּךְ.** הִיא רֶשֶׁת; כָּל שְׂבָכָה עֲשׂוּיָה כְּמוֹ רֶשֶׁת, וְהוּא קֵייפָ״א שֶׁבְּרָאשֵׁי הַנָּשִׁים:


**and he shall walk on the toils** Heb. שבכה. Every שבכה is made like a net, and that is the coif that is on the head of women.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Job 18:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:9:1)


**יֹאחֵז.** יֹאחֲזֶנּוּ בְּעָקֵב פַּח:


**shall seize** A trap shall seize him by the heel.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 18:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:10:1)


**טָמוּן בָּאָרֶץ.** תַּחַת רַגְלָיו:


**hidden...in the ground** under his feet.


###### Rashi on Job 18:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:10:2)


**חַבְלוֹ.** חֶבֶל מְצוּדָתוֹ אֲשֶׁר יִלָּכֵד בָּהּ:


**A noose...for him** The rope of his trap, with which he will be caught.


###### Rashi on Job 18:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:10:3)


**וּמַלְכֻּדְתּוֹ.** טָמוּן עֲלֵי נְתִיבוֹ, כְּשֵׁם שֶׁטּוֹמְנִין חֶבֶל מַלְכֹּדֶת לָעוֹפוֹת:


**and a trap for him** is hidden on the path just as they hide the noose of the trap for birds.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 18:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:11:1)


**בַּלָּהוֹת.** שֵׁדִים:


**terrors** Demons.


###### Rashi on Job 18:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:11:2)


**וֶהֱפִיצֻהוּ לְרַגְלָיו.** (יִנְפְּצֵהוּ) יַחְבְּטוּהוּ עַל הָאָרֶץ:


**and beat him off his fee** They knock him down to the ground.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 18:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:12:1)


**אוֹנוֹ.** בְּנוֹ (בראשית מט:ג):


**His son** Heb. אֹנוֹ, lit, his strength.


###### Rashi on Job 18:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:12:2)


**לְצַלְעוֹ.** אִשְׁתּוֹ (בראשית ב:כא):


**for his wife** Heb. לצלעוֹ lit. his rib [alluding to Eve].



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 18:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:13:1)


**יֹאכַל בַּדֵּי עוֹרוֹ.** וְיֹאכַל כָּל שְׁאָר בַּדָּיו – בָּנָיו וּבְנוֹתָיו:


**He shall eat the branches of his body** And he shall devour all the rest of his branches, viz. his sons and daughters.


###### Rashi on Job 18:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:13:2)


**בְּכוֹר מָוֶת.** שַׂר הַמָּוֶת, כְּמוֹ ״אָנִי בְּכוֹר אֶתְּנֵהוּ״ (תהלים פט:כח):


**the prince of death** Heb. בכור מות, as in (Ps. 89:28), “Also I will make him a prince (בכור).”



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 18:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:14:1)


**יִנָּתֵק מֵאָהֳלוֹ.** מֵאִשְׁתּוֹ:


**He shall be torn away from his wife** Heb. מאהלו, lit. from his tent.


###### Rashi on Job 18:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:14:2)


**מִבְטַחוֹ.** שֶׁהִיא בְּטוּחָה בּוֹ, (מִבְטַחוֹ) מִבְטַחַת שֶׁלּוֹ, שֶׁהִיא בּוֹטַחַת עָלָיו:


**who trusted him** Heb. מבטחו, his trust, who trusted him. (His trust), that she trusted him.


###### Rashi on Job 18:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:14:3)


**וְתַצְעִידֵהוּ.** וְהִיא תַּצְעִידֵהוּ וְתִשְׁלָחֵהוּ (מֵעָלֶיהָ) לַקֶּבֶר לְמֶלֶךְ הַשֵּׁדִים:


**she will cause him to walk.** yet she will cause him to walk and will send him away (from her) to the grave, to the king of the demons.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 18:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:15:1)


**תִּשְׁכּוֹן בְּאָהֳלוֹ** אַלְמָנָה:


**She shall dwell** in his tent as a widow.


###### Rashi on Job 18:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:15:2)


**מִבְּלִי לוֹ.** (שֶׁלֹּא תְּהֵא שֶׁלּוֹ) שֶׁהֲרֵי מֵת, וְסוֹף **יְזֹרֶה עַל נָוֵהוּ גָּפְרִית**:


**not being his** (that she will not be his) because he is dead, and ultimately brimstone shall be scattered on his dwelling.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Job 18:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:18:1)


**יְהַדְּפוּהוּ.** מִן הַשָּׁמַיִם:


**They shall drive him** from heaven.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 18:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:19:1)


**נֶכֶד.** בֶּן הַבֵּן:


**grandson** Heb. נכד, a son of the son.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 18:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:20:1)


**נָשַׁמּוּ אַחֲרוֹנִים.** תָּמְהוּ בְּשָׁמְעָם עַל אֵיד אֲשֶׁר בָּא לוֹ:


**the later people** They wondered when they heard of the misfortune that befell him.


###### Rashi on Job 18:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:20:2)


**שָׂעַר.** לֹא יִתָּכֵן לִהְיוֹת מִגִּזְרַת שֵׂעָר, שֶׁאֵין טַעַם שֵׂעָר בְּשִׁי״ן אֶלָּא בְּעַיִן, וְאֵינוֹ נֶהְפָּךְ לְקָמַץ בְּאַתְנַחְתָּא וְסוֹף פָּסוּק. לֹא שָׁמַעְתִּי:


**quaking** Heb. שָׂעַר, tempest. This cannot be of the form of שֵׂעָר, hair, because the accent in שָׂעַר is not (on the “sin,” but on the “ayin,”) and it is not converted to a “kamatz” when it is cantillated with an “ethnachta” or a “sof pasuk.” I did not hear this.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Job 18:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/18:21:1)


**אֵלֶּה מִשְׁכְּנוֹת עַוָּל.** זֶה אַחֲרִיתָם:


**These are only the dwellings of the unrighteous** This is their fate.



## Chapter 19


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 19:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:2:1)


**(מענה איוב):** 
**תּוֹגְיוּן.** לְשׁוֹן ״תּוּגָה״ (משלי יד:יג):


**Job’s Reply**
**will you grieve** Heb. תגיון, an expression of grief (תוגה).



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 19:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:3:1)


**עֶשֶׂר פְּעָמִים.** עֶשֶׂר פָּרָשִׁיּוֹת יֵשׁ עַד כָּאן:


**ten times** There are ten chapters until here.


###### Rashi on Job 19:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:3:2)


**תַּהְכְּרוּ לִי.** תִּתְנַכְּרוּ לִי:



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 19:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:4:1)


**אִתִּי תָּלִין.** אֲנִי הוּא הַלּוֹקֵחַ מַכּוֹת עַל מְשׁוּגָתִי:


**stay with me** I am the one who receives blows for my error.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 19:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:5:1)


**אִם אָמְנָם.** אַתֶּם נוֹסָפִים עָלַי לְצָרָתִי, **וְתוֹכִיחוּ עָלַי חֶרְפָּתִי** מַרְאִים וּמוֹכִיחִים חֶרְפָּתִי עַל פָּנַי:


**If indeed** you are added upon me to my trouble. **and prove against me my reproach** You show and prove my reproach to my face.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 19:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:7:1)


**אֶצְעַק.** עַל חָמָס וְלֹא אֵעָנֶה:


**I cry out** concerning **violence** but I am not answered.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 19:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:10:1)


**וַיַּסַּע.** לְשׁוֹן עֲקִירָה:


**he has uprooted** Heb. ויסע, an expression of uprooting.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 19:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:12:1)


**וַיָּסֹלּוּ.** לְשׁוֹן ״מְסִלָּה״ (ישעיה יא:טז):


**they build their road** Heb. ויסלו, an expression of a highway (מסלה).



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 19:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:13:1)


**אַךְ זָרוּ.** לְשׁוֹן ״אַכְזָר״ (איוב ל:כא):


**became strange** Heb. אך זרו, an expression of cruelty.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Job 19:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:17:1)


**לִבְנֵי בִטְנִי.** לְאוֹתָם שֶׁגִּדַּלְתִּי בְּבֵיתִי, כְּאִלּוּ הֵם בָּנַי:


**to the children of my body** To those who I raised in my house as though they were my own children.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Job 19:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:18:1)


**עֲוִילִים מָאֲסוּ בִי.** נִבְזֶה אֲנִי בְּעֵינֵי נְעָרִים, וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן בְּעֵינֵי שָׂרִים:


**children despised me** I was despised in the eyes of children, all the more so in the eyes of princes.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 19:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:20:1)


**דָּבְקָה עַצְמִי.** שֶׁאֲנִי כָּחוּשׁ מֵעֹבִי בְּשָׂרִי:


**My bones cleaved** because I am emaciated from thickness of flesh.


###### Rashi on Job 19:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:20:2)


**בְּעוֹר שִׁנָּי.** כָּל בְּשָׂרִי הָיָה לָקוּי בִּשְׁחִין וּבְתוֹלָעִים, חוּץ מֵחֲנִיכֵי שִׁנָּיו:


**by the skin of my teeth** All his flesh was afflicted with boils and worms except his gums.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Job 19:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:24:1)


**בְּעֵט בַּרְזֶל.** יֵחָצְבוּן בַּצּוּר, וְאַחַר כָּךְ מַעֲבִירִין אֶת הָעוֹפֶרֶת עֲלֵיהֶם, לָתֵת לָאוֹתִיּוֹת מַרְאֵה שַׁחֲרוּרִית, לְהַכִּירָם; וְכֵן דֶּרֶךְ חוֹקֵק אֶבֶן. וְלֹא יִתָּכֵן לְפָרֵשׁ עֵט שֶׁל עוֹפֶרֶת, שֶׁהֲרֵי רַךְ הוּא אֵצֶל הָאֶבֶן:


**With an iron pen** they should be inscribed in the rock, and afterwards they run lead over them to give the letters a black appearance, to make them recognizable. This is the practice of those who inscribe on stone. It cannot be explained to mean a lead pen because lead is soft in comparison with stone.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Job 19:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:25:1)


**וַאֲנִי יָדַעְתִּי גֹּאֲלִי חָי.** וָא״ו זֶה חוֹזֵר לְמַעְלָה: אַתֶּם תִּרְדְּפוּנִי, וַאֲנִי יָדַעְתִּי גּוֹאֲלִי חַי – לִיפָּרַע, וְהוּא יִתְקַיֵּם וְיַעֲמֹד:


**But I know that my Redeemer lives** This “vav” refers to the above. You persecute me, but I know that my Redeemer lives to requite you, and He will endure and rise.


###### Rashi on Job 19:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:25:2)


**וְאַחֲרוֹן עַל עָפָר יָקוּם.** לְאַחַר שֶׁיִּכְלוּ כָּל שׁוֹכְנֵי עָפָר, הוּא אַחֲרוֹן יַעֲמֹד:


**and the last on the earth, He will endure** After all earth dwellers will perish, He will endure last.


###### Rashi on Job 19:25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:25:3)


**יָקוּם.** יִתְקַיֵּם:



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Job 19:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:26:1)


**וְאַחַר עוֹרִי נִקְּפוּ זֹאת.** וְהֵם אֵינָם נוֹתְנִים לֵב לְגוֹאֲלִי, אֶלָּא אַחַר מַכַּת עוֹרִי נִקְּפוּ וְנִקְּרוּ הַקְּנִיטָה וְהָרְדִיפָה הַזֹּאת שֶׁאָמַרְתִּי, שֶׁהִיא לִי כְּנוֹקֵף וְחוֹתֵךְ בְּעוֹרִי. כְּמוֹ ״וְנִקַּף סִבְכֵי הַיַּעַר״ (ישעיהו י:לד):


**And after my skin, they have cut into this** Yet they do not pay heed to my Redeemer, but after the plague of my skin, they cut, strike, and pierce. This vexation and persecution that I mentioned, which is to me like one cutting into my skin, like (Isa. 10:34): “And the thickets of the forests shall be cut off (ונקף).”


###### Rashi on Job 19:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:26:2)


**וּמִבְּשָׂרִי.** אֲנִי רוֹאֶה מִשְׁפָּטִים:


**and from my flesh** I see judgments.


###### Rashi on Job 19:26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:26:3)


**אֱלוֹהַּ.** לְשׁוֹן מִשְׁפָּט וְיִסּוּרִין:


**judgment** Heb. אלוה, an expression of judgment and chastisements.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Job 19:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:28:1)


**כִּי תֹאמְרוּ מַה נִּרְדָּף לוֹ.** כְּמוֹ ״כִּי תֹאמַר בִּלְבָבְךָ״ (דברים ז:יז), שֶׁהוּא מְשַׁמֵּשׁ לְשׁוֹן דִּילְמָא. כְּלוֹמַר: וְשֶׁתִּהְיוּ נוֹתְנִים לִבְּכֶם לַחְמֹל, וְלוֹמַר: לָמָּה נִרְדְּפֶנּוּ?


**Shall you say, ‘Why should we persecute him?’** Like (Deut. 7:17), “Will you say in your heart,” in which [כי] is an expression of a question. That is to say, “Will you lay your heart to the matter to have pity and say, ‘Why should we persecute him?’”


###### Rashi on Job 19:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:28:2)


**וְשֹׁרֶשׁ דָּבָר.** הַנִּמְצָא בּוֹ מַה הִיא? לְעוֹלָם לֹא תַעֲלֶה בִלְבַבְכֶם הַטּוֹבָה הַזֹּאת! שֹׁרֶשׁ דָּבָר עַל מָה הוּא עוֹמֵד?


**and the root of the matter** that is found in him—what is it? This favor will never enter your mind [to determine] the root of the matter for which he suffers with pains.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Job 19:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:29:1)


**כִּי חֵמָה.** שֶׁאַתֶּם מַגְדִּילִים עָוֹן הַמֵּבִיא לִידֵי חֶרֶב:


**for the wrath** For you are committing much iniquity that will bring about the sword.


###### Rashi on Job 19:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/19:29:2)


**לְמַעַן תֵּדְעוּן.** וְתָבִינוּ סוֹד הַפּוּרְעָנוּת הַבָּא עַל הָרְשָׁעִים. וְיֵשׁ אֵם לַמָּסֹרֶת: שַׁדִּין כְּתִיב – כְּמוֹ שֶׁיֵּשׁ דִּין בָּעוֹלָם, כְּמוֹ ״שַׁקַּמְתִּי דְּבוֹרָה״ (שופטים ה:ז). כֵּן רָאִיתִי בְּתַנְחוּמָא (תנחומא, משפטים ג):


**in order that you know** and understand the secret of the retribution that will come upon the wicked. Also, the mesorah is authoritative and שדין is written, as though it would say that there is justice (שיש דין) in the world, like (Jud. 5:7), “I, Deborah, arose (שַׁקַמְתִּי), [like שֶׁקַמְתִּי].” I saw this in Tanchuma (Mishpatim 3)



## Chapter 20


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 20:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:2:1)


**(מענה צופר):**
**שְׂעִפַּי יְשִׁיבוּנִי.** מַחְשְׁבוֹתַי יְשִׁיבוּנִי מַעֲנֶה לְפִי, וּבַעֲבוּר שֶׁאֲנִי חָשׁ וְשׁוֹתֵק וְשַׂמְתִּי בִי שְׁתִיקוֹתַי יְשִׁיבוּנִי סְעִיפַי לַעֲנוֹת. אֲנִי שׁוֹמֵעַ מִמְּךָ יִסּוּרֵי כְלִמָּתִי, שֶׁאַתָּה תַכְלִימֵנִי, וְעַל כֵּן דְּבַר רוּחַ יַעֲנֵנִי מִבִּינָתִי לַהֲשִׁיבְךָ:


**Zophar’s answer**
**my thoughts will cause me to answer** My thoughts will answer me with an answer in my mouth, and because I keep my peace and remain silent, and I put my silence within me, my thoughts will give me an answer to reply. **The chastisement of my disgrace** I hear from you the chastisement of my disgrace, that you disgrace me. Therefore, a word of spirit shall answer me out of my understanding, to reply to you.


###### Rashi on Job 20:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:2:2)


**חוּשִׁי.** שְׁתִיקוּתִי, כְּמוֹ ״עֵת לַחֲשׁוֹת״ (קהלת ג:ז):


**my silence** Heb. חושי, my silence, like (Ecc. 3:7), “a time to be silent (לחשות).”



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 20:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:4:1)


**הֲזֹאת.** תֵּימַהּ: הֲיוֹדֵעַ אַתָּה מִדָּה הַמְהַלֶּכֶת עַל פְּנֵי כָּל הַדּוֹרוֹת **מִנִּי עַד**?


**Do...this** Heb. הזאת. This is a wonder. Do you know the manner in which this has been conducted throughout all generations since time immemorial?



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 20:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:5:1)


**כִּי רִנְנַת רְשָׁעִים.** כָּל יְמֵי הֱיוֹתָהּ, מִקָּרוֹב בָּאָה וְלֹא לְאֹרֶךְ יָמִים עוֹמֶדֶת:


**that the triumph of the wicked** throughout all its duration, has come but recently, and will not last for any length of time.


###### Rashi on Job 20:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:5:2)


**וְשִׂמְחַת חָנֵף עֲדֵי רָגַע.** בִּזְמַן מוּעָט תִּכְלֶה:


**and the joy of the flatterers is but a moment** in a short time, it will end.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 20:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:6:1)


**שִׂיאוֹ.** גָּבְהוֹ:



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 20:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:7:1)


**כְּגֶלְלוֹ.** גָּלָל שֶׁל רְעִי:


**his dung** Heb. כְּגֶלְלוֹ, dung of excrement.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 20:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:8:1)


**וְלֹא יִמְצָאוּהוּ.** יוֹדְעָיו:


**and they will not find him** i.e., his friends.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Job 20:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:9:1)


**שְׁזָפַתּוּ.** רָאֲתָהוּ:


**that glimpsed him** That saw him.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 20:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:10:1)


**בָּנָיו יְרַצּוּ דַלִּים.** עַל אַנְשֵׁי סְדוֹם נֶאֱמַר מַעֲנֶה זוֹ, שֶׁהָיוּ גַּזְלָנִים וְאַכְזָרִים עַל עֲנִיִּים:


**His sons will placate the poor** This reply was stated in reference to the people of Sodom, who were robbers and were cruel to the poor.


###### Rashi on Job 20:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:10:2)


**וְיָדָיו תָּשֵׁבְנָה.** חֲמַס כַּפָּיו:


**and his hands will return** He will return the plunder of his hands.


###### Rashi on Job 20:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:10:3)


**אוֹנוֹ.** כֹּחַ אָנְסוֹ:


**his plunder** The power of his force.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 20:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:11:1)


**עַצְמוֹתָיו מָלְאוּ עֲלוּמָיו.** כֹּחַ נַעֲרוּתָיו:


**His bones are full of his youth** The strength of his youth.


###### Rashi on Job 20:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:11:2)


**וְעִמּוֹ עַל עָפָר תִּשְׁכָּב.** כִּי יָמוּת פִּתְאוֹם עִם כֹּחוֹ:


**and it will lie on the dust with him** Because he will die suddenly with his strength.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 20:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:12:1)


**אִם תַּמְתִּיק.** כָּךְ וֶסְתּוֹ שֶׁל רָשָׁע: **אִם תַּמְתִּיק בְּפִיו רָעָה** וְאֵינוֹ רוֹאֶה לָהּ עַתָּה שָׁעָה שֶׁתָּחוּל, **יַכְחִידֶנָּה תַּחַת לְשׁוֹנוֹ**, עַד בֹּא הָעֵת שֶׁתַּצְלִיחַ:


**be sweet** So is the custom of a wicked man: if evil is sweet in his mouth and he does not see now the moment for it to take effect. **though he hide it under his tongue** until the time comes when it will prosper.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 20:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:14:1)


**לַחְמוֹ בְּמֵעָיו.** בְּיוֹם אֵידוֹ נֶהְפַּךְ לִמְרוֹרוֹת פְּתָנִים:


**His food...within him** on the day of his misfortune will turn into the venom of cobras.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 20:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:15:1)


**חַיִל.** מָמוֹן:


**wealth** Heb. חיל, money.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 20:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:16:1)


**אֶפְעֶה.** נָחָשׁ שָׂרָף:


**carpet viper** Heb. אֶפְעֶה, a fiery serpent.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Job 20:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:17:1)


**בִּפְלַגּוֹת נַהֲרֵי נַחֲלֵי.** גַּן עֵדֶן הַמְּתוּקִין כִּדְבַשׁ וְחֶמְאָה:


**the rivulets, the flowing rivers of** the Garden of Eden, which are as sweet as honey and cream.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Job 20:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:18:1)


**מֵשִׁיב יָגָע.** הַגֶּזֶל שֶׁגָּזַל:


**He will return that which he labored for** The loot that he stole.


###### Rashi on Job 20:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:18:2)


**כְּחֵיל תְּמוּרָתוֹ.** כַּאֲשֶׁר תִּהְיֶה תְּמוּרָתוֹ לְ״חֵיל״, נְכָסִים גְּדוֹלִים, אָז יְבִיאֵהוּ חִסָּרוֹן:


**when his gain becomes vast wealth** When his gain becomes vast wealth, then he will lose it.


###### Rashi on Job 20:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:18:3)


**וְלֹא יַעֲלֹס.** בּוֹ, וְכֵן ״יַעֲלֹז״ (תהלים צו:יב) וְכֵן ״יַעֲלֹץ״ (דברי הימים א טז:לב) שְׁלָשְׁתָּן מִתְחַלְּפוֹת:


**he will not rejoice** Heb. יעלס, with it. Similarly, יַעֲלֹז, and so יַעַלֹץ ; the three of them are interchangeable.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 20:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:19:1)


**כִּי רִצַּץ עָזַב דַּלִּים.** לְאַחַר שֶׁרִצַּץ אֶת דַּלִּים, עָזַב הַדַּלִּים וְנִטְרַד מִן הָעוֹלָם וְהָלַךְ לוֹ, וְהֵם חוֹזְרִים וְגוֹבִים מִמֶּנּוּ גְּזֵלוֹתָיו:


**When he oppressed, he abandoned the poor** After he oppressed the poor, he abandoned the poor and was banished from the world and went his way, and they return and collect their stolen items from him.


###### Rashi on Job 20:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:19:2)


**כִּי רִצַּץ.** כְּשֶׁרִצַּץ; כְּמוֹ ״כִּי יִהְיֶה לָהֶם דָּבָר בָּא אֵלַי״ (שמות יח:טז) – כְּשֶׁיִּהְיֶה לָהֶם:


[The expression] כִּי רָצַץ [means] when he oppressed, like (Exod. 18:16), “Whenever (כי) they have a concern, they come to me,” which means when they have.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 20:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:20:1)


**כִּי לֹא יָדַע.** אֶת בִּטְנוֹ מֵעוֹלָם שָׁלֵיו, בִּטְנוֹ אוֹמֵר לוֹ תָּמִיד: גְּזוֹל וְהָבֵא לְמַלְּאֵנִי! וְעַל כֵּן **בַּחֲמוּדוֹ.** בִּבְשַׂר יוֹפִי חֶמְדָּתוֹ:


**For he did not know** [He] never [knew] his stomach to be at rest. His stomach always says to him, “Rob and bring [food] to fill me up.” Therefore... **with his beauty** With the flesh of his coveted beauty.


###### Rashi on Job 20:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:20:2)


**לֹא יְמַלֵּט.** כִּי יְהֵא פּוֹחֵת וְהוֹלֵךְ, וְדוֹמֶה לוֹ ״וַתֶּמֶס כָּעָשׁ חֲמוּדוֹ״ (תהילים לט:יב):


**he will not save anything** because it will continuously diminish. Similar to this is (Ps. 39: 11): “You make his beauty melt away like a moth.”



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Job 20:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:21:1)


**אֵין שָׂרִיד לְאָכְלוֹ.** לֹא שִׁיֵּר מִמַּאֲכָלוֹ לְחַלֵּק לַעֲנִיִּים:


**Nothing remains of his food** He left nothing over from his food to share with the poor.


###### Rashi on Job 20:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:21:2)


**עַל כֵּן לֹא יָחִיל טוּבוֹ.** לֹא יַצְלִיחַ, כְּמוֹ ״יָחִילוּ דְרָכָיו״ (תהלים י:ה). וּבְאַנְשֵׁי סְדוֹם מְדַבֵּר, שֶׁהָיָה עֵינֵיהֶם צָרָה בָּאוֹרְחִים, כִּי כֵּן הֵעִיד עַל סְדוֹם ״גָּאוֹן שִׂבְעַת לֶחֶם וְגוֹ׳ וְיַד עָנִי וְאֶבְיוֹן לֹא הֶחֱזִיקָה״ (יחזקאל טז:מט). וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּ (סנהדרין צב.): **אֵין שָׂרִיד לְאָכְלוֹ.** לֹא הִשְׁרָה תַּלְמִידֵי חֲכָמִים עַל שֻׁלְחָנוֹ, כְּמוֹ ״וּבַשְּׂרִידִים אֲשֶׁר ה׳ קוֹרֵא״ (יואל ג:ה):


**therefore his goods shall not prosper** Heb. יחיל, shall not prosper, as in (Ps. 10:5), “His ways prosper (יחילו) .” He speaks of the people of Sodom, who were stingy with travelers, for so did Ezekiel testify (16:49) concerning Sodom: “proud with plenty of food etc. and yet, she did not strengthen the hand of the poor and needy.” And our Sages explained (Sanh. 92a) אין שריד לאכלוֹ to mean that he did not allow Torah scholars to sit at his table, like (Joel 3:5): “and among the survivors whom the Lord calls.”



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Job 20:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:22:1)


**בִּמְלֹאות שִׂפְקוֹ.** בְּהִתְמַלְּאוֹת תַּאֲוַת לִבּוֹ דַּי, אָז **יֵצֶר לוֹ.** תָּבֹא עָלָיו צָרָה:


**When his desire has been filled sufficiently** when the desire of his heart has been filled sufficiently, then... **he will be in straits** Trouble will come upon him.


###### Rashi on Job 20:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:22:2)


**סִפְקוֹ.** לְשׁוֹן דֵּי, כְּדִמְתַרְגְּמִינָן: ״כַּד סְפִיקוּ גַמְלַיָּא״ (אונקלוס בראשית כד:כב), ״וּמָצָא לָהֶם״ (במדבר יא:כב): ״הֲיִסְפְּקוּן״ (תרגום אונקלוס):


סִפְקוֹ is an expression of sufficiency, as is translated into Aramaic (Gen. 24:22): “When the camels finished (ספיקו) drinking”; (Num. 11:22): “so that it may be enough for them (היספקון).”


###### Rashi on Job 20:22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:22:3)


**כָּל־יַד עָמֵל תְּבוֹאֶנּוּ.** תֶּחֱזַק עָלָיו וְתָצַר יַד עֲנִיִּים:


**the hand of every laborer will come upon him** The hand of the poor shall overwhelm him and come upon him.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Job 20:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:23:1)


**יְהִי לְמַלֵּא בִטְנוֹ יְשַׁלַּח בּוֹ חֲרוֹן אַפּוֹ.** שְׁלִיחוּת חֲרוֹן אַפּוֹ יְהִי לְמַלֵּא בִטְנוֹ. וְאֵין זֶה לְשׁוֹן קְלָלָה אֶלָּא לְשׁוֹן נְבוּאָה וְעָתִיד:


**It shall come to pass that to fill his belly, He shall send upon him His burning wrath** The sending of His burning wrath will be [enough] to fill his belly. This is not an expression of a curse but an expression of prophecy and of the future.


###### Rashi on Job 20:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:23:2)


**וְיַמְטֵר עָלֵימוֹ.** הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**and rain upon them** The Holy One, blessed be He.


###### Rashi on Job 20:23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:23:3)


**בִּלְחוּמוֹ.** בְּזַעַף מִלְחַמְתּוֹ פַּחִים אֵשׁ וְגָפְרִית (תהלים יא:ו), כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: ״וַה׳ הִמְטִיר עַל סְדֹם וְגוֹ׳״ (בראשית יט:כד):


**with His battle** With the fury of His battle, coals, fire, and brimstone, as the matter is stated (Gen. 19: 24): “And the Lord rained upon Sodom etc.”



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Job 20:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:24:1)


**תַּחְלְפֵהוּ.** חִצִּים שֶׁתִּזְרוֹק הַקֶּשֶׁת יַחְלְפוּ אֶת גּוּפוֹ לַעֲבוֹר מֵעֵבֶר אֶל עֵבֶר. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: **תַּחְלְפֵהוּ**, כְּמוֹ תַּחֲלִיפֵהוּ (תְּקַדְּמֵהוּ) **קֶשֶׁת נְחוּשָׁה**:


**shall pierce him** Heb. תַּחְלְפֵהוּ The arrows that the bow will shoot will pierce his body, going through from one side to the other. Another version: תַּחְלְפֵהוּ is like תַּחְליִפֵהוּ (sic), a copper bow shall confront him.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Job 20:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:25:1)


**שָׁלַף.** הַשּׁוֹלֵף עָלָיו אֶת הַנֶּשֶׁק:


**drew** The one who drew the weapon upon him.


###### Rashi on Job 20:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:25:2)


**וַיֵּצֵא.** הַנֶּשֶׁק:


**and it emerged** [i.e.,] the weapon.


###### Rashi on Job 20:25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:25:3)


**מִגֵּוָה.** מִתּוֹךְ תַּעְרָהּ:


###### Rashi on Job 20:25:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:25:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:25:4)


**וּבָרָק.** פְּלַנְדוֹרְ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז, כְּמוֹ ״וּלְמַעַן הֱיוֹת לָהּ בָּרָק״ (יחזקאל כא:טו) – פוֹרְבִּי״ר:


**and its glittering blade** Splendeur, or splendor (?) in French, like (Ezek. 21: 15), “in order that it glitter (ברק) .” Fourbir in Old French, to polish.


###### Rashi on Job 20:25:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:25:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:25:5)


**מִמְּרוֹרָתוֹ יַהֲלֹךְ.** מִמְּרוֹרָתוֹ שֶׁל נֶשֶׁק יַהֲלֹךְ, וּמִשָּׁם יִהְיוּ אֵמִים וָפַחַד עַל הָרָשָׁע:


**from its bitterness** [From the bitterness] of the weapon it shall come forth, and from there shall be terrors and fear upon the wicked.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Job 20:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:26:1)


**לִצְפּוּנָיו.** לְצֹרֶךְ מַצְפּוּנֵי גְנוּזִים הַצְּפוּנִים לוֹ:


**for what awaits him** For the hidden things that lie in store for him.


###### Rashi on Job 20:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:26:2)


**אֵשׁ לֹא נֻפָּח.** אֵשׁ שֶׁל גֵּיהִנֹּם:


**fire that was not blown** The fire of Gehinnom.


###### Rashi on Job 20:26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:26:3)


**יֵרַע שָׂרִיד בְּאָהֳלוֹ.** תָּבֹא רָעָה לַשָּׂרִיד שֶׁיִּשָּׁאֵר בְּאָהֳלוֹ. כָּל הַפָּרָשָׁה כֻּלָּהּ בְּאַנְשֵׁי סְדוֹם (בראשית יט:א-כט):


**harm shall befall the survivor in his tent** Harm will come upon the survivor who will remain in his tent. The entire portion deals with the people of Sodom. “Harm shall befall the survivor in his tent” means that even Lot who escaped from them—harm befell his tent, i.e., his wife, who became a pillar of salt. I found this in the midrash of Rabbi Tanchuma (Buber, Vayera 8).


###### Rashi on Job 20:26:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:26:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:26:4)


**יֵרַע שָׂרִיד בְּאָהֳלוֹ.** אֲפִלּוּ לוֹט הַנִּמְלָט מֵהֶם בָּאָה רָעָה לְאָהֳלוֹ; זוֹ אִשְׁתּוֹ, שֶׁהָיְתָה נְצִיב מֶלַח (בראשית יט:כו). כֵּן מָצָאתִי בְּמִדְרַשׁ רַבִּי תַּנְחוּמָא (תנחומא, וירא ז):



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Job 20:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:27:1)


**מִתְקוֹמְמָה.** לְשׁוֹן אוֹיֵב הַקָּם כְּנֶגְדּוֹ:


**shall rise up** [This is] an expression of an enemy who rises up against him.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Job 20:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:28:1)


**יִגֶל.** לְשׁוֹן גָּלוּת:


**shall go into exile** Heb. יגל, an expression of exile.


###### Rashi on Job 20:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:28:2)


**נִגָּרוֹת.** יִהְיוּ אוֹצְרוֹתָיו נְתוּנוֹת לִמְשִׁיסָה וּלְבַז, כְּמוֹ ״כַּמַּיִם הַמֻּגָּרִים אָרְצָה״ (מיכה א:ד):


**shall flow** Heb. נגרות. His treasures shall be given to plunder and despoiling, like (Micah 1:4), “like water that flows (המגרים) to the earth” [sic].



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Job 20:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/20:29:1)


**אִמְרוֹ מֵאֵל.** הָאָמוּר עָלָיו מִלִּפְנֵי הַמָּקוֹם. **אִמְרוֹ** לְשׁוֹן זָכָר, מִגִּזְרַת ״אֲמָרַי הַאֲזִינָה״ (תהלים ה:ב); ״אִמְרָתוֹ״ (תהלים קמז:טו) – לְשׁוֹן נְקֵבָה, מִגִּזְרַת ״אֲמָרוֹת טְהוֹרוֹת״ (תהלים יב:ז):


**appointed him by God** Heb. אִמְרוֹ. What was said about him from before the Omnipresent. The word אִמְרוֹ is in the masculine gender, from the form of (Ps. 5:2), “Give ear to my words (אַמָרַי).” אִמְרָתוֹ appears (ibid. 147:15) in the feminine gender, from the form of (ibid. 12:7) “pure words (אַמָרוֹת).”



## Chapter 21


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 21:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:2:1)


**(מענה איוב):** 
**וּתְהִי זֹאת תַּנְחוּמוֹתֵיכֶם.** אֶת אֲשֶׁר תַּחֲרִישׁוּ וְתִשְׁמְעוּ אֵלַי, זֹאת תְּהִי לִי תַּנְחוּם מֵאִתְּכֶם:


**Job’s answer**
**and this shall be your consolations** The fact that you will keep your peace and listen to me this will be my consolation from you.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 21:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:3:1)


**שָׂאוּנִי.** סִבְלוּנִי:


**Bear with me** Heb. שאוני, endure me.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 21:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:4:1)


**הֶאָנֹכִי לְאָדָם שִׂיחִי.** לָמָּה תַּעַמְדוּ עַל צַעֲקָתִי? וְכִי אֲנִי לְאָדָם כָּמוֹנִי מֵשִׂיחַ, שֶׁיִּשְׁמַע קוֹלִי? **וְאִם מַדּוּעַ לֹא תִקְצַר רוּחִי**, לִצְעוֹק מִצָּרָה לִי?


**Am I speaking to man** Why should you make an issue of my complaint? Am I complaining to a man like me that he should listen to me? **Why then should I not be short of breath** to cry out from my straits?



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 21:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:5:1)


**פְּנוּ אֵלַי.** לִשְׁמֹעַ:


**Turn to me** to listen.


###### Rashi on Job 21:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:5:2)


**וְהָשַׁמּוּ.** הִתְּמְהוּ לִדְבָרַי:


**and be silent** Be astonished at my words.


###### Rashi on Job 21:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:5:3)


**וְשִׂימוּ יָד עַל פֶּה.** שֶׁלֹּא תֵדְעוּ לְהָשִׁיב:


**and place your hand upon your mouth** because you will not know what to answer.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 21:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:6:1)


**וְאִם זָכַרְתִּי.** דָּבָר שֶׁאֲנִי רוֹצֶה לוֹמַר מַדּוּעַ רְשָׁעִים יִחְיוּ?!


**Now when I remember** the thing I wish to say, “Why do the wicked live?”


###### Rashi on Job 21:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:6:2)


**וְנִבְהָלְתִּי.** שֶׁאֲנִי רוֹאֶה רִשְׁעֵי דּוֹר הַמַּבּוּל עָתְקוּ גַּם גָּבְרוּ חַיִל (בראשית ו:א-ד):


**I become frightened** for I see the wicked men of the Flood.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Job 21:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:9:1)


**שָׁלוֹם מִפָּחַד.** שֶׁלֹּא הָיוּ מַזִּיקִין שׁוֹלְטִין בָּהֶן:


**peace from fear** Demons had no power over them.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 21:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:10:1)


**שׁוֹרוֹ עִבַּר.** אֶת הַנְּקֵבָה:


**His bull impregnates** a female.


###### Rashi on Job 21:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:10:2)


**וְלֹא יַגְעִל.** לֹא יוֹרֶה בָּהּ זֶרַע פְּסֹלֶת, שֶׁיְּהֵא חוֹזֵר וְנִפְלָט מִגּוּפָהּ בְּלֹא הֵרָיוֹן. כָּל לְשׁוֹן הַגְעָלָה – פְּלִיטָה הוּא; וְכֵן בִּלְשׁוֹן מִשְׁנָה: מַגְעִילָן בְּרוֹתְחִין (עבודה זרה עה.); וְכֵן ״כִּי שָׁם נִגְעַל מָגֵן גִּבּוֹרִים״ (שמואל ב א:כא) – נִפְלַט מְשִׁיחָתוֹ, וְהָיָה כְּאִלּוּ לֹא נִמְשַׁח בְּשֶׁמֶן; שֶׁהָיוּ רְגִילִין לִמְשֹׁחַ מָגִנֵּי עוֹר, כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּהֵא חָלָק וּמַחֲלִיק אֶת הָרֹמַח וְהַחֲנִית הַמַּכֶּה בּוֹ; וְהוּא כְּמוֹ ״שָׂרִים מָשְׁחוּ מָגֵן״ (ישעיה כא:ה):


**and does not fail** He does not eject into her inferior sperm, which would be cast out of her body without impregnating her. Every expression of געילה means casting out, and so it is in the language of the Mishnah (Avodah Zarah 75a), One must purge them (מגעילן) with boiling water. Similarly (II Sam. 1:21), “for there the shield of the mighty was rejected (נגעל).” Its anointing was cast out, and it was as though it had not been anointed with oil, for they were accustomed to anointing the leather shields in order that it be smooth and cause the spear or javelin that strikes it to glide off. It is like (Isa. 21:5), “princes, anoint a shield.”


###### Rashi on Job 21:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:10:3)


**תְּפַלֵּט פָּרָתוֹ.** בָּעֵת תֵּלֵד אֶת וְלָדָהּ:


**his cow bears young** She gives birth to her young at the proper time.


###### Rashi on Job 21:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:10:4)


**וְלֹא תְשַׁכֵּל.** בְּלֹא עֵת:


**and does not abort** before its time.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 21:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:11:1)


**יְשַׁלְּחוּ כַצֹּאן עֲוִילֵיהֶם וְגוֹ׳.** בְּעֵת לֵידָתוֹ, הָיָה הוֹלֵךְ וּמְרַקֵּד מִיָּד; וְאִם פּוֹגֵעַ בְּמַזִּיק, נִלְחָם בּוֹ. וְהָיְתָה אוֹמֶרֶת לוֹ אִמּוֹ: הָבֵא לִי מִסְפָּרַיִם, וַאֲחַתּוֹךְ שָׁרְךָ! (הגדת בראשית) לֵךְ וְהַדְלֵיק לִי אֶת הַנֵּר! (בראשית רבה לו:א):


**They send forth their infants like sheep etc.** At the time of his birth, he would go and dance immediately, and if he would meet a demon, he would battle with him, and his mother would say to him, “Bring me a pair of scissors and I will cut your umbilical cord,” in Haggadath Bereishith, “Go and light a candle for me,” in Gen. Rabbah (36:1).



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 21:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:12:1)


**יִשְׂאוּ.** הָיוּ נוֹשְׂאִין קוֹל בְּשִׁיר **בְּתֹף וְכִנּוֹר. עוּגָב** אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא לְשׁוֹן שְׂחוֹק, עַל שֵׁם הָעֲגָבוֹת; אשביימנ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז. כָּךְ שָׁמַעְתִּי:


**They raise** They would raise their voice in song with the tambourine and the harp. The flute (עוגב) is only an expression of sensual laughter (עגבות) (aboyement in French, barking.) So I heard.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 21:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:13:1)


**וּבְרֶגַע שְׁאוֹל יֵחָתּוּ.** כְּשֶׁמַּגִּיעַ יוֹם מוֹתוֹ, הָיָה מֵת לְפִי שָׁעָה, בְּנַחַת בְּלֹא יִסּוּרִין:


**and in a moment they descend to the grave** When his dying day arrived, he would die in a moment, easily, without suffering.


###### Rashi on Job 21:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:13:2)


**יֵחָתּוּ.** יֵרְדוּ, וּבְתַרְגּוּם: ״נְחִיתוּ״:


**they descend** Heb. יחתו, they descend, which is in Targum נחיתוּ.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 21:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:15:1)


**וּמַה נּוֹעִיל.** מַה הֲנָאָה יֵשׁ לָנוּ כִּי נִפְגַּע בּוֹ? אֵין אָנוּ צְרִיכִין לוֹ אֲפִלּוּ לְטִפַּת גְּשָׁמִים, כִּי ״אֵד יַעֲלֶה מִן הָאָרֶץ״ (בראשית ב:ו):


**and what will it avail us** What benefit do we have if we pray to Him? We do not need Him for the drops of rain, because “a cloud would ascend from the earth” (Gen. 2:6).



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 21:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:16:1)


**הֵן לֹא בְיָדָם טוּבָם.** בִּתְמִיהַּ: וְכִי אֵין כָּל טוּבָם בְּיָדָם?


**Behold, is not their prosperity in their hand** This is a question: Is not all their prosperity in their hand?


###### Rashi on Job 21:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:16:2)


**וַעֲצַת רְשָׁעִים רָחֲקָה מֶנִּי.** אִיּוֹב הָיָה מְשַׁבֵּחַ בְּעַצְמוֹ, שֶׁרָאָה אוֹתָם וְלֹא נִמְנָה עִמָּהֶם:


**The counsel of the wicked has distanced itself from me** Job was boasting that he saw them [the wicked] and was not counted among them.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Job 21:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:17:1)


**כַּמָּה נֵר רְשָׁעִים יִדְעָךְ.** לְמַעְלָה הוּא חוֹזֵר **לָמָּה רְשָׁעִים יִחְיוּ,** כַּמָּה תַּאֲרִיךְ זְמַן זֶה? וְחָזַר וּמְקַלְלָם: **נֵר רְשָׁעִים יִדְעָךְ וְגוֹ׳**:


**How long? May the lamp of the wicked be snuffed out** This refers back to the above, to (verse 7) “Why do the wicked live on?” How long will You lengthen this time [the lifetime of the wicked]? Then he continues and curses them, “May the lamp of the wicked be snuffed out...”


###### Rashi on Job 21:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:17:2)


**חֲבָלִים.** גּוֹרָל הָרָאוּי לָהֶם:


**lots** Heb. חבלים, the lot that they deserve.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 21:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:19:1)


**אֱלוֹהַּ יִצְפֹּן לְבָנָיו אוֹנוֹ.** בִּתְמִיהַּ: וְכִי מִדָּה הִיא? שֶׁכֵּן זֶה הִרְשִׁיעַ כָּל יָמָיו, וִיהֵא בְּשָׁלוֹם וְיָמוּת בְּשָׁלוֹם, וְאוֹנוֹ – וְרִשְׁעוֹ יִצְפֹּן הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לְבָנָיו, לִלְקוֹת לָהֶם?!


**Should God lay away his violence for his sons** This is a question. Is this just, that this one should sin all his life and die in peace, and his violence and his wickedness the Holy One, blessed be He, should lay away for his sons, to punish them?


###### Rashi on Job 21:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:19:2)


**יְשַׁלֵּם אֵלָיו וְיֵדָע.** שֶׁבַח הוּא לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, שֶׁיְּשַׁלֵּם לָרָשָׁע עַצְמוֹ גְּמוּלוֹ, וְיָבִין שֶׁהִרְשִׁיעַ לַעֲשׂוֹת:


**Let Him requite him so that he should know** it is a praise to the Holy One, blessed be He, that He Himself requite the wicked man his recompense so that he understand that he dealt wickedly.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 21:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:20:1)


**יִרְאוּ עֵינָיו כִּידוֹ.** שִׁבְרוֹ, וְאֵינִי יָכוֹל לִמְצוֹא לוֹ דִּמְיוֹן בַּתּוֹרָה, כִּי אִם לְפִי עִנְיַן הַפָּסוּק נִרְאֶה שֶׁבֶר, וְכֵן הָרַב לֹא מָצָא לוֹ דִּמְיוֹן. (**כִּידוֹ.** לְשׁוֹן אֵידוֹ, אָלֶ״ף מִתְחַלֵּף בְּכָ״ף):


**Let his eyes see his ruin** Heb. כידו, his ruin, but I cannot find a comparison in the Bible. However, according to the context of the verse, it appears to mean ruin, and so, many did not find a comparison to it. (כידו An expression of אֵידוֹ, his ruin. The “aleph” is substituted by a “chaff.”)



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Job 21:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:21:1)


**כִּי מַה חֶפְצוֹ בְּבֵיתוֹ אַחֲרָיו וּמִסְפַּר חֳדָשָׁיו חֻצָּצוּ.** כִּי מַה הוּא חוֹשֵׁשׁ, שקלירי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז, וְחָפֵץ בְּבֵיתוֹ לְאַחַר מוֹתוֹ, לִדְאֹג בְּחַיָּיו בְּפֻרְעָנוּת הָעֲתִידָה לָבֹא עֲלֵיהֶם, אַחֲרֵי שֶׁמִּסְפַּר חֳדָשָׁיו נִקְבְּצוּ וְיִכְלוּ לִפְנֵי הָרָעָה שֶׁהִבְטִיחָם הַמָּקוֹם ״וְהָיוּ יָמָיו מֵאָה וְעֶשְׂרִים שָׁנָה: (בראשית ו:ג)? זֹאת הִיא שֶׁאָמַרְתִּי לָכֶם: ״פְּנוּ אֵלַי וְהָשַׁמּוּ״ (איוב כא:ה):


**For what does He care about his house after him since the number of his months has been cut off** For what does he care (saklirir in Old French) or desire in his house after his death, to worry in his lifetime about the retribution destined to befall them, since the number of his months has been cut off and they will end before the evil that the Omnipresent promised them, viz. (Gen. 6:3) “therefore his days shall be 120 years.” This is what I said to you (verse 5), “Turn to me and be silent.”



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Job 21:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:22:1)


**הֲלָאֵל יְלַמֵּד דָּעַת.** הֲיֵשׁ אֶחָד מִכֶּם אֲשֶׁר הוּא תַּחַת הָאֵל, שֶׁיְּלַמֵּד דַּעַת, מַה הִיא הַמִּדָּה הַזֹּאת?


**Can anyone teach knowledge for God** Is there anyone among you who is in the place of God, who can teach the knowledge of what this standard is [i.e., the standard of God’s judgment]?


###### Rashi on Job 21:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:22:2)


**וְהוּא רָמִים יִשְׁפּוֹט.** כְּגוֹן אֲנִי וְהַצַּדִּיקִים וְהַחֲשׁוּבִים הוּא מְיַסֵּר וְדָן וְנִפְרָע מֵהֶם, כְּמוֹ ״וְלֹא יִפְנֶה דֶּרֶךְ כְּרָמִים״ (איוב כד:יח) הָאָמוּר בְּנֹחַ וּבִמְתוּשֶׁלַח בְּאַגָּדַת חֵלֶק (סנהדרין קח.-קח:). לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: **הַלְאֵל יְלַמֶּד דָּעַת**, כְּלוֹמַר: הַאֲנִי צָרִיךְ לְלַמְּדוֹ דַּעַת לִשְׁפּוֹט אֱמֶת? הוּא עַצְמוֹ יוֹדֵעַ שֶׁכֵּן! אֲבָל הוּא יִשְׁפּוֹט דִּינִים רָמִים בְּגָבְהוֹ וּבְגָדְלוֹ, וְאֵינוֹ חָשׁ לְדַקְדֵּק. זֶה שָׁמַעְתִּי, וְלֹא הָרִאשׁוֹן:


**But He judges high-handedly** Heb. והוא רמים ישפוט. [He punishes the exalted.] Those like me, and the righteous and the esteemed, He chastises, judges, and requites, like (below 24:18), “and he will not turn in the way of the vineyards,” stated in regard to Noah and Methuselah in the Aggadah of [chapter] Chelek (Sanh. 108a). Another explanation: Can anyone teach God knowledge? That is, do I have to teach Him knowledge to judge fairly? He Himself knows that it is so, but He passes high- handed judgments with His loftiness and His greatness, and He does not care to be exact. I heard this one but not the first one.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Job 21:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:23:1)


**זֶה.** הָרָשָׁע:


**This one** The wicked man.


###### Rashi on Job 21:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:23:2)


**יָמוּת.** נֶעֱנַשׁ **בְּעֶצֶם תֻּמּוֹ**, בְּמַרְאֵה שְׁלֵימוּתוֹ, לֹא חָסֵר:


**dies** (Is punished.) **in his full strength** in the appearance of his perfection, lacking nothing.


###### Rashi on Job 21:23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:23:3)


**שַׁלְאֲנָן.** כְּמוֹ ״שַׁאֲנָן״ (ירמיהו מח:יא):


**tranquil** Heb. שַׁלְאֲנָן, like שַׁאֲנָן.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Job 21:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:24:1)


**עֲטִינָיו מָלְאוּ חָלָב.** לְשׁוֹן מִשְׁנָה הוּא (מנחות פו.): ״עוֹטְנוֹ בְּבֵית הַבַּד״ – צוֹבְרִים הַזֵּיתִים יַחַד וְשׁוּמָנָן מִתְעַגֵּל וּמִתְאַסֵּף לְתוֹכוֹ, כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּהֵא מוּכָן כְּשֶׁעוֹצְרוֹ בְּבֵית הַבַּד; וְאוֹתוֹ כְּלִי שֶׁצּוֹבְרִין אוֹתוֹ כְּדֵי לְהֵאָסֵף שַׁמְנוֹ לְתוֹכוֹ, נִקְרָא ״מַעֲטָן״. אַף כָּאן: חֶלְבּוֹ וְלַחְלוּחוֹ וְשׁוּמָנוֹ נִקְרָא עֲטִינָיו:


**His pails are full of milk** Heb. עטיניו. This is the language of the Mishnah (Menahoth 86a): “He packs it (עוטנו) in the olive press.” They gather the olives together, and their oil forms globules and gathers in its midst in order to be ready when he presses it in the press, and that vessel in which they gather it in order for its oil to gather within it is called “ma’atan.” Here too, his milk and his moisture and fat are called “atinav.”



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Job 21:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:25:1)


**וְזֶה יָמוּת בְּנֶפֶשׁ מָרָה.** הָרָמִים וְהַקְּדוֹשִׁים אֵלָיו, יִפְרַע מִיָּד מֵהֶם:


**And this one dies with a bitter spirit** Those that are exalted and holy to Him—them He requites immediately.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Job 21:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:26:1)


**יַחַד עַל עָפָר יִשְׁכָּבוּ.** וּלְאַחַר מוֹתָן אֵין נִכָּר לַבְּרִיּוֹת, מִי הָרַע מִי הַטּוֹב, כִּי כָזֶה כֵּן זֶה **יַחַד עַל עָפָר יִשְׁכָּבוּ**:


**Together on the earth they lie** After their death, it is not recognizable to the people who is good and who is bad, because this one is like that one; they lie on the earth together.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Job 21:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:27:1)


**הֵן יָדַעְתִּי מַחְשְׁבוֹתֵיכֶם.** וְהַמְּזִמּוֹת אֲשֶׁר עָלַי תַּחְמֹסוּ – תָּרֵיעוּ לַחְשֹׁב חָמָס:


**Lo, I know your thoughts and the devices that you wrongfully think against me** You do wrong to think injustice.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Job 21:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:28:1)


**כִּי תֹאמְרוּ וְגוֹ׳.** בִּשְׁבִיל הָרָעָה אֲשֶׁר בָּאַתְנִי אַתֶּם אוֹמְרִים: רְאוּ, **אַיֵּה בֵיתוֹ שֶׁל זֶה,** שֶׁהָיָה נָדִיב, **וְאַיֵּה אֹהֶל וּמִשְׁכְּנוֹת** שְׁאָר **רְשָׁעִים**? כְּלוֹמַר: כָּמוֹהוּ כָּמוֹהֶם, בְּרִשְׁעוֹ אָבַד בֵּיתוֹ (וְלָמָּה אַתֶּם בּוֹטְחִים בְּשַׁלְוַתְכֶם):


**For etc.** Because of the evil that has befallen me, you say, “Look, where is the house of this man who was a prince, and where is the tent and the dwellings of the other wicked men?” That is to say that he is like them. Because of his wickedness, his house was lost. (Now why do you rely on your tranquility?)



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Job 21:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:29:1)


**הֲלֹא שְׁאֶלְתֶּם וְגוֹ׳.** כָּל זֶה אַתֶּם מוֹשְׁלִים עָלַי מְשָׁלוֹת:


**Have you not asked etc.** All these parables you are composing about me.


###### Rashi on Job 21:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:29:2)


**וְאֹתֹתָם.** אֲשֶׁר הוֹדִיעוּ אֶתְכֶם, לֹא תְנַכְּרוּם מִשּׂוּם עַל לֵב, כְּמוֹ ״וַיִּתְנַכֵּר אֲלֵיהֶם״ (בראשית מב:ז):


**and their signs** which they made known to you, you shall not make strange to yourselves by not paying attention to them, like (Gen. 42:7), “But he made himself a stranger (ויתנכר) to them.”



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Job 21:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:30:1)


**כִּי לְיוֹם.** אָמְרוּ לָכֶם, אֲשֶׁר **לְיוֹם אֵיד יֵחָשֶׂךְ רָע**, הָרָשָׁע מוֹנֵעַ וְנֶחְשָׂךְ מֵרָעָה, וְשָׁמוּר לְיוֹם הַקָּבוּעַ לְאֵידוֹ. כְּמוֹ ״אֲשֶׁר חָשַׂכְתִּי לְעֵת צָר״ (איוב לח:כג) – הִצְנַעְתִּים וַחֲשַׂכְתִּים לְהוֹדִיעָם עַד יוֹם קְרָב וּמִלְחָמָה:


**That** They told you that... **the wicked man is reserved for the day of calamity** The wicked man is withheld and spared from evil and reserved for the day that is appointed for his calamity, like (below 38:23), “That I have saved for a time of trouble.” I laid them away and spared them to let them know until the day of battle and war.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Job 21:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:31:1)


**מִי יַגִּיד עַל פָּנָיו דַּרְכּוֹ.** מִי הוּא הַמֶּלֶךְ הַגָּדוֹל אֲשֶׁר יַגִּיד עַל פָּנָיו דַּרְכּוֹ? וְלֹא יִרְאוּ מִמֶּנּוּ, כַּמָּה הוּא נִשְׂגָּב זֶה הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, שֶׁעָשָׂה!


**Who will tell His way to His face** Who is the great king who will tell His way to His face and will not fear Him? How exalted He is! That is the Holy One, blessed be He, Who did.


###### Rashi on Job 21:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:31:2)


**וּמִי יְשַׁלֶּם לוֹ.** וּגְמוּל רִשְׁעוֹ מִי יְשַׁלֶּם לוֹ?


**and who will requite Him** Who will requite Him with the recompense for His wickedness?



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Job 21:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:32:1)


**וְהוּא לִקְבָרוֹת יוּבָל.** סוֹפוֹ יוּבַל לַקֶּבֶר:


**But he will be borne to the grave** He will ultimately be borne to the grave.


###### Rashi on Job 21:32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:32:2)


**וְעַל גָּדִישׁ יִשְׁקוֹד.** כְּשֶׁהוּא נִקְבָּר בַּשָּׂדוֹת אֵצֶל הַגְּדִישִׁים. (**וְעַל גָּדִישׁ יִשְׁקוֹד.** יָדוּנוּ אוֹתוֹ עַל שֶׁלֹּא קִיֵּם הַמִּצְווֹת הַשַּׁיָּכִים בַּשָּׂדֶה, לֶקֶט שִׁכְחָה וּפֵאָה, וּשְׁאָר מִצְווֹת הַנּוֹהֲגִים בָּעֹמֶר וּבַגָּדִישׁ; וְלָכֵן שָׁקְדוּ וִימַהֲרוּ עָלָיו הַפֻּרְעָנוּת הג״ה מר״ע):


**and he will hasten to be beside the stacks of grain** when he is buried in the fields beside the stacks of grain.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Job 21:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:33:1)


**רִגְבֵי.** בְּלַשְׁטְ״ז בְּלַעַ״ז:


**clods** balast in French (which the sailors bring into the holds of the ships in order to weigh them down.)


###### Rashi on Job 21:33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:33:2)


**וּלְפָנָיו אֵין מִסְפָּר.** לַנִּמְשָׁכִים לְאוֹתוֹ דֶּרֶךְ. כָּל אֵלֶּה מְזִמּוֹת תַּחְמְסוּ עָלַי (איוב כא:כז):


**as there were innumerable ones before him** Those who draw after the same way. All these devices you think against me.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on Job 21:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/21:34:1)


**וְאֵיךְ תְּנַחֲמוּנִי הָבֶל.** מֵאַחַר שֶׁ**תְּשׁוּבוֹתֵיכֶם נִשְׁאַר מָעַל,** נִשְׁכְּחָה וְאָבְדָה מִכֶּם תְּשׁוּבוֹת הַחָכְמָה; וּתְשׁוּבוֹתֵיכֶם לֹא נִשְׁאֲרָה בְּיֶדְכֶם אֶלָּא תְּשׁוּבַת מָעַל. **מָעַל.** שֵׁם דָּבָר הוּא, לְכָךְ נִנְקַד כֻּלּוֹ קָמַץ, וְטַעֲמוֹ מִלְּעֵיל. כָּךְ שָׁמַעְתִּי:


**Now how do you comfort me with futility** Since only betrayal remains of your replies, the replies of wisdom are forgotten and lost from you, and of your replies, [those] left in your hands are only replies of betrayal. **betrayal** This word (מעל) is a noun, which is why it is voweled with a *kamatz*, and the accent is on the first syllable.



## Chapter 22


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 22:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:2:1)


**(מענה אליפז)**:
**הַלְאֵל יִסְכָּן גָּבֶר.** הַלְצֹרֶךְ וּלְהַנָּאַת הַיּוֹצֵר יוֹעִיל גֶּבֶר וְיֵהָנֶה, כַּאֲשֶׁר יְלַמֵּד דַּעַת אֶל הַבְּרִיּוֹת, וְחָכְמָה? יִסְכָּן הָרִאשׁוֹן – לְשׁוֹן הֲנָיָה, כְּמוֹ ״וַתְּהִי לוֹ סֹכֶנֶת״ (מלכים א א:ב); וְהַשֵּׁנִי – לְשׁוֹן לִמּוּד, כְּמוֹ ״הַהַסְכֵּן הִסְכַּנְתִּי״ (במדבר כב:ל). וְגַם הָרִאשׁוֹן יִתָּכֵן לִהְיוֹת נִפְתָּר לְשׁוֹן לִמּוּד: הַלְטוֹבַת הַיּוֹצֵר וּלְצָרְכּוֹ יִלְמַד גֶּבֶר כַּאֲשֶׁר יִלְמַד הַשְׂכֵּל?


**Eliphaz’s reply**
**Does a man benefit God** Will a man avail and benefit for the need and the benefit of the Creator when he teaches the people knowledge and wisdom? The first יִסְכָּן is an expression of benefit [or pleasure], like (I Kings 1:2), “and she shall be to him a warmer (סֹכֶנֶת).” The second is an expression of teaching [or accustoming], like (Num. 22:30), “Have I ever been in the habit (ההסכן הסכנתי)?” The former may also be interpreted as an expression of teaching, thus: Does a man learn for the good of the Creator and for His need when he learns wisdom?



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 22:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:3:1)


**הֶחָפֵץ לְשַׁדַּי.** כְּמוֹ ״כִּי מַה חֶפְצוֹ בְּבֵיתוֹ אַחֲרָיו״ (איוב כא:כא)? הַהֲנָאָה וַחֲשָׁשׁ לוֹ, אִם תַּצְדִּיק מַעֲשֶׂיךָ בְּהִתְוַכֵּחַ לְפָנָיו, שֶׁיָּבֹא עִמְּךָ לְהִתְוַכֵּחַ בִּדְבָרֶיךָ?


**Does the Almighty care** Like (above 21:21), “For what does He care (חפצו) about his house after him?” What pleasure or concern does He have if you are righteous in your deeds, that you reason with Him that He come with you to contest your words?


###### Rashi on Job 22:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:3:2)


**וְאִם בֶּצַע.** מָמוֹן לוֹ, אִם תַּתֵּם דְּרָכֶיךָ?


**or...any gain** i.e., monetary gain for Him if you perfect your ways?



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 22:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:4:1)


**הֲמִיִּרְאָתְךָ יוֹכִיחֶךָ.** אוֹ אִם מִיִּרְאָה שֶׁיִּירָא מִפָּנֶיךָ יָבֹא וְיִתְוַכַּח עִמְּךָ?


**Will He reason with you because He fears you?** Or, out of fear, that He fears you, will He come and reason with you?



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 22:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:5:1)


**הֲלֹא.** יוֹדֵעַ הוּא כִּי **רָעָתְךָ רַבָּה**:


**Is not** [Is it not so] that He knows that your evil is great?



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 22:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:8:1)


**וְאִישׁ זְרוֹעַ לוֹ הָאָרֶץ.** בִּתְמִיהַּ וְכִי מֵחֲמַת שֶׁהָיִיתָ גָּדוֹל תִּירַשׁ אֶת הָאָרֶץ? כְּלוּם הוּא רָאוּי לְהִתְקַיֵּם?


**Does a strong man own the earth** This is a question. Because you were strong, should you inherit the earth?


###### Rashi on Job 22:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:8:2)


**יֵשֶׁב בָּהּ.** יַאֲרִיךְ בָּהּ, כְּמוֹ ״וַתֵּשְׁבוּ בְקָדֵשׁ״ (דברים א:מו):


**remain therein** Is he fit to remain in existence?



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 22:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:11:1)


**אוֹ חֹשֶׁךְ לֹא תִרְאֶה.** כְּלוֹמַר: לֹא תִתֵּן עֵינֶיךָ וּבִטְחוֹנְךָ בַּכֹּחַ, לֵאמֹר:
