**or darkness so that you do not see** That is to say, You shall not put your eyes and your trust in strength, and say...



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 22:12:1

**Is not God in the height of heaven** and will not lower Himself to see.


###### Rashi on Job 22:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:12:2)


**וּרְאֵה רֹאשׁ כּוֹכָבִים כִּי רָמּוּ.** כְּמוֹ ״וּרְאֵה עָנְיִי״ שֶׁבְּסוֹף מַעֲנֶה הַחֲמִישִׁי (איוב י:טו), כְּלוֹמַר: וְנוֹתֵן אַתָּה עַיִן בְּרֹאשׁ כּוֹכָבִים כִּי רָמּוּ. וּבִשְׁבִיל כָּךְ אָמַרְתָּ:


**and behold the topmost of the stars, which are lofty** Heb. וראה, like (above 10:15), “and seeing (וראה) my affliction” at the end of the fifth reply. That is to say, ‘And You gaze upon the topmost stars because they are lofty.’ Therefore, you said...

**Does He judge through the dark cloud?** Does He see and judge facing this darkness? Behold...

**עָבִים סֵתֶר.** לְפָנָיו וְלֹא יוּכַל לִרְאוֹת:

###### Rashi on Job 22:14:2

**וְחוּג שָׁמַיִם יִתְהַלָּךְ.** וְלֹא יֵדַע אֶת אֲשֶׁר בָּאָרֶץ:


**and He walks on the circle of the heavens** and does not know what is on the earth.


###### Rashi on Job 22:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:14:3)


**חוּג.** עוֹגֶל מְחוּגַת שָׁמַיִם, כְּמוֹ ״וּבַמְּחוּגָה יְתָאֳרֵהוּ״ (ישעיהו מד:יג). קוֹמְפָּ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**circle** Heb. חוג, the circle of the compass of the heavens, like (Isa. 44:13), “and with a compass (ובמחוגה) he rounds it,” compas in French

**a river** And the river of the Flood or the brimstone and fire of Sodom was poured into their foundation.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 22:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:20:1)


**אִם לֹא נִכְחַד קִימָנוּ.** הֲתִשְׁמֹר וְרָאִיתָ אִם לֹא נִכְחַד יְקוּמָם?


**Whether their substance was cut off** Will you watch and see whether their substance was cut off.


###### Rashi on Job 22:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:20:2)


**קִימָנוּ.** כְּמוֹ קוֹמָתוֹ שֶׁל אוֹתוֹ הַדּוֹר, כְּמוֹ ״בֵּית אֵל יִמְצָאֶנּוּ וְשָׁם יְדַבֵּר עִמָּנוּ״ (הושע יב:ה) – כְּמוֹ ׳עִמּוֹ׳:


**their substance** Heb קימנו, like קומתו, his substance; i.e., of that generation, like (Hosea 12:5), “in Bethel he shall fed Him, and there He shall speak with him (עמנו),” like עִמוֹ.


###### Rashi on Job 22:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:20:3)


**וְיִתְרָם.** גַּאֲוָתָם:


###### Rashi on Job 22:20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:20:4)


**אָכְלָה אֵשׁ.** בְּרוֹתְחִין נִדּוֹנוּ. רַבִּי תַּנְחוּמָא דָּרַשׁ (תנחומא, נח י), שֶׁהָיוּ בַּעֲלֵי קוֹמָה, וְלֹא מֵתוּ בַּתְּהוֹמוֹת, וּבָאוּ עֲלֵיהֶם רוֹתְחִין מִלְמַעְלָה; וְזֶה יִתְרָם – קִימָה יְתֵרָה שֶׁלָּהֶם, כָּךְ שָׁמַעְתִּי:


**consumed by the fire** They were judged with boiling water. Rabbi Tanchuma expounded that they were tall and did not die in the depths. Then boiling water fell upon them from above and this is the meaning of יִתְרָם, their unusually tall height. So I heard.

**הַסְכֵּן.** לְמוֹד לִהְיוֹת עִמּוֹ שָׁלֵם:


**Accustom yourself** Accustom yourself to be (wholehearted) with him.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:21:2)


**וּשְׁלָם.** וְתִהְיֶה שָׁלֵם וְלֹא תֶחְסַר **בָּהֶם** בַּדְּבָרִים הָאֵלֶּה:


**and you will be complete** And you will be complete, lacking nothing.


###### Rashi on Job 22:21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:21:3)


**תְּבוֹאָתְךָ טוֹבָה.** תְּבוֹאֲךָ טוֹבָה:

### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Job 22:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:24:1)


**וְשִׁית עַל עָפָר בָּצֶר.** עַל אֲשֶׁר תָּשׁוּב אֵלָיו, תָּשִׁית עַל הָאָרֶץ חֹסֶן וּמִבְצָרִים:


**Then you shall make a fortification on the earth** By returning to Him, you will establish on the earth strength and fortifications.


###### Rashi on Job 22:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:24:2)


**וּבְצוּר נְחָלִים.** הַסְּלָעִים גְּבוֹהִים שֶׁבַּנְּחָלִים עֲמוּקִים יִהְיֶה לְךָ אֹסֶף צְבִירַת אוֹפִיר – הִיא סְגֻלַּת מְלָכִים, שֶׁהָיוּ מְסַגְּלִים וְאוֹצְרִים זָהָב הַבָּא מֵאוֹפִיר:


**and on the rocks of the valleys** On the lofty rocks in the deep valleys, you shall have a collection of an accumulation of Ophir. This is the treasure of the kings, for they would gather and store gold that came from Ophir.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Job 22:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:25:1)


**וְהָיָה שַׁדַּי בְּצָרֶיךָ.** אִם שַׁדַּי זֶה קֹדֶשׁ תָּמַהְתִּי עַל פִּתְרוֹנוֹ, שֶׁמַּשְׁמָעוֹ קְלָלָה שֶׁיִּמָּנֶה עִם אוֹיְבָיו כְּמוֹ ״וְאַתְּ הָיִית בְּעֹכְרָי״ (שופטים יא:לה) עִם עוֹכְרָי. וְעַל יְדֵי צֹרֶךְ נְדוּנֶנּוּ מִן הַמִּקְרָאוֹת חֲסֵרִים (כְּמוֹ וְהָיָה שַׁדַּי שׁוֹפֵט בְּצָרִיךְ, וְאִם נוּכַל לְאָמְרוֹ חוֹל לְשׁוֹן דַּיָּן וְחֹסֶן זֶה פִּתְרוֹנוֹ) וְהָיָה חֹסֶן מִבְצָרֶיךָ כְּמוֹ **וְשִׁית עַל עָפָר בָּצֶר** כְּלוֹמַר הוֹן עָתֵק תִּהְיֶה נִבְצָר. וּלְפִי הָעִנְיָן כֵּן פִּתְרוֹנוֹ שֶׁהֲרֵי סוֹפוֹ מוֹכִיחוֹ **וְכֶסֶף תּוֹעָפוֹת לָךְ** לְשׁוֹן חֹסֶן כְּמוֹ ״וְתוֹעֲפֹת רְאֵם לוֹ״ (במדבר כג:כב):


**And the Almighty shall be of your adversaries** Heb. והיה שד-י בצריך. If this word is holy [i.e., is a reference to the Almighty], I am bewildered at its interpretation, because its meaning is a curse, that He will be counted with his enemies, as in (Jud. 11:35), “and you have become one of those who trouble me (בעכרי),” with those who trouble me. By necessity, we will adjudge it as one of the elliptical verses, [meaning: And the Almighty shall be the Judge over your adversaries. But if we can interpret it as profane, as an expression of a judge and of strength, the following is its interpretation:] And your fortifications (בצריך) shall be strong (שד-י), like “Then you shall make a fortification (בצר) on the earth.” That is, powerful wealth will be fortified, and according to the context, that is indeed its interpretation, as evidenced by the end of the verse, “and you shall have abundant (תועפות) silver,” an expression of strength, like (Num. 23:22), “his is like the power of (כתועפות) a wild ox.”



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Job 22:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:26:1)


**וְתִשָּׂא אֶל אֱלוֹהַּ פָּנֶיךָ.** לֹא תָבֹא לְהָרִים פָּנֶיךָ אֶלָּא לִשְׁאוֹל צְרָכֶיךָ הֵימֶנּוּ:


**and lift your countenance to God** You will not come to lift your face except to ask Him for your needs.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Job 22:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:27:1)


**וּנְדָרֶיךָ תְשַׁלֵּם.** מֻבְטָח אַתָּה, שֶׁיֵּרָצוּ קָרְבְּנוֹת תַּשְׁלוּמֵי נְדָרֶיךָ:


**and you will pay your wows** You will be confident that your sacrifices, the payments of your vows, will be accepted.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Job 22:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:28:1)


**וְיָקָם לָךְ.** כְּמוֹ וְיָקוּם לָךְ:


**and it will be accomplished for you** Heb. ויקם, lit. and it will rise, like וְיָקוּם לָךְ.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Job 22:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:29:1)


**כִּי הִשְׁפִּילוּ וַתֹּאמֶר גֵּוָה.** וְאִם רוֹאֲךָ דּוֹרֵךְ שָׁפָל, תֹּאמַר בְּהַבְטָחָה לְהַגְבִּיהוֹ, וְיִגְבַּהּ:


**If they are humble** If you see your generation humble, pledge that it be uplifted, and it will be uplifted.


###### Rashi on Job 22:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:29:2)


**וְשַׁח עֵינַיִם.** בַּצָּרָה הַבָּאָה (אֵלָיו) בַּעֲוֹנוֹ:


**a humble person** [One who is humbled] by the trouble that befalls [him] because of his iniquity,


###### Rashi on Job 22:29:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:29:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:29:3)


**יוֹשִׁיעַ.** אוֹתוֹ אֱלוֹהַּ בְּיָדְךָ וּבִתְפִלָּתְךָ:


God **will save** through your hands and through your prayer.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Job 22:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/22:30:1)


**יְמַלֵּט אִי נָקִי.** אֵין נָקִי, כְּמוֹ ״אִי כָבוֹד״ (שמואל א ד:כא) – אֵין כָּבוֹד, וְכֵן ״וּלְרוֹזְנִים אֵי שֵׁכָר״ (משלי לא:ד) – אֵין שֵׁכָר, אֵין נָאֶה לָהֶם שִׁכְרוּת. שְׁלָשְׁתָּן חִבְּרָם מְנַחֵם בְּמַחֲלֹקֶת אַחַת. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהֵן שְׁתֵּי תֵיבוֹת, מְפָרְשִׁין כֵּן: **וְנִמְלַט אֶת אִי נָקִי בְּבֹר כַּפֶּיךָ**, וְכֵן שָׁמַעְתִּי. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר שָׁמַעְתִּי, שֶׁהָיוּ אוֹמְרִים מִפִּי רַבִּי יַעֲקֹב, שֶׁהוּא לְשׁוֹן אָדָם הַשָּׁרוּי בְּצַעַר וּבַאֲנָקָה, וְהַיּוֹ״ד יְתֵרָה בַּתֵּיבָה, כְּמוֹ ״הַיֹּשְׁבִי בַשָּׁמָיִם״ (תהלים קכג:א). אֲבָל לֹא שָׁמַעְתִּי מִפִּי הָרַב:


**He shall deliver one who is not innocent** Heb. אי נקי, synonymous with אין נקִי, not innocent, like (I Sam. 4:21), “Ichabod (איכבוד),” meaning, there is no glory (אין כבוד). And so (Prov. 31:4), “neither is drunkenness for rulers (אי שכר),” there is no drunkenness (אין שכר). It does not befit them to become intoxicated. Menachem joined the three into one group (Machbereth Menachem p. 22). Although they are two words (?), they explain them thus. And that one who is not innocent will be delivered because of the purity of your hands. So I heard. I heard another version that was being said in the name of Rabbi Jacob: This is an expression of a man who lives in pain and sighing, and the “yud” is superfluous in the word, like (Ps. 123:1), “Who dwells (הישבי) in heaven.” But I did not hear this from the rabbi’s mouth.



## Chapter 23


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 23:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:2:1)


**(מענה איוב):** 
**גַּם הַיּוֹם.** אַחַר כָּל תַּנְחוּמִין הַלָּלוּ:


**Job’s Reply**
**Even today** after all these consolations.


###### Rashi on Job 23:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:2:2)


**מְרִי שִׂיחִי.** בִּמְקוֹמוֹ עוֹמֵד, כִּי אֵין תַּנְחוּם בְּדִבְרֵיכֶם:


**my speech is bitter** [The bitterness of my speech] remains in its place, for there is no consolation in your words.


###### Rashi on Job 23:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:2:3)


**יָדִי כָּבְדָה.** מַכַּת נִגְעִי תַּקִּיפָה יוֹתֵר מֵאַנְחָתִי; שֶׁאֵינִי קוֹבֵל וְצוֹעֵק לְפִי מַכָּתִי:


**my wound is heavier** The wound of my plague is stronger than my sigh, for I do not complain and cry about my wound.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 23:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:3:1)


**וְאֶמְצָאֵהוּ.** לְשׁוֹפְטִי:


**and I would find Him** My Judge.


###### Rashi on Job 23:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:3:2)


**תְּכוּנָתוֹ.** מְכוֹן כִּסְאוֹ:


**the place prepared for Him** The place prepared for His throne.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 23:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:4:1)


**מִשְׁפָּט.** הוֹכָחַת דְּבָרִים:


**case** [i.e., my] argument.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 23:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:5:1)


(**אֵדְעָה מִלִּים יַעֲנֵנִי.** וְגַם אִם יִסְתֹּם אוֹתִי מִלְּדַבֵּר לְפָנָיו, שֶׁמָּא יְדַבֵּר הוּא עִמִּי, **וְאֵדְעָה מִלִּים יַעֲנֵנִי** רַבִּי תַּנְחוּמָא (תנחומא, בראשית יג)):


**I would know the words that He would answer me** Even if He stops me from speaking before Him, perhaps He would speak with me and I would know the words He would answer me.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 23:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:6:1)


**הַבְּרָב כֹּחַ.** דְּבָרִים וְתוֹכָחוֹת שֶׁל חֲטָאָיו יָרִיב עִמָּדִי:


**with much power** of words and rebukes for His sins [other editions read: my sins] will He contend with me?


###### Rashi on Job 23:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:6:2)


**לֹא אַךְ.** מַכָּה וּכְפִיָּה. לֹא **יָשִׂים בִּי** אָכְפּוֹ וְאֵימָתוֹ. (וְעוֹד יִפָּתֵר:) **לֹא אַךְ הוּא יָשִׂים בִּי**, כִּי לֹא יָשִׂים בִּי אַךְ הָרִיב שֶׁל תּוֹכַחַת פְּשָׁעַי, וְלֹא תְשׂוּמוֹת עֲלִילוֹת:


**not...coercion** smiting and coercion. He will not cast His coercion and His awe upon me. (It may also be interpreted:) Nothing but [this] will He cast on me, for He will not cast on me anything but the contest of the rebukes for my sins; [that,] but not false accusations.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 23:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:7:1)


**שָׁם.** בִּמְקוֹם רִיבֵנוּ:


**There** in the place of our contest.


###### Rashi on Job 23:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:7:2)


**יָשָׁר.** כֹּשֶׁר מִפְעֲלוֹתַי יְהֵא נוֹכָח וְנִגְלֶה וְנִרְאֶה עִמּוֹ, לְפָנָיו:


**integrity** The propriety of my deeds will be proven, revealed, and it will appear with Him, i.e., before Him.


###### Rashi on Job 23:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:7:3)


**נוֹכָח.** מוּכָח, וְשָׁם **אֲפַלְּטָה לָנֶצַח** – כִּי לֹא יִמְצָא בִי עָוֹן:


**proven** and there I will extricate [myself] forever because He will find no iniquity in me.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 23:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:8:1)


**הֵן קֶדֶם.** לְמַעְלָה הוּא חוֹזֵר, לְ**מִי יִתֵּן יָדַעְתִּי וְגוֹ׳**:


**Behold...east** it refers to the above (verse 3), “Would that I knew etc.”


###### Rashi on Job 23:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:8:2)


**קֶדֶם.** מִזְרָח, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״קֵדְמָה מִזְרָחָה״ (שמות כז:יג):


**east** as it is stated (Exod. 27: 13): “on the east side (קדמה), eastward.”


###### Rashi on Job 23:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:8:3)


**אָחוֹר.** מַעֲרָב. הוֹאִיל וְהַמִּזְרָח – קִדְמַת עוֹלָם, נִמְצָא הַמַּעֲרָב – אֲחוֹרָיו שֶׁל עוֹלָם:


**and to the west** Heb. ואחור. Since the east is the front of the world (קדמה), the west is found to be the back of the world (אחור).



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Job 23:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:9:1)


**שְׂמֹאל.** צָפוֹן:


**north** Heb. שמאוֹל, lit. left.


###### Rashi on Job 23:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:9:2)


**בַּעֲשׂוֹתוֹ.** כְּשֶׁבְּרָאוֹ, לֹא עָשָׂה מְקוֹם כִּסְאוֹ שָׁם שֶׁאֶרְאֶנּוּ שָׁם:


**when He made it** When He created it, He did not make the place of His throne there, that I should see it there.


###### Rashi on Job 23:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:9:3)


**אָחַז.** תַּרְגּוּם שֶׁל ״אֶרְאֶה״ (בראשית כא:טז). וְתֵדַע, שֶׁהַטַּעַם תַּחַת הָאָלֶ״ף; וְאִם לְשׁוֹן אֲחִיזָה הוּא, הָיָה טַעֲמוֹ מִלְּרַע, תַּחַת הַחֵי״ת:


**see** Heb. אחז, the Aramaic translation of אֶרְאֶה, I will see. Proof of this is that the accent is under the “aleph.” Now, if it were an expression of holding (אחיזה), the accent would be at the end of the word, under the “cheth.”


###### Rashi on Job 23:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:9:4)


**יַעֲטֹף יָמִין.** כִּסָּה פְּנֵי הַדָּרוֹם בְּפָנָיו, לְמַעַן לֹא אֶרְאֶנּוּ:


**He wraps up the south** He covers the face of the south before me in order that I should not see Him.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 23:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:10:1)


**כִּי יָדַע דֶּרֶךְ וְגוֹ׳.** אֲשֶׁר **עִמָּדִי** מַהוּ, לְפִיכָךְ אֵינוֹ בָּא לָרִיב עִמָּדִי:


**Because He knows the way** that is **with me** therefore, He does not come to contend with me.


###### Rashi on Job 23:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:10:2)


**בְּחָנַנִי וְגוֹ׳.** אֲשֶׁר **כַּזָּהָב אֵצֵא** אִם יָרִיב עִמָּדִי:


**He has tested me** that **I will emerge like gold** if He contends with me.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 23:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:11:1)


**בְּאֲשׁוּרוֹ.** בְּמַעְגָּלוֹ, כְּמוֹ ״אִם תִּטֶּה אֲשׁוּרִי״ (איוב לא:ז), ״כּוֹנֵן אֲשׁוּרָיו״ (תהלים מ:ג):


**to His path** Heb. באשרו, to His path, like (below 31:7), “If my foot (אשרי) has turned away from the road”; (Ps. 40:3), “He has established my paths (אשרי).”


###### Rashi on Job 23:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:11:2)


**וְלֹא אָט.** אֵינִי רָגִיל לִנְטוֹת:


**and do not turn away** I am not accustomed to turn away.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 23:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:12:1)


**מֵחֻקִּי צָפַנְתִּי אִמְרֵי פִיו.** יוֹתֵר מֵחֻקֵּי מְזוֹנוֹתַי הָיִיתִי זָרִיז לִצְפֹּן אִמְרֵי פִיו, כְּמוֹ ״לֶחֶם חֻקִּי״ (משלי ל:ח) – מְזוֹנוֹתַי:


**more than my daily bread I kept the words of His mouth** More than my daily food I was eager to keep the words of His mouth. [The expression חקי] is like (Prov. 30:8), “my daily bread (לחם חקי),” my food.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 23:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:13:1)


**וְהוּא.** לְפִי שֶׁהוּא יָחִיד בָּעוֹלָם, וְיוֹדֵעַ דַּעְתָּן וּמַחֲשַׁבְתָּן שֶׁל בְּרִיּוֹת וּמַה יֵּשׁ לָהֶם לְדַבֵּר לְפָנָיו, לְפִיכָךְ תְּשׁוּבוֹת מוּכָנוֹת לְפָנָיו:


**But He** Since He is One in the world and knows the knowledge and the thoughts of the creatures, and what they have to reply to Him, therefore replies are prepared before Him.


###### Rashi on Job 23:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:13:2)


**וּמִי יְשִׁיבֶנּוּ.** לְפִיכָךְ **נַפְשׁוֹ אִוְּתָה וַיָּעַשׂ**:


**and who will answer Him** Therefore, what His soul desires, He does.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 23:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:14:1)


**כִּי יַשְׁלִים חֻקִּי.** יָדַעְתִּי כִּי לֹא יָשִׁיב יָדוֹ עַד אֲשֶׁר יַשְׁלִים חֹק גְּזֵרוֹתָיו – פֻּרְעָנִיּוֹת אֲשֶׁר גָּזַר עָלַי:


**For He will complete my sentence** I know that He will not draw back His hand until He completes the sentence of His decrees, the retribution that He has decreed against me.


###### Rashi on Job 23:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:14:2)


**וְכָהֵנָּה רַבּוֹת עִמּוֹ.** כִּי כָהֵנָּה רַבּוֹת עָשָׂה:


**and there are many such things with Him** For He did many such things.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 23:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:15:1)


**עַל כֵּן.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ גּוֹמֵל לָאָדָם כְּפִי דְרָכָיו:


**Therefore** Since He does not requite man according to his ways.


###### Rashi on Job 23:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:15:2)


**מִפָּנָיו אֶבָּהֵל.** וּכְשֶׁאֶתְבּוֹנֵן בִּדְרָכָיו, **אֶפְחַד מִמֶּנּוּ**:


**I am startled by Him** and when I ponder His ways, I am frightened of Him.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Job 23:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:17:1)


**כִּי לֹא נִצְמַתִּי מִפְּנֵי חֹשֶׁךְ.** בָּזֹאת אֲנִי נִבְהָל, כִּי לֹא נִצְמַתִּי לַחֲלוּטִין וּצְמִיתוּת קֶבֶר מִפְּנֵי הַחֹשֶׁךְ הַזֶּה הַבָּא עָלַי; וְכֵן לֹא כִסָּה מִפָּנַי אֶת הָאֹפֶל הַזֶּה עַד מוֹתִי:


**Because I was not cut off from before the darkness** Because of this I am startled; because I was not completely cut off and laid permanently in the grave before this darkness that has come upon me, and because He did not cover this thick darkness from before me until my death.


###### Rashi on Job 23:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/23:17:2)


**וּמִפָּנַי כִּסָּה אֹפֶל.** עַל כִּי לֹא שֶׁבְּרֹאשׁ הַפָּסוּק, חוֹזֵר:


**and [because He did not] cover the thick darkness from before me** This refers back to “because not” at the beginning of the verse.



## Chapter 24


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Job 24:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:1:1)


**מַדּוּעַ מִשַּׁדַּי לֹא נִצְפְּנוּ עִתִּים.** קַבָּלָה הוּא זֶה: לָמָּה לֹא נִצְפְּנוּ עִתֵּי יְמֵי הָאָדָם מִן הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, שֶׁלֹּא יֵדַע יוֹם מוֹת הָאָדָם? שֶׁמָּא לֹא יְמַהֵר פֻּרְעָנוּת, וִיקַדְּמֶנּוּ יוֹם הַמִּיתָה:


**Why were the times not hidden from the Almighty?** This is a plaint. Why were the times of man’s days not hidden from the Holy One, blessed be He, that He should not know the day of man’s death? Perhaps He would not hasten the retribution, and the day of death would precede it.


###### Rashi on Job 24:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:1:2)


**וְיוֹדְעָיו לֹא חָזוּ יָמָיו.** מִיּוֹם הָאָדָם, כְּמוֹ ״חֹדֶשׁ יָמִים״ (בראשית כט:יד). (ומְתַּרְגְּמִינָן ״עִדַן בְּעִדַן״ (סא״א)). דָּבָר אַחֵר: מַדּוּעַ יוֹדְעָיו וְאוֹהֲבָיו שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא אֵינָן יְכוֹלִין לִרְאוֹת סוֹף דְּרָכָיו וּמִדּוֹתָיו?


**And He Who knows him should not see his days** Of the day of man, like (Gen. 29:14), “a month of days (ימים),” (which is translated into Aramaic as “from time to time.”) [Another explanation is:] Why are those who are close and who love the Holy One, blessed be He, unable to see the end of His ways and His traits?


###### Rashi on Job 24:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:1:3)


**יָמָיו.** זוֹ הִיא דֶּרֶךְ שֶׁנָּהַג בְּיָמָיו, כְּמוֹ ״לֹא יֶחֱרַף לְבָבִי מִיָּמָי״ (איוב כז:ו). וּמָה הֵן הַמִּדּוֹת הַתְּמוּהוֹת?


This [word] ימיו, His days, denotes the way He acted in His time, like (below 27:6), “my heart will not reproach me because of my days.” [See Rashi ad loc.] Now what are the puzzling traits?



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 24:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:2:1)


**גְּבוּלוֹת יַשִּׂיגוּ.** אוֹתָן שֶׁדַּרְכָּן לְהַסִּיג גְּבוּלוֹת, שֶׁהֵן קוֹנִין שָׂדוֹת כְּנֶגֶד שָׂדוֹת אֲחֵרִים, וּבַלַּיְלָה הֵם מַרְבִּים גְּבוּלֵיהֶם, שֶׁהָיוּ עוֹשִׂים גְּדֵרֵיהֶם בַּלַּיְלָה בְּתוֹךְ תְּחוּם שְׁכֵינֵיהֶם. (וְזֶהוּ מַשִּׂיג דִּמְתַּרְגְּמִינָן ״מִשְׁגֶּה״ שֶׁהָיוּ מַשְׁגִּים הַגְּבוּל סא״א):


**[There are some who] remove the landmarks** Those who habitually remove the landmarks, who purchase fields adjoining the fields of others, and at night widen their [own] borders by placing their fences at night within the territory of their neighbors. (This is the meaning of removing the landmarks, which is translated into Aramaic as מְשַׁנֶּה, changing, because they would change the boundaries.) And they violently take away a flock and pasture it.


###### Rashi on Job 24:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:2:2)


**עֵדֶר גָּזְלוּ וַיִּרְעוּ.** לְעַצְמָן:



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 24:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:3:1)


**חֲמוֹר יְתוֹמִים יִנְהָגוּ.** וְעוֹשִׂין כָּל רָעוֹת הָאֲמוּרוֹת בָּעִנְיָן. סוֹף דָּבָר: **וֵאלוֹהַּ לֹא יָשִׂים תִּפְלָה** – לְהוֹדִיעָם עֲוֹנָם וּלְהַמְצִיאָם גְּמוּלָם:


**They lead away the orphan’s donkey** and commit all the evils stated in the chapter. The end of the matter is that “God does not impute it for unseemliness” (verse 12), to let them know their iniquity and to furnish them with their recompense.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 24:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:4:1)


**יַחַד חֻבְּאוּ.** עֲנִיֵּי אֶרֶץ מִפְּנֵי שֶׁיְּרֵאִים מֵהֶם:


**the poor of the land hide together** because they fear them.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 24:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:5:1)


**הֵן פְּרָאִים.** הַפְּרָאִים שֶׁהֵם לִמּוּדֵי מִדְבָּר (ירמיהו ב:כד), לֶאֱרֹב אֶת הָעוֹבְרִים:


**And he says: Behold, [as] wild donkeys in the desert** who are used to the desert, to lie in wait for the passersby.


###### Rashi on Job 24:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:5:2)


**יָצְאוּ בְּפָעֳלָם.** יָצְאוּ לִמְלַאכְתָּן, וּמַה הִיא הַפְּעֻלָּה? **מְשַׁחֲרֵי לַטָּרֶף.** מְבַקְּשִׁים לִטְרוֹף:


**they go out to their work** and what is the work? **seeking prey** They seek to prey.


###### Rashi on Job 24:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:5:3)


**עֲרָבָה לוֹ לֶחֶם לַנְּעָרִים.** לִמְשָׁרְתָיו וּנְעָרָיו שֶׁל רָשָׁע, הָעֲרָבָה וְהַמִּדְבָּר הָוֶה לוֹ לֶחֶם לְצָרְכָּם, שֶׁמִּשָּׁם הוּא חוֹטֵף וְאוֹכֵל וְגוֹזֵל מַה שֶּׁהֵם אוֹכְלִים:


**the wilderness yields bread for his young men** For the wicked man’s servants and young men, the wilderness and the desert supply food for their needs, for from there he seizes and eats and steals what they eat.


###### Rashi on Job 24:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:5:4)


**לַנְּעָרִים.** הֵם הַהוֹלְכִים אִתּוֹ לִגְזוֹל, כְּמוֹ ״רַק אֲשֶׁר אָכְלוּ הַנְּעָרִים״ (בראשית יד:כד):


**for...young men** They are the ones who go with him to rob, as in (Gen. 14:24), “only what the young men have eaten.”



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 24:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:6:1)


**בַּשָּׂדֶה.** שֶׁל אֲחֵרִים:


**In the field** of others.


###### Rashi on Job 24:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:6:2)


**בְּלִילוֹ.** יְבוּלוֹ:


**his produce** Heb. בְּלִילוֹ [like] יְבוּלוּ, his produce.


###### Rashi on Job 24:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:6:3)


**יִקְצוֹרוּ.** לִגְזוֹל:


**they reap** to steal it.


###### Rashi on Job 24:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:6:4)


**וְכֶרֶם רָשָׁע יְלַקֵּשׁוּ.** וּכְרָמִים שֶׁל אֲחֵרִים הָרְשָׁעִים הָאֵלּוּ נוֹטְלִים פִּרְיָם, **יְלַקֵּשׁוּ** אשפריימנ״ט נוֹטְלִים לֶקֶשׁ שֶׁלָּהֶם, לֶקֶשׁ לְשׁוֹן צֶמַח פְּרִי כְּמוֹ ״בִּתְחִלַּת עֲלוֹת הַלֶּקֶשׁ״ (עמוס ז:א) תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן צִימוּחַ וְכֵן יָסַד רַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר (בהושענא אדמה מארר) לֶקֶשׁ מֵאַרְבֶּה. (דָּבָר אַחֵר **וְכֶרֶם רָשָׁע יְלַקֵּשׁוּ**, יְלַקְּשׁוּ אוֹתָהּ מֵהַשְּׁחִיתָהּ, כִּי אֶת שֶׁל הָעֲנִיִּים וִיתוֹמִים הֵם אוֹכְלִים. **יְלַקֵּשׁוּ** כְּמוֹ ״וּפִיהֶם פָּעֲרוּ לְמַלְקוֹשׁ״ (איוב כט:כג) זְמַן אָרוֹךְ וּכְמוֹ ״לְקִישָׁיָּא לְלָבָן״ (אונקלוס בראשית ל:מב)):


**and the wicked gather the plants of the vineyard** Heb. יְלַקֵּשׁוּ. And [from] the vineyards of others these wicked men take their fruit; espreinment in Old French. They take their לֶקֶש. לֶקֶש is an expression of a growth of produce, as in (Amos 7:1), at the beginning of the shooting up of the grain, which Jonathan renders. צִמוּחַ, growing. And so did Rabbi Eleazar [Hakalir] establish (in the Hoshanah “Soil from curse”): produce לֶקֶש) from locusts. (Another interpretation: and they delay the vineyard of the wicked. They delay it from destruction, because they eat up [the vineyards] of the poor and the orphans. יְלַקֵּשׁוּ is like (below 29:23), “and they opened their mouth (למלקוש) for a long time,” and like (Gen. 30:42), “and the late ones (לקישיא) were Laban’s.”



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 24:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:7:1)


**עָרוֹם יָלִינוּ.** הֵם מְלִינִים אֶת הָעֲנִיִּים:


**They make them lie all night naked** They make the poor lie all night.


###### Rashi on Job 24:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:7:2)


**מִבְּלִי לְבוּשׁ.** שֶׁנָּטְלוּ כְּסוּיִן:


**without clothing and without a cover** because they took their cover.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 24:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:8:1)


**מִזֶּרֶם.** הַיּוֹרֵד מִן הֶהָרִים **יִרְטָבוּ** וְיִתְלַחְלְחוּ אוֹתָן הָעֲרוּמִים, לְפִי שֶׁ**מִבְּלִי כִסּוּי אֵין מַחְסֶה לְהֵחָסוֹת בּוֹ**:


**From the stream** that flows from the mountains, these naked people became wet and damp because they have no cover. They have no refuge under which to shelter themselves.


###### Rashi on Job 24:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:8:2)


**חִבְּקוּ צוּר.** נֶחְבָּאִים בִּנְקִיקֵי הַסְּלָעִים, וְגַם שָׁם הַזֶּרֶם יוֹרֵד וּמְקַלֵּחַ עֲלֵיהֶם וְרוֹטְבָם. רָטוֹב – תַּרְגּוּם שֶׁל ״לַח״ (בראשית ל:לז); וְכָל מִי שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְנֻגָּב קָרוּי ׳לַח׳:


**they embrace the rocks** They hide in the crevices in the rocks, and even there the stream flows down and sprays on them, soaking them. רָטוֹב is the Aramaic for לַח wet, and whoever is not dried is called לַח.


###### Rashi on Job 24:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:8:3)


**יִרְטָבוּ.** מוֹיִישְׁטָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**moist** (moite in Old French).



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Job 24:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:9:1)


**מִשֹּׁד יָתוֹם.** יְנִיקַת הַיָּתוֹם, מְקוֹם מוֹצָא מַאֲכָלוֹ וּמְזוֹנוֹתָיו. כְּמוֹ ״וְשֹׁד מְלָכִים תִּינָקִי״ (ישעיהו ס:טז):


**from the breast of the orphan** The sucking of the orphan, the source of his food and sustenance, as in (Isa. 60:16), “and the breast of kings you shall suck.”


###### Rashi on Job 24:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:9:2)


**יַחְבֹּלוּ.** לְשׁוֹן ״אִם חָבֹל תַּחְבֹּל״ (שמות כב:כה), וּכְמוֹ ״חֲבֹל לֹא יַחֲבֹל״ (יחזקאל יח:טז):


**they take a pledge** Heb. יחבלו, an expression of (Exod. 22:25), “If you take a pledge (חבל תחבל)”; (Ezek.18:16), “he did not take a pledge (חבל לא חבל).”



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 24:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:10:1)


**הָלְכוּ.** הֵם הִלְּכוּהוּ בְּלִי לְבוּשׁ:


**They made go** They made him go without clothing.


###### Rashi on Job 24:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:10:2)


**וּרְעֵבִים נָשְׂאוּ עֹמֶר.** וּמֵרְעֵבִים נָטְלוּ הָעֳמָרִים שֶׁל לֶקֶט. וְעוֹד יִפָּתֵר וּרְעֵבִים – אוֹתָם שֶׁהָיוּ נוֹשְׂאִים עֹמֶר, לְפִי שֶׁאֵלּוּ גּוֹזְלִים אוֹתָם מֵהֶם, וְהָיוּ נִשְׁאָרִים רְעֵבִים:


**and [from] the hungry they carried off the sheaves** And from the hungry they took the sheaves of gleaning [which are left for the poor]. It may also be interpreted thus: And those who were carrying the sheaves are hungry, because they were robbed of them and so they remain hungry.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 24:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:11:1)


**בֵּין שׁוּרוֹתָם יַצְהִירוּ.** וְיַעֲשׂוּ שֶׁמֶן, בַּעֲלֵי זֵיתִים:


**Between their rows they make oil** The owners of the olive trees make oil.


###### Rashi on Job 24:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:11:2)


**וִיקָבִים דָּרְכוּ.** בַּעֲלֵי כְּרָמִים:


**they tread the winepresses** The owners of the vineyards.


###### Rashi on Job 24:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:11:3)


**וַיִּצְמָאוּ.** לְאַחַר זְמַן; לְפִי שֶׁאֵלּוּ הָרְשָׁעִים גּוֹזְלִים אוֹתָם מֵהֶם:


**but they are thirsty** after a while, because these wicked men rob them of them [the wine].


###### Rashi on Job 24:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:11:4)


**בֵּין שׁוּרוֹתָם.** כִּמְדֻמֶּה לִי שֶׁעוֹשִׂין הַזֵּיתִים שׁוּרוֹת שׁוּרוֹת בְּבֵית הַבַּד, וְהַיִּצְהָר זָב בֵּינֵיהֶם:


**Between their rows** It seems to me that they pile up the olives in rows in the oil press and the oil flows between them.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 24:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:12:1)


**מֵעִיר מְתִים יִנְאָקוּ.** מְתֵיהָ וְטוֹבֵיהָ שֶׁל עִיר יִנָּאֲקוּ מֵחֲמַת אוֹתָן הָאוֹנְסִין:


**From the city people groan** The people and the dignitaries of the city groan because of those robbers.


###### Rashi on Job 24:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:12:2)


**לֹא יָשִׂים תִּפְלָה.** לְהַקְפִּיד עֲלֵיהֶם וְלִיקַּח נְקָמָה:


**does not impute it for unseemliness** to be strict with them and to take revenge.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 24:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:13:1)


**הֵמָּה.** הָרְשָׁעִים הָאֵלֶּה, הֵם דּוֹר הַמַּבּוּל שֶׁמָּרְדוּ בָּאוֹר, בִּשְׁבִיל הַמָּטָר שֶׁהָיָה עוֹלֶה מִן הָאָרֶץ (בראשית ב:ו), וְלֹא הָיוּ צְרִיכִים לִכְלוּם:


**They** These wicked people are the generation of the Flood, who rebelled because of אור, because of the rain; because it would ascend from the earth, they needed nothing.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 24:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:14:1)


**לָאוֹר יָקוּם.** אִם הָיוּ עוֹמְדִים בַּיּוֹם, הָיוּ הוֹרְגִים עוֹבְרֵי דְרָכִים:


**Should one rise in daylight** If they would rise by day, they would slay the wayfarers.


###### Rashi on Job 24:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:14:2)


**וּבַלַּיְלָה יְהִי כַגַּנָּב.** לַחְתּוֹר בָּתִּים:


**and at night he would be like a thief** to dig under houses.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 24:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:15:1)


**וְעֵין נוֹאֵף.** וְהַנּוֹאֵף שֶׁבָּהֶם הָיָה שׁוֹמֵר וּמַמְתִּין עַד **נֶשֶׁף, לֵאמֹר וְגוֹ׳**:


**And the eye of the adulterer** And the adulterer among them would wait until evening, saying...


###### Rashi on Job 24:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:15:2)


**וְסֵתֶר.** וּבַסֵּתֶר יָשִׂים פָּנָיו לַעֲבוֹר עֲבֵירָה, שֶׁעַל כָּל אֵלֶּה הָיוּ חֲשׁוּדִין דּוֹר הַמַּבּוּל: עַל הַגֶּזֶל, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וַתִּמָּלֵא הָאָרֶץ חָמָס״ (בראשית ו:יא), וְעַל הָעֲרָיוֹת, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״כִּי הִשְׁחִית כָּל בָּשָׂר״ (בראשית ו:יב):


**a mask** And he puts a mask on his face to commit a transgression, for the generation of the Flood was suspected of all these: of robbery, for it is stated (Gen. 6:11): “and the earth was filled with violence,” and of immorality, for it is stated (ibid. verse 12): “for all flesh had corrupted [its way on the earth].”



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 24:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:16:1)


**יוֹמָם חִתְּמוּ לָמוֹ.** אוֹתָם שֶׁהָיוּ חַלָּשִׁים מֵהֶם מֵעֲשׂוֹת לִסְטִיּוּת בַּיּוֹם, הָיוּ חוֹתְרִים בַּבָּתִּים בַּלַּיְלָה, וּבַיּוֹם הָיוּ חֲתוּמִים וּמְסֻגָּרִים בְּבָתֵּיהֶם:


**by day, they lock themselves in** Those who are weaker than they, too weak to commit robbery by day, would dig under houses at night; by day they were sealed and closed up in their houses.


###### Rashi on Job 24:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:16:2)


**לֹא יָדְעוּ אוֹר.** לֹא הָיוּ מַכִּירִין אֶת הָאוֹר, וְלֹא הָיָה עֵת מַהֲלָכָם:


**they know no light** They did not recognize the light because it was not [shining at] the time of their prowling.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Job 24:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:17:1)


**כִּי יַחְדָּו בֹּקֶר לָמוֹ צַלְמָוֶת.** שֶׁבַּיּוֹם הָיוּ נִסְגָּרִים, וּבַלַּיְלָה הָיוּ הוֹלְכִים לְמַחְתַּרְתָּם, וְלֹא הָיוּ יְרֵאִים מִן הַמַּזִּיקִים כְּלָל:


**For together morning is to them [as] the shadow of death** For by day they were locked in, and at night they would go to their tunnel, and they were not at all afraid of the demons.


###### Rashi on Job 24:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:17:2)


**כִּי יַכִּיר בַּלְהוֹת צַלְמָוֶת.** מַכִּיר הָיָה כָּל אֶחָד שֵׁדִים הַמַּבְהִילִים, וְלֹא הָיָה יָרֵא מֵהֶם. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּ (סנהדרין קט.): יוֹמָם חָתְמוּ לָמוֹ – בַּיּוֹם הָיוּ עוֹשִׂים סִימָנִים, הֵיכָן מַרְתֵּפוֹ שֶׁל בַּיִת וְהֵיכָן גְּנָזָיו שֶׁל עָשִׁיר; שֶׁהָיוּ מַפְקִידִין אֲפַרְסְמוֹן אֵצֶל בַּעֲלֵי בָתִּים עֲשִׁירִים, וְיוֹדְעִים הֵם שֶׁיַּנִּיחֶנּוּ בְּבֵית גְּנָזָיו, וּבַלַּיְלָה מְרִיחִין וְהוֹלְכִים שָׁם וְחוֹתְרִים שָׁם:


**for he recognizes the terrors of the shadow of death** Everyone could recognize the terrifying demons, and he did not fear them. Our Sages, however, explained: By day, they would seal for themselves—By day they would chart the location of the cellar of the house and a rich man’s treasurehouse, for they would deposit balsamum oil with wealthy householders, knowing that he (sic) would put it in his (sic) treasurehouse, and at night they would smell and go there and dig into there.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Job 24:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:18:1)


**קַל הוּא עַל פְּנֵי מַיִם.** כְּשֶׁהָיוּ הַצַּדִּיקִים נֹחַ וּמְתוּשֶׁלַח מוֹכִיחִים אוֹתָם, וְאוֹמְרִים לָהֶם שֶׁעָתִיד הַמַּבּוּל לָבֹא עֲלֵיהֶם, הָיוּ אוֹמְרִים: קַלִּים אָנוּ לָשׁוּט עַל הַמַּיִם, וְיֵשׁ לָנוּ גֹּמֶא וּדְבָרִים קַלִּים, לַעֲשׂוֹת מֵהֶם אֳנִיּוֹת וְלָשׁוּט:


**He is light upon the face of the water** When the righteous men, Noah and Methuselah, would rebuke them, they would say, “we are light [enough] to swim on the water, and we have papyrus and light materials from which to make boats and to float.”


###### Rashi on Job 24:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:18:2)


**תְּקֻלַּל חֶלְקָתָם בָּאָרֶץ לֹא יִפְנֶה דֶּרֶךְ כְּרָמִים.** הָיוּ מְקַלְּלִים אֶת חֶלְקָם בָּאָרֶץ לֵאמֹר לֹא יִפְנֶה דֶּרֶךְ הַטּוֹב בְּדַרְכֵי הַצַּדִּיקִים הָרָמִים וְשׁוֹכְנִים בְּרוּמוֹ שֶׁל עוֹלָם. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר **כְּרָמִים** הֵם הַצַּדִּיקִים וּפַרְנְסֵי הַדּוֹר, כְּמוֹ ״וְנָתַתִּי לָהּ אֶת כְּרָמֶיהָ מִשָּׁם״ (הושע ב:יז) תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן ״יַת פַּרְנָסָהָא״, הָיוּ מְקַלְּלִים אֶת חֶלְקָם בָּאָרֶץ לֵאמֹר לֹא יִפְנוּ דֶּרֶךְ הַטּוֹב נִשְׁבָּעִים שֶׁלֹּא יִפְנוּ אֶת דַּרְכָּם. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר **תְּקֻלַּל חֶלְקָתָם** מִתְקַלְקֶלֶת הָיְתָה חֶלְקָתָם שֶׁהָיוּ מַשְׁחִיתִים זַרְעָם לְבַטָּלָה, כְּמוֹ ״כִּי הִשְׁחִית כָּל בָּשָׂר אֶת דַּרְכּוֹ״ (בראשית ו:יב), **תְּקֻלַּל** לְשׁוֹן קִלְקוּל, כְּמוֹ ״בַּעֲוֹן אֲשֶׁר יָדַע כִּי מְקַלְלִים לָהֶם בָּנָיו״ (שמואל א ג:יג). לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר **תְּקֻלַּל חֶלְקָתָם** הַדּוֹרוֹת שֶׁאַחֲרֵיהֶם מְקַלְּלִים בָּהֶם מִי שֶׁפָּרַע מִדּוֹר הַמַּבּוּל, כָּךְ שָׁמַעְתִּי. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר **תְּקֻלַּל חֶלְקָתָם לֹא יִפְנֶה דֶּרֶךְ כְּרָמִים**, לֹא הָיוּ מְקַבְּלִים תּוֹכָחָה מִן הַטּוֹבִים. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר הָיוּ קַלִּים לָשׁוּט בִּסְפִינוֹת לַהֲרֹג וְלַחְמֹס מָמוֹן וְדֶרֶךְ יִשּׁוּב בְּנֵי אָדָם וּנְטִיעַת כְּרָמִים אֵין חוֹשֵׁשׁ לֵילֵךְ:


**their portion in the earth is cursed; he does not turn by the way of the vineyards** They would curse their portion in the earth, saying that they would not turn by the way of the good, in the way of the righteous, who are lofty and dwell in the height or the world. Vineyards represent the righteous men and the leaders of the generation, as in (Hosea 2:17), “And I will give her vineyards from there,” which Jonathan renders: her leaders. They would curse their portion in the earth, saying that they would not turn in the way of the good; they would swear that they would not turn their way. Another explanation: תקלל חלקתם בארץ Their portion in the earth was spoiled, for they would destroy their seed in vain, as in (Gen. 6:12), “for all flesh had corrupted its way.” תקלל is an expression of spoiling (קלקול), as in (I Sam. 3:13), “for the iniquity [of the matter] that he knew that his sons were bringing disgrace (מקללים) upon them.” Another explanation: Their portion shall be cursed—the following generations will curse by them: He who requited the generation of the Flood. So I heard. (Gen. Rabbah 30:2). Another explanation: **their portion is cursed; he does not turn by the way of the vineyards** They would not accept reproof from the good men. Another explanation: They were swift to sail in ships to kill and to plunder property, but in the way of the land that was inhabited by humans and planted with vineyards they did not care to go.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 24:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:19:1)


**צִיָּה גַם חֹם יִגְזְלוּ.** מִפְּנֵי רָעָתָם גָּרְמוּ לָהֶם שָׁנָה אַחַת לְהִשְׁתַּנּוֹת עוֹלָם מִשִּׁשָּׁה סְדָרָיו: ״זֶרַע וְקָצִיר וְקֹר וָחֹם וְקַיִץ וָחֹרֶף״ (בראשית ח:כב):


**Dryness and heat they steal away** Because of their evil, they caused for themselves that the world should change for one year from its six seasons: sowing, reaping, heat, cold, summer, and winter.


###### Rashi on Job 24:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:19:2)


**מִימֵי שֶׁלֶג.** כְּמוֹ ׳וּמִימֵי שֶׁלֶג׳, גַּם אוֹתָם גָּזְלוּ מִן הָעוֹלָם, לְפִי **שֶׁשְּׁאוֹל חָטָאוּ.** יֵשׁ תֵּיבוֹת חֲסֵרוֹת וָוִי״ן, כְּמוֹ ״וַאֲנִי כְּכֶבֶשׂ אַלּוּף״ (ירמיהו יא:יט), וְהוּא נִפְתָּר ׳כְּכֶבֶשׂ וְאַלּוּף׳. עוֹד הַרְבֵּה בְּמַחְבֶּרֶת מְנַחֵם בֶּן סָרוּק:


**snow water** Like: and snow water. This too they stole from the world, because they sinned so that they descended to the grave. In some words, the “vavin” are missing, like (Jer. 11:19), “And I was like a lamb, a bull,” which is interpreted as, a lamb and a bull. There are many more in Machbereth Menachem ben Saruk (p. 26).



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 24:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:20:1)


**יִשְׁכָּחֵהוּ רֶחֶם.** אִמּוֹ. דָּרַשׁ רַבִּי תַּנְחוּם (תנחומא, נח ז), שֶׁהָיוּ דּוֹר הַמַּבּוּל נוֹטְלִים אֶת בְּנֵיהֶם וְנוֹתְנִים אוֹתָם תַּחְתֵּיהֶם, לִסְתֹּם אֲרֻבּוֹת הַתְּהוֹם; וַאֲפִלּוּ אִמּוֹ לֹא הָיְתָה מְרַחֶמֶת עָלָיו:


**The womb forgets him** [i.e., the womb of] his mother. Rabbi Tanchum expounded that the generation of the Flood would take their children and place them underneath themselves in order to stop up the windows of the deep, and even his mother would have no mercy on him.


###### Rashi on Job 24:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:20:2)


**מְתָקוֹ רִמָּה.** לָמוּת וְלֶאֱכוֹל רִמָּה בְּשָׂרָם:


**he is sweet to the worms** to die, and for the worms to eat their (sic) flesh.


###### Rashi on Job 24:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:20:3)


**עוֹד לֹא יִזָּכֵר.** בְּשָׁנָה אַחַת נֶעֶקְרוּ. וְעַל אַרְכָּא שֶׁנִּיתַּן לָהֶם מִתְּחִילָּה, הָיָה אִיּוֹב קוֹבֵל; שֶׁהַרְבֵּה מֵתוּ וְלֹא רָאוּ בְּפוּרְעָנוּת זֶה:


**he will no longer be remembered** In one year they were [to be] uprooted, but Job was complaining about the reprieve that was given them [the wicked] from the start, for many died and did not see this retribution.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Job 24:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:21:1)


**רוֹעֶה עֲקָרָה לֹא תֵלֵד.** כָּךְ הָיָה מִנְהָגָם: נוֹשֵׂא שְׁתֵּי נָשִׁים, אַחַת לְתַשְׁמִישׁ וְאַחַת לְבָנִים. זוֹ שֶׁעוֹמֶדֶת לְתַשְׁמִישׁ, מַשְׁקֶה כּוֹס עִקָּרִין כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא תֵלֵד עוֹלָמִית, וּמַאֲכִילָהּ תַּפְנוּקִים וּמַרְחִיצָהּ וּמְקַשְּׁטָהּ, כְּדֵי שֶׁתְּהֵא נָאָה. וְזוֹ הָעוֹמֶדֶת לְבָנִים, מַלְבִּישָׁהּ בִּגְדֵי אַלְמָנוֹת וּמַרְעִיבָהּ. וְזֶהוּ שֶׁאוֹמֵר: **הָעֲקָרָה שֶׁלֹּא תֵלֵד** הָיָה **רוֹעֶה** וּמֵטִיב, וְאֶת **הָאַלְמָנָה** שֶׁעוֹמֶדֶת לְבָנִים **לֹא יְיֵטִיב**:


**He feeds the barren woman who will not bear** so was their custom: One would marry two wives, one for coitus and one for children. The one intended for coitus he would give a potion of roots to drink in order that she should never give birth, and he would feed her delicacies, bathe her, and adorn her, so that she should appear beautiful. The one intended for children he would dress in widow’s garments and starve. This is the meaning of his statement that the barren woman who will not give birth he would feed and adorn, but the “widow,” who was intended for children, he would not adorn.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Job 24:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:22:1)


**וּמָשַׁךְ.** הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא אוֹתָן אַבִּירִים בְּכֹחוֹ לְפוּרְעָנוּת וּלְמִחְיָה, מִתּוֹךְ אַרְכָּא שֶׁהֶאֱרִיךְ לָהֶם:


**And He draws** The Holy One, blessed be He, [draws] those mighty men with His strength to retribution and to sustenance by the reprieve that He granted them.


###### Rashi on Job 24:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:22:2)


**יָקוּם.** יוֹם אֶחָד:


**he rises** one day.


###### Rashi on Job 24:22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:22:3)


**וְלֹא יַאֲמִין בַּחַיִּין.** כְּמוֹ בַּחַיִּים: ״בַּיּוֹם הַזֶּה נִבְקְעוּ כָּל וְגוֹ׳״ (בראשית ז:יא):


**and does not believe in his life** Heb. בחיין, like בחיים ; (Gen. 7:11): “on that day all the fountains of the deep were torn apart etc.”



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Job 24:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:23:1)


**יִתֵּן לוֹ לָבֶטַח.** עַד יוֹם אֵידָם, וְנִשְׁעָנוּ:


**He grants him safety** until the day of their catastrophe and they rely [on it].



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Job 24:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:24:1)


**רוֹמּוּ מְעַט.** נִסְתַּלְּקוּ מִן הָעוֹלָם כִּמְעַט רֶגַע:


**They are taken away in a second** They are taken away from the world in a fleeting second.


###### Rashi on Job 24:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:24:2)


**וְהֻמְּכוּ.** נִדְכְּאוּ וְנִתְמַסְכְּנוּ:


**and they are crushed** crushed and impoverished.


###### Rashi on Job 24:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:24:3)


**כְּכֹל יִקָּפְצוּן.** כִּשְׁאָר כָּל הַנִּקְפָּצִים וְנִמְלָטִים בְּעֵת קָצִיר וּבָצִיר וּמָסִיק, שֶׁנִּקְפָּצִים יַחַד, כֵּן תַּמּוּ כְרֶגַע:


**like all they are gathered in** Like all things that are gathered in and taken away at the time of the harvest, vintage, and olive harvest; just as those are gathered in, so were they destroyed instantly.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Job 24:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:25:1)


**וְאִם לֹא אֵפוֹ.** כַּאֲשֶׁר אָמַרְתִּי, יָבֹא אֶחָד מִכֶּם וְיַכְזִיבֵנִי:


**And if it is not so, then** [If it is not] as I said, let one of you come and prove me a liar.


###### Rashi on Job 24:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/24:25:2)


**וְיָשֵׂם לְאַל מִלָּתִי.** שֶׁאֲנִי מְהַרְהֵר אַחֲרָיו לָדַעַת מִדּוֹתָיו, שֶׁמַּאֲרִיךְ אַף לָרְשָׁעִים. וְאֶת אוֹהֲבָיו, כְּמוֹנִי הוּא מְיַסֵּר וְאֵינוֹ מְמִיתָן; כְּמוֹ שֶׁקּוֹבֵל ״כִּי לֹא נִצְמַתִּי מִפְּנֵי חֹשֶׁךְ״ (איוב כג:יז). וְאֵלּוּ שֶׁ״גְּבוּלוֹת יַשִּׂיגוּ״ (איוב כד:ב), וְכָל אֵלֶּה עָשׂוּ, לֹא הֻכּוּ בְּיִסּוּרִין:


**and make my words as nought** [That is, my words] with which I criticize Him to know His standards, that He is slow to anger with the wicked, but He chastises those who love Him as I do, and He does not put them to death, as he complains above (23:17): “Because I was not cut off from before the darkness,” but those who remove the landmarks and commit all these [crimes] were not smitten with suffering.



## Chapter 25


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 25:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/25:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/25:2:1)


**(מענה בלדד)**: 
**הַמְשֵׁל וָפַחַד עִמּוֹ.** כְּלַפֵּי שֶׁאָמַר אִיּוֹב ״אֶעֶרְכָה לְפָנָיו מִשְׁפָּט״ (איוב כג:ד), אָמַר לוֹ: **הַמְשֵׁל וָפַחַד עִמּוֹ, הַמְשֵׁל** זֶה מִיכָאֵל, **וָפַחַד** זֶה גַּבְרִיאֵל. כְּלוּם אַתָּה יָכוֹל לְהָשִׁיב אֶת אֶחָד מֵהֶם?


**Bildad’s reply**
**Dominion and fear** Directed towards what Job said (23:4), “I would set out my case before Him,” he says to him, “Dominion and fear are with Him.” “Dominion” refers to Michael, and “fear” refers to Gabriel. Can you answer even one of them?


###### Rashi on Job 25:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/25:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/25:2:2)


**עוֹשֶׂה שָׁלוֹם בִּמְרוֹמָיו.** כְּשֶׁהַמַּזָּלוֹת עוֹלִים, כָּל אֶחָד סוֹבֵר: אֲנִי עוֹלֶה רִאשׁוֹן; לְפִי שֶׁאֵינוֹ רוֹאֶה מַה שֶּׁלְּפָנָיו, לְפִיכָךְ אֵינוֹ מִתְקַנֵּא. כָּךְ שָׁמַעְתִּי (תנחומא בובר, איוב). לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: **עוֹשֶׂה שָׁלוֹם בִּמְרוֹמָיו.** אֵשׁ וּמַיִם בְּלוּלִין, וְאֵין מַיִם מְכַבִּין אֶת הָאֵשׁ. כָּךְ שָׁמַעְתִּי:


**He makes peace in His heights** When the constellations ascend, each one thinks, “I will ascend first,” and because he does not see what is before him, he is not envious. So I heard. [Tanchuma Buber ad loc.] Another explanation: He makes peace in His heights—Fire and water are mixed, and the water does not extinguish the fire; I did not hear this.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 25:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/25:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/25:4:1)


**וּמַה יִּצְדַּק וְגוֹ׳.** יְלוּד אִשָּׁה שֶׁאָמַרְתָּ: ״בְּחָנֵנִי כַּזָּהָב אֵצֵא״ (איוב כג:י):


**How then can man be just...** that you said (23:10), “He has tested me that I will emerge like gold.”



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 25:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/25:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/25:5:1)


**עַד יָרֵחַ.** כְּמוֹ ״וְלֹא עָדָה עָלָיו שָׁחַל״ (איוב כח:ח):


**He removes the moon** Heb. עד, like (28:8), “neither did a lion pass (עדה) over it.” The meaning is that even the moon will be removed from its [source of] light in the future and it will not shine, as it is written (Isa. 24:23): “And the moon shall be ashamed etc.” And the stars will not be pure in His eyes in the future. How much less can man, who is a worm, be just with God!


###### Rashi on Job 25:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/25:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/25:5:2)


**וְלֹא יַאֲהִיל.** כְּמוֹ ״לֹא יָהֵלּוּ אוֹרָם״ (ישעיהו יג:י), וְאָלֶ״ף זוֹ בָּאָה כְּמוֹ ״וְלַאֲדִיב אֶת נַפְשֶׁךָ״ (שמואל א ב:לג) – אָלֶ״ף יְתֵרָה. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: **לֹא יַאֲהִיל** – לֹא יִהְיֶה לוֹ צֵל, כְּשֶׁאֵינוֹ מֵאִיר אֵין לוֹ צֵל. וְהָרִאשׁוֹן נִרְאֶה, וּשְׁנֵיהֶם שָׁמַעְתִּי:


**and will not shine** Heb. יאהיל, like (Isa. 13:10), “shall not allow their light to illuminate (יהלו),” and this “alef” serves the same function as (I Sam. 2:33), “and to sadden (ולאדיב) your heart,” in which the “alef” is superfluous. Another explanation: יַאֲהִיל, you will not have shadow. When it does not shine, no shadows are cast. I prefer the former, but I heard both.



## Chapter 26


### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 26:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:4:1)


**(מענה איוב):** 
**וְנִשְׁמַת מִי יָצְאָה מִמֶּךָּ.** מִי שָׂם בְּךָ הָרוּחַ הַזֶּה שֶׁיָּצָא מִפִּיךָ? מִי לֹא יָדַע זֹאת?


**Job’s Reply**
**and whose breath emerged from you?** Who put into you this breath that emerged from your mouth? Who does not know this?



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 26:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:5:1)


**הָרְפָאִים.** גֵּיהִנֹּם הַמְּרַפֶּה אֶת הַבְּרִיּוֹת:


**Gehinnom** Gehinnom, which weakens the creatures.


###### Rashi on Job 26:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:5:2)


**יְחוֹלָלוּ.** שִׁבְעָה חֲלָלִים שֶׁלּוֹ:


**is hollow** Its seven hollows.


###### Rashi on Job 26:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:5:3)


**מִתַּחַת מַיִם וְשֹׁכְנֵיהֶם:** בְּעִמְקֵי עֲמָקִים, וְגַם הוּא:


**beneath the water and its denizens** in the deepest of the depths, but that too...



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 26:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:6:1)


**עָרוֹם שְׁאוֹל נֶגְדּוֹ.** לָדַעַת וְלִרְאוֹת אֶת כָּל מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכוֹ:


**Sheol is naked before Him** to know and to see all that is within it.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 26:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:7:1)


**עַל בְּלִימָה.** אֵין כְּלוּם בַּיְּסוֹד, כִּי הֵם עוֹמְדִים בָּאֲוִיר עַל חֹזֶק זְרוֹעוֹתָיו שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא. וְדוּנַשׁ פֵּרֵשׁ בְּלִימָה – מִישׁוֹר; כְּמוֹ ״בְּמֶתֶג וָרֶסֶן עֶדְיוֹ לִבְלוֹם״ (תהלים לב:ט). (כָּךְ רָאִיתִי סא״א.)



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 26:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:8:1)


**וְלֹא נִבְקַע עָנָן.** מֵעוֹלָם, שֶׁיִּפְּלוּ מֵימָיו בְּיַחַד:


**and the cloud does not split** It never [split] so that its water [i.e., rain] should fall together.


###### Rashi on Job 26:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:8:2)


**תַּחְתָּם.** תַּחַת הַמַּיִם:


**beneath it** Beneath the water.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Job 26:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:9:1)


**מְאַחֵז.** בִּמְחִיצוֹת פְּנֵי כִסֵּא הַכָּבוֹד כֻּלּוֹ, כְּגוֹן ״יָשֶׁת חֹשֶׁךְ סִתְרוֹ״ (תהלים יח:יב):


**He closes in** with walls the face of His throne of glory, like (Ps. 18:12), “He made darkness His secret place.”


###### Rashi on Job 26:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:9:2)


**וּפַרְשֵׁז.** עַל הַכִּסֵּא עֲנָנוֹ, כְּמוֹ וּדְמוּת עַל רָאשֵׁי הַחַיּוֹת רָקִיעַ (יחזקאל א:כב):


**He spreads** over the throne His cloud (Ezek. 1:22), “And there was a likeness over the heads of the living creatures, of an expanse.”



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 26:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:10:1)


**חוֹק חָג.** קוֹמְפָּ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז, לְשׁוֹן **״וּבַמְּחוּגָה יְתָאֲרֵהוּ״** (ישעיהו מד:יג). סִבְּבוֹ בְּחוֹל, לִהְיוֹת לוֹ (הַחוֹל) חֹק – גְּבוּל עוֹלָם. וְלֹא יַעֲבֹר הַיָּם אוֹתוֹ חוּג, עַד שֶׁיִּכְלֶה אוֹר וְחֹשֶׁךְ:


**He encircled a boundary** (compas in French), an expression of (Isa. 44: 13), “and with a compass (ובמחוגה) he rounds it.” [He surrounded it with sand for the sand to be a circle for it. Does not appear in all editions.] The word חֹק denotes a perpetual boundary. The sea will not cross that circle until light and dark come to an end.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 26:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:11:1)


**יְרוֹפָפוּ.** כְּשֶׁבְּרָאָם וְהִקְרִישָׁם הָיוּ רוֹפְפִים, וְגָעַר בָּהֶם וְעָמְדוּ בְּיֹבֶשׁ וּבְחֹזֶק:


**trembled** when He created them, and He caused them to congeal. They were trembling, and He rebuked them, and they stood dry and strong.


###### Rashi on Job 26:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:11:2)


**וְיִתְמְהוּ.** תּוֹהֶה וְעוֹלֶה (ס״א: כְּאָדָם תּוֹהֶה וְעוֹמֵד) בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד:


**astonished** (wondering and ascending. Other editions: wondering and standing) in one place.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 26:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:12:1)


**רָגַע הַיָּם.** כְּמוֹ ״עוֹרִי רָגַע״ (איוב ז:ה), נִקְמַט; קְמָטִים קְמָטִים נַעֲשׂוּ, (כָּךְ כְּשֶׁאָמַר ״יִקָּווּ הַמַּיִם״ נֶאֶסְפוּ מִפְּשִׁיטָתָן וְנִקְמְטוּ קְמָטִים קְמָטִים) עַד שֶׁנִּקְווּ לְמָקוֹם אֶחָד הַמּוּכָן לָהֶם (בראשית א:ט):


**the sea wrinkled** Like (above 7:5), “my skin wrinkled.” Many wrinkles were formed. (So, when He said (Gen. 1:9), “Let the waters...gather,” they were gathered from their straightness and were wrinkled into many wrinkles) until they gathered into one place, which was prepared for them.


###### Rashi on Job 26:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:12:2)


**מָחַץ רָהַב.** מִצְרַיִם קְרוּיִין רְהָבִים (ישעיהו ל:ז):


**He smote Rahab** The Egyptians, who are called רהב, haughty.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 26:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:13:1)


**בְּרוּחוֹ שָׁמַיִם שִׁפְרָה.** בִּדְבָרָיו וּבְרוּחַ פִּיו אִיהֵל, כְּמוֹ ״וְנָטָה אֹהֶל שַׁפְרִירוֹ״ (ירמיה מג:י):


**By His breath He made the heavens a tent** Heb. שפרה. With His words and with the breath of His mouth, He made a tent, like (Jer. 43:10), “and he shall spread his royal pavilion (שפרירו).”


###### Rashi on Job 26:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:13:2)


**חוֹלְלָה יָדוֹ.** אֶת פַּרְעֹה הַנִּקְרָא ״נָחָשׁ בָּרִיחַ״ (ישעיהו כז:א). לְשׁוֹן חִיל וְצָרָה. וְכֵן בְּמָקוֹם אַחֵר ״הֲלֹא זֹאת הִיא הַמַּחְצֶבֶת רַהַב מְחוֹלֶלֶת תַּנִּין״ (ישעיהו נא:ט). לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: יִסְּדָה יָדוֹ אֶת לִוְיָתָן, כְּמוֹ ״גְּבָעוֹת חוֹלָלְתִּי״ (משלי ח:כה):


**His hand caused pain** to Pharaoh, who was called the barlike serpent (Isa. 26:1). חללה is an expression of pain and trouble. Likewise, elsewhere (ibid. 51:9): “are you not the one that hewed Rahab and slew (מחוללת), the sea monster?” Another explanation: His hand founded the leviathan, as in (Prov. 8: 25), “before the hills I was created (חוללתי).”



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 26:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:14:1)


**קְצוֹת דְּרָכָיו.** קַלּוֹת וּמוּעָטוֹת, לְפִי שְׁאָר גְּדוֹלוֹת שֶׁבּוֹ:


**the outskirts of His ways** The easiest and the smallest in comparison to His other qualities.


###### Rashi on Job 26:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/26:14:2)


**וּמַה שֵּׁמֶץ דָּבָר נִשְׁמַע בּוֹ.** מָה רֶמֶז דָּבָר אָדָם יָכוֹל לְהָבִין מִפְּעֻלּוֹתָיו. דָּבָר אַחֵר: שֵׁמֶץ כְּמוֹ ״לְשִׁמְצָה״ (שמות לב:כה), לִגְנוּת:


**and what implications of any matter can be understood** What hint of anything can a man understand from His deeds? Another explanation: שֶמֶץ is like (Exod. 32:25), “to the scandal (לשמצה),” uncomplimentary.



## Chapter 27


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Job 27:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:1:1)


**וַיֹּסֶף.** עָמְדוּ רֵיעָיו מֵעֲנוֹת, כַּאֲשֶׁר הוֹכִיחָם אֱלִיהוּא ״כִּי עָמְדוּ וְלֹא עָנוּ עוֹד״ (איוב לב:טז):


**Then Job again** His friends refrained from answering as Elihu reprimanded them, for they stood and replied no more (32:3).



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 27:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:2:1)


**חַי אֵל.** שְׁבוּעָה הִיא, כִּי בֶּאֱמֶת **הֵסִיר אֶת מִשְׁפָּטִי.** וּמִכָּאן דָּרַשׁ רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ (סוטה כז:), שֶׁמֵּאַהֲבָה עָבַד אִיּוֹב אֶת הַמָּקוֹם: אֵין אָדָם נוֹדֵר בְּחַיֵּי הַמֶּלֶךְ אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן אוֹהֵב אֶת הַמֶּלֶךְ:


**As God lives** I swear that indeed He took away my right. From here Rabbi Joshua derived that Job served the Omnipresent out of love. No one swears by the king’s life unless he loves the king.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 27:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:5:1)


**אִם אַצְדִּיק אֶתְכֶם.** אִם אוֹמַר: הַדִּין עִמָּכֶם לְחַיְּבֵנִי:


**that I should justify you** That I should say that you are right in condemning me.


###### Rashi on Job 27:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:5:2)


**לֹא אָסִיר תֻּמָּתִי.** לֹא אוֹדֶה לְדִבְרֵיכֶם, לוֹמַר שֶׁאֵינִי תָּם:


**I will not give up my innocence** I will not concede to your words and say that I am not innocent.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 27:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:6:1)


**בְּצִדְקָתִי הֶחֱזַקְתִּי.** לוֹמַר שֶׁאֲנִי צַדִּיק:


**I have maintained my righteousness** To say that I am righteous.


###### Rashi on Job 27:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:6:2)


**לֹא יֶחֱרַף לְבָבִי.** אוֹתִי מִיָּמַי – מִכָּל דֶּרֶךְ וּמִדָּה שֶׁנָּהַגְתִּי בְּיָמַי, וְעַל כָּרְחוֹ יְהִי כְּרָשָׁע אוֹיְבִי:


**my heart will not reproach** me because of my days, i.e., because of any manner or trait with which I deported myself in my days. Therefore, perforce, my enemy shall be like a wicked man.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 27:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:8:1)


**כִּי מַה תִּקְוַת חָנֵף.** כִּי לָמָּה אֶהְיֶה רָשָׁע וְגַזְלָן? מַה סוֹפוֹ שֶׁל רָשָׁע הַגּוֹזֵל מָמוֹן?


**For what is the hope of a flatterer** For why should I be a wicked man and a robber? What is the end of a wicked man who takes money by force?


###### Rashi on Job 27:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:8:2)


**כִּי יִשַּׁל.** כִּי יַשְׁלִיךְ הַמָּקוֹם אֶת נַפְשׁוֹ מֵעָלָיו, לְשׁוֹן ״שַׁל נַעַלְךָ״ (שמות ג:ה) ״וְנָשַׁל הַבַּרְזֶל״ (דברים יט:ה):


**when God casts off** Heb. ישל. When the Omnipresent casts his soul off him. This is an expression of (Exod. 3:5), “Remove (של) your shoes”; (Deut. 19:5), “and the iron [head] slips off (ונשל).”



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 27:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:10:1)


**אִם עַל שַׁדַּי יִתְעַנָּג.** כֶּפֶל הַתְּמִיהָה הִיא, כְּכָל הַתְּמִיהוֹת הַכְּפוּלוֹת; שֶׁכֵּן דֶּרֶךְ הָרִאשׁוֹנָה בְּה״א וְהַשְּׁנִיָּה בְּאִם, כְּמוֹ ״הֲיְרֻצוּן בַּסֶּלַע סוּסִים אִם יַחֲרוֹשׁ בַּבְּקָרִים״ (עמוס ו:יב), ״הֲיֵשׁ בִּלְשׁוֹנִי עַוְלָה וְאִם חִכִּי לֹא יָבִין הַוּוֹת״ (איוב ו:ל). אַף כָּאן: **הַצַּעֲקָתוֹ יִשְׁמַע אֵל אִם עַל שַׁדַּי יִתְעַנָּג**?! שֶׁמָּא, אוֹ מִי שֶׁהוּא רָשָׁע חָנֵף, יָכוֹל הוּא לְהִתְעַנֵּג וְלִסְמֹךְ עַל יְשׁוּעַת הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, וְלִקְרֹא אֵלָיו בְּעֵת צָרָתוֹ?! בִּתְמִיהָה:


**Or will he delight with the Almighty** The double interrogative is like all double interrogatives. the first one is customarily prefixed by a “hey” and the second one by אִם ; e.g., (Amos 6:12), “Will horses run (הירצון) on the rock, or (אִם) will one plow with cattle?” (Job 6:30), “Is there (היש) injustice in my tongue, or (אִם) will my palate not understand wickedness?” Here, too, will God hearken to his cry...or will he delight with the Almighty? Will one who is a wicked man or a flatterer perhaps be able to delight and rely on the salvations of the Holy One, blessed be He, and call to Him in time of trouble? This is a question.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 27:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:11:1)


**אוֹרֶה אֶתְכֶם.** מָה הַמִּדָּה שֶׁבְּיַד אֵל, וְדֶרֶךְ **אֲשֶׁר עִם שַׁדַּי לֹא אֲכַחֵד.** כְּמוֹ ״כִּי יָדַע דֶּרֶךְ עִמָּדִי״ (איוב כג:י):


**I will instruct you** what the standard is that is in God’s hand, and the way... **what is with the Almighty I will not conceal** Like (above 23:10), “Because He knows the way that is with me.”



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 27:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:12:1)


**כֻּלְּכֶם חֲזִיתֶם.** שֶׁזֶּה חֵלֶק אָדָם רָשָׁע:


**all of you have seen** that this is the portion of a wicked man.


###### Rashi on Job 27:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:12:2)


**וְלָמָּה זֶּה הֶבֶל תֶּהְבָּלוּ.** לִהְיוֹת רְשָׁעִים וַחֲנֵפִים:


**now why do you become altogether vain?** To be wicked and hypocritical.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 27:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:14:1)


**אִם יִרְבּוּ בָנָיו לְמוֹ חָרֶב.** יִרְבּוּ וְיָמוּתוּ בְּיִסּוּרִים וְתַחֲלוּאִים, עַד כִּי יִשְׂמְחוּ אַלְמְנוֹתָיו בְּמִיתָתָן, וְלֹא תִבְכֶּינָה:


**If his sons increase** they will increase for the sword. **in death** They will die amidst suffering and sicknesses, until his widows will be happy at their death and will not weep.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Job 27:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:17:1)


(**וְכֶסֶף נָקִי.** כַּסְפּוֹ שֶׁל רָשָׁע לְנָקִי יְחַלְּקֶנּוּ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא):


**and He will distribute [his] money to the innocent** The Holy One, blessed be He, will distribute the wicked man’s money to an innocent one.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Job 27:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:18:1)


**בָּנָה כְעָשׁ.** כְּתוֹלַע הַבּוֹנֶה חוֹר שֶׁאֵינוֹ מִתְקַיֵּם, וּכְסֻכָּה שֶׁעָשָׂה נוֹצֵר הַתְּאֵנָה (משלי כז:יח) וְהַכֶּרֶם, שֶׁאֵינָהּ שֶׁל קַיָּמָא. (עִנְיָן אַחֵר: נוֹצֵר הַכֶּרֶם – שֶׁאֵינוֹ שׁוֹמְרָהּ בְּכָל עֵת. זֶה שָׁמַעְתִּי (סא״א).)


**He builds...as a moth** As a moth, who builds his hole which does not endure, and like a booth that the watchman of the fig tree and the vineyard makes, which is not permanent. (Another explanation: that the watchman of the vineyard, who does not watch it at all times, made. I heard this. Absent in certain editions.)



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 27:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:19:1)


**עָשִׁיר יִשְׁכַּב.** יָמוּת בְּעָשְׁרוֹ, וּפְעָמִים שֶׁלֹּא יֵאָסֵף לִקְבוּרָה:


**He dies rich** He will die with his riches, but sometimes he will not be gathered in to burial.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 27:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:20:1)


**בַּלָּהוֹת.** שֵׁדִים:


**Terrors** Demons.


###### Rashi on Job 27:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:20:2)


**גְּנָבַתּוּ סוּפָה.** וְתַשְׁלִיכֵהוּ לִדְרָאוֹנִים:


**a tempest snatches him away** and casts him away in disgrace.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Job 27:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:22:1)


**וְיַשְׁלֵךְ עָלָיו.** הַמַּשְׁלִיךְ עָלָיו רָעָה, וְלֹא יַחְמֹל:


**And he will cast upon him** i.e., the one who casts evil upon him will show no compassion.


###### Rashi on Job 27:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:22:2)


**מִיָּדוֹ.** שֶׁל רָשָׁע וּמִכֹּחוֹ וּמִמְּקוֹמוֹ:


**from his hand** of the wicked man, and from his strength. from his place.


###### Rashi on Job 27:22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:22:3)


**בְּרוּחַ יִבְרָח.** בֶּן עֲצָתוֹ שֶׁהָיָה מְסַיְּעוֹ בַּתְּחִלָּה, יִבְרַח מֵעֲזוֹר יָדוֹ בִּשְׁעַת כִּשְׁלוֹנוֹ. כְּמוֹ שֶׁעָשָׂה חַרְבוֹנָה לְהָמָן (מגילה טז.):


**he will flee** His counselor, who assisted him from the start, will flee from assisting him at the time of his downfall, as Charbonah did to Haman (Esther 7:9).



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Job 27:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/27:23:1)


**יִשְׂפֹּק עָלֵימוֹ כַפֵּימוֹ.** הַיּוֹדְעִים (אוֹתוֹ) לְפָנִים, וְעַתָּה רוֹאִים בְּשִׁבְרוֹ:


**They clap their hands over them** Those who knew [him] long ago and now see his ruin.



## Chapter 28


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Job 28:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:1:1)


**כִּי יֵשׁ לַכֶּסֶף מוֹצָא.** גַּם זֶה טַעַם אַחֵר לִדְבָרוֹ הָרִאשׁוֹן, שֶׁאָמַר ״בְּצִדְקָתִי הֶחֱזַקְתִּי״ (איוב כז:ו): כִּי לָמָּה אֶהְיֶה רָשָׁע, אִם בִּשְׁבִיל כֶּסֶף וְזָהָב – לַכֹּל יֵשׁ מוֹצָא וְסוֹף, אֲבָל **הַחָכְמָה מֵאַיִן תָּבוֹא** – זֶה סוֹף הַפָּרָשָׁה – הִיא יְקָרָה מִכֹּל. עַל כֵּן כָּל יָמַי נָתַתִּי לִבִּי עָלֶיהָ לִלְמֹד:


**For silver has a mine** This, too, is another reason for his first statement that he said (27:6), “I have maintained my righteousness,” for why should I be wicked? If for silver or gold, each one has a source and an end, but from where does wisdom come? This is the end of the chapter; that is the most precious of all. Therefore, all my life, I set my heart on studying it.


###### Rashi on Job 28:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:1:2)


**וּמָקוֹם.** יֵשׁ לַזָּהָב, שֶׁמִּשָּׁם יָזוֹקּוּ וְיַתִּיכוּ אוֹתוֹ:


**and...a place** Gold has [a place] whence they refine and smelt it.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 28:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:2:1)


**וְאֶבֶן יָצוּק נְחוּשָׁה.** מִן הָהָר יָצַק הָאוּמָּן אֶת הַנְּחוּשָׁה; הֲרֵי תְּחִלָּה וָסוֹף יֵשׁ לְכָל דָּבָר, שֶׁכָּל הָעוֹלָם יִכְלֶה, וְכָל אֲשֶׁר בּוֹ:


**and copper is smelted from stone** From the mountain the smith smelts the copper. Hence, everything has a beginning and an end, for the whole world will end with all that is therein.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 28:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:3:1)


**קֵץ שָׂם.** הַמָּקוֹם מָתַי תִּהְיֶה הַחֹשֶׁךְ, שֶׁהַשָּׁמַיִם נִמְלָחוּ וְהָאָרֶץ תִּבְלֶה (ישעיהו נא:ו), וּלְכָל תַּכְלִית דָּבָר – הוּא חוֹקֵר לוֹ:


**He made an end** The Omnipresent [made an end] when there will be darkness, for the heavens shall vanish and the earth shall rot away, and He fathoms the end of everything.


###### Rashi on Job 28:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:3:2)


**אֶבֶן.** אֲשֶׁר מִשָּׁם יָצָא לוֹ חֹשֶׁךְ וְצַלְמָוֶת. אֶבֶן מְקוֹם מוֹצָא פּוּרְעָנוּת. לָכֵן נִקְרָא אֶבֶן בִּמְקוֹם פּוּרְעָנוּת, כְּאָדָם הַנּוֹגֵף אֶת רַגְלָיו בָּאֶבֶן, שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ צַעַר. כָּךְ שָׁמַעְתִּי. כְּמוֹ ״קַו תֹּהוּ וְאַבְנֵי בֹהוּ״ (ישעיהו לד:יא), ״דְּמִן טוּרָא אִתְגְּזֶרֶת אֶבֶן וְגוֹמֵר״ (דניאל ב:מה):


**a stone** A stone whence darkness and the shadow of death emanate. A stone is the place of the source of trouble. For this reason, a place of trouble is called a stone, like a man who dashes his foot against a stone, who suffers pain. So I heard. This is like (Isa. 34:11), “a line of waste and stones of destruction”; (Dan. 2:45), “that from the mountain a stone was cut.”



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 28:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:4:1)


**פָּרַץ נַחַל.** עַל סְדוֹם וַעֲמוֹרָה נַחֲלֵי אֵשׁ וְגָפְרִית (בראשית יט:כד):


**A stream burst forth** on Sodom and Gemorrah, streams of fire and brimstone.


###### Rashi on Job 28:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:4:2)


**מֵעִם גָּר.** מִמָּקוֹם שֶׁהוּא נוֹבֵעַ וְנוֹגֵר:


**from the place of its flow** Heb. מעם גר, from the place from which it gushes and flows (נוגר).


###### Rashi on Job 28:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:4:3)


**הַנִּשְׁכָּחִים מִנִּי רָגֶל.** אֵלּוּ אַנְשֵׁי סְדוֹם, שֶׁשִּׁכְּחוּ תּוֹרַת רַגְלֵי אוֹרְחִים מֵאַרְצָם. שֶׁהָיוּ מַשְׁכִּיבִים הָאוֹרֵחַ בְּמִטָּה; אִם הָיָה אָרוֹךְ יוֹתֵר, מְקַטְּעִין רַגְלָיו, וְאִם הָיָה קָצָר, מַאֲרִיכִין אוֹתוֹ. כָּךְ מְפֹרָשׁ בָּאַגָּדָה (סנהדרין קט:):


**those who cause the foot to be forgotten** These are the people of Sodom, who caused the feet of wayfarers to be forgotten from their land, for they would lay the wayfarer in a bed. If he was too long, they would cut off his feet, and if he was too short, they would stretch him. In this manner it is explained in the Aggadah.


###### Rashi on Job 28:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:4:4)


**דַּלּוּ.** עַל יְדֵי פְּרִיצַת נַחַל זֶה, וּמֵאֱנוֹשׁ נָעוּ:


**they are lifted up** through the bursting forth of the stream, and they are moved away from man.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 28:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:5:1)


**אֶרֶץ.** אֲשֶׁר מִמֶּנָּה יֵצֵא לֶחֶם נֶהְפַּךְ מְקוֹמָהּ כְּמוֹ אֵשׁ:


**A land—that from [it] bread emerged** its place was as though turned into fire.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 28:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:6:1)


**מְקוֹם סַפִּיר.** הָיוּ אֲבָנֶיהָ:


**a place of sapphire** its stones were.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 28:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:7:1)


**לֹא יְדָעוֹ עָיִט.** לֹא עָבַר עֲלֵיהֶן חַיִל שֶׁל לִיסְטִין:


**A path unknown to brigands** Heb. עיט. A band of robbers never passed by them.


###### Rashi on Job 28:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:7:2)


**וְלֹא שְׁזָפַתּוּ עֵין אַיָּה.** לְפִי שֶׁהִיא רוֹאָה יוֹתֵר מִשְּׁאָר עוֹפוֹת, וּלְכָךְ נִקְרְאָה רָאָה (דברים יד:יג), שֶׁהִיא עוֹמֶדֶת בְּבָבֶל וְרוֹאָה נְבֵלוֹת בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל. כָּךְ מְפֹרָשׁ בְּאַגָּדַת (חולין סג.). כְּלוֹמַר: לֹא חֲפָרוּהָ מְרַגְּלִים:


**and which the eye of a vulture did not see** Since it sees more than other birds, and is therefore called רָאָה, because it stands in Babylon and sees a carcass in Eretz Israel. That is how it is explained in the Aggadah of Hullin (632); i.e., spies never discerned it.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 28:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:8:1)


**לֹא עָדָה עָלָיו.** עָבַר, כְּדִמְתַרְגְּמִינָן ״אֲשֶׁר עָבַר בֵּין הַבְּתָרִים״ (בראשית טו:יז): ״דַּעֲדָא״ (תרגום אונקלוס):


**neither did... pass by it** Heb. עדה, passed, as we translate (Gen. 15 17), “which passed between these parts,” דִּעֲדָה.


###### Rashi on Job 28:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:8:2)


**שַׁחַץ וְשָׁחַל.** מִשִּׁבְעָה שֵׁמוֹת הָאַרְיֵה הֵם (סנהדרין צה.). שִׁשָּׁה שֵׁמוֹת יֵשׁ לָאַרְיֵה, וְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מָה עָשָׂה:


**Proud beasts** Heb. (שַחַץ) and lion (שַחַל) are some of the seven names of the lion (Sanh. 95a: The lion has seven names.) Now what did the Holy One, blessed be He, do?...



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Job 28:9:1
