[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:9:1)


**בַּחַלָּמִישׁ שָׁלַח יָדוֹ.** וְהָפַךְ שָׁרְשֵׁי הֶהָרִים מִלְמַעְלָה לְמַטָּה:


**upon the flinty rock** and turned over the roots of the mountains from top to bottom.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 28:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:10:1)


**בַּצּוּרוֹת יְאֹרִים בִּקֵּעַ.** חוֹזֵר לִתְחִלַּת עִנְיָנוֹ שֶׁאָמַר כִּי לְכָל דָּבָר יֵשׁ מוֹצָא, וּבַצּוּרוֹת בִּקֵּעַ לְמוֹצָא הַיְאוֹרִים וְכֵן **כָּל יְקָר רָאֲתָה עֵינוֹ.** מֵהֵיכָן יָצָא לַבְּרִיּוֹת (כְּמוֹ שֶׁעָשָׂה לְיִשְׂרָאֵל ״הִכָּה צוּר וַיָּזוּבוּ מַיִם״ (תהלים עח:כ)):


**In the rocks, He split rivers** He refers back to the beginning of his topic, that he said that everything has a source. He split the rocks for the source of the rivers, and so, “His eye saw every precious thing,” whence it would emerge for the creatures, [as He did for Israel; He struck a rock and its water flowed.]



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 28:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:11:1)


**מִבְּכִי נְהָרוֹת חִבֵּשׁ.** בִּבְרִיאַת הָעוֹלָם, כְּשֶׁבָּכוּ מַיִם הַתַּחְתּוֹנִים. **בְּכִי** כְּמוֹ ״נִבְכֵי יָם״ (איוב לח:טז):


**He bound up the depths of the rivers** Heb. מבכי. In the Creation of the world, when the lower waters wept (בכו). **the depths** Heb. מבכי, like (below 38:16), “the depths of (נבכי) the sea.”


###### Rashi on Job 28:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:11:2)


**חָבַשׁ.** כְּמוֹ ״וַיַּחֲבֹשׁ אֶת־חֲמֹרוֹ״ (בראשית כב:ג). כָּךְ שָׁמַעְתִּי. [ס״א: **מִבְּכִי וְגוֹ׳.** מִפְּנֵי בְּכִיָּתָן שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל, שֶׁהָיוּ בוֹכִין ״לֵאמֹר מַה נִּשְׁתֶּה״ (שמות טו:כד), חָבַשׁ וְתִקֵּן לָהֶם נַהֲרֵי מַיִם מִן הַסֶּלַע; ״וַיַּחֲבֹשׁ לָהֶם״ (ויקרא ח:יג) – מְתַרְגֵּם: ״וְאַתְקֵין״ (תרגום אונקלוס).]


**bound up** Heb. חבש, like (Gen. 22:3), “and saddled (ויחבש) his donkey.” I did not hear this. [Other editions: **Because of the weeping etc.** Because of the weeping of Israel, who were weeping, saying, “What will we drink,” He bound up and fixed for them water from the rock. (Lev. 8:13),”And he fixed hats (ויחבש) for them,” is translated into Aramaic as ואתקין.]


###### Rashi on Job 28:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:11:3)


**וְתַעֲלֻמָהּ יוֹצִיא אוֹר.** חַלּוֹן יֵשׁ בָּרָקִיעַ וּשְׁמוֹ תַּעֲלוּמָה, מִשָּׁם תּוֹלְדוֹת הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ לִתְקוּפָתוֹ (פרקי רבי אליעזר ו). כְּלוֹמַר: לְכָל דָּבָר יֵשׁ מוֹצָא וְסוֹף, רַק לַחָכְמָה – לֹא מוֹצָא לָהּ, אֶלָּא מִפִּיו, וְלֹא סוֹף עוֹלָמוֹת:


**and [from] Taalumah He brings out light** There is a window in the firmament, named Taalumah, from which the sun develops in its orbit [in Pirke d’Rabbi Eliezer] (ch. 6). That is to say that everything has a source and an end; only wisdom has no source except from His mouth, and never an end.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 28:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:12:1)


**וְהַחָכְמָה.** הַתּוֹרָה:


**But wisdom** i.e., Torah.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 28:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:13:1)


**וְלֹא תִמָּצֵא בְּאֶרֶץ הַחַיִּים.** בְּמִי שֶׁמְּחַיֶּה, אֶלָּא בְּמִי שֶׁמֵּמִית עַצְמוֹ עָלֶיהָ בִּיגִיעָה וּבְרָעָבוֹן:


**and it will not be found in the land of the living** with one who enlivens himself, but with one who “puts himself to death” with toil and hunger.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 28:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:14:1)


**תְּהוֹם אָמַר לֹא בִי הִיא.** אִם תִּשְׁאָלֶנָּה לְיוֹרְדֵי תְהוֹם הָעוֹסְקִים בְּמוֹצָא מַרְגָּלִיּוֹת וּבְמוֹצָא זָהָב וָכֶסֶף מִמְּקוֹם תְּהוֹמוֹת הָאָרֶץ, יֹאמְרוּ לְךָ: **לֹא בִי הִיא.** אֵינִי בָּקִי בְּהוֹרָאָה (שבת פט.. עיין שם היטב וצריך עיון):


**The deep says, “It is not in me,”** If you ask those who dive into the deep, who deal with pearls, with the source of pearls and with gold and silver mines from the place of the depths of the earth, they will tell you, “It is not in me.” I am not well-versed in halachic instruction.


###### Rashi on Job 28:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:14:2)


**וְיָם אָמַר.** וְיוֹרְדֵי הַיָּם לִסְחוֹרָה יֹאמְרוּ לְךָ **לֹא עִמָּדִי חָכְמָה.** לָמָּה? לְפִי שֶׁאֵינָם יְכוֹלִים לִקְנוֹתָהּ כִּשְׁאָר פְּרַקְמַטְיָא, לָתֵת מֵעָשְׁרָם בְּדָמֶיהָ. וּבָאַגָּדָה הוּא אוֹמֵר (שבת פט.), שֶׁבִּשְׁעַת מַתַּן תּוֹרָה בָּא הַשָּׂטָן לִפְנֵי הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא וְאָמַר לוֹ: הֵיכָן תּוֹרָה? אָמַר לוֹ: אֵצֶל בֶּן עַמְרָם. בָּא לוֹ אֵצֶל מֹשֶׁה, אָמַר לוֹ: הֵיכָן תּוֹרָה? אֵצֶל יָם. בָּא לוֹ אֵצֶל יָם, אָמַר לוֹ: הֵיכָן תּוֹרָה? **לֹא בִי הִיא**; לֵךְ אֵצֶל בֶּן עַמְרָם. בָּא לוֹ אֵצֶל בֶּן עַמְרָם, אָמַר לוֹ: הֵיכָן תּוֹרָה? אֵצֶל תְּהוֹם. בָּא לוֹ אֵצֶל תְּהוֹם, אָמַר לוֹ: הֵיכָן תּוֹרָה? אָמַר לוֹ: **אֵין עִמָּדִי**; לֵךְ אֵצֶל בֶּן עַמְרָם. בָּא לוֹ אֵצֶל בֶּן עַמְרָם, אָמַר לוֹ: הֵיכָן תּוֹרָה? אֵצֶל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא. וְזֶהוּ שֶׁאָמַר: **תְּהוֹם אָמַר לֹא בִי הִיא וְיָם אָמַר אֵין עִמָּדִי**:


**and the sea says** And those who go down to the sea for business will say to you, “Wisdom is not with me.” Why? Because they cannot purchase it like other merchandise, to give of their wealth for its price. And in the Aggadah (see Shabbath 89a), it is stated that at the time of the giving of the Torah, Satan came before the Holy One, blessed be He, and said to Him, “Where is the Torah?” He replied, “With the son of Amram.” He came to Moses, and said to him, “Where is the Torah?” “With the sea.” He came to the sea and asked it, “Where is the Torah?” “It is not in me. Go to the son of Amram.” He came to the son of Amram and said to him, “Where is the Torah?” “With the deep.” He came to the deep and asked, “Where is the Torah?” It replied, “It is not with me. Go to the son of Amram.” He came to the son of Amram and asked, “Where is the Torah?” “With the Holy One, blessed be He.” This is what the deep says, “It is not in me,” and the sea says, “It is not with me.”



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 28:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:15:1)


**לֹא יֻתַּן סְגוֹר.** ״זָהָב סָגוּר״ (מלכים א ו:כ) זֶהוּ זָהָב טוֹב, שֶׁבְּשָׁעָה שֶׁנִּפְתָּח, כָּל חֲנֻיּוֹת שֶׁל זָהָב נִסְגָּרוֹת:


**Fine gold cannot be given** Heb. סְגוּר. This is synonymous with זהב סגוּר, lit. closed gold. This is fine gold, for when it is open, all gold establishments close.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 28:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:16:1)


**לֹא תְסֻלֶּה.** לֹא תִשְׁתַּבַּח, לְשׁוֹן ״סֹלּוּ לָרֹכֵב בָּעֲרָבוֹת״ (תהלים סח:ה). הַמְשַׁבְּחָהּ לוֹמַר: חֲשׁוּבָה הִיא כְּכֶתֶם אוֹפִיר וּכְשֹׁהַם יָקָר – לֹא שֶׁבַח הוּא לָהּ, וְכֵן ״הַמְסֻלָּאִים בַּפָּז״ (איכה ד:ב):


**It cannot be praised** Heb. תסלה, an expression of (Ps. 68:5), “Extol Him (סלו) Who rides in Aravoth.” He who praises it saying, “It is as esteemed as the gold of Ophir and as precious onyx,”—this is no praise for it. Similar to this is (Lam. 4:2), “praised (המסלאים) with fine gold.”



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Job 28:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:17:1)


**זָהָב.** טָהוֹר, מַנְהִיר כְּמַרְגָּלִית:


**gold** Pure gold that shines like pearls.


###### Rashi on Job 28:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:17:2)


**וּתְמוּרָתָהּ וְגוֹ׳.** וּתְמוּרָתָהּ לֹא בִּכְלִי פָז:


**its exchange** is not a pure golden vessel.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Job 28:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:18:1)


**רָאמוֹת וְגָבִישׁ.** שֵׁמוֹת אֲבָנִים טוֹבוֹת שֶׁבַּיָּם הֵם:


**Coral and crystal** They are names of precious stones in the sea.


###### Rashi on Job 28:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:18:2)


**לֹא יִזָּכֵר.** אֶצְלָהּ:


**cannot be mentioned** in comparison to it.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 28:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:19:1)


**פִּטְדַת כּוּשׁ.** אֶבֶן טוֹבָה הִיא, כְּמוֹ ״אֹדֶם פִּטְדָה״ (שמות כח:יז). **כֶּתֶם** הִיא קְבוּצַת עֲדִי לְתַכְשִׁיטֵי נָשִׁים, וְנִקְרֵאת הַקְּבוּצָה כֶּתֶם בִּלְשׁוֹן עִבְרִי, וּבִלְשׁוֹן עֲרָבִי אַלְחַלוּ, וְהוּא ״נֶזֶם זָהָב וַחֲלִי כָתֶם״ (משלי כה:יב). וְכֵן ״וַתַּעַד נִזְמָהּ וְחֶלְיָתָהּ״ (הושע ב:טו). וְיֵשׁ פּוֹתְרִים כֶּתֶם – כֶּתֶר, וְלֹא נָכוֹן. כִּי לֹא מָצִינוּ פְּעֻלָּה בְּלָשׁוֹן זֶה, כַּאֲשֶׁר מָצִינוּ בִּלְשׁוֹן כֶּתֶר: מַכְתִּיר, כּוֹתָרוֹת:


**The topaz of Kush** It is a precious stone, as in (Exod. 28:17), “ruby, topaz (פטדה).” **with...jewelry** Heb. בכתם. That is a collection of jewelry for women’s adornments, and this collection is called כֶּתֶם in Hebrew, and “al halls” in Arabic. That is (Prov. 25:12) “Like a golden earring and jewelry of the finest gold (וחלי כתם),” and similarly (Hosea 2:15), “and adorned herself with her earrings and her jewelry (וחליתה).” Others interpret כֶּתֶם like כֶּתֶר, a crown, but it is incorrect because we do not find a verb with this root, as we find with כֶּתֶר. מַכְתִּיר, to crown or surround, כותרות capitals.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Job 28:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:21:1)


**וּמֵעוֹף הַשָּׁמָיִם.** מַלְאָכִים הַמְעוֹפְפִים:


**and from the winged creatures of the heavens** The angels who fly.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Job 28:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:22:1)


**אֲבַדּוֹן וָמָוֶת אָמְרוּ.** הַמְּאַבְּדִין וּמְמִיתִין עַצְמָן עָלֶיהָ אָמְרוּ: **בְּאָזְנֵינוּ שָׁמַעְנוּ שִׁמְעָהּ**, שֶׁהַמִּיגֵּעַ בָּהּ, מִתְקַיֶּמֶת בּוֹ. וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים, כַּאֲשֶׁר בָּאַגָּדָה אֲשֶׁר פֵּרַשְׁתִּי לְמַעְלָה, שֶׁבָּא הַשָּׂטָן לִפְנֵי הַמָּקוֹם, אָמַר לוֹ: הֵיכָן תּוֹרָה? אָמַר לוֹ וְכוּ׳. וְהַיְינוּ דִּכְתִיב: **אֲבַדּוֹן וָמָוֶת אָמְרוּ בְּאָזְנֵינוּ שָׁמַעְנוּ שִׁמְעָהּ.** כְּשֶׁנִּתְּנָה לְיִשְׂרָאֵל. אֲבָל:


**Destruction and death said** Those who destroy themselves and kill themselves over it said, “With our ears we heard its report that if one toils with it, it endures with him.” And others say as in the Aggadah that I explained above, that Satan came before the Omnipresent and asked Him, “Where is the Torah?” He said to him etc. That is what is written, “Destruction and death said, ‘With our ears we heard its report that it was given to Israel.’ But...”



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Job 28:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:23:1)


**אֱלֹהִים הֵבִין דַּרְכָּהּ וְהוּא יָדַע אֶת מְקוֹמָהּ.** שֶׁיּוֹדֵעַ הוּא הֵיכָן שׁוֹרָה. וְגַם יְשַׁבְּחוּהָ וְיֹאמְרוּ לָהּ, כִּי **אֱלֹהִים הֵבִין דַּרְכָּהּ.** נִסְתַּכֵּל בָּהּ וּבָרָא אֶת הָעוֹלָם, בְּאוֹתִיּוֹתֶיהָ כְּסִדְרָם וּמִשְׁקָלָן יָצַר כָּל הַיְּצוּרִים; כַּאֲשֶׁר כָּתוּב בְּסוֹד סֵפֶר יְצִירָה:


**God understood its way, and He knew its place** For He knows where it is situated, and they will also praise it and say of it that God understood its way; He looked at it and created the world with its letters in their order and according to their weight. He created all creatures as it is written in the secret of Sefer Yetzirah.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Job 28:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:24:1)


**כִּי הוּא לִקְצוֹת הָאָרֶץ יַבִּיט.** בָּהּ הֵיאַךְ יִבְנֵם וְיִבְרָאֵם, **וְתַחַת כָּל הַשָּׁמַיִם** צָרְכֵי בְּרִיָּתָן רָאָה בָּהּ:


**For He looks to the ends of the earth** how He should build them and create them, and under all the heavens He saw in it the needs of their creation.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Job 28:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:25:1)


**לַעֲשׂוֹת וְגוֹ׳.** בָּהּ מִשְׁקָל לָרוּחַ, לְכָל אֶרֶץ לְפִי כֹּחָהּ. וְכֵן הַמַּיִם לְפִי הַמִּדָּה, לְפִי מַה שֶּׁהָיְתָה אֶרֶץ צְרִיכָה לְהַשְׁקוֹתָהּ. יֵשׁ אֶרֶץ נְגוּבָה, וּצְרִיכָה הַרְבֵּה גְּשָׁמִים, וְיֵשׁ אֶרֶץ שֶׁאֵינָהּ צְרִיכָה כָּל כָּךְ:


**To make** with it a weight for the wind, for each land according to its strength, and so the water according to its measure, according to what each land required to water it. There is an arid land which requires much rain, and there is a land that does not require so much.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Job 28:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:26:1)


**חֹק.** גְּזֵרָה קְצוּבָה לְפִי כָּל אֲרָצוֹת, וּמַעְיָן וּפֶלֶג לְהַשְׁקוֹת כָּל אֶרֶץ וָאֶרֶץ. וּ**בַעֲשׂוֹתוֹ דֶּרֶךְ** לְכָל הַחֲזִיזִים, שֶׁלֹּא יֵצְאוּ שְׁנֵי קוֹלוֹת דֶּרֶךְ נֶקֶב אֶחָד, פֶּן יַחֲרִיב אֶת הָעוֹלָם:


**a decree** A set decree according to all the lands, and a spring and rivulet to water each land by His making a way for all the clouds, so that two thunders do not emerge through one hole, lest it destroy the world.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Job 28:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:27:1)


**אָז רָאָה.** רָאָה אוֹתָהּ, נִסְתַּכֵּל בָּהּ, וּבַעֲצָתָהּ יַעֲשֶׂה הַכֹּל:


**Then He saw** He saw it, looked at it, and with its counsel made everything.


###### Rashi on Job 28:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:27:2)


**וַיְסַפְּרָהּ.** סִפֵּר אוֹתִיּוֹתֶיהָ: כְּפוּלוֹת וּפְשׁוּטוֹת; רִאשׁוֹנָה וְאֶמְצָעִית וְאַחֲרוֹנִית – הִיא ׳אֱמֶת׳, חוֹתָמוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא; וְכֵן בִּשְׁאָר סְדָרִים בָּרָא כָּל דָּבָר וְדָבָר בָּאוֹתִיּוֹת הַלָּלוּ. וְהַכֹּל מְפֹרָשׁ בְּסוֹד סֵפֶר ׳יְצִירָה׳:


**and counted it** He counted its letters, the double ones [such as בּב] and the simple ones, the first one (א), middle one (מ), and the final one (ת). אֶמֶת, is the seal of the Holy One, blessed be He. And so with the remaining orders He created everything with these letters. Everything is explained in the secret of Sefer Yetzirah.


###### Rashi on Job 28:27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:27:3)


**הֱכִינָהּ.** זִמְּנָהּ לִיצִירָה:


**prepared it** He prepared it [wisdom] for the Creation.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Job 28:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/28:28:1)


**הֵן יִרְאַת אֲדֹנָי הִיא חָכְמָה.** זוֹ צְרִיכָה לָזוֹ, וְאֵין חָכְמָה יָפָה בְּלֹא יִרְאָה:


**Behold, the fear of the Lord is wisdom** One requires the other, and wisdom is unseemly without [the] fear [of the Lord].



## Chapter 29


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 29:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:2:1)


**(מענה איוב):**
**מִי יִתְּנֵנִי כְיַרְחֵי קֶדֶם.** כְּיָמִים קַדְמוֹנִים, שֶׁהָיִיתִי בִּגְדֻלָּתִי:


**Would that I were as [in] my early months** Like the early days, when I was in my greatness.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 29:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:3:1)


**בְּהִלּוֹ נֵרוֹ.** לְשׁוֹן אוֹר וְשִׂמְחָה:


**when He lit His candle** [This is] an expression of light and joy.


###### Rashi on Job 29:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:3:2)


**לְאוֹרוֹ.** הָיִיתִי הוֹלֵךְ; בְּכָל חֹשֶׁךְ וְצָרָה שֶׁהָיְתָה בָּאָה לָעוֹלָם, לִי הָיָה אוֹר:


**by His light** I would go. In every occasion of darkness and trouble that came to the world, I had light.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 29:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:4:1)


**בִּימֵי חָרְפִּי.** בִּימֵי קַדְמוּתִי, לָשׁוֹן אֲרַמִּי הוּא: ״חַרְפֵּי וְאַפְּלֵי״ (נידה סה:):


**in my early days** Heb. חרפי, in my early days. This is an Aramaism, early crops and late crops (חרפי ואפלי).


###### Rashi on Job 29:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:4:2)


**בְּסוֹד אֱלוֹהַּ עֲלֵי אָהֳלִי.** כְּשֶׁהָיוּ כְּשֵׁרֵי הַדּוֹר בָּאִים לְאָהֳלִי לְהִוָּסֵד בְּדִבְרֵי הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, בַּתּוֹרָה וּבְ׳גֶדֶר סְיָג לְשָׁעָה׳:


**when God’s counsel was upon my tent** When the righteous of the generation would come to my tent, to take counsel in the words of the Holy One, blessed be He, in the Torah and in enacting temporary safeguards.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 29:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:5:1)


**נְעָרָי.** מְשָׁרְתַי:


**my young men** My servants.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 29:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:6:1)


**הֲלִיכַי.** רַגְלַי:


**my feet** Heb. הליכי, my feet.


###### Rashi on Job 29:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:6:2)


**בְּחֵמָה.** כְּמוֹ בְּחֶמְאָה:


**with cream** Heb. בְּחֵמָה, like בחמאה.


###### Rashi on Job 29:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:6:3)


**וְצוּר יָצוּק עִמָּדִי פַּלְגֵי שָׁמֶן.** כְּלוֹמַר: נַחַת רוּחַ וְכָל תַּאֲוַת לֵב, הַנּוֹחִים כְּנַחֲלֵי שֶׁמֶן:


**and the rock that was with me would pour forth rivulets of oil** i.e., all the desires of my heart, which are as pleasing as rivulets of oil.


###### Rashi on Job 29:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:6:4)


**יָצוּק.** כְּמוֹ ״וְיָצַקְתָּ״ (שמות כה:יב):


**would pour** as in (Lev. 2:6), “and you shall pour.”



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 29:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:7:1)


**קָרֶת.** תִּקְרָה גְּבוֹהָה מוּכֶנֶת כְּכִסֵּא, לָשֶׁבֶת לְזִקְנֵי הָעִיר:


**the ceiling** a high ceiling, prepared for a chair for the elders of the city to sit on.


###### Rashi on Job 29:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:7:2)


**בָּרְחוֹב וְגוֹ׳.** וְכַאֲשֶׁר **בָּרְחוֹב הָיִיתִי מֵכִין מוֹשָׁבִי**:


**and when I was in the square**, I would prepare my seat.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 29:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:8:1)


**הָיוּ נְעָרִים** רוֹאִים אוֹתִי וְנֶחְבָּאִים, וִישִׁישִׁים עָמְדוּ מִפָּנַי:


**Youths saw me** They would see me and hide, and old men stood in my presence.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Job 29:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:9:1)


**שָׂרִים עָצְרוּ בְמִלִּים.** וּמְצַפִּים שֶׁאֶפְתַּח אֲנִי תְּחִלָּה:


**Princes refrained from talking** and would wait until I started the conversation.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 29:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:11:1)


**כִּי אֹזֶן.** אֲשֶׁר שָׁמְעָה מִלָּתִי **וַתְּאַשְּׁרֵנִי**:


**For the ear** that heard my word praised me.


###### Rashi on Job 29:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:11:2)


**וְעַיִן רָאֲתָה.** בְּכָל מָקוֹם בְּגָדְלִי וְכֹשֶׁר מִדָּתִי:


**and the eye [that] saw** my greatness and the propriety of my traits in all places.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 29:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:13:1)


**בִּרְכַּת אוֹבֵד.** אָדָם מֵת בַּעֲוֹנוֹ וְנִטְרַד:


**The blessing of the lost** who died because of his iniquity and was banished.


###### Rashi on Job 29:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:13:2)


**עָלַי תָּבֹא.** כִּי הָיִיתִי זָן אֶת בָּנָיו וְאֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ (בבא בתרא טז.), שֶׁהָיָה גּוֹזֵל שְׂדֵה יְתוֹמִים וּמַשְׁבִּיחָהּ וּמַחֲזִירָהּ לָהֶם. וּפְשָׁטֵיהּ דִּקְרָא אֵינוֹ נִרְאֶה כָּךְ, שֶׁאֵין קָרוּי אוֹבֵד – אֶלָּא דָּבָר שֶׁהוּא עַצְמוֹ אוֹבֵד; כְּמוֹ ״כִּכְלִי אֹבֵד״ (תהלים לא:יג), וְכֵן ״תָּעִיתִי כְּשֶׂה אֹבֵד״ (תהלים קיט:קעו); ״צֹאן אֹבְדוֹת״ (ירמיה נ:ו):


**would come upon me** because I would sustain his children and his wife. Our Sages said, however, that he [Job] would rob the orphans of their field, improve it and [then] return it to them (Baba Bathra 16a). But the simple meaning of the verse does not appear to be so, for no one is called אובד, but a thing that is itself lost, like (Ps. 31:13), “like a lost vessel”; and so (ibid. 119:176), “I have gone astray like a lost sheep”, (Jer. 50:6), “lost sheep.”


###### Rashi on Job 29:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:13:3)


**וְלֵב אַלְמָנָה אַרְנִין.** שֶׁהָיָה מַטִּיל שְׁמוֹ עַל אַלְמָנוֹת, לֵאמֹר: קְרוֹבָתִי הִיא; אוֹ: אֲנִי אַשִּׂיאֶנָּה! כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּקְפְּצוּ עָלֶיהָ תּוֹבְעִים:


**and I would make the widow’s heart sing for joy** for he would attach his name to widows, saying, “She is my kinswoman,” or “I will marry her,” in order that men should be anxious to marry her.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 29:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:14:1)


**צֶדֶק לָבַשְׁתִּי וַיִּלְבָּשֵׁנִי.** רָדַפְתִּי אַחֲרֵי הַצֶּדֶק וְהוּא נִמְצָא לִי כִּמְעִיל וְצָנִיף, שֶׁהֵם תַּכְשִׁיטִים נָאִים; כֵּן הָיָה מִשְׁפָּטִי נָאֶה וְטָהוֹר:


**I put on righteousness and it clothed me** I pursued righteousness and it is present with me as a coat and a turban, which are beautiful ornaments; so was my judgment beautiful and pure.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Job 29:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:18:1)


**וָאֹמַר עִם קִנִּי אֶגְוָע.** בִּשְׁבִיל כֹּשֶׁר מִדָּתִי, הָיִיתִי אוֹמֵר בְּלִבִּי: כְּשֶׁאָמוּת יִהְיֶה קִנִּי וּבֵיתִי נָכוֹן עִמִּי:


**And I said, I will perish with my nest** Because of the propriety of my traits, I would think to myself that when I die, I would have my nest and my house established with me.


###### Rashi on Job 29:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:18:2)


**וְכַחוֹל אַרְבֶּה יָמִים.** עוֹף וּשְׁמוֹ חוֹל. וְלֹא נִקְנְסָה עָלָיו מִיתָה, שֶׁלֹּא טָעַם מֵעֵץ הַדַּעַת; וּלְבַסּוֹף אֶלֶף שָׁנָה מִתְחַדֵּשׁ וְחוֹזֵר לְנַעֲרוּתוֹ:


**and I will multiply days as the phoenix** Heb. וכחול. This is a bird named חול, phoenix, upon which the punishment of death was not decreed because it did not taste of the Tree of Knowledge, and at the end of one thousand years, it renews itself and returns to its youth.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 29:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:19:1)


**שָׁרְשִׁי פָתוּחַ וְגוֹ׳.** כָּל זֶה הָיִיתִי אוֹמֵר בְּלִבִּי, שֶׁלֹּא יִיבַשׁ שָׁרְשִׁי וְלֹא יָמוּת קְצִירִי; כְּמוֹ ״וְעָשָׂה קָצִיר״ (איוב יד:ט) – וְהֵן יוֹנְקוֹת עֲנָפִים רַכִּים שֶׁל אִילָן. וְאָמַרְתִּי:


**My root will be open etc.** All this I was saying to myself, that my root would not dry up, and my branches would not die, like (above 14:9), “and it will produce a branch (קציר).” They are יונקות, tender branches of a tree.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 29:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:20:1)


**שֶׁכְּבוֹדִי.** יִתְחַדֵּשׁ עִמָּדִי תָּמִיד:


**And I thought that my honor would be renewed.** which is with meconstantly.


###### Rashi on Job 29:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:20:2)


**וְקַשְׁתִּי.** וְאֵיתָנִי תַּחֲלִיף תָּמִיד לְטוֹבָה:


**and my bow** And my strength will constantly change for the better.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Job 29:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:21:1)


**לִי שָׁמְעוּ וְיִחֵלּוּ.** הַשּׁוֹמְעִים דְּבָרַי הָיוּ מְיַחֲלִים שֶׁיִּתְקַיְּמוּ, וְהָיוּ מִתְקַיְּמוֹת. כִּי מָסֹרֶת חָכְמָה הָיָה בְּיָדִי:


**They listened to me and waited** Those who listened to my words would wait until they would be realized, and they would be realized because the tradition of wisdom was in my hand.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Job 29:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:22:1)


**תִּטֹּף מִלָּתִי.** לְשׁוֹן נְבוּאָה, וְכֵן ״לֹא תַטִּיף עַל בֵּית יִשְׂחָק״ (עמוס ז:טז). כְּלוֹמַר: כִּנְבוּאָה הָיָה דְבָרִי:


**my speech would drip** Heb. תטף. This is an expression of prophecy. Similarly (Amos 7:16), “and do not prophesy (תטיף) concerning the house of Isaac.” That is to say that my word was like prophecy.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Job 29:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:23:1)


**לְמַלְקוֹשׁ.** לִזְמַן אָרוֹךְ הָיוּ מְדַבְּרִים, כִּי רוֹב שְׁעוֹתֵיהֶם שְׁתִיקָה, שֶׁהָיוּ תְּאֵבִים לִשְׁמוֹעַ דְּבָרַי:


**after a long time** After a long time they would speak, because most of their time was spent in silence, for they were anxiously waiting to hear my words.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Job 29:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:24:1)


**לֹא יַאֲמִינוּ.** שֶׁאֲנִי מְשַׂחֵק עִמָּהֶם, מִפְּנֵי חֲשִׁיבוּתִי:


**they would not believe it** that I was jesting with them, because of my esteem[ed position].


###### Rashi on Job 29:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:24:2)


**וְאוֹר פָּנַי לֹא יַפִּילוּן.** הָיוּ יְרֵאִים לְהִתְקָרֵב אֵלַי וּלְהָגִיס לִבָּם בִּי:


**and they would not cast down the light of my countenance** They were afraid to get near me and to become familiar with me.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Job 29:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:25:1)


**אֶבְחַר דַּרְכָּם.** כָּל דַּרְכֵיהֶם וַעֲצָתָם אֲנִי הָיִיתִי בּוֹחֵר לָהֶם, שֶׁהָיוּ שׁוֹאֲלִים לִי: אֵיזֶה דֶּרֶךְ נֵלֵךְ? וּמַה נַּעֲשֶׂה?


**I would choose their way** All their ways and their counsel I would choose for them, for they would ask me, “Which way shall we go and what shall we do?”


###### Rashi on Job 29:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/29:25:2)


**וְאֵשֵׁב רֹאשׁ.** אִם הָיוּ מְסֻבִּים, אֲנִי הָיִיתִי מֵסֵב בְּרֹאשׁ הַמְּסֻבִּים:


**and sit at the head** if they were reclining at a banquet, I would recline at the head of the diners.



## Chapter 30


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 30:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:2:1)


**גַּם כֹּחַ יְדֵיהֶם לָמָּה.** הָיָה לִי בְּאוֹתָן הַיָּמִים רְשָׁעִים הָיוּ, וְלֹא תּוֹעֶלֶת בָּהֶם; כִּי פֻּרְעָנוּת בָּא עַל יָדָם:


**Moreover, why did I need the strength of their hands** in those days they were wicked, and they were useless, for trouble came because of them.


###### Rashi on Job 30:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:2:2)


**עָלֵימוֹ אָבַד כָּלַח.** זִקְנָה:


**old age** Heb. כָּלַח, old age.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 30:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:3:1)


**בְּחֶסֶר וּבְכָפָן.** הָיוּ גַּלְמוּד:


**Because of want and because of hunger** they were in solitude.


###### Rashi on Job 30:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:3:2)


**הָעוֹרְקִים צִיָּה.** שֶׁמַּבְרִיחִין אוֹתָן בְּיָמָיו מִקֶּרֶב בְּנֵי אָדָם, לֵילֵךְ בַּחֹשֶׁךְ:


**they would flee to desolation** For in his days, they would drive them from civilization to go in darkness.


###### Rashi on Job 30:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:3:3)


**אֶמֶשׁ שׁוֹאָה וּמְשׁוֹאָה.** בְּרוּאִינָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**darkness of waste and desolation** broine in Old French, obscurity.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 30:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:4:1)


**הַקּוֹטְפִים מַלּוּחַ עֲלֵי שִׂיחַ.** בִּהְיוֹתָם בַּמִּדְבָּרוֹת הָיוּ קוֹטְפִים לָהֶם מְלוּחִים עַל אִילָנוֹת הַיְּעָרִים וְאוֹכְלִים. **מַלּוּחַ** שֵׁם עֵשֶׂב הוּא, וּבִלְשׁוֹן אֲרַמִּי נִקְרָא קָקוּלִין (ירושלמי פאה ד), וּבִלְשׁוֹן מִשְׁנָה מְלוּחִים, מַצוֹ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז, כְּאוֹתָהּ שֶׁשָּׁנִינוּ (קדושין סו.): הֶעֱלוּ מְלוּחִים עַל שֻׁלְחָנוֹת שֶׁל זָהָב:


**They pluck salt-wort on shrubs** When they were in the deserts, they would pluck for themselves salt-wort [that grew] on the trees of the forests and eat. **salt-wort** Heb. מלוח. It is the name of an herb. In Aramaic (Pes. 114a), it is called קַקוּלִין and in the language of the Mishnah מַלוּחִים (malves in French), mallows, as we learned in (Kiddushin 66a): “They brought up mallows on golden tables.”


###### Rashi on Job 30:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:4:2)


**שִׂיחַ.** אִילָן, כְּמוֹ ״אַחַד הַשִּׂיחִים״ (בראשית כא:טו):


**shrubs** a tree, like (Gen. 21:15), “under one of the shrubs (השיחים).”



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 30:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:5:1)


**מִן גֵּו.** מִן קֶרֶב עִיר הָיוּ נִגְרָשִׁין:


**from the midst** From the midst of the city they were driven.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 30:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:6:1)


**בַּעֲרוּץ נְחָלִים.** יֵשׁ אֶרֶץ מְלוּחָה, מִתְבַּקַּעַת, שֶׁשָּׁם הָיוּ שׁוֹכְבִים **בְּחוֹרֵי הֶעָפָר** וְחוֹרֵי נְקִיקֵי הַסְּלָעִים:


**in the crevices of the valleys** There is a salty land that splits, where they would lie in the holes in the earth and the holes in the crevices of the rocks.


###### Rashi on Job 30:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:6:2)


**וְכֵפִים.** תַּרְגּוּם שֶׁל ״סְלָעִים״ (מלכים א יט:יא). דָּבָר אַחֵר: **בַּעֲרוּץ נְחָלִים.** הוּא הַנְּקִיק שֶׁעוֹשֶׂה הַנַּחַל סְבִיבוֹתָיו:


**and rocks** Heb. וכפים, the Aramaic translation of סלעים, rocks. (Another explanation:) **in the crevice of the streams** That is the crevice that the stream makes around itself.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 30:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:7:1)


**חָרוּל יְסֻפָּחוּ.** אוֹרְשִׁיאַ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז. הָיוּ נֶאֱסָפִים, כְּמוֹ ״וְנִסְפְּחוּ עַל בֵּית יַעֲקֹב״ (ישעיהו יד:א):


**under the nettles they gather** (Orties in French), nettles. They would gather, as in (Isa. 14:1), “and join (ונספחו) the house of Jacob.”



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 30:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:8:1)


**נִכְאוּ.** בְּיָמַי מִן הָאָרֶץ, שֶׁהָיִיתִי מַשְׁפִּילָם; לְשׁוֹן ״נְכֵה רוּחַ״ (ישעיה סו:ב):


**were broken (in my days) from the land** for I would humble them. [נִכְאוּ is] an expression of (Isa. 66:2), “of crushed spirit (נכה רוח).”



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 30:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:11:1)


**כִּי יִתְרִי פִּתַּח.** יֶתֶר קַשְׁתִּי פִּתַּח (כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַרְתִּי לְמַעְלָה וְקַשְׁתִּי תַּחְלִיף), כְּלוֹמַר: כֹּחִי הֵתִישׁ מִי שֶׁבְּיָדוֹ לַעֲשׂוֹת:


**For He untied my bowstring** He untied the string of my bow (that I said above that my bow would become stronger); i.e., He weakened my strength—He Who has the power to do so.


###### Rashi on Job 30:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:11:2)


**וְרֶסֶן מִפָּנַי שִׁלֵּחוּ.** רִסְנִי שֶׁהָיוּ מְרֻסָּנִים בּוֹ, שִׁלְּחוּהוּ מִפָּנַי, כִּי אֵינָם יְרֵאִים מֵעַתָּה מִמֶּנִּי:


**and they have cast off the bridle** My bridle, with which they were bridled to me—they cast it off before me because they no longer fear me.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 30:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:12:1)


**עַל יָמִין.** עַל יְמִינִי עוֹמְדִים פִּרְחָחִים קְטַנִּים, כִּשְׁאָר הֶדְיוֹטִים; שֶׁאֵינִי חָשׁוּב בְּעֵינֵיהֶם לְהִכָּנַע מִפָּנַי:


**On my right** on my right, stand “little sprouts” as [with] any other simple person, because I am no longer so esteemed in their sight [that they] subordinate themselves before Me.


###### Rashi on Job 30:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:12:2)


**רַגְלַי שִׁלֵּחוּ.** אִם הַמָּקוֹם צַר לָהֶם, דּוֹחֲפִים אוֹתִי הָלְאָה:


**they push away my feet** If the place is crowded, they push me away.


###### Rashi on Job 30:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:12:3)


**וַיָּסֹלּוּ עָלַי אָרְחוֹת אֵידָם.** כָּל דֶּרֶךְ נְבָלָה שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהֶם לַעֲשׂוֹת, עוֹשִׂים מְסִלָּתָן לְפָנַי וְאֶצְלִי, וְלֹא יִירָאוּ:


**and they cast up against me their ways of destruction** Any shameful way [i.e., act] that they have to do, they make their way before me or beside me [to perform it] and they have no fear.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 30:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:13:1)


**נָתְסוּ נְתִיבָתִי.** הַטּוֹבָה וְאֵין חוֹשְׁשִׁין לָהּ, נָתְסוּ כְּמוֹ ״נָתְצוּ״ (מלכים ב כה:י) וְסָמֶ״ךְ מִתְחַלֶּפֶת בְּצָדִ״י:


**They break up my path** Heb. נתסוּ. [My] good [path] and do not care for it. נתסוּ is like נתצוּ the “sammech” replacing the “zaddi.”


###### Rashi on Job 30:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:13:2)


**לְהַוָּתִי יוֹעִילוּ.** לְהַקְנִיטֵנִי מוֹסִיפִין:


**they serve to increase my calamity** they continue to provoke me.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 30:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:14:1)


**כְּפֶרֶץ רָחָב.** שֶׁבַּגָּדֵר שֶׁהַכֹּל בָּאִים וְעוֹבְרִים שָׁם, כֵּן **יֶאֱתָיוּ** יַחַד עָלַי לְהַכְלִימֵנִי:


**As [through] a wide breach** in a fence, through which everyone comes and passes through, so they come together against me to embarrass me.


###### Rashi on Job 30:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:14:2)


**תַּחַת שׁוֹאָה הִתְגַּלְגָּלוּ.** כְּלוֹמַר: מִתְגַּלְגְּלִים וּבָאִין בְּסֵתֶר, עַד שֶׁמַּגִּיעִין אֶצְלִי לְהַקְנִיטֵנִי, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא אֶשָּׁמֵט מֵהֶם:


**under darkness they roll** i.e., they come rolling along in secret until they reach me, to provoke me, so that I should not be able to escape from them.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 30:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:15:1)


**הָהְפַּךְ עָלַי.** נֶהְפַּךְ עָלַי:


**turned upon me** Heb. הָהְפַךְ, [equivalent to] נֶהְפַּךְ.


###### Rashi on Job 30:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:15:2)


**בַּלָּהוֹת.** שֵׁדִים:


**Terrors** demons.


###### Rashi on Job 30:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:15:3)


**תִּרְדֹּף כָּרוּחַ.** רָעָה:


**as a spirit** An evil [spirit].


###### Rashi on Job 30:15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:15:4)


**אֶת נְדִיבָתִי.** רוּחַ נְדִיבָה שֶׁהָיָה שׁוֹרָה עָלַי מִתְּחִלָּה:


**my nobility** The noble spirit that rested upon me from the beginning.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 30:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:16:1)


**וְעַתָּה.** אֶל הַצָּרָה הַזֹּאת תִּשְׁתַּפֵּךְ נַפְשִׁי, כְּאָדָם שֶׁתִּתְפַּעֵם רוּחוֹ; **כִּי יֹאחֲזוּנִי**, וְכוֹפִין אוֹתִי לְהִשְׁתַּפֵּל נַפְשִׁי:


**And now** to this trouble my soul pours out like a man whose spirit is troubled, for...


###### Rashi on Job 30:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:16:2)


**יְמֵי עֹנִי.** שֶׁאֲנִי בָּהֶם:


**days of affliction** in which I am [now] seize me and coerce me to pour out my soul.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Job 30:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:17:1)


**נִקַּר.** כְּמוֹ ״הַעֵינֵי הָאֲנָשִׁים הָהֵם תְּנַקֵּר״ (במדבר טז:יד). **נִקַּר.** פוֹרִיי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז; הַתּוֹלָעִים מְנַקְּרִין בְּשָׂרִי מֵעַל עַצְמִי:


**are picked off** Heb. נקר, like (Num. 16:14), “will you put out (תנקר) the eyes of those people?” (forer in French, to bore). The worms pick my flesh off my bones.


###### Rashi on Job 30:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:17:2)


**וְעֹרְקַי.** גִּידַי, אֵין לָהֶם מְנוּחָה. ׳גִּיד׳ בִּלְשׁוֹן עֲרָבִי ׳עוֹרֶק׳; כָּךְ פֵּירֵשׁ דּוּנַשׁ. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: **עֹרְקַי.** רוֹדְפַי, הַמַּבְרִיחִין אוֹתִי:


**my sinews** Heb. עֹרְקַי. My sinews have no rest. Sinew in Arabic is “orek.” So did Dunash explain it (Teshuvoth Dunash p.85). Another explanation: עֹרְקַי, my pursuers who cause me to flee. (Machbereth Menachem p. 139)



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Job 30:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:18:1)


**יִתְחַפֵּשׂ לְבוּשִׁי.** מִשְׁתַּנֶּה לְבוּשִׁי, גֶּלֶד אַחַר גֶּלֶד; כְּשֶׁאָדָם פּוֹשֵׁט אֶת בְּגָדָיו וְלוֹבֵשׁ בְּגָדִים אֲחֵרִים, מְשֻׁנִּים מֵרִאשׁוֹנִים, קוֹרֵא אוֹתָם ׳בִּגְדֵי חֹפֶשׂ׳; כְּמוֹ ״הִתְחַפֵּשׂ וָבֹא בַמִּלְחָמָה״ (מלכים א כב:ל), וּמְתֻרְגָּם: ״אִשְׁתַּנִּי״ (תרגום יונתן):


**is my garment changed** My “garment” changes, layer after layer. When a person removes his garments and puts on other garments different from the first ones, he calls these בִּגְדֵי חֹפֶשׁ, like (I Kings 22:30), “I will disguise myself (התחפש) and go into battle.” This is translated into Aramaic by the word אשתני, I will change.


###### Rashi on Job 30:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:18:2)


**כְּפִי כֻתָּנְתִּי יַאַזְרֵנִי.** שֶׁאוֹזֵר וּמַעֲנִיק צַוָּארִי, כֵּן יַאַזְרֵנִי אוֹתִי לְבוּשִׁי כָּל סְבִיבִי:


**like the opening of my shirt it girds me** Like the opening of my shirt, which girds and encompasses my neck, so do my garments gird me all around.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 30:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:19:1)


**הוֹרָנִי לַחֹמֶר.** הוֹרָנִי יְלַמְּדֵנִי הַשְּׁחִין הַזֶּה לֵישֵׁב כְּמוֹ בַחֹמֶר, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַרְנוּ ״וְהוּא יוֹשֵׁב בְּתוֹךְ הָאֵפֶר״ (איוב ב:ח). דָּבָר אַחֵר: **הוֹרָנִי לַחֹמֶר** פֵּירוּשׁוֹ: הִשְׁלִיכַנִי, לְשׁוֹן ״יָרָה בַיָּם״ (שמות טו:ד):


**It directed me to the mud** These boils directed me and taught me to sit in the mud, as we said above (2:8). “and he sat down in the ashes.” Another explanation: הוֹרַנִי means: it cast me, an expression of (Exod. 15:4), “He cast (ירה) into the sea.”


###### Rashi on Job 30:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:19:2)


**וָאֶתְמַשֵּׁל.** נִדְמֵיתִי. וּבְמִדְרַשׁ רַבִּי תַּנְחוּמָא (תנחומא בובר, וישלח ח): אָמַר רַבִּי בֶּרֶכְיָה: סָרֵס אֶת הַמִּקְרָא: אֲנִי נִמְשַׁלְתִּי לְאַבְרָהָם בְּצִדְקָתִי שֶׁקָּרָא עַצְמוֹ ״עָפָר וָאֵפֶר״ (בראשית יח:כז), וְהוּא דָּנַנִּי כְּרִשְׁעֵי דּוֹר הַפְּלַגָּה (שֶׁמָּרְדוּ בּוֹ בְּבִנְיַן הַמִּגְדָּל) שֶׁכָּתוּב בָּהֶם ״וְהַחֵמָר הָיָה לָהֶם לַחֹמֶר״ (בראשית יא:ג):


**and I was compared** Was likened. And in the Midrash of Rabbi Tanchuma (Buber, Vayishlach 8): Said Rabbi Berechiah, “Transpose the verse.” I was compared to Abraham in my righteousness, who called himself dust and ashes (Gen.18:27), and He judges me like the wicked of the Generation of the Dispersion (who rebelled against Him by building the tower), concerning whom it is written (Gen. 11:3): “and the mortar served them as clay.”



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 30:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:20:1)


**עָמַדְתִּי.** לִרְאוֹת, לִשְׁתּוֹק וּלְהָבִין; כְּמוֹ ״וּבְפִתְחוֹ עָמְדוּ כָל הָעָם״ (נחמיה ח:ה), וְ״לֹא עָנוּ עוֹד״ (איוב לב:טו):


**I stood** to see, to remain silent, and to understand, as in (Neh. 8:5), “And when he opened [it], all the people stood,” and no longer replied.


###### Rashi on Job 30:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:20:2)


**וַתִּתְבּוֹנֵן בִּי.** מְחַשֵּׁב עָלַי לִשְׁנוֹת אֶת נְגָעַי:


**and You ponder me** You think about me to change my plagues.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Job 30:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:22:1)


**תִּשָּׂאֵנִי אֶל רוּחַ.** שֵׁדִים:


**You lift me up to the spirit** of the demons.


###### Rashi on Job 30:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:22:2)


**תַּרְכִּיבֵנִי וּתְמֹגְגֵנִי תּוּשִׁיָּה.** חַלָּשׁוּת וְתַשּׁוּת כֹּחַ הָיָה מְמוֹגְגֵנִי:


**You cause me to ride [on it], and weakness melts me away** Weakness and feebleness of strength melted me away.


###### Rashi on Job 30:22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:22:3)


**וּתְמוֹגְגֵנִי תּוּשִׁיָּה.** לְשׁוֹן נְקֵבָה, וְעַל תּוּשִׁיָּה עוֹמֵד. וְאֵין תָּי״ו זוֹ כְּתָי״ו שֶׁל **תִּשָּׂאֵנִי**:


**and weakness melts me away** Heb. ותמגגני, a feminine form, the subject of which is תֻּשִיָה, and this “tav” is not like the “tav” of “You lift me up (תשאני).”



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Job 30:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:23:1)


**כִּי יָדַעְתִּי.** כִּי יוֹדֵעַ הָיִיתִי אֲשֶׁר **לַמָּוֶת תְּשִׁיבֵנִי**, מָקוֹם שֶׁהוּא **מוֹעֵד לְכָל חָי.** בַּקֶּבֶר:


**For I knew** For I knew that You would return me to death, a place which is a meeting place for all living, namely the grave.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Job 30:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:24:1)


**אַךְ לֹא בְעִי.** וּבַהַשְׁחָתָה הָיָה רָגִיל הַשּׁוֹפֵט לִשְׁלֹחַ יָד, כְּמוֹ ״לְעִי הַשָּׂדֶה״ (מיכה א:ו):


**But not with destruction** was the Judge accustomed to stretch forth His hand; with destruction, like (Micah 1:6), “into a heap (לעי) in the field.”


###### Rashi on Job 30:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:24:2)


**אִם בְּפִידוֹ.** אִם הָיָה שׁוֹלֵחַ פִּיד, שֶׁהוּא שֶׁבֶר, עַל בְּרִיּוֹתָיו:


**if with His misfortune** If He would send a misfortune, which is a breach upon His creatures.


###### Rashi on Job 30:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:24:3)


**לָהֶן שׁוּעַ.** הָיָה מְשַׁעֲשְׁעָן תַּנְחוּמִין בְּמִקְצָת. וְכֵן פֵּרְשׁוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ (ילקוט שמעוני איוב תתקיח): אֵין הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מַכֶּה אוּמָּה וּמוֹשִׁיבָהּ צָדוּ, אֶלָּא מֵבִיא פִּיד לָזוֹ וּמְשַׁעֲשְׁעָהּ בַּחֲבֶרְתָּהּ שֶׁלָּקְתָה כְּבָר, וּמִתְנַחֶמֶת בָּהּ. הֵבִיא פִּיד לְאַשּׁוּר וְשִׁעֲשֵׁעַ בְּמִצְרַיִם, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״הֲתֵיטְבִי מִנֹּא אָמוֹן״ (נחום ג:ח); הֵבִיא פִּיד לְמִצְרַיִם וְשִׁעֲשֵׁעַ בְּאַשּׁוּר, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״הִנֵּה אַשּׁוּר... בַּלְּבָנוֹן״ (יחזקאל לא:ג). וְעוֹד יִפָּתֵר **לָהֶן שׁוּעַ.** תְּפִלָּה וּתְחִנָּה מוֹעֶלֶת בָּהֶם:


**He would dandle them** He would dandle them with partial consolations. And so did our Sages (Pesikta d’Rav Kahana p. 1 27a) explain it: The Holy One, blessed be He, does not smite a nation and leave it desolate, but He brings misfortune to one and dandles it with its fellow that suffered already, and it is consoled thereby. He brought misfortune upon Assyria and dandled it with Egypt, as it is said (Nahum 3:8): “Are you better than the great No- Amon?” He brought misfortune upon Egypt and dandled them with Assyria, as it is said (Ezek. 31: 3): “Behold Assyria was [a cedar] in the Lebanon.” The words להן שוע may also be interpreted as: Prayer and supplication avail them.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Job 30:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:25:1)


**אִם לֹא בָכִיתִי לִקְשֵׁה יוֹם.** יוֹדֵעַ וּמַכִּיר, אִם לֹא הָיִיתִי רַחֲמָנִי וּבוֹכֶה עַל עֲנִיִּים קְשֵׁי יוֹם, **וְעָגְמָה נַפְשִׁי לָאֶבְיוֹנִים.** כִּי עַל כָּל אֵלֶּה בָּאַתְנִי זֹאת:


**...whether I did not weep for one who had a difficult time** [You] know and recognize whether I was not compassionate and [did not] weep for the poor, who had difficult times, and [whether] my soul [did not] grieve for the needy, that this befell me because of all these.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Job 30:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:26:1)


**כִּי טוֹב קִוִּיתִי וְגוֹ׳.** כְּלוֹמַר: כִּי הַגְּמוּל הַזֶּה רָאוּי לִי:


**For I hoped for good etc.** That is to say that this reward was fit for me.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Job 30:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:27:1)


**וְלֹא דָמּוּ.** אֵין יְכוֹלִין לִדּוֹם וּלְהַחֲרִישׁ:


**and did not become silent** They cannot be silent.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Job 30:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:28:1)


**בְּלֹא חַמָּה.** לֹא שְׁזָפַתְנִי הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ, וְהִנְנִי קוֹדֵר:


**without the sun** The sun did not gaze upon me, yet I am blackened.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Job 30:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:29:1)


**אָח הָיִיתִי לְתַנִּים וְגוֹ׳.** תַּנִּים וּבְנוֹת יַעֲנָה עֲסוּקִים בִּבְכִי כָּל יְמֵיהֶם:


**I was a brother to jackals etc.** Jackals and ostriches spend all their days wailing.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Job 30:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/30:30:1)


**חָרָה.** לְשׁוֹן דָּבָר הַנִּחְרָה וּמִתְיַיבֵּשׁ מִפְּנֵי חוֹם הָאֵשׁ, כְּמוֹ ״נָחַר מַפֻּחַ״ (ירמיהו ו:כט), ״וְתוֹכוֹ נִחָר״ (יחזקאל טו:ד):


**dried out** Heb. חרה, an expression of a thing that is burned and dried through the heat of the fire, like (Jer. 6:29), “The bellows is heated (נחר)”; (Ezek. 15:4), “and the middle of it is burned (נחר).”



## Chapter 31


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Job 31:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:1:1)


**בְּרִית כָּרַתִּי לְעֵינָי.** שֶׁלֹּא לְהִסְתַּכֵּל בְּאֵשֶׁת אִישׁ:


**I made a covenant with my eyes** not to gaze upon a married woman.


###### Rashi on Job 31:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:1:2)


**וּמָה אֶתְבּוֹנֵן עַל בְּתוּלָה.** מָה לִּי לְהִתְבּוֹנֵן בָּהּ? כָּךְ הָיְתָה חֲסִידוּתִי, שֶׁלֹּא לָתֵת עַיִן אֲפִלּוּ בִּפְנוּיָה; שֶׁמָּא לְאַחַר יָמִים תִּנָּשֵׂא, וְנִמְצֵאתִי כָּרוּךְ אַחֲרֶיהָ. כָּךְ מְפֹרָשׁ בְּאָבוֹת דְּרַבִּי נָתָן (אבות דרבי נתן):


**and why should I gaze upon a virgin?** Why should I gaze upon her? Such was Job’s piety, not to lay an eye even on an unmarried woman. [He would say,] “Perhaps, after a time, she will be married, and I will find myself attracted to her.” So is [this verse] explained in Avoth D’Rabbi Nathan (2:5).



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 31:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:2:1)


**וּמֶה חֵלֶק וְגוֹ׳.** שֶׁגְּמָלַנִי כָּךְ?


**Now what is the portion etc.** that He recompensed me so?



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 31:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:3:1)


**הֲלֹא אֵיד לְעַוָּל.** לוֹ רָאוּי, וְלֹא לִי:


**Is not misfortune, for the unjust** He deserves it, not I.


###### Rashi on Job 31:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:3:2)


**וְנֵכֶר.** דּוּגְמַת ״וְאַל תֵּרֶא בְיוֹם אָחִיךָ בְּיוֹם נָכְרוֹ״ (עובדיה א:יב):


**and deliverance** Heb. ונכר, after the pattern of (Obadiah 1:12) “[And] you should not have looked on the day of your brother on the day of his being delivered (נכרו).”



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 31:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:4:1)


**וְכָל צְעָדַי יִסְפּוֹר.** וְרוֹאֶה אִם הָלַכְתִּי עִם שָׁוְא:


**and count all my steps** and see whether I went with falsehood.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 31:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:5:1)


**וַתַּחַשׁ.** וַתְּמַהֵר, כְּמוֹ ״אֲחִישֶׁנָּה״ (ישעיה ס:כב); ״כִּי גָז חִישׁ״ (תהלים צ:י):


**hastened** Heb. ותחש, and hurried, like (Isa. 60:22), “I will hasten it (אחישנה)”; (Ps. 90:10) “for it is soon (חיש) cut off.”



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 31:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:7:1)


**מָאוּם.** הוּא מוּם, דְּבַר שֶׁמֶץ. וְאָלֶ״ף זוֹ כְּתוּבָה וְלֹא קְרִיָא בַּמָּסֹרֶת הַגְּדוֹלָה, וְהוּא מִגִּזְרַת **לֹא תֹאסִפוּן** (שמות ה:ז). אִם זוֹ עָשִׂיתִי, הָיִיתִי רָאוּי לָבֹא עָלַי קְלָלָה זוֹ:


**wrong** Heb. מאום, that is a blemish, a shameful thing. This “alef” is written but not read, in the Great Masorah, and it is of the form of (Exod. 5:7), “You shall not continue (תאספון).” If I did this, I would deserve that this curse come upon me.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 31:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:8:1)


**אֶזְרְעָה וְאַחֵר יֹאכֵל וְצֶאֱצָאַי יְשֹׁרָשׁוּ.** יֵעָקְרוּ הַשָּׁרָשִׁין:


**may I sow and another eat, and my produce be uprooted** their roots should be torn up.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Job 31:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:9:1)


**וְעַל פֶּתַח רֵעִי אָרָבְתִּי.** לְאִשְׁתּוֹ – יָשִׁיב לִי גְּמוּלִי:


**and if I lurked at my neighbor’s doorway** for his wife, He should requite me...



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 31:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:10:1)


**תִּטְחַן לְאַחֵר אִשְׁתִּי.** רַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּהוּ (במדבר רבה ט:כד) לְשׁוֹן תַּשְׁמִישׁ, כְּמוֹ ״וַיְהִי טוֹחֵן בְּבֵית הָאֲסוּרִין״ (שופטים טז:כא):


**may my wife grind for someone else** Our Sages explained this as an expression of sexual intercourse, as in (Jud. 16:21), “and he did grind in the prison house.”



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 31:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:11:1)


**כִּי הִיא זִמָּה.** וְיָרֵא הָיִיתִי אִם עָשִׂיתִי, שֶׁהוּא **עָוֹן פְּלִילִי**, עָוֹן שֶׁמִּשְׁפַּט יִסּוּרִין רְאוּיִין לָבֹא עַל יָדוֹ:


**For that is lewdness** and I feared that if I committed it, that **it is iniquity deserving punishment** iniquity for which judgment of suffering is fit to come.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 31:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:12:1)


**וּבְכָל תְּבוּאָתִי תְשָׁרֵשׁ.** הָאֵשׁ הַזֹּאת, לָכֵן נִמְנַעְתִּי מֵעֲשׂוֹת. כְּשֶׁהוּא מְדַבֵּר בִּלְשׁוֹן עִקּוּר, אוֹמֵר תְּשָׁרֵשׁ; וּבִלְשׁוֹן שֹׁרֶשׁ אוֹמֵר ״תַּשְׁרֵשׁ״ (תהלים פ:י). וְכֵן ״יְשֹׁרָשׁוּ״ (איוב לא:ח) – לְשׁוֹן עִקּוּר; ״יַשְׁרֵשׁ״ (ישעיהו כז:ו) – לְשׁוֹן שֹׁרֶשׁ:


**and it uproots all my grain** i.e., this fire. Therefore, I refrained from committing [this sin]. When he speaks with an expression of uprooting, he says תְּשָׁרֵשׁ, and to express roots, he also uses תַּשְׁרֵשׁ. Similarly, יְשֹׁרָשׁוּ is an expression of uprooting and יַשְׁרִשׁוּ is an expression of a root.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 31:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:14:1)


**וּמָה אֶעֱשֶׂה וְגוֹ׳.** זֹאת נָתַתִּי אֶל לִבִּי, וְכִי יִפְקֹד אֶת דְּרָכַי, **מָה אֲשִׁיבֶנּוּ**?


**then what will I do etc.** I took this to heart, and when He visits, what will I answer Him?



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 31:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:15:1)


**וַיְכוֹנְנוּ בָרֶחֶם.** אֲנִי בְּבֶטֶן אִמִּי, וְהוּא בְּבֶטֶן אִמּוֹ, הֱכִינָנוּ יוֹצֵר אֶחָד:


**and form us both in the womb?** I in my mother’s womb and he in his mother’s womb—did not One creator form us?



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 31:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:16:1)


**אֲכַלֶּה.** שֶׁלֹּא מִלֵּאתִי תַּאֲוָתָהּ, שֶׁהוּא כִּלְיוֹן עֵינַיִם. הַמִּתְאַוֶּה וְאֵינוֹ מַגִּיעַ, קָרוּי ״כִּלְיוֹן עֵינַיִם״ (דברים כח:סה):


**I cause...to fail** in that I did not fulfill her desire, which is “failure of the eyes.” One who desires and does not achieve is called “failure of the eyes.”



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Job 31:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:18:1)


**כִּי מִנְּעוּרַי גְּדֵלַנִי.** הַכִּשָּׁרוֹן הַזֶּה כְּאָב הַמְגַדְּלֵנִי וְהַמְלַמְּדֵנִי, כֵּן לִמְּדַנִי יֹשֶׁר לִבִּי מִנְּעוּרַי:


**For, from my youth it raised me** This virtue [raised me] as a father who raises me and teaches me. So did the uprightness of my heart teach me from my youth.


###### Rashi on Job 31:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:18:2)


**וּמִבֶּטֶן אִמִּי אַנְחֶנָּה.** לְמִדָּה זוֹ:


**and from my mother’s womb I led it** This trait.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Job 31:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:22:1)


**מִשִּׁכְמָה.** הוּא רֹחַב הַכָּתֵף:


**from its shoulder blade** That is the width of the shoulder.


###### Rashi on Job 31:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:22:2)


**מִקָּנֶה.** הוּא עֶצֶם הָעֶלְיוֹן, הֶעָגֹל כְּקָנֶה; וְעַל שֶׁהוּא עָגֹל כְּקָנֶה, קוֹרֵא אוֹתוֹ כֵּן:


**from its bone** That is the upper bone, which is round as a reed (קנה), and because it is round as a reed, it is called so.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Job 31:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:23:1)


**כִּי פַחַד אֵלַי וְגוֹ׳.** פַּחַד הָיָה בְּעֵינַי אֵידוֹ, שֶׁהוּא מֵבִיא עַל הָרְשָׁעִים; לָכֵן חָדַלְתִּי מֵעֲשׂוֹת:


**fear to me** His misfortune, which He brought upon the wicked, was fear in my eyes; therefore, I refrained from doing it.


###### Rashi on Job 31:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:23:2)


**וּמִשְּׂאֵתוֹ.** וּמִכְּבוֹדוֹ:



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Job 31:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:24:1)


**כִּסְלִי.** כְּמוֹ ״אֲשֶׁר יָקוֹט כִּסְלוֹ״ (איוב ח:יד) – תִּקְוָתִי וּמַחֲשַׁבְתִּי. וְעַל יְדֵי שֶׁכְּלָיוֹת עַל הַכְּסָלִים הֵם (ויקרא ג:ד), וְהַכְּלָיוֹת יוֹעֲצוֹת (תהלים טז:ז), נִקְרֵאת הַמַּחֲשָׁבָה ׳כֶּסֶל׳:


**my hope** Heb. כסלי, like (8:14), “whose confidence (כסלו) shall be cut off.” My hope and my thought, and because the kidneys are on the flanks (כסלים), and the kidneys advise, the thought is called כֶּסֶל.


###### Rashi on Job 31:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:24:2)


**וְלַכֶּתֶם.** קְבוּצוֹת זָהָב וְאוֹצָרוֹת:


**and to jewelry** A gathering of gold and treasures.


###### Rashi on Job 31:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:24:3)


**אָמַרְתִּי מִבְטַחִי.** לְהִתְחַזֵּק בָּהֶם עַל הָעֲנִיִּים:


**I said, ‘My confidence.’** therewith to strengthen myself over the poor.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Job 31:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:25:1)


**אִם אֶשְׂמַח כִּי רַב חֵילִי.** לֹא הָיִיתִי נוֹהֵג שִׂמְחָה בִּפְנֵי הָעֲנִיִּים בְּעָשְׁרִי, שֶׁלֹּא לְהַקְנִיאָם בִּי:


**If I rejoiced because my wealth** I did not behave with joy in the presence of the poor so as not to make them envious of me.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Job 31:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:26:1)


**אִם אֶרְאֶה אוֹר כִּי יָהֵל.** חַמָּה בִּתְקוּפַת גְּבוּרָתָהּ וְהִילָּה:


**If I saw light shining** The sun in the orbit of its might, and its brightness.


###### Rashi on Job 31:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:26:2)


**וְיָרֵחַ יָקָר הוֹלֵךְ.** מַגִּיהַּ וְהוֹלֵךְ, כְּמוֹ ״אוֹר יְקָרוֹת״ (זכריה יד:ו), ״כִּיקַר כָּרִים״ (תהלים לז:כ):


**and the moon becoming brighter** Heb. יקר. Progressively brighter, like (Zech. 14:6), “bright (יקרות) light”; (Ps. 37:20), “like the glory (כיקר) off the meadows.”



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Job 31:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:27:1)


**וַיִּפְתְּ בַּסֵּתֶר לִבִּי.** לַשֶּׁמֶשׁ וְלַיָּרֵחַ לֵאמֹר: אֱלוֹהוּת הֵם, כִּקְצָת עוֹבְדֵי כוֹכָבִים הַנִּדָּחִים (וְעוֹבְדִים) לְכָל צְבָא הַשָּׁמַיִם:


**And my heart was secretly enticed** to the sun and the moon, saying that they are gods, like some heathens, who have gone astray (and worship) all the host of heaven.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Job 31:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:30:1)


**וְלֹא נָתַתִּי לַחֲטֹא חִכִּי.** לְהַקְנִיט שׂוֹנְאִי וְלִתְבֹּעוֹ מָמוֹן שֶׁאֵין לִי עָלָיו, כְּדֵי לְהַשְׁבִּיעוֹ, **וְלִשְׁאֹל בְּאָלָה נַפְשׁוֹ** שֶׁל שׂוֹנְאִי:


**but I did not allow my palate to sin** to provoke my enemy and to sue him for money that he does not owe me, in order to adjure him and to ask for the soul of my enemy with an oath.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Job 31:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:31:1)


**לֹא נִשְׂבָּע.** מִתּוֹךְ שִׂנְאָה שֶׁהָיוּ שׂוֹנְאִים אוֹתִי, עַל שֶׁהָיִיתִי מַטְרִיחָם בְּהַכְנָסַת אוֹרְחִים:


**We will not be sated** Because of the hatred that they bore against me for burdening them with hospitality toward guests.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Job 31:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:32:1)


**לָאֹרַח.** לֹא הָיוּ דַּלְתוֹת בֵּיתִי פְּנִימִיִּים וּמוּקָּפִין בֵּית שַׁעַר מִבַּחוּץ, אוֹ מוּסָּבִין בְּקַרְנֵי זָוִית, לְהַשְׁבִּית אֶת הָאוֹרְחִים מִלָּבוֹא, אֶלָּא פְּתוּחִין לְאֵם דֶּרֶךְ, לָבוֹא כָּל הַבָּא:


**to the road** The doors of my house were not inside and enclosed by a gatehouse outside, or surrounded by corners to stop the wayfarers from coming, but open to the crossroads so that whoever wishes to come may do so.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Job 31:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:33:1)


**בְּחֻבִּי.** בְּמַחֲבוֹאִי:


**in my hiding place** Heb. בחבי, like בְּמַחַבוֹאִי.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on Job 31:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:34:1)


**כִּי אֶעֱרוֹץ.** מִתְּחִלָּה הָיִיתִי מַכְנִיעַ הָמוֹן רַבָּה שֶׁל רְשָׁעִים, וְעַתָּה אַף בֵּין כַּשְׂדִּיִים, ״זֶה הָעָם לֹא הָיָה״ (ישעיהו כג:יג):


**For I would subdue** Originally I would subdue a great multitude of wicked men, but now, even among the Chaldeans—“This people has never been” (Isa. 23:13).


###### Rashi on Job 31:34:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:34:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:34:2)


**יְחִתֵּנִי.** יְיָרְאֵנִי:



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on Job 31:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:35:1)


**הֵן תָּוִי שַׁדַּי יַעֲנֵנִי.** וּלְוַאי שֶׁיָּעִיד עָלַי הַמָּקוֹם בַּדִּין, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַר עָלַי: ״כִּי אֵין כָּמוֹהוּ בָּאָרֶץ... תָּם וְיָשָׁר וִירֵא אֱלֹהִים״ (איוב א:ח). גַּם מֹשֶׁה שֶׁכָּתַב סִפְרוֹ וְסִפְרִי יָעִיד עָלַי. כֵּן הָיָה אִיּוֹב אוֹמֵר: תְּנָה לְתַוּוֹת אוֹתִי וְאֶת כּוֹשְׁרִי:


**behold my inscription; may the Almighty bear witness for me** Would that the Omnipresent bear witness for me in judgment, as He wrote concerning me (1:8): “For there is none like him on earth, a sincere and upright man, God-fearing.” Also Moses, who wrote his Book and my Book, will bear witness concerning me. (Another explanation:) This is what Job says, “Give it to inscribe me and my virtue.”


###### Rashi on Job 31:35:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:35:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:35:2)


**שַׁדַּי יַעֲנֵנִי.** לְבַקָּשָׁה זֹאת:


**may the Almighty answer me** for this request.


###### Rashi on Job 31:35:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:35:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:35:3)


**וְסֵפֶר כָּתַב אִישׁ רִיבִי.** יִכְתֹּב גַּם הוּא סִפְרוֹ, לְהִתְוַכֵּחַ עִמִּי:


**and may my opponent write a book** May he too write a book to contend with me.



### Verse 36

###### Rashi on Job 31:36:1

**אֶשָּׂאֶנּוּ.** לִכְתּוֹב אֶת סְפָרִי:


**Would I not carry him** The writer of the book.


###### Rashi on Job 31:36:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:36:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:36:2)


**אֶעֶנְדֶנּוּ.** אֶקְשְׁרֶנּוּ לְסֵפֶר עֲטָרוֹת לִי:


**bind it** I will bind the book to me as crowns.



### Verse 37

###### Rashi on Job 31:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:37:1)


**מִסְפַּר צְעָדַי אַגִּידֶנּוּ.** לְאוֹתוֹ הָאִישׁ:


**I will tell him the number of my steps** to that man.



### Verse 38

###### Rashi on Job 31:38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:38:1)


**אִם עָלַי אַדְמָתִי תִזְעָק.** עַל לֶקֶט שִׁכְחָה וּפֵאָה וּמַעַשְׂרוֹת, שֶׁלֹּא הוֹצֵאתִי מַעַשְׂרוֹת כָּרָאוּי:


**If my soil complains about me** concerning the gleanings, the forgotten sheaves, and the end [of the field], as well as tithes, [claiming] that I did not extract tithes properly.


###### Rashi on Job 31:38:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:38:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:38:2)


**וְיַחַד תְּלָמֶיהָ יִבְכָּיוּן.** שֶׁחָרַשְׁתִּי בְּשׁוֹר וּבַחֲמוֹר יַחְדָּיו (דברים כב:י):


**and its furrows weep together** that I plowed with an ox and with a donkey together.



### Verse 39

###### Rashi on Job 31:39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:39:1)


**בְּלִי כָסֶף.** לִשְׂכֹּר שְׂכִירִים וּלְעָשְׁקָם. דָּבָר אַחֵר: זֶה מַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי, דִּכְתִיב ״וְצַרְתָּ הַכֶּסֶף בְּיָדְךָ״ (דברים יד:כה):


**without money** to hire workers and to oppress them [by not paying them]. Another explanation. This refers to the second tithe, concerning which it is written (Deut. 14:25): “And you shall tie the money in your hand.”


###### Rashi on Job 31:39:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:39:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:39:2)


**וְנֶפֶשׁ בְּעָלֶיהָ.** אָרִיס הָעוֹבֵד בָּהּ:


**and I caused pain to its owner** The sharecropper who tills it.



### Verse 40

###### Rashi on Job 31:40:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:40:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:40:1)


**בָּאְשָׁה.** (דָּבָר מַבְאִישׁ) כְּמוֹ ״בְּאוּשִׁים״ (ישעיה ה:ב). וְאִם לֹא עָשִׂיתִי כֵּן **תַּחַת חִטָּה יֵצֵא חוֹחַ.** תָּנָא רַבִּי אוֹשַׁעְיָא: לִמֶּדְתְךָ תּוֹרָה דֶּרֶךְ אֶרֶץ: שָׂדֶה שֶׁמַּעֲלָה חוֹחִים, יָפֶה לְזוֹרְעָהּ חִטִּים, שֶׁמַּעֲלָה בְּאוּשִׁים יָפֶה לְזוֹרְעָהּ שְׂעוֹרִים. כָּךְ הוּא בַּיַּלְקוּט (ילקוט שמעוני איוב תתקיח). וּמַה טַּעַם? **תַּחַת חִטָּה יֵצֵא חוֹחַ**:


**noisome weeds** Heb. באשה, (a malodorous plant) like בְּאֻשִׁים (Isa. 5:2). And if I did not do so, may thistles emerge instead of wheat. (Rabbi Oshia taught: The Torah teaches you practical advice, that in a field beset with thistles it is advisable to sow wheat, and in a field beset with weeds, it is advisable to sow barley. This is the reading of Yalkut.) Now what is the reason? “Instead of wheat, thistles shall emerge.”


###### Rashi on Job 31:40:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:40:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/31:40:2)


**תַּמּוּ וְגוֹ׳.** עַד כָּאן הוּא חוֹזֵר וּמִתְנַבֵּא כַּמָּה נְבוּאוֹת, וְאַתְּ אוֹמֵר תַּמּוּ דִּבְרֵי אִיּוֹב? אֶלָּא כָּךְ אוֹמֵר אִיּוֹב: אִם לֹא עָשִׂיתִי כֵן, יִתַּמּוּ דִּבְרֵי אִיּוֹב, וְאַל יְהִי פִּתְחוֹן פֶּה לוֹמַר לְפָנֶיךָ ״בִּעַרְתִּי הַקֹּדֶשׁ״ (דברים כו:יג). כָּךְ רָאִיתִי בַּפְּסִיקְתָּא בְּפָרָשַׁת ׳עַשֵּׂר תְּעַשֵּׂר׳ (דברים יד:כב) מְפֹרָשׁ כֵּן, מִן הַפְּסוּקִים הָאֵלּוּ:


Until this point, he repeatedly prophesies many prophecies, and you say: Job’s words are ended? Rather, Job says as follows, “If I have not done so, may Job’s words be ended, and may he have no pretext for saying before You (Deut. 26:13), ‘I have cleared away what is holy.’” I saw this in the Pesikta of the portion עשר תעשר (ibid. 14), explained in this manner from these verses.



## Chapter 32


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 32:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:2:1)


**(מענה אליהוא:)** 
**מִמִּשְׁפַּחַת רָם.** אַבְרָהָם, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״הָאָדָם הַגָּדוֹל בָּעֲנָקִים״ (יהושע יד:טו) – זֶה אַבְרָהָם:


**Elihu’s Address**
**of the family of Ram** Abraham, as it is stated (Josh. 14:15): “the greatest man among the Anakim.” This is Abraham.


###### Rashi on Job 32:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:2:2)


**מֵאֱלֹהִים.** יוֹתֵר מִן הַמָּקוֹם:


**than God** More than the Omnipresent.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 32:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:3:1)


**וַיַּרְשִׁיעוּ אֶת אִיּוֹב.** זֶה אֶחָד מִן הַמִּקְרָאוֹת שֶׁתִּקְּנוּ סוֹפְרִים אֶת לְשׁוֹן הַכָּתוּב: ״וַיַּרְשִׁיעוּ כְּלַפֵּי הַמָּקוֹם בִּשְׁתִיקוֹתָם״ הָיָה לוֹ לִכְתֹּב, אֶלָּא שֶׁכִּנָּה הַכָּתוּב. וְכֵן ״וַיָּמִירוּ אֶת כְּבוֹדָם בְּתַבְנִית שׁוֹר״ (תהלים קו:כ) – ״כְּבוֹדִי״ הָיָה לוֹ לִכְתֹּב, אֶלָּא שֶׁכִּנָּה הַכָּתוּב. וְכֵן ״וְאַל אֶרְאֶה בְּרָעָתִי״ (במדבר יא:טו) – ״בְּרָעָתָם״ הָיָה לוֹ לִכְתֹּב, אֶלָּא שֶׁכִּנָּה הַכָּתוּב. וְכֵן הַרְבֵּה מְקוֹמוֹת (ספרי במדבר פד) וּבַמָּסֹרֶת הַגְּדוֹלָה:


**and condemned Job** This is one of the verses wherein the Scribes rectified the language of the Scripture. “And they condemned,” as directed against the Omnipresent, by remaining silent, should have been written, but Scripture euphemized. Similarly (Num. 11:15), “and do not let me see my misfortune.” It should have been written: “and do not let me see their misfortune,” but Scripture euphemized, and so many [other] instances, in Sifrei (ad loc.) and in the Great Masorah.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 32:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:4:1)


**חִכָּה.** הִמְתִּין:


###### Rashi on Job 32:4:2

**כִּי זְקֵנִים הֵמָּה מִמֶּנּוּ לְיָמִים.** הָאֲחֵרִים, לָכֵן חָדַל לוֹ עַד שֶׁשָּׁתְקוּ הֵם:


**because they were older than he in days** [i.e.,] the others [were older than he]. Therefore, he refrained until they were still.
