**זָחַלְתִּי.** יָרֵאתִי:



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 32:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:8:1)


**אָכֵן רוּחַ הִיא בֶאֱנוֹשׁ.** הַחָכְמָה, וְלֹא עַל יְדֵי יָמִים וְזִקְנָה, אֶלָּא רוּחַ הַמָּקוֹם הוּא:


**Indeed, it is a spirit in man** Wisdom [is a spirit in man] and [comes] not through days or old age, but it is the spirit of the Omnipresent.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 32:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:12:1)


**וְעָדֵיכֶם אֶתְבּוֹנָן.** הִתְבּוֹנַנְתִּי בָּכֶם וְרָאִיתִי, כִּי אֵין מוֹכִיחַ לְאִיּוֹב:


**Yea, I attended to you** I attended to you, and I saw that there is no one to convince Job.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 32:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:13:1)


**פֶּן תֹּאמְרוּ מָצָאנוּ חָכְמָה.** בִּשְׁתִיקוּתֵינוּ, שֶׁלֹּא לְהַקְנִיטוֹ עוֹד, כִּי **אֵל יִדְּפֶנּוּ וְלֹא אִישׁ.** לֹא הָיָה לָכֶם לְמַעֵט כְּבוֹד הַמָּקוֹם בְּכָךְ:


**Lest you say** You shall not say **We have found wisdom** by remaining silent in order not to provoke him anymore. **for God will crush him and not a man** You should not have belittled the honor of the Omnipresent thereby.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 32:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:14:1)


**וְלֹא עָרַךְ אֵלַי מִלִּין.** קַבָּלָה הִיא, אוֹ שֶׁלֹּא עָרַךְ אֵלַי מִלִּין:


**He did not set up words against me** This is a plaint: Woe that he did not set up words against me.


###### Rashi on Job 32:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:14:2)


**וּבְאִמְרֵיכֶם לֹא אֲשִׁיבֶנּוּ.** לֹא בְּאוֹתָן אֲמָרִים שֶׁהֲשִׁיבוֹתֶם לוֹ, הָיִיתִי מְשִׁיבוֹ:


**I would not reply to him with your sayings** I would not reply to him with those sayings with which you replied to him.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 32:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:15:1)


**הֶעְתִּיקוּ.** סָרוּ מֵהֶן מִלִּין:


**have removed themselves** Words have turned away from them.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 32:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:19:1)


**הִנֵּה בִטְנִי.** מָלֵא רוּחַ, דְּבָרִים לְהָשִׁיב בְּתַאֲוָה:


**Behold my belly** is full of wind, words to answer eagerly.


###### Rashi on Job 32:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:19:2)


**כְּיַיִן לֹא יִפָּתֵחַ.** כְּיַיִן חָדָשׁ שֶׁאֵינוֹ נִפְתָּח, וְהוּא נוֹפֵחַ, וְאַף בְּתוֹךְ אוֹבוֹת חֲדָשִׁים, וּמְבַקְּעָם:


**like wine that is not opened** Like new wine that is not opened, and which blows up, and even [if it is] within new wineskins, it bursts them.


###### Rashi on Job 32:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:19:3)


**אוֹבוֹת.** נוֹדוֹת עֲשׂוּיִין כְּקוֹמַת אָדָם, גְּבוֹהִים:


**like...wineskins** Wineskins made as tall as a man’s height.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Job 32:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:21:1)


**לֹא אֲכַנֶּה.** לֹא אֲשַׁנֶּה דְבָרַי לְהַחֲלִיפוֹ לִכְבוֹדוֹ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַרְתִּי לְמַעְלָה ״וַיַּרְשִׁיעוּ אֶת אִיּוֹב״ (איוב לב:ג) – זֶהוּ ׳כִּנּוּי׳, שֶׁמְּשַׁנֶּה אֶת הַדִּבּוּר בִּשְׁבִיל כְּבוֹדוֹ. וְאַף זֹאת לֹא אֶעֱשֶׂה, כִּי בְּפֶה מָלֵא אוֹכִיחֶנּוּ:


**neither will I euphemize** I will not change my words to replace them [with milder expressions] in deference to his [Job’s] honor, as I said above, “And they condemned Job.” This is a euphemism, which changes the expression because of His honor. I will not even do this, but I will reprove him explicitly.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Job 32:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/32:22:1)


**כִּמְעַט יִשָּׂאֵנִי.** יְסַלְּקֵנִי מִן הָעוֹלָם. וְיֵשׁ פּוֹתְרִים יִשָּׂאֵנִי כְּמוֹ ״וַתִּשָּׂא הָאָרֶץ מִפָּנָיו״ (נחום א:ה):


**or else...would carry me away** He would take me away from the world. Others interpret יִשָׂאֵנִי like (Nahum 1:5), “and the land raised up (ותשא) from before me [a pillar of smoke].”



## Chapter 33


### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 33:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:4:1)


**רוּחַ אֵל עָשָׂתְנִי.** וְאֵינִי חָזָק יוֹתֵר מִמְּךָ:


**The spirit of God made me** and I am no stronger than you.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 33:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:6:1)


**הֵן אֲנִי כְפִיךָ לָאֵל.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁשָּׁאַל פִּיךָ לְהִתְוַכֵּחַ לְמִי שֶׁלֹּא יְבַעֲתֶךָ (איוב יג:כא), הֵן אֲנִי בִּמְקוֹמוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא וּבִשְׁבִילוֹ, לְדַבֵּר דְּבָרָיו:


**Behold I am like your mouth to God** As your mouth asked to debate with one who would not terrify you, behold I am instead of the Holy One, blessed be He, and on His behalf, to speak His words.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 33:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:7:1)


**וְאַכְפִּי.** כְּפִיָּתִי, כְּמוֹ ״אָכַף עָלָיו פִּיהוּ״ (משלי טז:כו):


**and my coercion** Heb. אכפי, my coercion, like (Prov. 16:26), “his mouth forces (אכף).”



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Job 33:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:9:1)


**חָף.** טָהוֹר וּמְשֻׁפְשָׁף, כְּמוֹ (ביצה לה:) ״נָזִיר לֹא יָחוֹף רֹאשׁוֹ״, וּכְמוֹ (שבת נ.) ״בַּכֹּל חָפִין אֶת הַכֵּלִים״:


**I am clean** Heb. חף, pure and cleansed, like (Bezah 35b), “A nazirite may not cleanse (יחף) his head...” and like (Shabbath 50a),” we may cleanse (חפין) vessels with anything.”



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 33:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:10:1)


**תְּנוּאוֹת.** עֲלִילוֹת, לְשׁוֹן ״תּוֹאֲנָה״ (שופטים יד:ד) – לָשׁוּב מִן הַמָּקוֹם:


**pretenses** Heb. תאונות, pretenses to turn away from the Omnipresent, an expression of (Jud. 14: 4), “that he sought a pretense (תאנה).”



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 33:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:12:1)


**כִּי יִרְבֶּה אֱלוֹהַּ מֵאֱנוֹשׁ.** אֵין הַמָּקוֹם צָרִיךְ כָּל כָּךְ לְדַקְדֵּק אַחַר הָאָדָם, כִּי הַרְבֵּה יֵשׁ לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא צְדָקוֹת יוֹתֵר מֵאֱנוֹשׁ:


**for God exceeds man** God does not have to be so exact with man because the Holy One, blessed be He, has more virtues than man.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 33:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:16:1)


**אָז יִגְלֶה אֹזֶן אֲנָשִׁים.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁעָשָׂה לַאֲבִימֶלֶךְ בַּחֲלוֹם הַלַּיְלָה (בראשית כ:ג):


**Then He opens the ear of men** as He did to Abimelech in the dream of the night.


###### Rashi on Job 33:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:16:2)


**וּבְמוֹסָרָם יַחְתֹּם.** חוֹתְמָן וְאוֹסְרָן בְּיִסּוּרִין עַל עֲוֹנָם:


**and with their chastisement He seals [them]** He seals them and binds them with suffering because of their iniquity.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Job 33:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:17:1)


**לְהָסִיר אָדָם.** בְּחָלְיוֹ:


**To restrain man** in his illness.


###### Rashi on Job 33:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:17:2)


**מַעֲשֶׂה.** שֶׁהָיָה בְדַעְתּוֹ לַעֲשׂוֹת:


**a deed** that he had intended to do.


###### Rashi on Job 33:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:17:3)


**וְגֵוָה מִגֶּבֶר יְכַסֶּה.** בְּיִסּוּרָיו, לְמַעַן הַכְנִיעוֹ וּלְהַשְׁפִּילוֹ לָשׁוּב, כְּדֵי שֶׁיֶּחְשַׂךְ נַפְשׁוֹ מִמִּיתָה גְּמוּרָה:


**and He covers haughtiness from man** with his suffering, in order to vanquish him and humble him to repent, so that he withhold his soul from utter death.


###### Rashi on Job 33:17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:17:4)


**גֵּוָה.** כְּמוֹ גַּאֲוָה:


**haughtiness** Heb. וְגֵוָה, like גַאֲוָה.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Job 33:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:18:1)


**וְחַיָּתוֹ מֵעֲבֹר בַּשָּׁלַח.** בְּשִׁלְחוֹ וְחַרְבּוֹ שֶׁל מַלְאַךְ הַמָּוֶת, כְּלוֹמַר: לְטוֹבָתְךָ הֵבִיא עָלֶיךָ יִסּוּרִין:


**and his life from perishing by the sword** by the weapon and the sword of the Angel of Death; that is to say, He brought suffering upon you for your good.


###### Rashi on Job 33:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:18:2)


**שֶׁלַח.** כְּלִי זַיִן, וְכֵן ״יָדוֹ אַחַת מַחֲזֶקֶת הַשֶּׁלַח״ (נחמיה ד:יא):


**by the sword** Heb. בשלח, a weapon, and so in Ezra (Neh. 4:11): “and one was holding the weapon (השלח).”



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 33:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:19:1)


**וְהוּכַח.** לְשׁוֹן תּוֹכָחָה, שֶׁיָּשׁוּב מֵעֲוֹנוֹ:


**And he is chastised** an expression of reproof that he repent of his iniquity,


###### Rashi on Job 33:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:19:2)


**וְרוֹב עֲצָמָיו.** שֶׁהוּא אֵיתָן, מוֹכִיחָן **בְּמַכְאוֹב**:


**and most of his bones**, which are strong, He chastises with pain.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 33:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:20:1)


**וְזִהֲמַתּוּ חַיָּתוֹ לָחֶם.** מְמַאֶסֶת נַפְשׁוֹ וְחַיָּתוֹ שֶׁל חוֹלֶה כָּל מַאֲכָל:


**And his living spirit causes him to abhor food** The soul and the life of the sick person cause him to abhor all food.


###### Rashi on Job 33:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:20:2)


**וְנַפְשׁוֹ.** מְזַהֶמֶת כָּל מַאֲכַל תַּאֲוָה:


**and his soul** abhors all tasty food.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Job 33:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:21:1)


**יִכֶל בְּשָׂרוֹ מֵרֹאִי.** יֶחְשַׁךְ בְּשָׂרוֹ מִמַּרְאֶה, כְּמוֹ ״כָּלוּ בַדְּמָעוֹת עֵינַי״ (איכה ב:יא), ״מְכַלּוֹת עֵינַיִם״ (ויקרא כו:טז):


**His flesh is consumed from sight** Heb. יכל בשרו מראי, His flesh is withdrawn from sight, like (Lam 2:11): “My eyes are spent (כלו) with tears”; (Lev. 26:16), “that cause the eyes to pine way (מכלות).


###### Rashi on Job 33:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:21:2)


**וְשֻׁפּוּ עַצְמוֹתָיו.** מִתְנַתְּקִין מִמְּקוֹמוֹ, וְשֻׁפּוּ כְּמוֹ דְּשָׁף מִדּוּכְתֵּיהּ (חולין מב:):


**and his bones are dislocated** Heb. ושפו. They are dislocated from their place. [The word] שֻׁפּוּ is like (Hullin 42b), “that sprung (שף) from its place.”


###### Rashi on Job 33:21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:21:3)


**לֹא רֻאּוּ.** אֵין לָהֶם מַרְאֶה וְתֹאַר:


**to unsightliness** They have neither appearance nor form.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Job 33:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:23:1)


**אֶחָד מִנִּי אָלֶף.** בְּמַגִּידֵי פִּשְׁעוֹ, וְהוּא יָלִיץ יָשְׁרוֹ:


**one out of a thousand** of those who testify to his guilt, and he intercedes with his uprightness.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Job 33:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:24:1)


**וַיְחֻנֶּנּוּ.** הַמָּקוֹם:


**He is gracious to him** i.e., the Omnipresent.


###### Rashi on Job 33:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:24:2)


**פְּדָעֵהוּ.** כְּמוֹ פְּדָאֵהוּ, אָלֶ״ף מִתְחַלֶּפֶת בְּעַיִ״ן. וְהָעִנְיָן מוֹכִיחַ: **מָצָאתִי כֹפֶר**, וְהוּא ״פִּדְיוֹן״ (שמות כא:ל):


**Redeem him** Heb. פדעהו, like פְדָאֵהוּ. The “alef” is replaced by an “ayin.” The context proves it. “I found ransom,” which is redemption.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Job 33:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:25:1)


**רֻטֲפַשׁ בְּשָׂרוֹ.** רוֹטְפַשׁ אֶשְׁקוֹשָׁ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**His flesh is agitated from shaking** Heb. רֻטֲפַש means escos in French, shaken.


###### Rashi on Job 33:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:25:2)


**מִנֹּעַר.** נִתְעוֹרֵר בְּשָׂרוֹ מִן הַנּוֹעַר, שֶׁהוּא מְנוֹעָר וּמְשֻׁנָּק בְּיִסּוּרֵי חָלְיוֹ. וְאֵין לַתֵּיבָה זוֹ דִּמְיוֹן בַּמִּקְרָא, וּלְפִי הָעִנְיָנִים הֻצְרַכְנוּ לְפָרְשׁוֹ כְּמַשְׁמָעוּת הָעִנְיָן. וְהַפּוֹתְרוֹ בִּלְשׁוֹן ׳רֶפֶשׁ׳, וְאוֹמֵר הַטֵּי״ת נִתּוֹסְפָה בּוֹ, כְּמוֹ ״נִצְטַדָּק״ (בראשית מד:טז) – טוֹעֶה הוּא מְאֹד. כִּי אֵין טֵי״ת מִתּוֹסֶפֶת בַּתֵּיבָה, כִּי אִם בִּלְשׁוֹן ׳מִתְפַּעֵל׳ אוֹ ׳נִתְפַּעֵל׳, וּבַתֵּיבָה שֶׁיְּסוֹדָהּ צַדִּ״י; וְלֹא בִּשְׁאָר תֵּיבוֹת. כְּמוֹ ״צֶדֶק״ (איוב ח:ג) וְ״נִצְטַדָּק״ (בראשית מד:טז); וּכְמוֹ ״צָרוּעַ״ (ויקרא יג:מד) – ׳נִצְטָרַע׳; ״צִיר נֶאֱמָן״ (משלי כה:יג) – ״וַיֵּלְכוּ וַיִּצְטַיָּרוּ״ (יהושע ט:ד); ״צֵדָה״ – ״הִצְטַיַּדְנוּ״ (יהושע ט:יב):


**from shaking** His flesh was agitated from the shaking, that it was shaken [by the suffering of] his illness. This word [רֻטֲפַש] has no similarity in Scripture, and according to the context, we found it necessary to explain according to the meaning of the topic. But the one who interprets it as an expression of mire (רפש) and claims that the “teth” is added to it, as in (Gen. 44:16), “How can we clear ourselves (נצטדק)?” is in great error, because the “teth” is added to a word only in the reflexive voice, and to a word in which there is a radical “zaddi,” but nor in other words. for example, צדק and נצטדק, and like (Lev. 13:44) צרוּע, stricken with “zaraath,” and נצטרע, was stricken with “zaraath.” (Prov. 25: 13), “an emissary (ציר) faithful,” and (Josh. 9:4), “and they went and disguised as ambassadors (ויצטירו)”; (Gen. 45:21) “צֵדָה, provision,” and (Josh. 9:12) “הִצְטַיַדְנוּ, we took... for our provision.”


###### Rashi on Job 33:25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:25:3)


**יָשׁוּב.** בְּכֹחוֹ וּבִרְפוּאָה לִימֵי בַּחֲרוּתוֹ:


**he will be restored** with his strength and with healing to the days of his youth.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Job 33:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:26:1)


**בִּתְרוּעָה.** בִּתְפִלָּה:



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Job 33:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:27:1)


**יָשׁוּר עַל אֲנָשִׁים.** יַעֲשֶׂה שׁוּרוֹת שֶׁל אֲנָשִׁים כְּשֶׁנִּצּוֹל מֵחָלְיוֹ, וְיִתְוַדֶּה לְיוֹצְרוֹ:


**He makes a row of men** He makes a row of men when he is saved from his illness and confesses [before them] to his Creator.


###### Rashi on Job 33:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:27:2)


**וְלֹא שָׁוָה לִי.** לֹא נִהְיָה לִי שָׂכָר בִּדְבָרָיו:


**but I did not profit** I had no profit in its matters.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Job 33:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/33:29:1)


**פַּעֲמַיִם שָׁלוֹשׁ.** מְיַסְּרוֹ עַל עֲוֹנוֹתָיו בְּחָלְיוֹ, שֶׁלֹּא לְהַשְׁחִיתוֹ; וְאִם יוֹתֵר יַכְעִיס לְפָנָיו, יִדְאַג מִן גֵּיהִנֹּם וּמִן הַמִּיתָה; שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וְעַל אַרְבָּעָה לֹא אֲשִׁיבֶנּוּ״ (עמוס א:ג):


**twice or thrice** He chastises him for his iniquities with his illness in order not to destroy him, but if he provokes Him further, he should be concerned with Gehinnom or death, as it is written, “Yea for four, I will not return them” (Amos 1:3).



## Chapter 34


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 34:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:3:1)


**(מענה אליהו):** 
**כִּי אֹזֶן מִלִּין תִּבְחָן.** כַּאֲשֶׁר הַחֵיךְ יִטְעַם הָאֹכֶל:


**For the ear tests words** as the palate tastes food.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 34:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:5:1)


**מִשְׁפָּטִי.** הוֹכָחַת דְּבָרִים:


**my right** [my] To prove my words.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 34:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:6:1)


**אֲכַזֵּב.** אֲכַזִּיב שׁוֹפְטִי, שֶׁלֹּא דָנַנִּי אֱמֶת:


**I call a liar** I accuse my Judge of being a liar, because He did not judge me fairly.


###### Rashi on Job 34:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:6:2)


**אָנוּשׁ.** חוֹלֶה מַכָּתִי, כְּמוֹ ״וַיֵּאָנַשׁ״ (שמואל ב יב:טו):


**incurable** Heb. אנוש, My wound is sore, like (II Sam. 12:15), “and it became mortally ill (ויאנש).”


###### Rashi on Job 34:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:6:3)


**חִצִּי.** נִגְעִי:


**my wound** Heb. חצי [lit. my arrow].



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Job 34:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:9:1)


**לֹא יִסְכָּן גָּבֶר.** לֹא יֵהָנֶה אָדָם, אִם מַתְמִים דְּרָכָיו:


**A man derives no benefit** A man derives no benefit if he perfects his ways.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 34:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:13:1)


**מִי פָקַד עָלָיו אָרְצָה.** מִי מוֹשֵׁל בּוֹ, לֵאמֹר: לָמָּה כֵּן עָשִׂיתָ וְלֹא צִוִּיתִיךָ כֵּן? כִּי מִי צִוָּהוּ בְּמֶמְשַׁלְתּוֹ, וְלָמָּה יִמְצָא תּוֹאֲנוֹת לַבְּרִיּוֹת? אֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ לְהָשִׁיב דָּבָר לְמוֹשֵׁל בּוֹ, לֵאמֹר: בִּשְׁבִיל זֹאת הֲרֵעוֹתִי לוֹ:


**Who gave Him a charge over the earth** Who rules over Him, saying, ‘Why did You do so?’ or ‘I did not command You so’? For who gave Him a charge over His dominion, and why should He find pretexts for His creatures? He need not answer one who rules over Him by saying ‘Because of this I harmed him.’



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 34:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:14:1)


**אִם יָשִׂים אֵלָיו לִבּוֹ.** עַל אָדָם, לְהַשְׁחִיתוֹ, לָמָּה יָבֹא בַּעֲלִילָה עָלָיו? הֲרֵי מִיָּד יֶאֱסֹף אֵלָיו **רוּחוֹ וְנִשְׁמָתוֹ.** רוּחַ וּנְשָׁמָה שֶׁלּוֹ שֶׁהִיא נְפוּחָה בּוֹ, וְאֵין מוֹחֶה בְּיָדוֹ, וְיִגְוַע כָּל בָּשָׂר יָחַד:


**If He puts** His heart on man to destroy him, why should He come upon him with a pretext? He can immediately gather in to Himself... **His spirit and His soul** His spirit and His soul that He blew into him, and no one will stop Him. Thus, all flesh will perish together.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 34:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:16:1)


**וְאִם בִּינָה.** וְאִם אַתָּה רוֹצֶה הָבִין, שִׁמְעָה זֹאת. **בִּינָה** זוֹ אֵינָהּ שֵׁם דָּבָר, אֶלָּא לְשׁוֹן צִוּוּי, כְּמוֹ ״בִּינָה הֲגִיגִי״ (תהלים ה:ב). לְפִיכָךְ שְׁתַּיִם אֵלּוּ טַעֲמָן לְמַעְלָה, בַּבֵּי״ת, וְכָל שְׁאָר בִּינָה טַעֲמָן לְמַטָּה, בַּנּוּ״ן:


**And if you wish, understand** And if you wish, understand. **hearken to this** This בּינה is not a noun but an imperative expression, like (Ps. 5:2), “consider (בּינה) my meditation.” Therefore, these two are accented on the first syllable, on the “beth,” whereas all the others are accented on the final syllable, on the “nun.”



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Job 34:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:17:1)


**הַאַף שׂוֹנֵא מִשְׁפָּט.** מִשְׁפָּטוֹ שֶׁל שׁוֹפֵט צֶדֶק **יַחֲבוֹשׁ** הַמָּקוֹם? וְאִם תֹּאמַר: אֵינוֹ מִשְׁפָּט, כִּי לֹא כַדִּין עָשָׂה, אֶפְשָׁר זֹאת מִי שֶׁהוּא צַדִּיק מְאֹד תַּרְשִׁיעַ?


**Will...even one who hates judgment** Will the Omnipresent heal [even the one who hates the judgment] of the just Judge? If you say that [to do so] is not judgment, for He acted not in accordance with the law, is it possible that you should condemn One Who is very righteous?


###### Rashi on Job 34:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:17:2)


**כַּבִּיר.** לְשׁוֹן מְאֹד:


**very** Heb. כביר, an expression denoting “exceedingly.”



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Job 34:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:18:1)


**הַאֲמֹר לְמֶלֶךְ בְּלִיָּעַל.** וְכִי הָגוּן וְרָאוּי לֹאמַר לְמֶלֶךְ: בְּלִיָּעַל, וְלֹאמַר **רָשָׁע אֶל נְדִיבִים**?!


**Is it proper to say of a king, ‘scoundrel’** Is it fit and proper to say of a king, ‘scoundrel,’ or ‘wicked’ of nobles?



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 34:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:19:1)


**אֲשֶׁר לֹא נָשָׂא פְּנֵי שָׂרִים.** כְּלוֹמַר: לְמֶלֶךְ עוֹלָם, אֲשֶׁר אֵין מַשּׂוֹא פָנִים לְפָנָיו:


**Who did not respect princes** That is to say, the King of the world, before Whom there is no favoritism.


###### Rashi on Job 34:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:19:2)


**שׁוֹעַ.** שַׂר, כְּמוֹ ״וְלַכִּילַי לֹא יֵאָמֵר שׁוֹעַ״ (ישעיהו לב:ה):


**a prince** Heb. שוע, a prince, as in (Isa. 32:5), “nor shall a deceitful person be said to be noble (שוע).”



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 34:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:20:1)


**רֶגַע יָמוּתוּ.** כְּשֶׁיִּרְצֶה:


**In a moment, they die** when He wishes.


###### Rashi on Job 34:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:20:2)


**וַחֲצוֹת לָיְלָה.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁעָשָׂה לְמִצְרַיִם (שמות יא:ד-ח):


**and at midnight** as He did to the Egyptians,


###### Rashi on Job 34:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:20:3)


**יְגֹעֲשׁוּ עָם.** כְּלוֹמַר: בְּרֶגַע יַעַבְרוּ:


**an [entire] people quakes** in a moment and passes away.


###### Rashi on Job 34:20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:20:4)


**וְיָסִירוּ אַבִּיר.** כֹּחַ שֶׁלָּהֶם:


###### Rashi on Job 34:20:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:20:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:20:5)


**לֹא בְיָד.** לֹא יִשְׁנוּ בְיָדָם וְאֵין לָהֶם כֹּחַ:


**it is not in their hands** it is not in their hands, and they have no strength.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Job 34:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:23:1)


**כִּי לֹא עַל אִישׁ יָשִׂים עוֹד.** כְּלוֹמַר: אָמַרְתָּ: ״לֹא אַךְ יָשִׂים בִּי״ (איוב כג:ו), רַק חֲטָאַי לְבַדִּי; וַאֲנִי מְשִׁיבְךָ: לֹא יָשִׂים הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא עַל בְּרִיּוֹתָיו עֲלִילוֹת תּוֹסֶפֶת עַל פִּשְׁעָם. **עוֹד** לְשׁוֹן תּוֹסֶפֶת:


**For He will not place additional [guilt] on a man** That is to say: You said (23:6), ‘He will not place upon me anything but my sins,’ only my sins alone, and I reply to you that the Holy One, blessed be He, will not place on His creatures additional accusations over their transgression. [The word] עוד is an expression of addition.


###### Rashi on Job 34:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:23:2)


**לָהֲלֹךְ אֶל אֵל בַּמִּשְׁפָּט.** כְּשֶׁהוּא בָּא עַל בְּרִיּוֹתָיו לְהִתְוַכֵּחַ עִמָּהֶם:


**to go to God in judgment** When He comes upon His creatures to debate with them.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Job 34:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:24:1)


**יָרֹעַ.** יְרוֹצֵץ, כְּמוֹ ״רֹעַ הִתְרוֹעֲעָה״ (ישעיה כד:יט):


**He shatters** Heb. ירע, [like] ירוֹצֵץ. He shatters, and like (Isa. 24:19), “has broken (התרעעה).”


###### Rashi on Job 34:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:24:2)


**וַיַּעֲמֵד אֲחֵרִים.** בִּמְקוֹמָם:


**and He stands others** in their stead.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Job 34:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:25:1)


**לָכֵן יַכִּיר מַעְבָּדֵיהֶם.** לְפִי שֶׁהוּא מַכִּיר מַעְבָּדֵיהֶם הַטּוֹב וְהָרַע, וְאֵין צֹרֶךְ לוֹ לְהִתְוַכֵּחַ:


**Because He recognizes their deeds** Because He recognizes their deeds, whether good or evil, He does not need to debate.


###### Rashi on Job 34:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:25:2)


**וְהָפַךְ לַיְלָה.** עֵת חֹשֶׁךְ וְצָרָה לָהֶם, **וְיִדַּכָּאוּ**:


**He turns night** A time of darkness and trouble for them, and they are crushed.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Job 34:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:26:1)


**תַּחַת רְשָׁעִים.** בִּמְקוֹמָם:


**In the place of the wicked** Heb. תחת, in their location.


###### Rashi on Job 34:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:26:2)


**סְפָקָם.** לְאַנְשֵׁי סְדוֹם (בראשית יט:א-כט):


**He smote them** The people of Sodom.


###### Rashi on Job 34:26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:26:3)


**בִּמְקוֹם רוֹאִים.** לְעֵין כֹּל:


**in the place of viewers** Before everyone’s eyes.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Job 34:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:27:1)


**אֲשֶׁר עַל כֵּן סָרוּ מֵאַחֲרָיו.** כְּמוֹ ״עַל כֵּן יָדַעְתָּ חֲנֹתֵנוּ בַּמִּדְבָּר״ (במדבר י:לא). כְּמוֹ: מִפְּנֵי אֲשֶׁר סָרוּ מֵאַחֲרָיו וְהֵבִיאוּ לְפָנָיו וְעָלָיו צַעֲקַת דַּלִּים:


**Because they turned away from following Him** Heb. אשר על-כן as in (Num. 10:31), “because (על- כן) you knew our camping in the desert.” [This is] like: because (מפני אשר) they turned away from following Him, and they brought before Him and upon Him the cry of the poor.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Job 34:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:29:1)


**וְהוּא יַשְׁקִיט.** מַשְׁקִיט אֶת הַדַּלִּים מֵאוֹתָן הָעוֹשְׁקִים, **וּמִי יַרְשִׁיעַ** עוֹד? וּבְעוֹד **שֶׁהִסְתִּיר פָּנִים, מִי יְשׁוּרֶנּוּ**?


**When He quiets** He gives the poor [some peace and] quiet from those who oppress [them]. Now who will deal wickedly anymore? And as long as He hid His face, who will see Him?


###### Rashi on Job 34:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:29:2)


**וְעַל גּוֹי וְעַל אָדָם יָחַד.** כִּיחִידִים כִּמְרֻבִּים שָׁוֶה לְפָנָיו, לְהַשְׁקִיט וּלְהַסְתִּיר פָּנִים:


**whether [it be done] to a nation or to a man, alike** As for individuals, [or] as for a multitude it is the same to Him to quiet [them] and to hide His face.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Job 34:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:30:1)


**מִמְּלֹךְ אָדָם חָנֵף מִמֹּקְשֵׁי עָם.** עַל דַּלִּים שֶׁמָּלַךְ:


**And it shall be quiet, that a flattering man shall not reign because of the snares of the people** Over the poor, who reigned.


###### Rashi on Job 34:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:30:2)


**מִמּוֹקְשֵׁי עָם.** עָוֹן, וְלֵאלוֹהַּ כָּזֶה לֹא הָיָה לְךָ לוֹמַר ״בְּלִיַּעַל״ וְ״רָשָׁע״ (איוב לד:יח):


**because of the snares of the people** [who were guilty] of iniquity, and concerning such a God, you should not have said, ‘scoundrel,’ and ‘wicked.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Job 34:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:31:1)


**כִּי אֶל אֵל הֶאָמַר.** לְהֵאָמֵר אֵלָיו. נָכוֹן וְרָאוּי מֵאֵת כָּל סוֹבְלֵי יִסּוּרִים לְהֵאָמֵר: **נָשָׂאתִי**, וּמְקַבֵּל וְסוֹבֵל אֲנִי מִשְׁפָּטֶיךָ, **וְלֹא אֶחְבֹּל** בְּעַצְמִי. וְעוֹד צָרִיךְ הַנִּדּוֹן לוֹמַר:


**For to God should be said** To be said to Him. It is fitting and proper for all those who suffer pains to say, ‘I bear, accept, and tolerate Your judgments, and I will not destroy myself.’ Further, the one who is judged must say...



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Job 34:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:32:1)


**בִּלְעֲדֵי אֶחֱזֶה.** לְבַד מַה שֶּׁאֲנִי יוֹדֵעַ לִרְאוֹת וּלְהָבִין בִּדְבָרֶיךָ, **אַתָּה הוֹרֵנִי, וְאִם אָוֶן פָּעַלְתִּי, לֹא אוֹסִיף**:


**Besides what I see** Besides what I know to perceive in Your words, You instruct me, and if I have committed injustice, I will not continue.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Job 34:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:33:1)


**הַמֵעִמְּךָ יְשַׁלְּמֶנָּהּ כִּי מָאַסְתָּ וְגוֹ׳.** כָּךְ אָמַר אֱלִיהוּ לְאִיּוֹב: הַמֵעִמְּךָ הָיָה לוֹ לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לְהִמָּלֵךְ בְּפֵרָעוֹן תַּשְׁלוּמִין מִמְּךָ?


**From you should He pay it, for you despised etc.** so did Elihu say to Job, “Should the Holy One, blessed be He, have taken counsel from you about repaying you?”


###### Rashi on Job 34:33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:33:2)


**כִּי מָאַסְתָּ.** לֵאמֹר: ״מָאַסְתִּי לֹא לְעוֹלָם אֶחְיֶה״ (איוב ז:טז), ״יַתֵּר יָדוֹ וִיבַצְּעֵנִי״ (איוב ו:ט)?! סָבוּר אַתָּה שֶׁיִּגְבֶּה מִמְּךָ לְפִי דַעְתְּךָ וּרְצוֹנְךָ, וְאַתָּה תִבְחַר הַתַּשְׁלוּמִין, וְלֹא אֲנִי? בִּמְקוֹם הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא דִּבֵּר אֱלִיהוּ, בִּתְמִיהַּ:


**for you despised** saying (above 7: 16), “I despised [my life]; I will not live forever”; (above 6:9) “enlarge His hand and finish me off.” Do you think that He would collect from you according to your thoughts and your desire, and you would choose the payment—not I? Elihu spoke instead of the Holy One, blessed be He, with a question.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on Job 34:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:34:1)


**אַנְשֵׁי לֵבָב יֹאמְרוּ לִי.** כִּי אִיּוֹב לֹא בְדַעַת יְדַבֵּר:


**Men of understanding will say to me** that Job does not speak with knowledge.



### Verse 36

###### Rashi on Job 34:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:36:1)


**אָבִי יִבָּחֵן.** אָבִי. אַתָּה הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא אָב לַכֹּל, הַלְוַאי **יִבָּחֵן אִיּוֹב עַד נֶצַח**, לְמַעַן תִּהְיֶה תְּשׁוּבָה **לְאַנְשֵׁי אָוֶן.** וְאַף לִבְנֵי אָדָם דִּבֵּר הַכָּתוּב כֵּן, כְּמוֹ ״אָבִי דָּבָר גָּדוֹל וְגוֹ׳״ (מלכים ב ה:יג). (דָּבָר אַחֵר: אָבִי. כְּמוֹ ״וְתָפֵר הָאֲבִיּוֹנָה״ (קהלת יב:ה), לְשׁוֹן רָצוֹן. כָּךְ חִבְּרוֹ מְנַחֵם. וְכֵן פִּתְרוֹנוֹ: **אָבִי יִבָּחֵן אִיּוֹב עַד נֶצַח.** רְצוֹנִי וּמְאַוְּיִי שֶׁיִּבָּחֵן אִיּוֹב עַד נֶצַח סא״א).


**My Father! May [Job] be tried** You are my Father. The Holy One, blessed be He, is the Father of all. If only Job were tried to eternity, in order that there be repentance among men of iniquity. And also to men Scripture speaks in this manner (II Kings 5:13): “‘Master (אבי), if the prophet spoke to you to do a difficult thing etc.’” Another explanation: אבי is like (Ecc. 12:5), “and the sexual desire (האביונה) will fail,” an expression of desire. Menachem (Machbereth p. 11) associated it in this manner, and this is its interpretation: אָבִי יִבָּחֵן אִיוֹב עַד נֶצַח, my wish and my desire are that Job should be tried to eternity (omitted in some editions).



### Verse 37

###### Rashi on Job 34:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:37:1)


**יִסְפּוֹק.** לְשׁוֹן רוֹב דְּבָרִים וַהֲרָמַת קוֹל:


**he speaks loquaciously** Heb. יספוק is an expression of many words and raising the voice.


###### Rashi on Job 34:37:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:37:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/34:37:2)


**בֵּינֵינוּ יִסְפּוֹק.** הוּא אוֹמֵר: מַה לָּנוּ וּלְדִבְרֵי אִיּוֹב? ״אַף אָמְנָם שָׁגִיתִי אִתִּי תָּלִין״ (איוב יט:ד)! וְאֵינוֹ יוֹדֵעַ שֶׁאָנוּ נֶעֱנָשִׁים, וּמַסְפִּיק לָנוּ עֲוֹנוֹת, עַל שֶׁאָנוּ שׁוֹמְעִים. כָּךְ מְפֹרָשׁ בְּוַיִּקְרָא רַבָּה (ויקרא רבה ד:ו):


**among us he speaks loquaciously** He asks, Why should we be concerned with his words? (above 19:4), ‘And even if I have indeed erred, let my error stay with me,’ and he does not know that we are punished, and he increases iniquities for us because we are listening. So it is explained in Leviticus Rabbah (4:6).

**Surely you who say** Surely you, whose cry comes only because of Him, and you say and cry that you do not see Him to debate with Him. That is to say that those who complain and cry, He does not hasten to answer, yet you cry that you do not see Him?
