###### Rashi on Job 35:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/35:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/35:15:2)


**וְלֹא יָדַע בַּפַּשׁ מְאֹד.** בְּרוֹב חֲטָאֲךָ הִתְנַהֵג הַבּוֹרֵא כְּאִלּוּ לֹא יְדָעָם:


**and He did not know because of the great multiplicity** Because of your many sins, the Creator acted as though He did not know them.


###### Rashi on Job 35:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/35:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/35:15:3)


**בַּפַּשׁ.** לְשׁוֹן רוֹב, כְּמוֹ ״וּפִשְׁתֶּם״ (מלאכי ג:כ), וּכְמוֹ ״כִּי תָפוּשׁוּ וְגוֹ׳״ (ירמיה נ:יא); ״כִּי פָשָׂה הַנֶּגַע״ (ויקרא יג:נא):


**of the...multiplicity** Heb. בפש, an expression of multiplicity, like (Malachi 3:20), “and be fat (ופשתם),” and like (Jer. 50:11), “as you become fat (תפושו)”; (Lev. 13:51), “that the mark has spread (פשה).”



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 35:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/35:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/35:16:1)


**יַכְבִּיר.** לְשׁוֹן מְאֹד:


**he increases** Heb. כביר, an expression meaning, exceedingly.



## Chapter 36


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Job 36:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:1:1)


**וַיֹּסֶף אֱלִיהוּא.** רָאִיתִי בְּטַעֲמֵי רַבִּי מֹשֶׁה הַדַּרְשָׁן, שֶׁלְּפִיכָךְ אָמַר שְׁלֹשָׁה מַעֲנוֹת, כְּנֶגֶד שְׁלֹשֶׁת רֵיעֵי אִיּוֹב. וְזֶה רְבִיעִי, לְפִיכָךְ נִקְרָא תּוֹסֶפֶת:


**Then Elihu continued** I saw in the reasons of Rabbi Moshe Hadarshan (the preacher), that he delivered three addresses corresponding to Job’s three companions, and this is the fourth one. Therefore it is called an addition.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 36:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:2:1)


**כַּתַּר לִי זְעֵיר וַאֲחַוֶּךָּ.** כֻּלּוֹ לְשׁוֹן אֲרַמִּי הוּא: הוֹחֵל לִי מְעַט וַאֲגִידְךָ. כַּתַּר לְשׁוֹן הוֹחֵל, כַּאֲשֶׁר תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן ״וּלְתוֹרָתוֹ אִיִּים יְיַחֵלוּ״ (ישעיהו מב:ד): ״יְכַתְּרוּן״:


**Wait awhile for me and I will tell you** This is completely Aramaic: Wait for me a little and I will tell you. [The word] כַּתַּר is an expression of waiting, as Jonathan rendered (Isa. 42:4): “and for his instruction, islands shall long (יכתרון).”


###### Rashi on Job 36:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:2:2)


**לֶאֱלוֹהַּ.** לַבָּאִים בִּמְקוֹמוֹ:


**God has** Those representing Him have.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 36:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:4:1)


**תְּמִים דֵּעוֹת עִמָּךְ.** הִנֵּה אַתְחִיל עַתָּה לְדַבֵּר עִמָּךְ, שֶׁאַתָּה סָבוּר שֶׁדַּעְתְּךָ שְׁלֵמָה:


**you have perfect knowledge** Behold, I will now begin to speak with you since you think that your knowledge is perfect.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 36:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:5:1)


**הֵן אֵל כַּבִּיר.** בְּחָכְמָה וּבְרַחֲמִים:


**Behold God is great** in wisdom and in mercy.


###### Rashi on Job 36:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:5:2)


**וְלֹא יִמְאָס.** עָנִי:


**and will not despise** the poor man.


###### Rashi on Job 36:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:5:3)


**כַּבִּיר כֹּחַ לֵב.** הוּא לְהִתְנַקֵּם, לְפִיכָךְ לֹא יְחַיֶּה רָשָׁע:


**He is great in strength of heart** to take revenge. Therefore, He will not allow a wicked man to live.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 36:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:7:1)


**לֹא יִגְרַע מִצַּדִּיק עֵינָיו.** עַד אֲשֶׁר עִם מְלָכִים יוֹשִׁיבֵם:


**He does not withdraw His eyes from the righteous** until He seats him with kings.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 36:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:8:1)


**וְאִם אֲסוּרִים.** וְאִם תִּרְאֶה צַדִּיקִים שֶׁיִּהְיוּ אֲסוּרִים בַּזִּיקִים שֶׁל יִסּוּרִין וָחֳלָיִים, אוֹ יִלָּכְדוּ **בְּחַבְלֵי עֹנִי.** בִּלְשׁוֹן גּוֹרָל וּמֵיתָרִים נִקְרָא חַבְלֵי, וּבִלְשׁוֹן דְּאָגָה נִקְרָא ״חֶבְלֵי יוֹלֵדָה״ (הושע יג:יג). **בְּחַבְלֵי עֹנִי.** קוֹדְרֵ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז. אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא שֶׁחָטְאוּ לְפָנָיו, וּבָא לִיפָּרַע מֵהֶם לְטוֹבָתָם, לְנַקּוֹתָם, וּלְהַזְהִירָם לָשׁוּב אֵלָיו:


**And if** you see righteous men who are bound with chains of suffering and ailments, or caught with ropes of poverty. In an expression of lots or ropes, they are called חבלים, in an expression of worry, they are called חֶבְלֵי יוֹלֵדָה, pangs of a childbearing woman. **with ropes of poverty** Heb. בחבלי cordes in French. It is only because they sinned before Him, and He comes to requite them for their own good, to cleanse them and to admonish them to return to Him.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Job 36:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:9:1)


**וַיַּגֵּד לָהֶם פָּעֳלָם.** בַּיִּסּוּרִין הַלָּלוּ מוֹדִיעָם שֶׁחָטְאוּ לְפָנָיו. וְכָל דִּבְרֵי אֱלִיהוּא תַּנְחוּמִין שְׁלֵמִים הָיוּ, וְלֹא קִנְתּוּרִין; כְּלוֹמַר: אַל תִּדְאַג עַל יִסּוּרִין, אִם צָדַקְתָּ, כִּי לְטוֹבָה הֵן לְךָ:


**He tells them their deed** With these sufferings, He lets them know that they sinned before Him. All the words of Elihu were complete consolations and not chidings; i.e., do not worry about the sufferings if you are righteous, because they are for your good,



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 36:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:10:1)


**וַיֹּאמֶר.** כִּי בְּיִסּוּרִין יְשׁוּבוּן מֵאָוֶן:


**and He says** that with sufferings, they will repent of iniquity.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 36:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:11:1)


**אִם יִשְׁמְעוּ.** וְיַעַבְדוּהוּ:


**If they understand** and worship Him.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 36:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:13:1)


**וְחַנְפֵי לֵב.** שֶׁהֵם רְשָׁעִים, הֵם יָשִׂימוּ אָף – בְּבֹא יִסּוּרִין עֲלֵיהֶם, מְחָרְפִים וּמְגַדְּפִים:


**But the wicked of heart** who are wicked—they bring about wrath; when suffering comes upon them, they curse and blaspheme,


###### Rashi on Job 36:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:13:2)


**וְלֹא יְשַׁוְּעוּ.** לְפָנָיו בִּתְחִנָּה:


**and they do not beg mercy** with supplication


###### Rashi on Job 36:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:13:3)


**כִּי אֲסָרָם.** כְּשֶׁהוּא אוֹסְרָן בְּיִסּוּרִין, לְפִיכָךְ לֹא יִרְפָּאֵם:


**when He binds them** with sufferings; therefore He does not heal them.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 36:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:14:1)


**תָּמוּת בַּנֹּעַר.** וּבְשִׁנּוּק שֶׁעֲלֵיהֶם נַפְשָׁם:


**shall die by strangulation** Their soul [shall die] by the strangulation that is upon them,



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 36:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:15:1)


**יְחַלֵּץ עָנִי.** אֶת הֶעָנִי, הַשָּׂם עַצְמוֹ רָשׁ בִּדְבַר תַּחֲנוּנִים, **יְחַלֵּץ** מִגֵּיהִנֹּם בִּשְׁבִיל עָנְיוֹ שֶׁהֵבִיא עָלָיו:


but the poor man, who makes himself poor in the matter of supplications...**He extricates** from Gehinnom because of his poverty, which He brought upon him.


###### Rashi on Job 36:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:15:2)


**וְיִגֶל בַּלַּחַץ אָזְנָם.** בַּלַּחַץ שֶׁהֵבִיא עָלָיו, מְגַלֶּה אֶת אָזְנוֹ, לֵאמֹר: שׁוּב אֵלַי!


**and opens their ear through oppression** Through the oppression that He brought upon him, He opens his ear to say, “Return to Me.”



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 36:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:16:1)


**וְאַף.** זֹאת עוֹד גְּדוֹלָה עָשָׂה לְךָ בְּיִסּוּרִין הַלָּלוּ: הֲסִיתְךָ וְהִדִּיחֲךָ מִגֵּיהִנֹּם, שֶׁפִּיהָ צַר וְתַחְתֶּיהָ רָחָב:


**Moreover** This is yet another great benefit that He did for you with these sufferings: He allured you and enticed you out of Gehinnom, whose mouth is narrow and whose base is wide.


###### Rashi on Job 36:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:16:2)


**לֹא מוּצָק.** כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּהֵא עֲשָׁנָהּ צָבוּר לְתוֹכָהּ. וְלֹא עוֹד, אֶלָּא שֶׁתִּזְכֶּה לָעוֹלָם הַבָּא, **וְנַחַת שֻׁלְחָנְךָ מָלֵא דָשֶׁן**:


**not cramped** so that its smoke should be gathered into its midst, and furthermore, that you should merit the world to come; and the bedecking of your table shall be full of fatness.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Job 36:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:17:1)


**וְדִין רָשָׁע מָלֵאתָ.** וְאִם נִתְמַלֵּאתָ בְּיִסּוּרֵי הָרְשָׁעִים, הַדִּין וְהַמִּשְׁפָּט הֵם הַיִּסּוּרִין; **הֵם יִתְמְכוּ** לְךָ לֶעָתִיד מִגֵּיהִנֹּם, וְיֵשׁ לְךָ לְקַבְּלָם בְּאַהֲבָה:


**And if you have had your fill of the sentence of the wicked** And if you have had your fill of the sufferings of the wicked, the judgment and the sentence—which are [represented by] the sufferings—will support you in the future from Gehinnom, and you must accept them lovingly.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Job 36:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:18:1)


**כִּי חֵמָה.** גְּדוֹלָה תִּהְיֶה:


**For wrath** [There] shall be great [wrath].


###### Rashi on Job 36:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:18:2)


**פֶּן יְסִיתְךָ.** יִצְרְךָ לְדַבֵּר דְּבָרִים:


**lest it entice you** [Lest] your evil inclination [entice you to speak] words.


###### Rashi on Job 36:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:18:3)


**בְּשָׂפֶק.** בְּשָׂפֶק זֶה אֲשֶׁר תִּשְׂפֹּק, וְתֶרֶב אֲמָרֶיךָ לָאֵל. וּמֵאַחַר שֶׁתַּעֲלֶה הַחֵמָה, רָב מָמוֹן אַל יַטֶּךָּ מִמֶּנָּה:


**to multiply** Heb. בשפק, lit. with multiplicity; [i.e., with] which you multiply and increase your words toward God; and after you have raised His wrath, much ransom, [i.e.,] much money will not turn you away [rescue you] from it.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 36:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:19:1)


**הֲיַעֲרֹךְ שׁוּעֲךָ לֹא בְצָר.** וְכִי בָּאתָה לַעֲרֹךְ צְעָקָה, שֶׁלֹּא תְּבוֹאֲךָ שׁוּם צָרָה וְשׁוּם מַאֲמַצֵּי כֹחַ לְאוֹנְסְךָ, אִם כֵּן בַּמֶּה יִגְבֶּה אֶת חוֹבוֹ?


**Will you set up your prayer so that no trouble** Have you come to set up a cry that no trouble befall you, or any forces of strength to rob you? If so, with what will He collect His debt?



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 36:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:20:1)


**אַל תִּשְׁאַף הַלָּיְלָה.** כְּלוֹמַר: וְאֵין לְךָ לִבְחֹר בְּיִסּוּרִים אֲחֵרִים; אַל תִּשְׁאַף וְתִתְאַוֶּה הַלַּיְלָה שֶׁבָּא לְאַמְרָפֶל, לְמִצְרַיִם וּלְסַנְחֵרִיב, לְסַלֵּק עַמִּים מִן הָעוֹלָם, כְּשֶׁהֵם תַּחְתָּם – בִּמְקוֹמָם, בְּשַׁלְוָה; כִּי הֵם אָבְדוּ בָּעוֹלָם הַזֶּה וּבָעוֹלָם הַבָּא:


**Do not yearn for the night** That is to say that you should not choose other sufferings. Do not yearn for, or desire the night that came to Amraphel, to Egypt, and to Sennacherib, to take away peoples from the world when they are in their place in tranquility, for they perished both from this world and from the world to come.


###### Rashi on Job 36:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:20:2)


**לַעֲלוֹת.** כְּמוֹ ״אַל תַּעֲלֵנִי בַּחֲצִי יָמָי״ (תהילים קב:כה), ״כַּעֲלוֹת גָּדִישׁ בְּעִתּוֹ״ (איוב ה:כו):


**are taken away** Heb. לעלות, like (Ps. 102:25), “do not take me away (תעלני) in the midst of my days.”



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Job 36:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:21:1)


**הִשָּׁמֶר אַל תֵּפֶן אֶל אָוֶן.** לָדוּן וְלוֹמַר: הָיָה לוֹ לְשָׁפְטֵנִי בְּיִסּוּרֵי עֲנִיּוּת:


**Beware, do not turn to iniquity** to contend and to say, “He should have judged me with the sufferings of poverty.”


###### Rashi on Job 36:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:21:2)


**כִּי עַל זֶה בָּחַרְתָּ מֵעֹנִי.** כִּי עַל הַיִּסּוּרִין הַלָּלוּ יֵשׁ לִבְחֹר יוֹתֵר מֵעֲנִיּוּת אָדָם שֶׁנָּהֲגוּ בּוֹ כָּבוֹד, וְלָמוּד בְּעֹשֶׁר כָּל יָמָיו, וְיַעֲמֹד עַל הַמֶּקַח וְאֵין לוֹ דָּמִים, וּבוֹשׁ וְשָׁב לְבֵיתוֹ בְּפַחֵי נֶפֶשׁ. וְאִם תֹּאמַר: אִיּוֹב עָנִי הָיָה עִם הַיִּסּוּרִין? לֹא מָצִינוּ שֶׁאִבֵּד, אֶלָּא בְּהֵמוֹתָיו, אֲבָל לֹא כֶּסֶף וְזָהָב. וַאֲפִלּוּ אִבֵּד בְּהֵמוֹתָיו עִם כֶּסֶף וְזָהָב, טוֹב לוֹ לִהְיוֹת עָנִי וְחוֹלֶה, מִהְיוֹת תָּמִים וְהוֹלֵךְ בַּשְּׁוָקִים וְאֵין בְּיָדוֹ כְּלוּם. וְכֵן מְפֹרָשׁ בְּמִדְרַשׁ רַבִּי תַּנְחוּמָא מִן הַמִּקְרָא הַזֶּה (תנחומא, משפטים יא), כִּי נוֹחִין הָיוּ יִסּוּרֵי אִיּוֹב מִיִּסּוּרֵי עֲנִיּוּת. וְכֵן בְּאַגָּדוֹת הַגְּמָרָא (בבא בתרא קטז.):


**for...this** These sufferings are to be chosen above poverty. A man to whom people behaved with respect and who has been accustomed to wealth all his life, will now stand and bargain, and having no money [to pay with], will be embarrassed and return home in disappointment. Now if you say that Job was a poor man in addition to his sufferings, we do not find that he lost anything except his livestock, but not silver or gold. And even if he lost his cattle with silver and gold, it is better for him to be a poor sick man than to be healthy and go in the marketplaces empty-handed [and be embarrassed as well]. In this manner, it is explained in the midrash of Rabbi Tanchuma (Mishpatim 11) from this verse, that Job’s sufferings are preferable to the sufferings of poverty, and so in the Aggadoth of the Talmud (Baba Bathra 116a).



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Job 36:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:22:1)


**הֵן אֵל יַשְׂגִּיב בְּכֹחוֹ.** מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁכֹּחוֹ גָּדוֹל וְאֵין לְהִנָּצֵל מִפָּנָיו, לְפִיכָךְ **אֵין כָּמוֹהוּ מוֹרֶה.** כְּשֶׁמּוֹרֶה לַחוֹטֵא לָשׁוּב אֵלָיו, מַתְרֶה קוֹדֶם מַכָּה, לְפִי שֶׁהוּא יוֹדֵעַ שֶׁאֵין יָכוֹל לְהִשָּׁמֵר מִמַּכָּתוֹ; כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיָה מַתְרֶה בְּפַרְעֹה עַל כָּל מַכָּה וּמַכָּה: ״הִנְנִי מַמְטִיר כָּעֵת מָחָר בָּרָד״ (שמות ט:יח), וְ״הִנְנִי מֵבִיא מָחָר אַרְבֶּה״ (שמות י:ד); וְכֵן כֻּלָּם. וּבָשָׂר וָדָם, כְּשֶׁרוֹצֶה לְהִנָּקֵם מֵאוֹיְבָיו, בָּא עָלָיו פִּתְאוֹם; שֶׁאִם יַתְרֶה בּוֹ, יַחֲזֹר וְיִנָּצֵל מִמֶּנּוּ:


**Behold God deals loftily in His power** Because His power is great, and one cannot be saved from before Him. Therefore...**who is a teacher like Him?** When He teaches a sinner to return to Him, He warns Him before the plague because He knows that he cannot beware of His plague, as He would warn Pharaoh concerning each plague (Ex. 8:17), “I shall let loose the beasts of the wilderness upon you etc.”; (ibid. 10:4), “I will bring locusts...tomorrow,” and so all of them. [In contrast,] when a mortal king wishes to wreak vengeance upon his enemies, he comes upon him suddenly, for, if he warns him, he will repent and be saved from him.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Job 36:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:23:1)


**מִי פָקַד עָלָיו דַּרְכּוֹ.** יְצַוֶּה עָלָיו מֵעוֹלָם: אֶת הַדֶּרֶךְ הַזֶּה תֵּלֵךְ:


**Who charged Him with His way** [Who] commanded Him from time immemorial, “You shall follow this way”?



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Job 36:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:24:1)


**זְכֹר.** דְּרָכָיו, וּבְהַזְכִּירְךָ תַּשְׂגִּיא מֵאֵלֶיךָ שֶׁבַח פֹּעַל מִדּוֹתָיו:


**Mention** His ways, and when you mention [them], you will automatically magnify the praise of the works of His attributes.


###### Rashi on Job 36:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:24:2)


**אֲשֶׁר שֹׁרְרוּ אֲנָשִׁים.** אֲשֶׁר רָאוּ אֲנָשִׁים, כְּמוֹ ״אֲשׁוּרֶנּוּ וְלֹא קָרוֹב״ (במדבר כד:יז):


**which men have seen** Heb. שררו, which men have seen, like (Num. 24:17), “I behold it (אשורנו), but it is not near.”



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Job 36:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:25:1)


**חָזוּ בוֹ.** בְּפָעֳלוֹ:


**have seen it** His work.


###### Rashi on Job 36:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:25:2)


**מֵרָחוֹק.** מִיְּצִירַת עוֹלָם עַד עַכְשָׁיו:


**from afar** from the Creation or the world until now.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Job 36:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:26:1)


**מִסְפַּר שָׁנָיו וְלֹא חֵקֶר.** לְפִיכָךְ צָרִיךְ לֶאֱנוֹשׁ לְהַבִּיט מֵרָחוֹק, לִשְׁאוֹל מַה שֶּׁאֵרַע בָּרִאשׁוֹנִים, וְיִלְמַד מֵהֶם:


**the number of His years is unfathomable** Therefore man must look from afar to ask what happened to the first people, and he will learn from them.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Job 36:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:27:1)


**כִּי יְגָרַע.** כִּי יַרְבֶּה, כְּמוֹ ״וְתִגְרַע שִׂיחָה״ (איוב טו:ד). וְיֵשׁ פּוֹתְרִים: יִגְרָעֵם מִן הַשָּׁמַיִם, לְהוֹרִידָם אָרֶץ:


**For He increases** Heb. יגרע, for He increases, like (above 15:4), “and increase (ותגרע) speech.” Others interpret: He withdraws them from the heavens to bring them down to earth.


###### Rashi on Job 36:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:27:2)


**וְיָזֹקּוּ.** הָרְקִיעִים אֶת הַמָּטָר:


**they pour** The firmaments [pour] the rain.


###### Rashi on Job 36:27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:27:3)


**לְאֵדוֹ.** לַעֲנָנוֹ:


**into His cloud** Heb. לְאֵדוֹ, into His cloud.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Job 36:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:29:1)


**אַף אִם יָבִין.** אוֹ אִם יָבִין אָדָם מַה יֵּשׁ בְּמִפְרְשֵׂי עָב, בִּתְמִיהַּ:


**Or will one understand** Or will man understand what is in the spreadings of the clouds? This is a question.


###### Rashi on Job 36:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:29:2)


**תְּשֻׁאוֹת.** לְשׁוֹן עֲרָפֶל **סֻכָּתוֹ**:


**the darkness** Heb. תשאות, an expression of the thick darkness of His pavilion.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Job 36:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:30:1)


**הֵן פָּרַשׂ.** עַל הֶעָב אֶת אוֹרוֹ, **וְשָׁרְשֵׁי הַיָּם כִּסָּה** בּוֹ:


**Behold He spread** (on the cloud His rain) and covered the roots of the sea therewith.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Job 36:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:31:1)


**כִּי בָם.** בֶּעָבִים יָדִין עַמִּים – יְיַסְּרֵם בַּעֲצִירַת גְּשָׁמִים, בָּרָעָב וְכָל מַכָּה. חָטְאוּ דּוֹר הַמַּבּוּל – ״וַאֲרֻבֹּת הַשָּׁמַיִם נִפְתָּחוּ״ (בראשית ז:יא), אַנְשֵׁי סְדוֹם – ״גָּפְרִית וָמֶלַח שְׂרֵפָה כָל אַרְצָהּ״ (דברים כט:כב), סִיסְרָא – ״מִן הַשָּׁמַיִם נִלְחֲמוּ עִם סִיסְרָא״ (שופטים ה:כ):


**For...therewith** With the clouds He judges peoples and chastises them by withholding rain, causing famine, as well as all sorts of plagues. When the Generation of the Flood sinned (Gen. 7:11), “and the floodgates of the heavens were opened.” As for the people of Sodom (Deut. 29:22), “Sulfur and salt, all the soil is burned up.” As for Sisera (Jud. 5:20), “From heaven they fought...against Sisera.”


###### Rashi on Job 36:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:31:2)


**יִתֵּן אֹכֶל לְמַכְבִּיר.** מִשָּׁם בָּאִים מְזוֹנוֹת: ״הִנְנִי מַמְטִיר לָכֶם לֶחֶם מִן הַשָּׁמָיִם״ (שמות טז:ד). מָשָׁל לְנַחְתּוֹם הָעוֹמֵד לִפְנֵי הַכִּבְשָׁן – נִכְנַס שׂוֹנְאוֹ, חוֹתֶה גֶּחָלִים וְזוֹרֵק בּוֹ; נִכְנַס אוֹהֲבוֹ, מוֹצִיא פַּת חַמָּה וְנוֹתֵן לוֹ:


**He gives food to one who has many** From there comes sustenance (Ex. 16:4): “See, I am about to have bread rain down from heaven for you.” This is to be compared to a baker who is standing before the oven. If an enemy comes in, he rakes coals and casts them at him; if his friend comes in, he takes out hot bread and gives it to him.


###### Rashi on Job 36:31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:31:3)


**לְמַכְבִּיר.** לְמִי שֶׁטְּפָלָיו מְרֻבִּים וְכַבִּירִים, וְצָרִיךְ לִמְזוֹנוֹת:


**to the one who has many** to the one whose children are many and numerous; to one who increases children and requires food.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Job 36:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:32:1)


**עַל כַּפַּיִם.** עַל הֶעָנָנִים, וְכֵן ״נִשָּׂא לְבָבֵנוּ אֶל כַּפָּיִם״ (איכה ג:מא), עַל שֵׁם ״עָב קְטַנָּה כְּכַף אִישׁ״ (מלכים א יח:מד). וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ (תענית ז:): עַל חָמָס וְעָוֹן כַּפַּיִם **כִּסָּה אוֹר**, הַמָּטָר נֶעֱצָר:


**Over the clouds** Heb. כפים, over the clouds. Similarly (Lam. 3:41), “Let us lift our heart to the clouds (כפים),” based on (I Kings 18:44), “...a cloud as small as a man’s palm (ככף).” But our Rabbis said (Taanith 7b): Because of the sin of (the robbery of) the hands **He covers the rain** The rain is withheld.


###### Rashi on Job 36:32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:32:2)


**וַיְצַו עָלֶיהָ.** לָבוֹא עַל יְדֵי מַפְגִּיעַ בִּתְפִלָּה, אוֹהֲבוֹ שֶׁל דּוֹר וְגָדוֹל וְחָכָם שֶׁבּוֹ, יַגִּיד עָלָיו לִפְנֵי הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא צָרַת הַדּוֹר וּצְרָכָיו:


**and He commands it** to come through one who entreats with prayer. (The beloved one of the generation, and the great man and sage thereof will relate to Him—before the Holy One, blessed be He—the straits of the generation and their necessities].



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Job 36:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:33:1)


**יַגִּיד עָלָיו רֵעוֹ.** יִדְרֹשׁ לָהֶם לִבְדֹּק לָהֶם בְּמַעֲשֵׂיהֶם וְלָשׁוּב אֵלָיו:


**Their beloved one preaches to them** He preaches to them to search their deeds for themselves and to return to Him.


###### Rashi on Job 36:33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:33:2)


**מִקְנֶה.** וְזֶהוּ קִנְיָן שֶׁנִּקְנִין בּוֹ הַגְּשָׁמִים:


**the acquisition** This is the acquisition whereby the rain is acquired.


###### Rashi on Job 36:33:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:33:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/36:33:3)


**אַף עַל עוֹלֶה.** גַּם לְמַעְלָה לְמַעְלָה, כְּמוֹ ״יָשׁוּבוּ אֶל עָל״ (הושע ז:טז), אוֹ ״הֻקַם עָל״ (שמואל ב כג:א). וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּהוּ (תענית ח.): קִנְיַן אַף וְחֵמָה עַל הַמֵּגִיס דַּעְתּוֹ. אֲבָל הַנִּקּוּד, שֶׁנִּקַּד מִקְנֶה – פַּתָּח, מוֹכִיחַ, שֶׁהוּא אֵינוֹ דָבוּק לְאַף; שֶׁאִלּוּ הָיָה דָבוּק, הָיָה נָקוּד קָמַץ, וְלֹא הָיָה בּוֹ פִּסּוּק הַטַּעַם, שֶׁהוּא נִטְעָם בִּ׳רְבִיעִי׳:


**it also ascends on high** Heb. על. It also [ascends] higher and higher [in this manner], like (Hosea 7: 16), “They will return, but not to the Most High (על)” or (II Sam. 23:1), “raised on high (על).” [See Comm. Dig. to Hosea 7:16.] Our Sages, however, interpret it (Taanith 8a) as the acquisition of anger and wrath upon one who becomes haughty [after his prayers have been answered], but the vowelization, viz. that מִקְנֶה is vowelized with a “pattah” [meaning a “seggol”], does not substantiate [the theory] that it is connected to אַף, for were it connected to אַף, it would be vowelized with a “kamatz” [meaning a “tzeireh”], and it would not be punctuated by a cantillation sign that denotes a pause, viz. a revi’i.



## Chapter 37


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Job 37:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:1:1)


**וְיִתַּר.** וְיִקְפֹּץ, כְּמוֹ ״לְנַתֵּר בָּהֶם עַל הָאָרֶץ״ (ויקרא יא:כא). וְזוֹ הִיא שֶׁלִּבּוֹ תּוֹהֶה:


**and spring** Heb. ויתר, and spring, like (Lev. 11:21) “to hop (לנתר) with them upon the earth.” And this means that his heart is astonished.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 37:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:2:1)


**שִׁמְעוּ שָׁמוֹעַ בְּרֹגֶז קוֹלוֹ.** הָרְעָמִים שֶׁהוּא מַרְעִים בָּרָקִיעַ, כְּהֶגֶה הַיּוֹצֵא מִפִּיו:


**Hear attentively the noise of His voice** The thunders that He thunders in the sky are like a sound that emanates from His mouth.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 37:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:3:1)


**תַּחַת כָּל הַשָּׁמַיִם יִשְׁרֵהוּ.** רְאִיתִיו, כְּמוֹ ״אֲשׁוּרֶנּוּ״ (במדבר כד:יז). וְהֵם אוֹתָם אוֹרוֹת שֶׁהַבְּרָקִים מְאִירִים:


**Beneath the entire heaven I have seen it** Heb. ישרהו, I have seen it, like (Num. 24:17), “I behold it (אשורנו).” They are the lights illuminated by the lightnings.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 37:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:4:1)


**אַחֲרָיו.** שֶׁל אוֹתוֹ אוֹר, **יִשְׁאַג** הָרַעַם **וּמַרְעִים בְּקוֹל גְּאוֹנוֹ**, וְהֵם בְּרָקִים וּרְעָמִים הַמְּבִיאִים אֶת הַמָּטָר:


**In its wake** [i.e., in the wake] of that light, the thunder roars, and it thunders with the voice of its pride. Those are the lightnings and the thunders, which bring the rain.


###### Rashi on Job 37:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:4:2)


**וְלֹא יְעַקְּבֵם.** הַמָּקוֹם:


**and He will not cut them off** The Omnipresent [will not cut off the thunder and the lightning].


###### Rashi on Job 37:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:4:3)


**כִּי יִשָּׁמַע.** לְפָנָיו קוֹלוֹ שֶׁל מַפְגִּיעַ (איוב לו:לב):


**for...will be heard** before Him the voice of the one who prays.


###### Rashi on Job 37:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:4:4)


**יְעַקְּבֵם.** יַכְרִיתֵם, וּלְשׁוֹן מִשְׁנָה שָׁנִינוּ (סוטה טז.): בְּשָׁלֹשׁ מְקוֹמוֹת הֲלָכָה עוֹקֶבֶת מִקְרָא:


**He will...cut them off** Heb. יעקבם, He will cut them off, [and in the language of the Mishnah we learned (Sotah 16a): in three instances, the halachah supplants (עוקבת) the text].



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 37:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:6:1)


**כִּי לַשֶּׁלֶג יֹאמַר.** עַל יְדֵי הַקּוֹלוֹת הַלָּלוּ:


**For He says to the snow** through these voices.


###### Rashi on Job 37:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:6:2)


**הֱוֵא אָרֶץ וְגֶשֶׁם מָטָר וְגֶשֶׁם מִטְרוֹת עֻזּוֹ.** כְּלוֹמַר מֵאַרְבַּע רוּחוֹת יָבֹא:


**‘Be upon the earth’; likewise to the shower of rain and to the showers of His mighty rain** That is to say that it comes from the four directions.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 37:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:7:1)


**בְּיַד כָּל אָדָם יַחְתּוֹם.** כְּשֶׁאָדָם חוֹטֵא לְפָנָיו, הָאָדָם עַצְמוֹ חוֹתֵם כְּתַב יָדוֹ בְּיוֹם מוֹתוֹ עַל עֲבֵירוֹת שֶׁעָבַר, הַכְּתוּבוֹת לְפָנָיו:


**By the hand of every man he seals** When a man sins before Him, the man himself signs his signature on the day of his death to the sins that he committed, which are written before him.


###### Rashi on Job 37:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:7:2)


**לָדַעַת כָּל אַנְשֵׁי מַעֲשֵׂהוּ.** לְהוֹדִיעַ עַל מָה הוּא נִידּוֹן:


**so that every man should know his deed** to let him know for what he is being judged.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 37:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:8:1)


**וַתָּבֹא חַיָּה בְמוֹ אָרֶב.** כְּלוֹמַר: וְעוֹד שְׁלוּחִים הַרְבֵּה יֵשׁ לַמָּקוֹם לִיפָּרַע פּוּרְעָנוּתָיו, שִׁלּוּחַ חַיּוֹת רָעוֹת וְסוּפָה וְקָרָה:


**The beasts go into ambush** That is to say that the Omnipresent has many more agents through which to mete out His punishments: the inciting of wild beasts, the tempest, and the cold.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Job 37:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:9:1)


**וּמִמְּזָרִים.** אוֹצָר יֵשׁ וּשְׁמוֹ מְזָרִים:


**and from Mezarim** There is a treasury, named Mezarim.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 37:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:10:1)


**מִנִּשְׁמַת.** מֵרוּחוֹ:


**From the breath of** By His wind.


###### Rashi on Job 37:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:10:2)


**וְרֹחַב מַיִם בְּמוּצָק.** בִּיצִיקָה לְפֻרְעָנוּת:


**and the breadth of the waters in a good** When they are poured out in retribution.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 37:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:11:1)


**אַף בְּרִי.** שֵׁם מַלְאָךְ הַמְמֻנֶּה עַל הָעֲנָנִים, וְהוּא **יָפִיץ עֲנַן** מְטָרוֹ שֶׁל הַמָּקוֹם:


**Aph Beri** The name of the angel who is appointed over the clouds, and he scatters the Omnipresent’s rain cloud.


###### Rashi on Job 37:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:11:2)


**עֲנַן אוֹרוֹ.** עֲנַן מְטָרוֹ:



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 37:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:12:1)


**וְהוּא.** הַמְּמֻנֶּה:


**And he** who is thus appointed.


###### Rashi on Job 37:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:12:2)


**מְסִבּוֹת מִתְהַפֵּךְ בְּתַחְבּוּלוֹתָיו.** שֶׁל מָקוֹם:


**turns around in circuits by His guidance** [i.e., by the guidance] of the Omnipresent.


###### Rashi on Job 37:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:12:3)


**לְפָעֳלָם.** שֶׁל גְּשָׁמִים. לְפֹעַל הַמּוּטָּל עֲלֵיהֶם לַעֲשׂוֹת, אִם טוֹב וְאִם רַע, מִתְהַפֵּךְ בִּמְסִבּוֹת הַרְבֵּה. כְּאוֹתוֹ שֶׁשָּׁנִינוּ (ראש השנה יז:): הֲרֵי שֶׁהָיוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל צַדִּיקִים גְּמוּרִים בְּרֹאשׁ הַשָּׁנָה, וּפָסְקוּ לָהֶם גְּשָׁמִים; לְסוֹף סָרְחוּ – לִפְחוֹת מֵהֶם אִי אֶפְשָׁר, שֶׁכְּבָר נִגְזְרָה גְּזֵרָה; אֶלָּא הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מוֹרִידָם שֶׁלֹּא בִזְמַנָּם, וְעַל אֶרֶץ שֶׁאֵינָהּ צְרִיכָה לָהֶם. וְעִתִּים לְטוֹבָה; כֵּיצַד? וְכוּ׳:


**for their work** namely, of the rains; for the work which is incumbent upon them to do, either good or evil, he is turned about with many circuits, as [the passage] that we learned (Rosh Hashanah 17b): if Israel were perfectly righteous on Rosh Hashanah so that [much] rain was decreed for them, and they should later become sinful—it would be impossible to decrease it [the amount of rain] because the decree would already have gone forth; but the Holy One, blessed be He, would let it come down out of its season and upon a land that did not require it. “And sometimes to do good”; how is this? etc.


###### Rashi on Job 37:12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:12:4)


**כָּל אֲשֶׁר יְצַוֵּם.** לְאוֹתָן גְּשָׁמִים לַעֲשׂוֹת לָאָרֶץ. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **וְהוּא מְסִבּוֹת מִתְהַפֵּךְ בְּתַחְבּוּלוֹתָיו.** מַקִּיף עֲנָנִים וְרוּחוֹת סְבִיבוֹת הֶהָרִים, שֶׁיִּהְיוּ שׁוֹתִים מִכָּל רוּחוֹתֵיהֶם. בְּאַגָּדַת ׳וְהָיָה עֵקֶב׳ (ספרי דברים לט):


**whatever He commands them** Those rains, to do on earth. [Another explanation: And he turns around in circuits with His guidance—He causes the clouds and the winds to encompass the mountains so that they should be watered from all sides. In the Aggadah of “Vehayah ekev” (Sifrei Ekev 39).]



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 37:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:13:1)


**אִם לְשֵׁבֶט.** אִם לְפוּרְעָנוּת בְּנֵי אָדָם, מוֹרִידָם בֶּהָרִים וּגְבָעוֹת, שֶׁאֵין מְקוֹם זְרִיעָה:


**Whether for the rod** Whether for the punishment of men, He lets them fall on mountains and hills, which are not places for sowing.


###### Rashi on Job 37:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:13:2)


**אִם לְאַרְצוֹ.** לְהוֹרִידָם כַּאֲשֶׁר נִגְזַר בִּתְחִלַּת דִּין, לֹא לְשֵׁבֶט וְלֹא לִפְנִים מִן הַשּׁוּרָה, אֶלָּא בְּבֵינוֹנִית:


**whether for His land** To let them fall as originally decreed, neither for the rod nor within the boundary of justice [i.e., for more benefit than strict justice warrants], but midway between [these two extremes].


###### Rashi on Job 37:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:13:3)


**אִם לְחֶסֶד.** שֶׁנַּעֲשׂוּ הַדּוֹר עַכְשָׁיו יוֹתֵר צַדִּיקִים מִשֶּׁהָיוּ בְּרֹאשׁ הַשָּׁנָה, וְאוֹתָן גְּשָׁמִים שֶׁנִּגְזְרוּ בְּבֵינוֹנִית, מִתְהַפֵּךְ בְּתַחְבּוּלוֹת מְסִבּוֹתֵיהֶם, לֵירֵד בְּחֶסֶד בִּזְמַנָּם, וְעַל הָאָרֶץ הַצְּרִיכָה לָהֶם; שֶׁלֹּא תֵּרֵד הַטִּפָּה מֵהֶם לְאִבּוּד:


**or for kindness** If the generation became more righteous than they were on Rosh Hashanah, then the rain which was decreed to fall in an average manner, shall be turned about by the guidance of their circuits to fall, with kindness, in their season and upon the land that requires them, so that not a drop of them should go to waste.


###### Rashi on Job 37:13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:13:4)


**יַמְצִיאֵהוּ.** לַמָּטָר, לְאַחַת מִן הַמִּדּוֹת הַלָּלוּ:


**that He cause it to come** The rain, in one of these ways.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 37:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:15:1)


**בְּשׂוּם אֱלוֹהַּ.** חֹק בְּרִית עֲלֵיהֶם, הֵיאַךְ תְּלוּיִים בְּמַאֲמָר:


**how God places** the law of the covenant upon them, how they are suspended by His decree.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 37:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:16:1)


**מִפְלְשֵׂי עָב.** גִּילּוּי עָב, לְשׁוֹן מָבוֹי מְפוּלָּשׁ. כְּלוֹמַר: לְאָרְכָּן וּלְרָחְבָּן שֶׁל עָבִים:


**the revelations of the clouds** Heb. מפלשי, the revelation of the clouds, an expression of an open (מפלש) alley, i.e., to the length and the width of the clouds.


###### Rashi on Job 37:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:16:2)


**הֲתֵדַע.** עֲלֵיהֶם מִפְלְאוֹת תְּמִים דֵּעִים:


**Do you know** concerning them, the wonders of Him Who is perfect in knowledge?



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Job 37:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:17:1)


**אֲשֶׁר בְּגָדֶיךָ חַמִּים.** בַּמֶּה בְּגָדֶיךָ חַמִּים? **בְּהַשְׁקִיטִי אֶרֶץ מֵרוּחַ דְּרוֹמִית**, שֶׁאֵינָהּ הוֹרֶגֶת אֶת הָעוֹלָם בְּקָרָה; שֶׁרוּחַ מִזְרָחִית מְחַמֶּמֶת, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״רוּחַ קָדִים חֲרִישִׁית״ (יונה ד:ח) – שֶׁמַּשְׁתֶּקֶת כָּל הָרוּחוֹת מִפָּנֶיהָ:


**How your garments are warm** How your garments are warm when He silences the earth from the south wind, so that it should not kill the world with cold, because the east wind warms it. As it is stated (Jonah 4:8): “a stilling east wind,” which quiets all other winds before it.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Job 37:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:18:1)


**חֲזָקִים.** הָרְקִיעִים:


**strong** the skies.


###### Rashi on Job 37:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:18:2)


**כִּרְאִי מוּצָק.** כִּרְאִי שֶׁהַנָּשִׁים רוֹאוֹת בּוֹ, (מוּצָק טְרִישְׁטְיֵי״ר וְהוּא חָזָק בְּיוֹתֵר):


**as a molten mirror** Like a mirror into which women look. **[molten** tristre, which is very strong.]



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 37:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:19:1)


**הוֹדִיעֵנוּ מַה נֹּאמַר לוֹ.** לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, בְּרִיבֵנוּ עִמּוֹ. **לֹא נוּכַל לַעֲרֹךְ אֵלָיו מִפְּנֵי חֹשֶׁךְ.** אֲשֶׁר סִתְרוֹ וּסְבִיבוֹתָיו (תהלים יח:יב):


**Let us know what we shall say to Him** To the Holy One, blessed be He, when we contend with Him. We will not be able to set up a case with Him because of the darkness which conceals and surrounds Him.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 37:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:20:1)


**הֲיְסֻפַּר לוֹ כִּי אֲדַבֵּר.** וְכִי כְּמִדַּת הַבְּרִיּוֹת הוּא, שֶׁצָּרִיךְ שֶׁיְּסַפְּרוּ אֵלָיו דְּבָרִים הַיּוֹצְאִים מִפִּינוּ?


**Need it be told to Him when I speak** Is He like mortals, that He must be told what we are saying?


###### Rashi on Job 37:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:20:2)


**אִם אָמַר אִישׁ.** אוֹמֵר מִפִּיו:


**or need a man tell** utter with his mouth.


###### Rashi on Job 37:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:20:3)


**כִּי יְבֻלָּע.** יְגֻלֶּה אֵלָיו מִיָּד, כְּמוֹ ״פֶּן יְבֻלַּע לַמֶּלֶךְ״ (שמואל ב יז:טז) יִגָּלֶה. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּ (מגילה יח.) **הַיְסֻפַּר** כָּל שִׁבְחוֹ לוֹ **כִּי אֲדַבֵּר.** כִּי נְדַבֵּר בּוֹ וּבִגְבוּרָתוֹ, **אִם אָמַר אִישׁ**, לְסַפְּרָם כֻּלָּם **כִּי יְבֻלָּע.** מִמְּקוֹמוֹ:


**what is kept a secret** so that it should be revealed to Him immediately, like (II Sam. 17:16), “lest the king be told secretly (יבלע)” [lest] it be revealed. But our Sages (Meg. 18a) explained: Can all His praise **be told Him, that I should speak** That we should speak of Him and His might? **Has any man said** to tell them all, **he would surely be destroyed** from his place.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Job 37:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:21:1)


**וְעַתָּה לֹא רָאוּ אוֹר.** בְּסוֹף דְּבָרָיו אֶל אֱלִיהוּא חָזַר לְקַנְתֵּר אוֹתָן שְׁלֹשֶׁת רֵיעֵי אִיּוֹב עַל לֹא מָצְאוּ מַעֲנֶה (איוב לב:א-ג), וְאָמַר: וְעַתָּה, שְׁלֹשֶׁת רֵיעֶיךָ אֵלֶּה, שֶׁהָיָה לָהֶם לַהֲשִׁיבְךָ כָּל דְּבָרִים אֵלּוּ שֶׁאָמַרְתִּי לְךָ, **לֹא רָאוּ אוֹר.** נַעֲשׂוּ הֵמָּה כִּנְפָלִים אֲשֶׁר לֹא רָאוּ חָכְמָה (איוב ג:טז):


**And now, they did not see the rain** At the conclusion of his words, Elihu returns to chide Job’s three companions for not finding an answer. He therefore says, “And now, these three companions of yours, who should have answered you all these things that I said to you... did not see the rain” They are like stillborn babies, who did not see wisdom.


###### Rashi on Job 37:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:21:2)


**בָּהִיר הוּא בַּשְּׁחָקִים.** דּוֹמֶה לַבְּהִירִים הַנִּרְאִים בַּשְּׁחָקִים, לְהוֹרִיד גֶּשֶׁם:


**it is [like] bright [clouds] in the skies** It is like many bright clouds that appear in the skies to bring down rain,


###### Rashi on Job 37:21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:21:3)


**וְרוּחַ עָבְרָה.** וְרוּחַ בָּא וּמְטַהֲרָתָן וּמַעֲבִירָתָן, וְאֵין מָטָר יוֹרֵד. כָּךְ הוּא דֻּגְמָא שֶׁלָּהֶם: הִתְחִילוּ לַהֲשִׁיבְךָ, וְסוֹף עָמְדוּ וְלֹא עָנוּ (איוב לב:טז):


but the wind comes and clears them away, makes them pass away, and no rain falls. That is their analogy; they began to answer you, but at the end, they remained silent and did not answer.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Job 37:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:22:1)


**מִצָּפוֹן זָהָב יֶאֱתֶה.** הֲרֵי הוּא כְּחוֹזֵר עַל הָרִאשׁוֹנוֹת. דָּבָר אַחֵר: מִדְרַשׁ חֲכָמִים (תענית ז:): כְּשֶׁהָרָקִיעַ מָלֵא בֶּהָרוֹת עֲנָנִים עֲנָנִים לְהוֹרִיד גֶּשֶׁם, **רוּחַ עָבְרָה וַתְּטַהֲרֵם**, וְאֵין גֶּשֶׁם יוֹרֵד. זֶה מִדְרַשׁ חֲכָמִים, וְאֵינָהּ מִתְיַשֶּׁבֶת עַל סֵדֶר הַמִּקְרָאוֹת:


**Gold[en splendor] comes from the north** It is as though he is returning to his previous statements. [“And now, they did not see the rain.”] Another explanation: The homily of the Sages (Taanith 7b) is that when the sky is full of spots, [i.e., full] of many clouds, to cause rain to fall, a wind passes and cleanses them away, and no rain falls. This is a homily of the Sages, but it does not fit the sequence of the verses.


###### Rashi on Job 37:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:22:2)


**מִצָּפוֹן זָהָב יֶאֱתֶה.** מְנַשֵּׁב רוּחַ צְפוֹנִית וּמַעֲבִיר הַגֶּשֶׁם, וְהַחַמָּה זוֹרַחַת; כְּמוֹ ״הַמְּרִיקִים מֵעֲלֵיהֶם הַזָּהָב״ (זכריה ד:יב):


**Gold comes from the north** The north wind blows and drives away the rain. Then the sun shines, like (Zech. 4:12), “that empty out the gold[en oil] from themselves.”


###### Rashi on Job 37:22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:22:3)


**עַל אֱלוֹהַּ.** מִלְּפָנָיו:


**on God** From before Him.


###### Rashi on Job 37:22:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:22:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:22:4)


**מִצָּפוֹן זָהָב יֶאֱתֶה.** נִצְפַּן הַזָּהָב הַטּוֹב לְאוֹתָן שֶׁיֵּאָתִיּוּ אֶת עַצְמָן לְמִצְווֹת הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, וּמַאֲמִינִים בֵּאלוֹהַּ הַנּוֹרָא מְאֹד:


**(Gold comes from the north** The good gold is hidden for those who bring themselves to the commandments of the Holy One, blessed be He, and believe in the very awesome God.)



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Job 37:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:23:1)


**שַׁדַּי לֹא מְצָאנוּהוּ שַׂגִּיא כֹחַ.** לְעוֹרֵר מִשְׁפָּטָיו עַל הַבְּרִיּוֹת בְּגֹדֶל כֹּחוֹ, כִּי אִם בְּרַחֲמִים. וְכַפָּרַת הַבְּרִיּוֹת שָׂם בְּדָבָר מוּעָט לְפִי הַיְּכֹלֶת: קֹמֶץ מִנְחָה, מַחֲצִית הַשֶּׁקֶל, תּוֹרִים וּבְנֵי יוֹנָה, בָּקָר וְצֹאן; וְלֹא טָרַח לְבַקֵּשׁ רְאֵם וְיַחְמוּר:


**We have not found the Almighty great in power** to arouse His judgments upon the creatures with the greatness of His power, but with clemency. He makes atonements for the creatures with small things, according to their ability: a handful of [fine flour from] a meal offering, a half shekel, turtle doves and young pigeons, cattle and flocks; and He did not burden them to seek a wild ox or an antelope.


###### Rashi on Job 37:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:23:2)


**וּמִשְׁפָּט.** יִסּוּרִין:


**and with judgment** Sufferings.


###### Rashi on Job 37:23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:23:3)


**לֹא יְעַנֶּה.** הַבְּרִיּוֹת יוֹתֵר מִדַּאי, וְכֵן לֹא יְעַנֶּה הַצַּדִּיק יוֹתֵר מִדַּאי בְּמַה שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָׁר, כִּי אִם ״אִישׁ כְּמַתְּנַת יָדוֹ״ (דברים טז:יז):


**He does not afflict** the creatures excessively. Likewise, He does nor afflict them to [force them into] becoming overly righteous to an impossible extent, but each man according to his ability.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Job 37:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/37:24:1)


**לָכֵן יְרֵאוּהוּ אֲנָשִׁים.** מִלְּהִתְוַכֵּחַ אֵלָיו, (כִּי) **לֹא יִרְאֶה כָּל חַכְמֵי לֵב.** הַמִּתְחַכְּמִים אֵלָיו, כִּי אֵין חָכְמָתָם בְּעֵינָיו כְּלוּם. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּהוּ עַל אִיּוֹב (ראש השנה יז.), שֶׁאָמַר לוֹ אֱלִיהוּא: לְפִי שֶׁהָיִיתָ מֵטִיל אֵימָה יְתֵרָה עַל דּוֹרְךָ חִנָּם, לְפִיכָךְ לֹא זָכִיתָ לְהִתְקַיֵּם לְךָ בָּנִים חַכְמֵי לֵב:


**Therefore, men fear Him** to debate with Him. (because) **He does not look upon any wise of heart** who want to deal wisely with Him, because their wisdom is as nothing in His eyes. Our Sages (Rosh Hashanah 17a) explained this as referring to Job. Elihu says to him, “Because you would impose excessive fear upon your generation, you did not merit having wise-hearted sons remain alive to you.”



## Chapter 38


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Job 38:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:1:1)


**(מענה מן הסערה): 
מִן הַסְּעָרָה:** מְנֵה שְׂעָרוֹת שֶׁלְּךָ, וַאֲשִׁיבְךָ דְּבָרִים כְּנֶגְדָּן. מִן הַסְּעָרָה שֶׁחֵרַפְתַּנִי ״אֲשֶׁר בַּסְּעָרָה יְשׁוּפֵנִי״ (איוב ט:יז) – מִמֶּנָּה אָשִׁיב לְךָ, כַּאֲשֶׁר תִּשְׁמַע בְּסוֹף הָעִנְיָן: ״מִי פִלַּג לַשֶּׁטֶף תְּעָלָה״ (איוב לח:כה) – גּוּמָא לְכָל שֵׂעָר. דָּבָר אַחֵר: בַּסְּעָרָה הִכָּהוּ, בַּסְּעָרָה רִפְּאָהוּ. כִּדְמְפָרֵשׁ בְּסִפְרֵי (ילקוט שמעוני תורה רלה):


**from the tempest** Heb. מן הסערה. Count your hairs and I will give you as many answers. From the tempest with which you reproached Me (above 9:17), “He Who would crush me with a tempest,” I will answer you, as you will hear at the end of the chapter (verse 25), “Who has separated a place for the hair?” a shaft for every hair. Another explanation: With a tempest He afflicted him, and with a tempest He healed him, as is explained in Sifrei(?).



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 38:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:2:1)


**מַחְשִׁיךְ עֵצָה.** עֵצָה חֲשֵׁכָה וּסְכָלָה. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **מִי זֶה מַחְשִׁיךְ עֵצָה... בְּלִי דָעַת.** אָמַר הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא: מִי זֶה הוּא מַחְשִׁיךְ עֵצָה – מְקַלְקֵל בְּמִלִּים וּבְרֹב דְּבָרִים? שֶׁכָּתַבְתִּי ״אִישׁ תָּם וְיָשָׁר״ בִּתְחִלַּת הַסֵּפֶר (איוב א:א), כְּדֵי לָחוּל שְׁמִי עָלָיו; וְהוּא בָּא וְהֶחְשִׁיךְ וְקִלְקֵל מַה שֶּׁיָּעַצְתִּי בְּרֹב דְּבָרָיו! וְאִיּוֹב עָנָהוּ: ״מִי זֶה מַעְלִים עֵצָה״ (איוב מב:ג) – אִלּוּ הָיִיתִי יוֹדֵעַ עֲצָתְךָ, לֹא הָיִיתִי מַרְבֶּה בִדְבָרִים. וְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא הֱשִׁיבוֹ: וַהֲלֹא אַבְרָהָם לֹא הָיָה יוֹדֵעַ, וְעָמַד בְּעֶשֶׂר! כָּךְ מָצָאתִי:


**who gives dark counsel** Dark and foolish counsel. Another explanation: Who is this who gives dark counsel...without knowledge? Said the Holy One, blessed be He: Who is this who darkens counsel, frustrates words, i.e., with a multiplicity of words; for I wrote: “and that man was sincere and upright,” at the beginning of the Book, in order that My name should rest on Him, but he came and darkened and frustrated what I counseled with his many words. Whereupon Job replies to Him (below 42:3): “Who is this Who hides counsel?” Had I known Your counsel, I would not have spoken so much. Then the Holy One blessed be He, answers him, “Neither did Abraham know; yet he passed ten” [tests]. So I found.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 38:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:3:1)


**אֱזָר נָא כְגֶבֶר חֲלָצֶיךָ.** רֶמֶז לוֹ שֶׁיִּתְעוֹרֵר מִן חָלְיוֹ וְיִסּוּרָיו:


**Now gird your loins like a man** He intimated to him that he should arouse himself from his illness and his sufferings.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 38:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:4:1)


**אֵיפֹה.** אֵי פֹּה:


**Where** Heb. אֵי פֹה, lit. where here.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 38:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:5:1)


**מְמַדֶּיהָ.** מִדּוֹתֶיהָ:


**its measures** Heb. מְמַדֶיהָ [like] מִדוֹתֶיהָ.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 38:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:6:1)


**יָרָה אֶבֶן פִּנָּתָהּ.** בְּאֶמְצַע הַיָּם, וּמִשָּׁם נִשְׁתַּת כָּל הָעוֹלָם:


**laid its cornerstone** in the middle of the sea, whence the entire world was founded. Where were you?



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 38:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:7:1)


**בְּרָן יַחַד.** מִתְּחִלָּה כּוֹכְבֵי אוֹר:


**When...sing together** from the beginning the stars of light.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 38:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:8:1)


**בִּדְלָתַיִם יָם.** חוֹל גְּבוּל לוֹ:


**the sea with doors** The sand, which is its boundary.


###### Rashi on Job 38:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:8:2)


**בְּגִיחוֹ.** בְּהַמְשָׁכָתוֹ יוֹצֵא מִן הַתְּהוֹם, כְּמוֹ ״גֹּחִי מִבָּטֶן״ (תהלים כב:י) – מוֹשְׁכִי:


**when it broke forth** Heb. בגיחו, when it broke forth from the deep, like (Ps. 22:10), “that You drew me (גחי) forth from the womb”; drew me forth.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Job 38:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:9:1)


**בְּשׂוּמִי עָנָן.** סְבִיבוֹת יָם אוֹקְיָינוֹס, שֶׁהוּא הֶיקֵּף הָעוֹלָם; וַעֲנָנִים סוֹבְבִים לוֹ כִּלְבוּשׁ:


**When I made the cloud** around the ocean, which encompasses the world, and clouds encompass it like raiment.


###### Rashi on Job 38:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:9:2)


**חֲתֻלָּתוֹ.** תַּכְרִיכוֹ, כְּמוֹ ״וְהָחְתֵּל לֹא חֻתָּלְתְּ״ (יחזקאל טז:ד):


**its wrapping** Heb. חתלתו, its wrapping, like (Ezek. 16:4), “nor were you swaddled (והחתל לא חתלת).”



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 38:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:10:1)


**וָאֶשְׁבֹּר.** עָשִׂיתִי לוֹ שְׁבָרִים סָבִיב, לְעַכְּבוֹ בְּתוֹכָם. וְהֵם לוֹ חֹק אֲשֶׁר לֹא יַעַבְרֵם (ירמיה ה:כב), כְּמוֹ ״וַיִּרְדְּפוּם לִפְנֵי הַשַּׁעַר עַד הַשְּׁבָרִים״ (יהושע ז:ה) – וְהֵם תְּעָלוֹת סָבִיב לָעִיר:


**and broke** I made breaks in it [the sea] all around to keep it back within them, and they are its boundary, which it shall not pass, as in (Joshua 7:5), “and they pursued them until the quarries (השברים)” which are the ditches around the city.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 38:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:12:1)


**הֲמִיָּמֶיךָ.** מִיּוֹם שֶׁנּוֹלַדְתָּ:


**In [all] your days** From the day you were born.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 38:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:13:1)


**לֶאֱחֹז בְּכַנְפוֹת הָאָרֶץ.** וְעוֹד **הֲמִיָּמֶיךָ צִוִּיתָ לֶאֱחֹז בְּכַנְפֵי הָאָרֶץ** כְּאָדָם שֶׁאוֹחֵז בְּכַנְפֵי הַטַּלִּית וּמְנַעֲרָהּ? כֵּן אֲנִי עָתִיד לֶאֱחֹז בְּכַנְפוֹתֶיהָ וּלְנַעֵר אֶת הָרְשָׁעִים. וְכֵן הוּא אוֹמֵר ״וַה׳ יַטֶּה אֶת יָדוֹ וְכָשַׁל עוֹזֵר״ (ישעיהו לא:ג) כְּאָדָם הָאוֹחֵז בְּיָדוֹ דָּבָר, וּמַטֶּה יָדוֹ, וְהוּא נוֹפֵל:


**To grasp the corners of the earth** Moreover, in all your days, did you command [anyone] to grasp the corners of the earth like a man who grasps the corners of a garment and shakes it? So am I destined to grasp it [the earth] by its corners and shake the wicked [out of it]. Likewise, Scripture states (Isa. 31:3): “and the Lord shall turn His hand, and the helper shall stumble,” like a person who, holding something in his hand, drops it by inclining his hand.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 38:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:14:1)


**תִּתְהַפֵּךְ.** בְּצִיּוּרִין:


**changes** in formation.


###### Rashi on Job 38:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:14:2)


**כְּחֹמֶר חוֹתָם.** מִטֶּבַע צוּרַת הָאָדָם בְּמוֹתָן, וּבִתְחִיַּת הַמֵּתִים יִחְיוּ וְיַעַמְדוּ בִּלְבוּשֵׁיהֶם:


**The seal...like clay** The cast of man’s countenance [changes] when he dies, and at the resurrection of the dead they will live again, and they will stand up in their clothing.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 38:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:15:1)


**וְיִמָּנַע.** אָז **מֵרְשָׁעִים אוֹרָם.** וְכֵן הוּא אוֹמֵר: **וּזְרוֹעַ רָמָה תִּשָּׁבֵר**:


**And...shall be withheld** then from the wicked their light, and the high arm shall be broken.

**the locks of the sea** Heb. נבכי, like (Ex. 14:3), “They are trapped (נבכים) in the land.”

###### Rashi on Job 38:17:1

### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Job 38:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:23:1)


**אֲשֶׁר חָשַׂכְתִּי.** הִמְנַעְתִּי:


**That I saved** I withheld.


###### Rashi on Job 38:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:23:2)


**לְעֵת צָר.** לַחֲמֵשֶׁת מְלָכִים בְּגִבְעוֹן (יהושע י:טז-כז):


**for a time of trouble** For the five kings in Gibeon.


###### Rashi on Job 38:23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:23:3)


**לְיוֹם קְרָב.** מִלְחֶמֶת גּוֹג וּמָגוֹג (יחזקאל לח-לט):


**for a day of battle** The war of Gog and Magog.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Job 38:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:24:1)


**יֵחָלֶק אוֹר.** תִּימוֹר שֶׁל חַמָּה מַפְצִיעַ לְכָאן וּלְכָאן כְּקַרְנֵי אַיָּל:


**is the light parted** The irradiation of the sun, which diverges this way and that way like the horns of a hind.


###### Rashi on Job 38:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:24:2)


**קָדִים.** שֶׁמֶשׁ הַמִּזְרָח:


**the eastern one** The sun of the east.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Job 38:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:25:1)


**מִי פִלַּג לַשֶּׁטֶף תְּעָלָה.** בַּעֲרַבְיָא קוֹרִין לְשַׂעֲרָה שִׁיטְפָא: כָּל שֵׂעָר וְשֵׂעָר שֶׁבָּרֹאשׁ פִּלַּגְתִּי לוֹ תְּעָלָה, גּוּמָא, לִינֹק מִמֶּנָּה; שֶׁאִלּוּ שְׁנַיִם יוֹנְקוֹת מִגּוּמָא אַחַת, מַחְשִׁיכוֹת מְאוֹר עֵינָיו שֶׁל אָדָם. וְהֵם לֹא נִתְחַלְּפוּ לִי, בֵּין אִיּוֹב לְאוֹיֵב נִתְחַלֵּף לִי?! וְזֶהוּ ״מִן הַסְּעָרָה״ (איוב לח:א). דָּבָר אַחֵר: **לַשֶּׁטֶף.** טִפַּת גֶּשֶׁם שׁוֹטֵף: פִּלַּגְתִּי לְכָל טִפָּה גּוּמָא בֶּעָבִים; שֶׁאִלּוּ יוֹרְדוֹת שְׁתֵּי טִפּוֹת מִגּוּמָא אַחַת, מְטַשְׁטְשׁוֹת אֶת הָאָרֶץ, וְנַעֲשֵׂית טִיט:


**Who has separated a place for the hair** Heb. לטשף. In Arabia, a hair is called שִׁיטְפָא. To every hair on the head I have given תְעָלָה, a separate shaft from which to draw sap. If two would draw from the same shaft, a person’s eyesight would fail. These have not been changed by Me, how then should I change אִיוֹב, Job, for אוֹיֵב, enemy? And this is the meaning of מן הסערה, from the hair. Another explanation: לַשֶטֶף a streaming raindrop. I set apart for each drop a hole in the clouds, for if they were to come down [together] from one opening, they would soften the earth and turn it into a mire.


###### Rashi on Job 38:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:25:2)


**וְדֶרֶךְ לַחֲזִיז קוֹלוֹת.** מִי פִלַּג? כָּל קוֹל וָקוֹל יֵשׁ לוֹ שְׁבִיל, וְאִם שְׁנַיִם הוֹלְכִים בִּשְׁבִיל אֶחָד, אֵין הַבְּרִיּוֹת סוֹבְלוֹת אֶת הַקּוֹל:


**and a way for the cloud of the thunderclaps** who parted? Every thunderclap has its own path, and if two of them would go along one path, the creatures would not be able to bear the sound.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Job 38:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:27:1)


**שׁוֹאָה.** אֶרֶץ שֶׁהִיא חֲשֵׁכָה בְּרָעָב:


**darkness** A land that is dark because of hunger.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Job 38:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:28:1)


**אֶגְלֵי טָל.** גַּלֵּי טָל, וְהָאָלֶ״ף יְתֵרָה; כְּמוֹ ״וַאֲחַוֶּה דַעְתִּי בְאָזְנֵיכֶם״ (איוב יג:יז) – אָלֶ״ף יְתֵרָה בְּרֹאשׁ הַתֵּיבָה, לִהְיוֹתוֹ שֵׁם דָּבָר; וְכֵן ״אַבְחַת חֶרֶב״ (יחזקאל כא:כ) – כְּמוֹ נִבּוּחַ חֶרֶב, צַעֲקַת חֶרֶב. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: **אֶגְלֵי.** כְּמוֹ אַגְנֵי טָל, כְּמִין אֲגָנוֹת – פְּלָגִים, שֶׁהַטַּל נָתוּן בָּהֶם. לָמֶ״ד מִתְחַלֵּף בְּנוּ״ן; וְכֵן בְּסֵפֶר נְחֶמְיָה: ״נִשְׁכָּה״ (נחמיה יג:ז) כְּמוֹ ״לִשְׁכָּה״ (נחמיה יג:ה):


**waves of dew** Heb. אגלי, waves of גלי, dew, and the “aleph” is superfluous, as in (above 13: 17), “and to my speech (ואחותי) in your ears.” The “aleph” is superfluous at the beginning of the word because it is a noun, and similarly (Ezek. 21:20), “the scream of (אבחת) the sword,” like נבוח, barking, the scream of the sword. Another explanation: is like אַגְנֵי, basins of dew. A type of basins, divisions in which the dew is deposited. The “lammed” is interchangeable with a “nun,” and so in the Book of Ezra (Neh. 13:7), chamber (נִשְׁכָּה) like לִשְׁכָּה.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Job 38:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:29:1)


**כְּפוֹר.** גְּרִיסָ״א:


**and the frost** glass or glassa in Old French.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Job 38:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:30:1)


**כָּאֶבֶן מַיִם יִתְחַבָּאוּ.** עַל יְדֵי הַקֶּרַח שֶׁמַּגְלִיד טִפָּה שֶׁעַל פְּנֵיהֶם:


**Like a stone...hides** because of the ice that congeals in drops upon their faces.


###### Rashi on Job 38:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:30:2)


**וּפְנֵי תְהוֹם יִתְלַכָּדוּ.** בּוֹ, וְיִתְאַחֲזוּ כְּאֶחָד:


**and the face of the deep is caught** and held together by it.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Job 38:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:31:1)


**מַעֲדַנּוֹת כִּימָה.** קִשּׁוּר כִּימָה, שֶׁלֹּא תֵצֵא כָּל קוֹרוֹתָיו, וְיַחֲרִיב אֶת הָעוֹלָם בְּצִנָּה:


**the chains of the Pleiades** [Tie] the chains of the Pleiades so that all its cold should not go forth and destroy the world with cold.


###### Rashi on Job 38:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:31:2)


**אוֹ מוֹשְׁכוֹת כְּסִיל תְּפַתֵּחַ.** לְהוֹצִיא חַמָּה, לְהָפִיג צִנָּתָהּ שֶׁל כִּימָה:


**or loose the straps of Orion** to bring out its heat, to mitigate the cold of the Pleiades.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Job 38:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:32:1)


**מַזָּרוֹת.** כָּל שְׁאָר מַזָּלוֹת:


**constellations** All the other constellations.


###### Rashi on Job 38:32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:32:2)


**וְעַיִשׁ.** כּוֹכָב גָּדוֹל שֶׁבַּכִּימָה, וְהַרְבֵּה כּוֹכָבִים קְבוּעִים בּוֹ; וְנָטַל שְׁנֵי כוֹכָבִים מִמֶּנָּה, לִפְתֹּחַ אֲרֻבּוֹת הַמַּבּוּל, וְנִבְקְעוּ בְּמַזַּל טָלֶה; וְעָתִיד הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לְהַחֲזִירָם לָהּ (תנחומא):


**and...Ayish** The largest star in the Pleiades, to which many stars are attached. He took two stars from it to open the windows of the Deluge, and they were placed in the constellation of Aries and the Holy One, blessed be He, is destined to restore them to her. Tanchuma.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Job 38:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:33:1)


**מִשְׁטָרוֹ.** שְׁטִירַת הַמַּזָּלוֹת הַמְּמֻנִּים לְהָבִיא עַל הָאָרֶץ ״קֹר וָחֹם קַיִץ וָחֹרֶף״ (בראשית ח:כב):


**his dominion** The dominion of the constellations appointed to bring upon the earth cold and heat, summer and winter.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on Job 38:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:34:1)


**הֲתָרִים לָעָב קוֹלֶךָ.** לְצַוּוֹת עָלָיו לְהִתְקַשֵּׁר בְּחֶשְׁכַת מַיִם לִהְיוֹת **שִׁפְעַת מַיִם** מְתְכַסֶּךָ, וְהוּא קַדְרוּת הֶעָבִים, כְּמוֹ ״אוֹ חֹשֶׁךְ לֹא תִרְאֶה״ (איוב כב:יא). אוֹ **שִׁפְעַת מַיִם.** עַל פִּיךָ יְכַסֶּךָּ מִן הַשָּׁמַיִם:


**Can you lift up your voice to the cloud** to command it to tie up with the darkness of water so that an abundance of water cover you? That is the darkening of clouds, like (above 22:11), “or darkness so that you do not see.” **[or] will an abundance of water** from Heaven cover you by your commands.



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on Job 38:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:35:1)


**וְיֹאמְרוּ לְךָ.** בַּמָּקוֹם שֶׁנִּשְׁתַּלְּחוּ שָׁם **הִנֵּנוּ**, עָשִׂינוּ שְׁלִיחוּתְךָ! שֶׁאֵין צְרִיכִין לַחֲזֹר לִמְקוֹמָן, וּלְהָשִׁיב שׁלוּחָן, שֶׁהַשְּׁכִינָה בְּכָל מָקוֹם:


**and say to you** in the place where they were sent, ‘Here we are; we have performed our errand,’ because they need not return to their place to reply to their Sender, for the Shechinah is everywhere.



### Verse 36

###### Rashi on Job 38:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:36:1)


**בַּטֻּחוֹת.** אֵלּוּ הַכְּלָיוֹת:


**in the inward parts** These are the reins.


###### Rashi on Job 38:36:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:36:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:36:2)


**לַשֶּׂכְוִי.** זֶה תַּרְנְגוֹל; לְשׁוֹן חֲכָמִים (ראש השנה כו.). וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים: זֶה הַלֵּב, שֶׁהוּא שׂוֹכֶה וְצוֹפֶה וּמְחַשֵּׁב אֶת הַנּוֹלָדוֹת:


**the rooster** Heb. לשכוי. This is the rooster in the language of the Sages (Rosh Hashanah 26a). And some say: This is the heart, which looks (שוכה), watches, and considers the coming events.



### Verse 37

###### Rashi on Job 38:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:37:1)


**יְסַפֵּר.** יְדַבֵּר וְיַזְהִיר תַּפְקִידָם:


**charges** Heb. יספר, will speak and charge with their mission.


###### Rashi on Job 38:37:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:37:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:37:2)


**וְנִבְלֵי.** עֲשׂוּיִין כְּנוֹדוֹת, וְהַגְּשָׁמִים כְּנוּסִים בּוֹ שָׁם, כְּמוֹ נֵבֶל יָיִן (שמואל א י:ג):


**the bottles** Made like bottles in which the rains are collected, as in (I Sam. 1:24), “and an earthenware jug (ונבל) of wine.”



### Verse 38

###### Rashi on Job 38:38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:38:1)


**בְּצֶקֶת עָפָר.** בְּיוֹם שֶׁיָּצַקְתִּי עָפָר:


**When the dust was poured** On the day that I poured the dust.


###### Rashi on Job 38:38:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:38:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:38:2)


**לַמּוּצָק.** לַיְּסוֹד עוֹלָם בְּאֶמְצָעִיתוֹ:


**into a mass** for the foundation of the world, into its midst.


###### Rashi on Job 38:38:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:38:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:38:3)


**וּרְגָבִים יְדֻבָּקוּ.** סְבִיבוֹתָיו לַצְּדָדִין, עַד כִּי נִתְמַלֵּא אָרְכּוֹ וְרָחְבּוֹ:


**and the clods cleaved together** around it on all sides, until its length and width were filled.



### Verse 40

###### Rashi on Job 38:40:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:40:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:40:1)


**יָשׁוֹחוּ.** עוֹמְדִים צְפוּפִים:


**they crouch** They stand crouching to lie in wait.


###### Rashi on Job 38:40:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:40:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:40:2)


**לְמוֹ אָרֶב.** לֶאֱרוֹב. כֵּן דֶּרֶךְ הָאוֹרְבִים, לָשׁוּחַ וְלָכוֹף עַצְמָן, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יַכִּירוּ בָּהֶם:


**to lie in wait** so is the habit of those who lie in wait, to crouch and to stoop themselves so that they will not be recognized.



### Verse 41

###### Rashi on Job 38:41:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:41:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/38:41:1)


**יִתְעוּ לִבְלִי אֹכֶל.** שֶׁאֲבִיהֶם שׂוֹנְאָם. הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מְזַמֵּן לָהֶם יַתּוּשִׁין, נִבְרָאִים מִצּוֹאָתָן, וְנִכְנָסִין לְתוֹךְ פִּיהֶם:


**they wander for lack of food** for their own fathers hate them, and the Holy One, blessed be He, prepares gnats for them, which are created from their dung, and they enter their mouths.



## Chapter 39


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Job 39:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:1:1)


**יַעֲלֵי סָלַע.** אֶשְׁטַנְבּוֹ״ק, וְשׂוֹנְאָה אֶת וְלָדָהּ; וְעוֹלָה לְרֹאשׁ סֶלַע גָּבוֹהַּ כְּשֶׁכּוֹרַעַת לֵילֵד, כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּפּוֹל לָאָרֶץ וְיָמוּת. וְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מְזַמֵּן לוֹ נֶשֶׁר וּמְקַבְּלוֹ בִּכְנָפָיו:


**mountain goats** Estainboc in Old French. It hates its young, and, when preparing to give birth, goes to the top of a high rock, so that its young should fall to the earth and die. But the Holy One, blessed be He, prepares an eagle for it [the young], which receives it on its wings.


###### Rashi on Job 39:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:1:2)


**חוֹלֵל אַיָּלוֹת תִּשְׁמֹר.** לְשׁוֹן ״חִיל יוֹלֵדָה״ (ירמיהו ו:כד). הָאַיָּלָה – רַחְמָהּ צַר, וְאֵין הַוָּלָד יָכוֹל לָצֵאת; וּבִשְׁעַת לֵידָתָהּ אֲנִי מְזַמֵּן לָהּ דְּרָקוֹן, וּמַכִּישָׁהּ בְּרַחְמָהּ, וְנִפְתָּח. וְאִם מַקְדִּים וּמְאַחֵר רֶגַע, מִיָּד מֵתָה. וּבֵין אוֹתָם רְגָעִים אֵינִי מַחֲלִיף, וּבֵין ׳אִיּוֹב׳ לְ׳אוֹיֵב׳ נִתְחַלֵּף לִי?!


**Do you wait for the hinds to calve?** Heb. חלל, an expression of (Jer. 6:24), “pain (חיל) as of a woman in travail.” The hind’s womb is narrow, and the young cannot come forth; and, at the time of birth, I prepare a snake for her that bites her womb so that it opens; should it be a moment too early or too late, she would die immediately. I know to distinguish these moments, so should the distinction between Job (אִיוֹב) and enemy (אוֹיֵב) escape Me?



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 39:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:3:1)


**תְּפַלַּחְנָה.** מִתְבַּקֵּעַ רֶחֶם שֶׁלָּהֶם, וּמוֹצִיא הַוְּלָדוֹת, וְחֶבְלֵי לֵידָה שֶׁלָּהֶם:


**they bring forth** Their womb splits open and gives forth the young, and their birth pangs...


###### Rashi on Job 39:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:3:2)


**תְּשַׁלַּחְנָה.** מֵעֲלֵיהֶם בְּלִדְתָּן, לְרֶגַע שֶׁאֲנִי מַזְמִין לָהֶם:


**they send forth** from themselves in their birth at the moment that I prepare for them.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 39:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:4:1)


**יַחְלְמוּ.** יֶחֱזְקוּ:


###### Rashi on Job 39:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:4:2)


**וְלֹא שָׁבוּ לָמוֹ.** מִשֶּׁיִּרְבּוּ קְצָת, יִגְדְּלוּ בַּבָּר, בַּזְּרָעִים וּבַדְּשָׁאִים, וְאֵינָם צְרִיכִים לְגִדּוּל אֲבִיהֶם וְאִמָּם:


**and do not return to them** When they have grown a little, they are raised on the grain, on seeds and grasses, and they do not require the raising of their father or mother.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 39:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:5:1)


**חָפְשִׁי.** שֶׁאֵין אָדָם יָכוֹל לְגַדֵּל פֶּרֶא, לְלַמְּדוֹ עֲבוֹדַת בְּהֵמוֹת:


**free** For no man can raise a wild donkey to teach it the work of cattle.


###### Rashi on Job 39:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:5:2)


**עָרוֹד.** חֲמוֹר הַבָּר:


###### Rashi on Job 39:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:5:3)


**מִי פִתֵּחַ.** מוֹסְרוֹתָיו, שֶׁאֵין עָלָיו עֹל אָדָם:


**who loosed** his bands so that the yoke of man is not upon him.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 39:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:8:1)


**יְתוּר הָרִים מִרְעֵהוּ.** הוּא עַצְמוֹ תָּר לוֹ מְקוֹם מִרְעֶה:


**He spies out the mountains for his pasture** He personally spies out for himself a place of pasture.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Job 39:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:9:1)


**רֵים.** כְּמוֹ ״רְאֵם״ (במדבר כג:כב):


**wild ox** Heb. רֵים, like רְאֵם.


###### Rashi on Job 39:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:9:2)


**עָבְדֶּךָ.** לְעָבְדְּךָ:


**serve you** To serve you.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 39:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:10:1)


**בְּתֶלֶם עֲבֹתוֹ.** (אוֹ קָשַׁרְתָּ רְאֵם בַּעֲבוּר תֶּלֶם שֶׁל שׁוֹרְךָ?) **עֲבֹת.** חֶבֶל שֶׁקּוֹשְׁרִין (שְׁוָורִים) לְתַלְמֵי הַמַּחֲרֵשָׁה:


**to a furrow with his rope** (Or did you tie a wild ox because of the furrow of your ox?) **rope** Heb. עבתו, a rope with which they tie [oxen] to the furrows of the plow.


###### Rashi on Job 39:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:10:2)


**יְשַׂדֵּד.** יְתַקֵּן הַשָּׂדֶה:


**will he harrow**, prepare the field.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 39:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:11:1)


**הֲתִבְטַח בּוֹ.** לֶאֱסֹף תְּבוּאָתְךָ, בִּשְׁבִיל שֶׁכֹּחוֹ רַב, וְיָכוֹל לִסְבֹּל הַרְבֵּה?


**Will you rely on him** to gather your grain because his strength is great and he can bear great burdens?


###### Rashi on Job 39:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:11:2)


**וְתַעֲזֹב אֵלָיו יְגִיעֶךָ.** לֶאֱסֹף אֶל הַבַּיִת:


**or will you leave your toil for him** to gather into the house?



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 39:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:13:1)


**כְּנַף רְנָנִים נֶעֱלָסָה.** כֵּן שֵׁם עוֹף גָּדוֹל רְנָנִים, וּלְשׁוֹן מִשְׁנָה קָרוּי בַּר יוֹכְנִי (בכורות נז:):


**The wing of the renanim rejoices** This is the name of a huge bird, called in the language of the Mishnah, Bar Yochni (Bechoroth 57b).


###### Rashi on Job 39:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:13:2)


**אֶבְרָה.** כָּל עוֹף קָרוּי אֶבְרָה, עַל שֵׁם שֶׁפּוֹרֵחַ. כְּלוֹמַר: אוֹ עוֹף שֶׁשְּׁמוֹ **חֲסִידָה**, וְעוֹף שֶׁשְּׁמוֹ **נֹצָה.** רְאֵה מָה מִנְהָגָהּ:


**the winged** Heb. אֶבְרָה. Every bird is called אֶבְרָה because it flies; i.e., either the bird named “stork,” or the bird called nozah. Note her behavior.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 39:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:14:1)


**תַּעֲזֹב לָאָרֶץ בֵּיצֶיהָ.** וְהוֹלֶכֶת וּמִתְחַמֶּמֶת לְמֵרָחוֹק **עַל עָפָר**:


**For she leaves her eggs on the ground** and she goes and warms herself on the dust at a distance.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 39:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:15:1)


**וַתִּשְׁכַּח כִּי רֶגֶל תְּזוּרֶהָ.** לְבֵיצָתָהּ תְּבַקְּעֶנָּה:


**She forgets that a foot will crack it** Heb. תְּזוּרֶהָ [i.e., it will crack] her egg. [תְּזוּרֶהָ means:] will crack it.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 39:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:16:1)


**הִקְשִׁיחַ.** הִקְשָׁה בָּנֶיהָ מִלִּבָּהּ, כְּאִלּוּ אֵינָם שֶׁלָּהּ:


**She is hardened** She deals harshly with her young ones, [distancing them] from her heart as though they were not hers.


###### Rashi on Job 39:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:16:2)


**לְרִיק יְגִיעָהּ.** וְאֵינָהּ יְרֵאָה שֶׁתְּאַבְּדֵם:


**her toil is in vain** and she is not afraid to lose them.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Job 39:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:18:1)


**כָּעֵת בַּמָּרוֹם תַּמְרִיא.** כָּל לְשׁוֹן הַמְרָאָה כְּאָדָם שֶׁזּוֹנֶה לִבּוֹ לָצֵאת מִמִּדָּתוֹ וּמִגִּדּוּלוֹ וּמֵאַרְצוֹ, לְשׁוֹטֵט לוֹ בָּאֲרָצוֹת, וּלְנַסּוֹת לוֹ מִדּוֹת אֲחֵרוֹת. וְכֵן ״בֵּן סוֹרֵר וּמוֹרֶה״ (דברים כא:יח). וּבִלְשׁוֹן גְּמָרָא: אִימְרָאִי כַּלְּתֵיהּ דְּרַב זְבִיד, אִימְרָאִי וְנָפְקָא (כתובות סג: ושם איתא אימרדא ועיין ברש״י בחולין נח ומציעא עו ד״ה ואימרו):


**Like the time that she goes off on high** [When she flies up, she scorns the horse. She is not afraid that he will tread on the eggs and crack them.] Every expression of הַמְרָאָה applies to a man whose heart prompts him to stray from his lifestyle, his upbringing, and his country, to roam in other countries and to try other lifestyles. Similarly (Deut. 21:18), “a stubborn and rebellious (וּמרֶה) son”; and in the language of the Talmud it is אִמְרָאִי: “Rav Zevid’s daughter-in-law rebelled (אִמְרָאִי) and went away” (Kethuboth 63b, Venice edition) (cf. Rashi to Hullin 58b, Baba Mezia 77a).



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 39:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:19:1)


**רַעְמָה.** לְשׁוֹן רַעַם וּבְעָתָה, כְּמוֹ ״רָעֲמוּ פָנִים״ (יחזקאל כז:לה):


**fierceness** Heb. רעמה, an expression of thunder and fright, like (Ezek. 27:35), “Their faces are as though thunderstruck (רעמו).”



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 39:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:20:1)


**כָּאַרְבֶּה.** מְדַלֵּג וּמְקַפֵּץ כָּאַרְבֶּה וּמַרְעִישׁ סְבִיבָיו:


**like a locust** He skips and leaps like a locust and shakes himself all over.


###### Rashi on Job 39:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:20:2)


**נַחְרוֹ.** כְּשֶׁהוּא נוֹפֵחַ בִּנְחִירָיו בְּקוֹל אֵימָה הוּא:


**his snorting** When he blows with his nostrils, it is with an awesome sound.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Job 39:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:21:1)


**יַחְפְּרוּ בָעֵמֶק.** כְּמוֹ ״וְיַחְפְּרוּ לָנוּ אֶת הָאָרֶץ״ (דברים א:כב), שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ הַפָּרָשִׁים אוֹרְבִים בָּעֲמָקִים וּבַנְּחָלִים:


**They spy in the valley** Heb. יחפרו, like (Deut. 1:22), “that they may spy out (ויחפרו) the land for us,” for it is the habit of the horsemen to lie in wait in the valleys and in the ravines.


###### Rashi on Job 39:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:21:2)


**וְיָשִׂישׂ בְּכֹחַ.** וְהַסּוּס שָׂמֵחַ וְיוֹצֵא לִקְרַאת נָשֶׁק:


**and rejoices with strength** The horse rejoices and goes forth toward the weapons.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Job 39:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:22:1)


**וְלֹא יֵחָת.** וְלֹא יִירָא:


**and is not dismayed** He will not be frightened.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Job 39:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:23:1)


**תִּרְנֶה אַשְׁפָּה.** מְלֵאָה חִצִּים, וּמְקַשְׁקְשׁוֹת זוֹ בְּזוֹ וְנִשְׁמַע קוֹלָם:
