**The quiver...rattles** [i.e., the quiver that is] full of arrows, and they knock against each other so that their sound is heard.


###### Rashi on Job 39:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:23:2)


**לַהַב חֲנִית.** כְּלִי שֶׁל בַּרְזֶל (וַחֲנִית שֶׁל בַּרְזֶל) קָרוּי לַהַב, כְּמוֹ ״וַיָּבֹא גַם הַנִּצָּב אַחַר הַלַּהַב״ (שופטים ג:כב):


**the blade of the spear** An iron utensil (and an iron spear) is called לַהַב, as in (Jud. 3:22), “and the haft also went in after the blade (הלהב).”



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Job 39:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:24:1)


**יְגַמֶּא אָרֶץ.** עוֹשֶׂה גֻּמּוֹת בְּרַגְלָיו. עִנְיָן אַחֵר: **יְגַמֶּא** כְּמוֹ ״הַגְמִיאִינִי נָא״ (בראשית כד:יז):


**he hollows out the ground** Heb. יגמא. He makes holes (גומות) with his feet. Another explanation: יְגַמֶא is like (Gen. 24:17), “Let me please swallow (הגמיאיני).”


###### Rashi on Job 39:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:24:2)


**וְלֹא יַאֲמִין.** מֵרוֹב שִׂמְחָה, שֶׁהוּא מִתְאַוֶּה לַמִּלְחָמָה:


**and he does not believe** out of the great joy that he longs for battle.


###### Rashi on Job 39:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:24:3)


**כִּי קוֹל שׁוֹפָר.** שֶׁל מִלְחָמָה:


**that it is the sound of a shofar** of battle.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Job 39:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:25:1)


**בְּדֵי שׁוֹפָר.** לְשׁוֹן ״דֵּי הָשִׁיב לוֹ״ (ויקרא כה:כח) – בְּהַרְבֵּה שׁוֹפָר:


**To many shofaroth** Heb. בדי, an expression of (Lev. 25:28), “sufficient (די) to get it back,” to many shofaroth.


###### Rashi on Job 39:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:25:2)


**יֹאמַר הֶאָח.** לָשׁוֹן שֶׁאוֹמְרִים (הָעוֹבְרִים) כְּשֶׁהֵן שְׂמֵחִין, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאוֹמְרִים ״אֲהָהּ״ (יהושע ז:ז) מֵחֲמַת צַעַר:


**he says, ‘Hurrah!’** An expression that (the passersby) (the slaves—Berechiah) say when they are happy, much as they say, ‘Woe!’ out of pain.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Job 39:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:26:1)


**יַאֲבֶר נֵץ.** יִגְדַּל הַנֵּץ כָּנָף. וּמַלְאָךְ הוּא דּוֹמֶה לִדְמוּת נֵץ, וּמַעֲמִיד קֹשִׁי הָרוּחַ דְּרוֹמִית בִּפְרִישׂוּת כְּנָפָיו, שֶׁלֹּא תַּחֲרִיב אֶת הָעוֹלָם:


**the hawk grows a wing** Heb. יאבר, the hawk grows a wing. That is an angel resembling a hawk, who stays the severity of the south wind by spreading out his wings, lest it destroy the world.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Job 39:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:27:1)


**אִם עַל פִּיךָ יַגְבִּיהַּ נָשֶׁר.** הַשְּׁכִינָה, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: ״כְּנֶשֶׁר יָעִיר קִנּוֹ״ (דברים לב:יא) – הָיָה מְסַלֵּק פָּמַלְיָא שֶׁלּוֹ, שֶׁלֹּא יַזִּיקוּ לְאַהֲרֹן בְּבוֹאוֹ אֶל הַקֹּדֶשׁ בְּיוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים:


**Or is it by your order that the eagle flies high** [This refers to] the Shechinah, as it is said (Deut. 32:11): “As an eagle first stirs up its nest.” He would remove His court so that they should not injure Aaron when he entered the sanctuary on Yom Kippur.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Job 39:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:28:1)


**סֶלַע יִשְׁכֹּן וְיִתְלוֹנָן.** בָּטוּחַ הָיָה אַהֲרֹן בְּמַחְתַּת הַקְּטֹרֶת, כְּשֶׁנְּתָנָהּ עַל אֶבֶן שְׁתִיָּה (יומא נג:):


**He dwells on the rock and lodges** Aaron was confident when he placed the censer with the incense on the foundation stone [in the Holy of Holies].



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Job 39:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:29:1)


**מִשָּׁם חָפַר אֹכֶל.** מִתְפַּלֵּל עַל צָרְכֵי כָּל הַשָּׁנָה. וְאַחַר כָּל הַכָּבוֹד הַזֶּה רָאָה בְּמִיתַת שְׁנֵי אֶפְרוֹחָיו נָדָב וַאֲבִיהוּא, שֶׁמֵּתוּ לְהִתְקַדֵּשׁ שְׁמִי בָּהֶם (ויקרא י:א-ב), שֶׁנָּפְלָה אֵימָתִי עַל הַשְּׁאָר:


**From there he searches for food** He would pray for the needs of the entire year, and after all this honor he saw the death of his two eaglets, Nadab and Abihu, who died so that My name might be sanctified through them, for My fear fell upon those who remained.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Job 39:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:30:1)


**יְעַלְעוּ.** וְאֵינָן אֶלָּא כְּפִי מִדַּת בָּלְעוֹ דָּם וּמַיִם, וְנִרְאֶה כְּבוֹלֵעַ הַרְבֵּה, כְּאוֹמֵר ״עַל עַל״. וְכֵן בִּלְשׁוֹן חֲכָמִים בְּהַשְׁקָאַת סוֹטָה (סוטה כ.): מְעַרְעֲרִין אוֹתָהּ וּמַשְׁקִין אוֹתָהּ עַל כָּרְחָהּ. וְכֵן ״זַעֲקַת שֶׁבֶר יְעֹעֵרוּ״ (ישעיהו טו:ה) – לְשׁוֹן קְרִיאַת גָּרוֹן דּוֹמֶה לְכָךְ. וּלְפִי הַדֶּרֶךְ דִּבֵּר הַנָּבִיא:


**gulp down** Heb. יעלעו. Only according to the measure that one is able to swallow of blood or water, but it appears as if one swallows much, in imitation of the sound “al al.” Similarly, in the language of the Sages, concerning the suspected adulteress [who must] drink [the cursing water] (Sotah 20a), “they make her swallow (מערערין) and drink against her will.” [Here too, the sound “ar ar” is alluded to.] Similarly (Isa. 15:5), “a cry of destruction they shall raise (יעוערו),” the prophet using the onomatopoetic word in imitation of the sound produced by the throat.


###### Rashi on Job 39:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/39:30:2)


**וּבַאֲשֶׁר חֲלָלִים שָׁם הוּא.** לֶאֱכוֹל. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ דִּמּוּ כָּל עִנְיָן זֶה בְּאַהֲרֹן, בִּפְסִיקְתָּא דְּ׳אַחֲרֵי מוֹת׳ (פסיקתא רבתי):


**and where there are slain, there he is** to eat. Our Sages explain this entire passage as an allegory applying to Aaron, in the Pesikta to Acharei Moth (Pesikta Rabbathi, ch. 48, Meir Ayin edition, ch. 47).



## Chapter 40


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 40:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:2:1)


**(מענה ה׳):**
**הֲרֹב עִם שַׁדַּי יִסּוֹר.** הַאִם שַׁדַּי – אָדָם יִשְׂתָּרֵר?


**Will one who contends with the Almighty make himself master?** May a man make himself master to contend with the Almighty?


###### Rashi on Job 40:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:2:2)


**מוֹכִיחַ אֱלוֹהַּ יַעֲנֶנָּה.** הַבָּא לְהִתְוַכֵּחַ, יַעֲנֶה דְּבָרָיו?


**He who argues with God, let him answer it** He who comes to argue, let him answer his words.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 40:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:5:1)


**(מענה איוב):** 
**אַחַת דִּבַּרְתִּי.** כְּלוֹמַר: מְעַט אָמַרְתִּי וְלֹא אוֹסִיף עוֹד. וְיֵשׁ פּוֹתְרִים: **אַחַת דִּבַּרְתִּי,** אֲשֶׁר אָמַרְתִּי (איוב ט:כב) ״תָּם וְרָשָׁע הוּא מְכַלֶּה״, **לֹא אֶעֱנֶה** עוֹד:


**I said one [thing** i.e., I have spoken little and I will add no more. Others explain: **I said one** What I said (above 9:22), “[It is all one; therefore I said,] ‘He destroys both the innocent and the wicked,’” I will no longer answer.


###### Rashi on Job 40:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:5:2)


**וּשְׁתַּיִם.** שֶׁאָמַרְתִּי: ״אַל תַּעַשׂ עִמָּדִי״ (איוב יג:כ), לֹא אוֹסִיף:


**and two** What I said (above 13:20), “Do not do two things to me,” I will not add.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 40:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:8:1)


**(מענה ה׳ מן הסערה):** 
**הַאַף תָּפֵר מִשְׁפָּטִי.** הַאַף תּוֹכִיחַ שֶׁדְּבָרֶיךָ לְהָפֵר מִשְׁפָּטִי, וּלְהַרְאוֹת שֶׁהוּא מְעֻקָּל?


(**God’s answer from the tempest**)
**Will you even make void My judgment** Will you even prove with your words to make My judgment void and to show that it is perverted?



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 40:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:10:1)


**עֲדֵה.** לְשׁוֹן עֲדִי וְתַכְשִׁיט:


**Bedeck yourself** Heb. עדה, an expression of an ornament (עדי) and decoration.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 40:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:12:1)


**וְהָדוֹךְ.** לְשׁוֹן ״דָּכוּ בַמְּדוֹכָה״ (במדבר יא:ח):


**and crush** Heb. והדך, an expression of (Num. 11:8), “or crushed (דכו) it in mortars.”



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 40:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:15:1)


**בְּהֵמוֹת.** מוּכָן לֶעָתִיד:


**behemoth** Prepared for the future.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 40:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:16:1)


**כֹּחוֹ בְמָתְנָיו.** בֵּיצָיו מְעוּכִים וְנִסְתָּרִים, וְלֹא נִתְּקוּ לְגַמְרֵי:


**his strength is in his loins** His testicles are squashed and concealed, and have not been completely torn off.


###### Rashi on Job 40:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:16:2)


**בִּשְׁרִירֵי.** כְּמוֹ ״שָׁרְרֵךְ״ (שיר השירים ז:ג):


**in the navel** Heb. בשרירי, like (Song 7:3), “Your navel (שררך).”



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Job 40:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:17:1)


**יַחְפֹּץ.** כְּמוֹ **יַחְפֹּז** (להלך כג) – מִתְקַשֶּׁה. וְכֵן בִּלְשׁוֹן חֲכָמִים ׳דַּחֲפִיזָא כִּי אוּפְתָּא׳ (חולין מז:) – קָשֶׁה כָּעֵץ:


**hardens** Heb. יחפץ like יַחְפּוֹז, hardens. Similarly, in the language of the Sages: דַחַפִּיזָא כִּי אוּפְתָּא (Hullin 47b), hard as wood.


###### Rashi on Job 40:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:17:2)


**פַּחֲדָיו.** בֵּיצָיו:


**his testicles** Heb. פַחֲדָיו.


###### Rashi on Job 40:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:17:3)


**יְשֹׂרָגוּ.** גְּדִילִים כְּמוֹ ״וַיִּשְׂתָּרְגוּ״ (איכה א:יד), אוֹ שְׂרִיגֵי מִטָּה בִּלְשׁוֹן מִשְׁנָה (כלים כד:ח), וְהוּא לְשׁוֹן גָּדִיל:


**are knit together** Heb. ישרגו, twisted cords, like (Lam. 1:14), “they are knit together (ישתרגו),” or the webbing (שריגי) of a bed in the language of the Mishnah, (Moed Katan 1:8), and it is an expression of twisted cords.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Job 40:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:18:1)


**אֲפִיקֵי.** חוֹזֶק:


###### Rashi on Job 40:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:18:2)


**גְּרָמָיו.** עֲצָמָיו:


###### Rashi on Job 40:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:18:3)


**כְּמַטִּיל.** כְּמַשָּׂאוֹי שֶׁל בַּרְזֶל:


**as a load** Heb. כִּמְטִיל, as a burden of iron.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 40:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:19:1)


**רֵאשִׁית דַּרְכֵי אֵל.** מִבְּרֵאשִׁית נִבְרָא רֹאשׁ לַבְּהֵמוֹת:


**He is the first of God’s ways** He was created at the beginning to be the chief of all animals.


###### Rashi on Job 40:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:19:2)


**הָעֹשׂוֹ.** הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא בְּעַצְמוֹ יִשְׁחֲטֶנּוּ:


**his Maker** The Holy One, blessed be He, Himself will slaughter him.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 40:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:20:1)


**כִּי בוּל הָרִים יִשְׂאוּ לוֹ.** וּבְסֵפֶר תְּהִלִּים כָּתוּב ״בְּהֵמוֹת בְּהַרְרֵי אָלֶף״ (תהלים נ:י), אֶלֶף הָרִים נָשְׂאוּ לוֹ יְבוּל וּבְכָל יוֹם רוֹעֶה אֶת כֻּלָּם. (עד כאן יסד רש״י מכאן ואילך אינו לשון רש״י):


**For the mountains bear food for him** And it is written in the Book of Psalms (50:10): “Behemoth upon a thousand mountains.” A thousand mountains bear food for him. He feeds on all of them every day, [and they are grown again the next day].



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Job 40:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:21:1)


**צֶאֱלִים.** צֵל אִילָנוֹת:


**the shadows** the shades of trees.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Job 40:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:22:1)


**יְסֻכֻּהוּ צֶאֱלִים צִלֲלוֹ.** אוֹתוֹ צֵל שֶׁל **סֵתֶר קָנֶה וּבִצָּה** מְסֻבָּךְ וּמְסֻכָּךְ בְּאִילָנוֹת הַרְבֵּה:


**Do the shadows cover him as his shadow** [Does] the shadow of the covert of the reeds and the swamp, which is entangled [or surrounded—Berechiah] and hedged around with [many] trees.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Job 40:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:23:1)


**הֵן יַעֲשֹׁק נָהָר וְלֹא יַחְפּוֹז.** פְּרָת, שֶׁמֵּימָיו פָּרִים וְרָבִים, וְהוּא נָהָר גָּדוֹל – עוֹשְׁקוֹ וְגוֹזְלוֹ מִן הַבְּרִיּוֹת בִּשְׁתוֹתוֹ:


**Behold, he plunders the river, and [he] does not harden** The Euphrates, which is a large river whose waters increase, he oppresses and plunders from the creatures by his drinking.


###### Rashi on Job 40:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:23:2)


**וְלֹא יַחְפּוֹז.** לֹא מִתְקַשֶּׁה בִּטְנוֹ עַל שְׁתִיָּתוֹ, כְּמוֹ **יַחְפֹּץ זְנָבוֹ**:


**and [he] does not harden** [His belly does not harden from his drinking, like (verse 17), “His tail hardens.”


###### Rashi on Job 40:23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:23:3)


**יִבְטַח כִּי יָגִיחַ יַרְדֵּן.** בָּטוּחַ הוּא לִמְשֹׁךְ כָּל הַיַּרְדֵּן לְפִיהוּ, אִלּוּ הָיָה שׁוֹתֶה שָׁם. כְּמוֹ ״גֹּחִי מִבָּטֶן״ (תהלים כב:י):


**he trusts that he will draw the Jordan** He is confident [of his ability] to draw the whole Jordan into his mouth were he to drink there: like (Ps. 22: 10), “Who drew me (גחי) from the womb.”



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Job 40:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:24:1)


**בְּעֵינָיו יִקָּחֶנּוּ.** הַמָּקוֹם יִקָּחֶנּוּ בְּהַבִּיטוֹ בּוֹ, וְנִרְעָד מֵאֵימָתוֹ:


**With His eyes He will take him** The Omnipresent will take him by looking at him, and he quakes from fear of Him.


###### Rashi on Job 40:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:24:2)


**בְּמוֹקְשִׁים.** כְּלֵי אוּמָּנוּת שֶׁל צַיָּדִים, שֶׁשּׁוֹלִים בָּהֶם דָּגִים:


**with snares** Fishermen’s tools, with which they catch fish.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Job 40:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:25:1)


**תִּמְשֹׁךְ לִוְיָתָן וְגוֹ׳.** כַּאֲשֶׁר תִּמְשְׁכֶנּוּ בְּחַכָּה וְתָשִׂים אַגְמוֹן בְּאַפּוֹ, הֲיְדַבֵּר אֵלֶיךָ תַּחֲנוּנִים? כְּלוֹמַר: לֹא תוּכַל (לְהִתְקָרֵב אֵלָיו) בִּכְלִי מְצוּדָה:


**Can you pull the leviathan etc.** When you pull him with a harpoon and insert a fishhook into his nose, will he offer much supplication to you? It means that you are unable to approach him with fishing implements.


###### Rashi on Job 40:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:25:2)


**תַּשְׁקִיעַ לְשׁוֹנוֹ.** תַּשְׁקִיעַ חֶבֶל בִּלְשׁוֹנוֹ, לְהוֹצִיאוֹ מִמְּקוֹמוֹ:


**or press down his tongue** Let the rope down (on his tongue) to draw him out of his place.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Job 40:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:26:1)


**אַגְמוֹן.** הוּא כָּפוּף, כְּמוֹ ״כִּפָּה וְאַגְמוֹן״ (ישעיה ט:יג); וְעָשׂוּי שֶׁל בַּרְזֶל, לָצוּד בּוֹ דָּגִים:


**a fishhook** Heb. התשים. It is bent, like (Isa. 9:13), “a large arch and a small arch (אגמן), and it is made of iron to catch fish with it.


###### Rashi on Job 40:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:26:2)


**הֲתָשִׂים.** אִם תָּשִׂים אַגְמוֹן בְּאַפּוֹ לְהוֹצִיאוֹ מִן הַיָּם?


**Can you insert** Will you insert a fishhook into his nose to take him out of the sea?


###### Rashi on Job 40:26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:26:3)


**וּבְחוֹחַ.** כְּלִי שֶׁל צַיָּדֵי דָּגִים:


**or...with a barb** A fishermen’s implement.


###### Rashi on Job 40:26:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:26:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:26:4)


**תִּקּוֹב.** כְּמוֹ תִּנְקֹב:


**or pierce** Heb. תִּקּב, like תִּנְקֹב.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Job 40:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:27:1)


**הֲיַרְבֶּה אֵלֶיךָ תַּחֲנוּנִים.** אִם יַרְבֶּה לְךָ תַּחֲנוּנִים וִיבַקֵּשׁ עַל נַפְשׁוֹ, שֶׁתַּנִּיחֵהוּ?


**Will he offer much supplication to you** Will he offer much supplication to you and beseech you to spare his life?


###### Rashi on Job 40:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:27:2)


**אִם יְדַבֵּר.** כֶּפֶל מִלָּה:


**or will he speak** This is a repetitious word.


###### Rashi on Job 40:27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:27:3)


**רַכּוֹת.** דְּבָרִים שֶׁל תַּחֲנוּנִים:


**soft words** Words of supplication.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Job 40:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:28:1)


**הֲיִכְרוֹת בְּרִית עִמָּךְ.** אִם יִכְרוֹת בְּרִית לְעָבְדְךָ, שֶׁתִּקָּחֶנּוּ לִהְיוֹת לְךָ עֶבֶד עוֹלָם?


**Will he make a covenant with you** Will he make a covenant to serve you, that you will take him as a lifelong slave?



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Job 40:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:29:1)


**הֲתִשְׂחַק.** אִם תְּשַׂחֵק וּתְטַיֵּל בּוֹ, כְּאִלּוּ הוּא צִפּוֹר קָטָן?


**Will you play** Will you play and stroll with him as though he were a small bird,


###### Rashi on Job 40:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:29:2)


**וְאִם תִּקְשְׁרֶנּוּ** לְצַוָּארֵי (ס״א לְצֹרֶךְ) נְעָרֶיךָ הַקְּטַנִּים, שֶׁיְּטַיְּלוּ בּוֹ; כְּצִפֳּרִים קְטַנִּים שֶׁנּוֹתְנִין לְתִינוֹקוֹת, לְטַיֵּל וּלְפַיְּסוֹ:


**and will you tie him** to the necks of (other editions: for the sake of) your young lads, that they should stroll with him as with small birds that they give children to stroll and to placate him?



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Job 40:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:30:1)


**יִכְרוּ עָלָיו חֲבֵרִים.** אִם יִכְרוּ וְיַחְכְּמוּ עָלָיו חֲבֵרִים וּמְכַשְּׁפִים, לָצוּד אוֹתוֹ לְהוֹצִיאוֹ מִן הַיָּם עַל יְדֵי מְכַשְּׁפוֹת, שֶׁיֶּחֱצוּהוּ וִיחַלְּקוּהוּ בֵּין תַּגָּרִים; כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁעוֹשִׂים צַיָּדֵי הַיָּם, שֶׁצָּדִין דָּגִים גְּדוֹלִים בְּחָכְמָתָם, וּבָאִים הַתַּגָּרִים וְקוֹנִים מֵהֶם:


**Will charmers dig pits for him** Will charmers and sorcerers dig pits to hunt him? To take him out of the sea by means of sorcery [so] that they can divide him and share him among the merchants, as the sea fishermen do? That is, they catch large fish with their cunning, and the merchants come and purchase from them.


###### Rashi on Job 40:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:30:2)


**יִכְרוּ.** לְשׁוֹן ״כּוֹרֶה שׁוּחָה״ (ירמיה יח:כב):


**dig pits** Heb. יכרו, an expression of (Jer. 18:22), digging a pit (כורה שוחה).


###### Rashi on Job 40:30:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:30:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:30:3)


**חֲבֵרִים.** לְשׁוֹן ״וְחֹבֵר חָבֶר״ (דברים יח:יא):


**charmers** Heb. חַבָרִים, an expression of (Deut. 18:11), “a charmer (וְחבֵר חָבֶר).”


###### Rashi on Job 40:30:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:30:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:30:4)


**כְּנַעֲנִים.** תַּגָּרִים:



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Job 40:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:31:1)


**הֲתְמַלֵּא.** אִם תְּמַלֵּא תַּאֲוָתְךָ, וְתַעֲשֶׂה סֻכּוֹת מֵעוֹרוֹ:


**Will you fulfill** Will you fulfill your desire and make tents of his skin?


###### Rashi on Job 40:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:31:2)


**וּבְצִלְצַל דָּגִים רֹאשׁוֹ.** וְאִם תַּעֲשֶׂה מֵרֹאשׁוֹ צִלְצַל דָּגִים? כֶּפֶל לָשׁוֹן:


**or a shade of fishes of his head** Will you make from his head a shade of fish? This is a repetitious expression.


###### Rashi on Job 40:31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:31:3)


**צִלְצַל.** סֻכַּת דָּגִים. וְלַסֻּכָּה עֲשׂוּיָה מֵרֹאשׁוֹ קוֹרֵא אוֹתָהּ **צִלְצַל דָּגִים**, לְפִי שֶׁעֲשׂוּיָה מִמֶּנּוּ:


**a shade** Heb. צִלְצַל, a tent of fish. A tent made from his head is called a shade of fish because it is made from him.


###### Rashi on Job 40:31:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:31:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:31:4)


**צִלְצַל.** אַמְבּוֹרִיץ בְּלַעַ״ז, לְשׁוֹן צֵל הוּא, וְהוּא מִתֵּיבוֹת כְּפוּלוֹת, כְּמוֹ ״יְרַקְרַק אֲדַמְדַּם״ (ויקרא יג:מט), ״פְּתַלְתֹּל״ (דברים לב:ה), ״קַעֲקַע״ (ויקרא יט:כח):


צִלְצַל is ombre in French. It is an expression of shadow, and that is one of the double words, like: green (ירקרק), red (אדמדם) [both in Lev. 13:49], twisted (פתלתל) [Deut. 32:5], tattoo (קעקע) [Lev. 19:28].



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Job 40:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:32:1)


**שִׂים עָלָיו כַּפֶּךָ.** הִתְגַּבֵּר לַעֲרֹךְ לְפָנָיו, שֶׁתָּשִׂים עָלָיו כַּפֶּיךָ לְהַכּוֹתוֹ:


**Put your hand over him** Strengthen yourself to wage battle against him, that you will put your hand over him to strike him.


###### Rashi on Job 40:32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:32:2)


**זְכֹר מִלְחָמָה אַל תּוֹסַף.** וְאַל תּוֹסִיף זְכֹר גְּבוּרָתְךָ, לְהִלָּחֵם בּוֹ בַּמִּלְחָמָה:


**remember the battle, do not stop** And do not stop. Remember your might to wage war against him.


###### Rashi on Job 40:32:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:32:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/40:32:3)


**אַל תּוֹסַף.** מִן יָסַף, כְּמוֹ ״וְלֹא יָסַף עוֹד לְדַעְתָּהּ״ (בראשית לח:כו); וּבְלַעַ״ז רֶקְרֵייטְרַ״שׁ. **אַל תּוֹסַף.** סָמֶ״ךְ נִפְתַּחַת, מִפְּנֵי סוֹף פָּסוּק; כְּמוֹ ״אַל תּוֹתַר״ (בראשית מט:ד); ״וַתֹּתַר״ (רות ב:יד); וְכֵן הַרְבֵּה כָּמוֹהוּ:


**and do not [rest]** תּוֹסַף is derived from יסף, like (Gen.38:26), “and he did not cease (יָסַף) being intimate with her” (regrettera in French), will regret. [In the expression] אַל-תּוֹסַף, the “sammech” is vowelized with a “pattah” because of the pause accent, as in (Gen. 49:4), “You shall not gain superiority (תּוֹתַר) “; (Ruth 2:14), “and she left over (וַתּתַר),” and many like it.



## Chapter 41


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Job 41:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:1:1)


**הֵן תּוֹחַלְתּוֹ נִכְזָבָה.** הֵן אִם תּוֹחַלְתּוֹ נִכְזָבָה, שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא בּוֹ כֹּחַ לְהִלָּחֵם וְלָזוּז מִמְּקוֹמוֹ:


**Behold his hope is in vain** Behold if his hope is in vain, for he will have no strength to wage war and to move from his place.


###### Rashi on Job 41:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:1:2)


**הֲגַם אֶל מַרְאָיו יֻטָּל.** אִם גַּם אֶל מַרְאִית פָּנָיו יֻטַּל וְיֻשְׁלַךְ אָדָם, לַעֲמֹד לְפָנָיו לְהִסְתַּכֵּל בּוֹ, מִפְּנֵי אֵימָתוֹ:


**shall not one be cast down even at the sight of him** if even at the sight of his face a man will be cast down from standing before him to look at him, because of the fear of him.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 41:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:2:1)


**לֹא אַכְזָר כִּי יְעוּרֶנּוּ.** אֵין אַכְזָר גִּבּוֹר וְעָרִיץ, אֲשֶׁר יְעִירֶנּוּ מִשְּׁנָתוֹ, וִיעוֹרְרֶנּוּ מִמְּקוֹמוֹ לַהֲקִימוֹ:


**There is no one so fierce that he can stir him up** There is no one so fierce, mighty, and strong who can arouse him from his sleep and stir him up from his place to raise him up.


###### Rashi on Job 41:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:2:2)


**וּמִי הוּא וְגוֹ׳.** אֲשֶׁר יִתְיַצֵּב וְיַעֲמֹד לְפָנַי, אֲשֶׁר אֲנִי עוֹשֶׂה אֶת כָּל אֵלֶּה:


**and who is he** who will stand up before Me, Who makes all these?



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 41:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:3:1)


**מִי הִקְדִּימַנִי.** וּמִי הוּא אֲשֶׁר הִקְדִּים פָּנַי בְּהַקְבָּלָתוֹ, לְעָבְדֵנִי וְלִשְׁמֹר מִצְוֹתַי, **וַאֲשַׁלֵּם** לוֹ שְׂכָרוֹ?


**Who came to meet Me** Who is it who came to meet Me by accepting to worship Me and keep My commandments, and I will pay him his reward.


###### Rashi on Job 41:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:3:2)


**תַּחַת כָּל הַשָּׁמַיִם לִי הוּא.** כָּל אֲשֶׁר בַּשָּׁמַיִם לִי הוּא, וּבְיָדִי לִפְרֹעַ לוֹ שְׂכָרוֹ וּפְעֻלָּתוֹ וּמַעֲבָדוֹ:


**Everything under the heavens is Mine** Everything that is under the heavens is Mine, and I have the power to pay him his reward, his recompense, and his remuneration.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Job 41:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:4:1)


**לֹא אַחֲרִישׁ בַּדָּיו.** לֹא אַחֲרִישׁ עַל בָּנָיו שֶׁל צַדִּיק, שֶׁהָלַךְ לְפָנַי בְּתָמִים, וּלְבָנָיו יִשְׁתַּלְּמוּ שְׂכַר טוֹב אֲבִיהֶם, וְיִמְצְאוּ טוֹבָה בִּזְכוּתוֹ:


**I will not be silent concerning his sons** I will not be silent concerning the sons of a righteous man who went before Me sincerely, and his sons will be paid the good reward of their father, and they will find good in his merit.


###### Rashi on Job 41:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:4:2)


**וּדְבַר גְּבוּרוֹת וְחִין עֶרְכּוֹ.** וּדְבַר גְּבוּרוֹתָיו אֲשֶׁר נִתְגַּבֵּר לַעֲשׂוֹת הַטּוֹב וְהַיָּשָׁר, וּתְחִנַּת סִדּוּרוֹ שֶׁל צַדִּיק אֲשַׁלֵּם שְׂכָרוֹ גַּם לְבָנָיו אַחֲרָיו:


**either for his acts of heroism and the arrangement of his supplication** Either for his heroic deeds, that he strengthened himself to do what is good and upright, and the arrangement of the righteous man’s supplication [is that] I will pay his reward also to his sons after him.


###### Rashi on Job 41:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:4:3)


**בַּדָּיו.** עֲנָפָיו, וְהֵם בָּנָיו, כְּמוֹ ״וַתַּעַשׂ בַּדִּים וַתִּשְׁלַח פֹּארוֹת״ (יחזקאל יז:ו):


**his sons** Heb. בדיו, his branches, and they are his sons, like (Ezek. 17:6), “and brought forth branches (בדים) and sent out sprigs.”



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 41:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:5:1)


**מִי גִלָּה פְּנֵי לְבוּשׁוֹ.** חוֹזֵר עַל הָרִאשׁוֹנוֹת, לְדַבֵּר עַל לִוְיָתָן. מִי יוּכַל לְגַלּוֹת קַשְׂקַשּׂוֹת שֶׁהֵן **בְּכֶפֶל רִסְנוֹ**, בְּכֶפֶל שְׂפָתָיו כְּשֶׁהֵן פְּתוּחוֹת מִי הוּא אֲשֶׁר יִכָּנֵס שָׁם. בְּכֶפֶל הֵן הַשְּׂפָתַיִם, שֶׁהֵן כְּפוּלוֹת: אַחַת לְמַעְלָה וְאַחַת לְמַטָּה, שֶׁאֵין בְּרִיָּה רַשָּׁאָה לִיכָּנֵס שָׁם מִפְּנֵי אֵימָתוֹ:


**Who exposed the face of his raiment** He returns to the previous topic to speak of the leviathan: who can expose the scales, which are... **within his double bridle** Into the fold of his lips, when they are open, who is it who will enter there? [The expression] בְּכֶפֶל, double, denotes the lips, which are double, one above and one below, into which no creature may enter because of fear of him.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 41:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:6:1)


**דַּלְתֵי פָנָיו.** הֵן הַשְּׂפָתַיִם; כְּשֶׁהוּא סוֹגֵר פִּיו, מִי יוּכַל לְפָתְחָן?


**the doors of his face** They are the lips when he closes his mouth; who can open them?


###### Rashi on Job 41:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:6:2)


**סְבִיבוֹת שִׁנָּיו אֵימָה.** אֲשֶׁר מִפְּנֵי פַּחְדּוֹ וְאֵימָתוֹ אֵין בְּרִיָּה יְכוֹלָה לְהִתְקָרֵב לְשִׁנָּיו:


**Around his teeth is terror** That because of his fright and his terror, no creature can approach his teeth.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 41:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:7:1)


**גַּאֲוָה אֲפִיקֵי מָגִנִּים.** גַּאֲוָה גְּדוֹלָה יֵשׁ לוֹ בְּחֹזֶק מָגִנָּיו, הֵן קַשְׂקַשִּׂים שֶׁעָלָיו, הַמְּגִנִּין עָלָיו כְּמָגֵן:


**There is pride in his strong shields** He takes great pride in the strength of his shields; they are the scales that are on him, which protect him like a shield.


###### Rashi on Job 41:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:7:2)


**סָגוּר.** הוּא חוֹתַם שִׁרְיוֹנוֹ, שֶׁהוּא צַר וְסָגוּר וְעָצוּם; שֶׁכָּל קַשְׂקֶשֶׂת וְקַשְׂקֶשֶׂת דְּבוּקָה בּוֹ וַאֲדוּקָה לַחֲבֶרְתָּהּ, שֶׁאֵין בְּרִיָּה יְכוֹלָה לְהַכּוֹתוֹ בֵּין הַדְּבָקִים (מלכים א כב:לד). וְזֶהוּ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: **אֶחָד בְּאֶחָד יִגַּשׁוּ.** שֶׁכֻּלָּן מֻגָּשׁוֹת וּתְכוּפוֹת זוֹ לָזוֹ:


**closed** That is the seal of his coat of mail, which is narrow, closed, and strong, for each scale is attached to him and attached to the next one, so that no creature can strike him between the scales. This is what is stated: They are so close to each other, that all of them are brought together and are close to each other.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Job 41:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:9:1)


**יִתְלַכְּדוּ וְלֹא יִתְפָּרָדוּ.** עֲדַיִן כּוֹפֵל לְשׁוֹנוֹ, שֶׁנִּלְכָּדוֹת וְנֶאֱחָדוֹת כָּל אַחַת וְאַחַת בַּחֲבֶרְתָּהּ, שֶׁלֹּא תִּהְיֶינָה מִתְפָּרְדוֹת זוֹ מִזּוֹ:


**they stick together and cannot be separated** He is still repeating his language, that they [the scales] are stuck together and each one is joined to the next one so that they should not separate from each other.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 41:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:10:1)


**עֲטִישֹׁתָיו תָּהֶל אוֹר.** בְּכָל עֲטִישָׁה וַעֲטִישָׁה שֶׁהוּא מִתְעַטֵּשׁ, יָהֶל וְתִזְרַח אוֹר:


**His sneezes flash forth light** With every sneeze that he sneezes, light flashes forth and shines.


###### Rashi on Job 41:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:10:2)


**תָּהֶל.** כְּמוֹ ״בְּהִלּוֹ נֵרוֹ״ (איוב כט:ג):


**flash forth** as in (above 29:3), “when He lit (בהלו) His candle.”


###### Rashi on Job 41:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:10:3)


**וְעֵינָיו כְּעַפְעַפֵּי שָׁחַר.** עֵינָיו זוֹרְחוֹת וּמְאִירוֹת כְּדִמְדּוּמֵי חַמָּה, כְּמוֹ לְעֵת בֹּקֶר שֶׁהַשֶּׁמֶשׁ מִתְאַדֶּמֶת:


**and his eyes are like the rays of dawn** His eyes shine and illuminate like the redness of the sun, like the time of morning when the sun becomes red.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 41:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:11:1)


**מִפִּיו.** מִלַּהֶבֶת הַיּוֹצֵאת מִמֶּנּוּ:


**From his mouth** From the flame that goes forth from his mouth.


###### Rashi on Job 41:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:11:2)


**מִפִּיו לַפִּידִים יַהֲלֹכוּ.** לַפִּידִים יוֹצְאוֹת מִפִּיו:


**From his mouth go firebrands** Firebrands come forth from his mouth.


###### Rashi on Job 41:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:11:3)


**כְּיִדוֹדֵי אֵשׁ.** כְּמוֹ לַפִּידִים:


**sparks of fire** Like firebrands.


###### Rashi on Job 41:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:11:4)


**יִתְמַלָּטוּ.** פִּתְרוֹנוֹ לְפִי עִנְיָנוֹ שוינ״ט אישקמוצי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**go forth like foam** Heb. יתמלטו . Its interpretation is according to the context, escumicer in Old French [going forth like foam].



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 41:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:12:1)


**מִנְּחִירָיו יֵצֵא עָשָׁן.** כְּסִיר נָפוּחַ עַל הָאֵשׁ, שֶׁמַּעֲלֶה עָשָׁן:


**From his nostrils smoke goes forth** like a bubbling pot on the fire, which raises smoke.


###### Rashi on Job 41:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:12:2)


**וְאַגְמוֹן.** פִּתְרוֹנוֹ לְפִי עִנְיָנוֹ, לְשׁוֹן כְּלִי חֶרֶשׂ הוּא, כְּמוֹ **דּוּד נָפוּחַ**; כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״סִיר נָפוּחַ אֲנִי רֹאֶה״ (ירמיהו א:יג):


**and an earthenware vessel** ואגמן. Its interpretation is according to its context: an expression of an earthenware vessel, like a bubbling pot; according to the matter that is stated (Jer. 1:13): “I see a bubbling pot.”



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 41:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:13:1)


**נַפְשׁוֹ גֶּחָלִים תְּלַהֵט.** גֶּחָלִים בּוֹעֲרוֹת וְלוֹהֲטוֹת, וְלַהֲבוֹת דּוֹלְקוֹת יוֹצְאוֹת מִפִּיו:


**His breath kindles coals** Burning and blazing coals. **and flame** And burning flames emerge from his mouth.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 41:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:14:1)


**בְּצַוָּארוֹ יָלִין עֹז.** גְּבוּרָה:


**Strength lodges in his neck** Mightiness.


###### Rashi on Job 41:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:14:2)


**וּלְפָנָיו תָּדוּץ דְּאָבָה.** שֶׁאֵין דַּאֲבוֹן וְעִצָּבוֹן לְפָנָיו, שֶׁאֵינוֹ מִתְיָרֵא מִשּׁוּם בְּרִיָּה. תָּדוּץ לְשׁוֹן דִּיצָה וְשִׂמְחָה:


**and sadness rejoices before him** For there is no sadness or grief before him, since he does not fear any creature. [The word] תָּדוּץ is an expression of joy and happiness.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 41:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:15:1)


**מַפְּלֵי בְשָׂרוֹ דָבֵקוּ.** נִדְבָּקוֹת וּתְכוּפוֹת זוֹ בָּזוֹ, שֶׁהַדָּגִים הֵן נַעֲשִׂין חֲתִיכוֹת חֲתִיכוֹת בִּבְשָׂרוֹ, וּמְדֻבָּקוֹת זוֹ בָּזוֹ. וּבְדָג גָּדוֹל כְּשֶׁהוּא מְבֻשָּׁל, נִרְאוֹת חֲתִיכוֹת כְּשֶׁהֵן שׁוֹכְבוֹת זוֹ עַל זוֹ וְזוֹ אֵצֶל זוֹ. וְכֵן הוֹלֵךְ סִדּוּר הַמִּלָּה: תְּחִלָּה דִּבֵּר בְּקַשְׂקַשּׂוֹתָיו שֶׁל לִוְיָתָן וְאַחַר כֵּן בַּחֲתִיכוֹת בְּשָׂרוֹ וְחֹזֶק לִבּוֹ:


**The flakes of his flesh are joined together** Heb. דבקו. They adhere to each other closely, for the fish are composed of many pieces in its flesh, and they adhere to one another. In a large fish, which is cooked, the pieces are noticeable when they are lying one upon the other and one beside the other, and so is the sequence of the world: First he speaks of the scales of the leviathan and then of the flakes of his flesh and the strength of his heart.


###### Rashi on Job 41:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:15:2)


**מַפְּלֵי.** לְשׁוֹן כְּפִילוּת חֲתִיכוֹת. וְיֵשׁ לוֹמַר כְּמוֹ פְּלָאֵי פְּלוּיֵי שֶׁבִּלְשׁוֹן גְּמָרָא (נדה כא:) לְשׁוֹן בִּקּוּעַ בִּקּוּעֵי חֲתִיכוֹתָיו:


**flakes** Heb. מפלי, an expression of folds of pieces. It is also possible to explain it as פְלָאֵי פלוּיֵי, [if it is split] in the language of the Talmud, an expression of the splits of his flakes.


###### Rashi on Job 41:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:15:3)


**דָּבְקוּ.** בֵּי״ת קְמוּצָה בְּקָמַץ קָטָן, כְּמוֹ ״וּלְשׁוֹנָם לְחִכָּם דָּבֵקָה״ (איוב כט:י), וְכֵן הַרְבֵּה בְּאֶתְנַחְתָּא וְסוֹף פָּסוּק:


**are joined together** the “beth” is vowelized by a small “kamatz” (tzeireh), like (Job 29:1O), “and their tongues stuck (דבקה) to their palates,” and many other cantillated by “ethrahta” or “sof pasuk.” [These are the pause accents.]


###### Rashi on Job 41:15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:15:4)


**יָצוּק עָלָיו.** כָּל מַפָּל וּמַפָּל יָצוּק זֶה עַל זֶה אֲשֶׁר **לֹא יִמּוֹט** וְלֹא יִתְפָּרֵד זֶה מֵעַל זֶה. יָצוּק לְשׁוֹן ״מוּצָק״ (איוב לז:יח), וְהוּא לְשׁוֹן עָשׂוּי, כְּמוֹ יָרוּד מִן יָרַד, יָצוּק מִן יָצַק, ״יָדוּעַ״ (ישעיה נג:ג) מִן יָדַע:


**poured out upon it** Heb. יצוק. Every flake poured out one upon the other, so that it should not move or separate one from the other. [The word] יָצוּק is an expression of being poured, and it is the passive participle, like יָרוּד, descended, from ירד ; יָצוּק, poured, from יצק ; יָדוּעַ, known, from ידע.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Job 41:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:16:1)


**לִבּוֹ יָצוּק כְּמוֹ אָבֶן.** לִבּוֹ בְּקִרְבּוֹ חָזָק וּמְיֻסָּד כְּאֶבֶן הַקְּבוּעָה וּנְתוּנָה בִּיסוֹד הַבִּנְיָן בְּחֹזֶק:


**His heart is as strong as stone** His heart within him is as strong and basic as a permanent stone that is placed securely in the foundation of a building.


###### Rashi on Job 41:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:16:2)


**יָצוּק כְּפֶלַח תַּחְתִּית.** כּוֹפֵל מִלָּתוֹ, שֶׁמְּיֻסָּד לִבּוֹ וְקָבוּעַ כְּפֶלַח שֶׁל רֵחַיִם, שֶׁנָּתוּן בְּתַחְתִּיתוֹ שֶׁל פֶּלַח רֶכֶב. שֶׁפֶּלַח רֶכֶב שֶׁלְּמַעְלָה – הוּא סוֹבֵב וּמִתְגַּלְגֵּל וְטוֹחֵן, וְשֶׁל מַטָּה – אֵינוֹ זָז מִמְּקוֹמוֹ כִּי קָבוּעַ הוּא בִּמְקוֹמוֹ, אֲשֶׁר לֹא יִמּוֹט (ישעיהו מ:כ). הוּא נִקְרָא ׳פֶּלַח׳, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וַתַּשְׁלֵךְ אִשָּׁה אַחַת פֶּלַח רֶכֶב עַל רֹאשׁ אֲבִימֶלֶךְ״ (שופטים ט:נג), וְאֶת שֶׁלְּמַטָּה קָרוּי פֶּלַח תַּחְתִּית:


**and as strong as a lower millstone** This clause is repetitious, meaning that his heart is as basic and permanent as the millstone that is placed under the upper millstone, for the upper millstone revolves and rotates and grinds, whereas the lower one does not move from its place because it is fastened to its place, so that it should not move. That is called פֶּלַח [רֶכֶב] as it is stated (Jud. 9:53): “And a certain woman cast an upper millstone: פֶּלַח רֶכֶב, upon Abimelech’s head.” The lower one is called פֶלַח תַּחתִּית the lower piece.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Job 41:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:17:1)


**מִשֵּׂתוֹ יָגוּרוּ אֵלִים.** מִפַּחְדּוֹ מִתְיָרְאִים גִּבּוֹרִים וַחֲזָקִים, בֵּין דָּגִים בֵּין שְׁאָר בְּרִיּוֹת:


**From his fear the mighty are frightened** From his fear, the mighty and strong, both fishes and other creatures, are frightened.


###### Rashi on Job 41:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:17:2)


**מִשְּׁבָרִים יִתְחַטָּאוּ.** גַּלֵּי הַיָּם, שֶׁקְּרוּיִין מִשְׁבָּרִים, הֵן חֲסֵרִין לְפָנָיו; שֶׁהוּא שׁוֹבְרָן בִּפְרִיחָתוֹ, כַּאֲשֶׁר הוּא פּוֹרֵחַ וְצָף בְּגַלֵּי הַיָּם:


**the breakers fail** The waves of the sea, which are called breakers, fail before him, because he breaks them with his swimming, as he swims and floats in the waves of the sea.


###### Rashi on Job 41:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:17:3)


**יִתְחַטָּאוּ.** לְשׁוֹן חִסָּרוֹן, כְּמוֹ ״וְהָיִיתִי אֲנִי וּבְנִי שְׁלֹמֹה חַטָּאִים״ (מלכים א א:כא):


[The word] יִתְחַטָּאוּ is an expression of a lack, like (I Kings 1:21), “and I and my son Solomon will be lacking (חַטָּאִים).”



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Job 41:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:18:1)


**מַשִּׂיגֵהוּ חֶרֶב בְּלִי תָקוּם.** גִּבּוֹר הַמַּשִּׂיגֵהוּ לְהִלָּחֵם בְּחֶרֶב – לֹא יִתְקַיֵּם חַרְבּוֹ, כִּי הוּא יִשְׁבְּרֶנָּה:


**He who overtakes him with a sword—it will not remain** A mighty man, who overtakes him to wage battle with a sword—his sword will not remain, because he will break it.


###### Rashi on Job 41:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:18:2)


**חֲנִית מַסָּע וְשִׁרְיָה.** וְכֵן כָּל כְּלֵי מִלְחָמָה הַלָּלוּ שֶׁבְּיַד הַמַּשִּׂיג, לֹא יִתְקַיְּמוּ וְלֹא יַעַמְדוּ לְפָנָיו שֶׁל לִוְיָתָן. .**מַסָּע.** יֵשׁ לוֹמַר שֶׁהוּא אֶחָד מִכְּלֵי מִלְחָמָה, כְּמוֹ חֶרֶב וַחֲנִית וְשִׁרְיָה. וְיֵשׁ לוֹמַר מַסָּע דּוּגְמַת ״אֶבֶן שְׁלֵמָה מַסָּע וְגוֹ׳״ (מלכים א ו:ז). וְזֶהוּ פִּתְרוֹנוֹ: **חֲנִית מַסָּע** שֶׁהוּא גָּדוֹל וְאֵינוֹ נוֹחַ לְטַלְטֵל כָּל כָּךְ, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא גָּדוֹל וְחָזָק מְאֹד; כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בְּגָלְיַת הַפְּלִשְׁתִּי ״וְחֵץ חֲנִיתוֹ כִּמְנוֹר אֹרְגִים וְלַהֶבֶת חֲנִיתוֹ שֵׁשׁ מֵאוֹת וְגוֹ׳״ (שמואל א יז:ז):


**[neither will] a spear, a slingshot, or a coat of mail** Likewise, none of these weapons that are in the hands of the overtaker will either remain or stand up before the leviathan. The word מַסָע may be said to be one type of weapon, like the sword, spear, and coat of mail(?). מַסָע may also be interpreted after the sample of (I Kings 6:7), “whole stones as they were transported (מַסָע) etc.,” and this is its interpretation: a transported spear, which is large and not so easily moved, because it is large and very strong; as the matter is stated concerning Goliath the Philistine (I Sam. 17:7), “And the shaft of his spear was like a weaver’s beam, and the spear’s head was six hundred shekels, etc.”


###### Rashi on Job 41:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:18:3)


**וְשִׁרְיָה.** שִׁרְיוֹן:


**a coat of mail** Heb. ושריה, a coat of mail (שריון).



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Job 41:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:19:1)


**יַחְשֹׁב לְתֶבֶן בַּרְזֶל.** כְּלֵי זַיִן שֶׁל בַּרְזֶל חֲשׁוּבִים לְפָנָיו כְּתֶבֶן, וּכְלֵי זַיִן שֶׁל נְחֹשֶׁת הֵם לְפָנָיו כִּרְקֵבוּת עֵץ.


**He regards iron as straw** Iron weapons are regarded by him as straw, and copper weapons are to him as rotten wood.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Job 41:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:20:1)


**לֹא יַבְרִיחֶנּוּ בֶן קָשֶׁת.** לֹא יִבְרַח מִפְּנֵי בַּעַל קֶשֶׁת וְחִצִּים, וְאַבְנֵי בַּלִּיסְטְרָאוֹת הֲרֵי הֵן לְפָנָיו כְּקַשׁ:


**No arrow can put him to flight** He will not flee before an archer, and arrows and slingstones are as stubble to him.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Job 41:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:21:1)


**תּוֹתָח.** הוּא כְּלִי זַיִן, וּפִתְרוֹנוֹ לְפִי עִנְיָנוֹ:


**Catapult [stones]** a weapon, to be interpreted according to the context.


###### Rashi on Job 41:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:21:2)


**וְיִשְׂחַק לְרַעַשׁ כִּידוֹן.** לְרַעַשׁ מְהוּמַת בַּעֲלֵי מִלְחָמוֹת נוֹטְלֵי כִּידוֹן, הוּא מְשַׂחֵק וּמְטַיֵּל; שֶׁאֵינוֹ מִתְיָרֵא מִפְּנֵיהֶם:


**and he plays at the noise of the harpoon** At the noise of the tumult of warriors who carry harpoons, he plays and strolls, for he does not fear them.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Job 41:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:22:1)


**תַּחְתָּיו חַדּוּדֵי חָרֶשׂ.** בִּמְקוֹם חֲנִיָּתוֹ יֵשׁ זָהֳרוּרֵי שֶׁמֶשׁ, כִּי סְנַפִּירִים שֶׁלּוֹ, אֲשֶׁר תַּחְתָּיו – בְּבִטְנוֹ, מְאִירוֹת וּמַזְרִיחוֹת כַּשֶּׁמֶשׁ:


**Under him are rays of the sun** in the place he rests, there are rays of the sun, because his fins, which are under him on his belly, illuminate and shine like the sun.


###### Rashi on Job 41:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:22:2)


**חַדּוּדֵי.** כְּמוֹ ״חֶרֶב חַדָּה״ (יחזקאל ה:א), שֶׁהִיא שְׁנוּנָה, צַחָה וּמְלֻבָּנָה; וְעַל שֵׁם כָּךְ קוֹרֵא לְזַהֲרוּרֵי – חַדּוּדֵי:


**rays** Heb. חדודי, like a sharp sword, which is sharpened, bright, and whitened. Because of this, he calls rays חַדוּדֵי.


###### Rashi on Job 41:22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:22:3)


**חֶרֶשׂ.** שֶׁמֶשׁ, כְּמוֹ ״הָאוֹמֵר לַחֶרֶס לֹא יִזְרָח וְגוֹ׳״ (איוב ט:ז), ״בְּטֶרֶם תָּבֹא הַחַרְסָה״ (שופטים יד:יח):


**sun** Heb. חרשׂ, the sun, like (above 9:7), “He who spoke to the sun (לחרס) and it did not shine”; (Jud. 14:18); “before the sun (החרסה) set. “


###### Rashi on Job 41:22:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:22:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:22:4)


**יִרְפַּד חָרוּץ עֲלֵי טִיט.** מִלָּה כְּפוּלָה, יִרְפַּד חָרוּץ מוּסָב עַל חִדּוּדֵי חֶרֶס, עֲלֵי טִיט מוּסָב עַל תַּחְתִּיתוֹ, בִּמְקוֹם שֶׁהוּא שָׂם עַל הַחוֹל, זֶה טִיט רִפּוּדוֹ מַזְרִיחַ כְּזָהָב חָרוּץ, כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״רְפִידָתוֹ זָהָב״ (שיר השירים ג:י), חָרוּץ מִין זָהָב, כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״מֵחָרוּץ תְּבוּאָתָהּ״ (משלי ג:יד), ״טוֹב פִּרְיָהּ מֵחָרוּץ וּמִפָּז״ (משלי ח:יט). יִרְפַּד לְשׁוֹן מַצָּע וּמִשְׁכָּב כְּמוֹ ״רְפִידָתוֹ זָהָב״ (שיר ג:י):


**where he lies is gold upon the mire** This is a repeated clause. “Where he lies is gold,” refers to “rays of the sun”; “upon the mire” refers to “under him,” in the place where he is on the sand; this is the mire where he lies. It shines like precious gold, as it is stated (Song 3:10): “its couch of gold.” חָרוּץ is a type of gold, as it is stated (Prov. 8:19): “My fruit is better than gold (מחרוץ), yea than fine gold.” Where he lies—an expression of a couch for lying down, like (Song 3:10), “its couch (רפידתו) of gold.”



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Job 41:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:23:1)


**יַרְתִּיחַ כַּסִּיר מְצוּלָה.** כְּסִיר קָטָן נָפוּחַ, שֶׁמַּעֲלֶה מְהֵרָה רְתִיחוֹת תְּכוּפוֹת וּרְצוּפוֹת, כֵּן הוּא מַרְתִּיחַ אֶת הַמְּצוּלָה שֶׁהִיא גְּדוֹלָה וַעֲמֻקָּה:


**He makes the deep seethe like a pot** Like a small bubbling pot, that quickly raises bubbles one after another, so does he cause the deep, which is large and deep, to seethe.


###### Rashi on Job 41:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:23:2)


**יָם יָשִׂים כַּמֶּרְקָחָה.** כְּמִרְקַחַת רִקּוּחַ בְּשָׂמִים, שֶׁהִיא מְעוֹרָה וּמְעֹרֶבֶת, כְּמוֹ כֵן יָשִׂים וִיעָרֵב אֶת הַיָּם כְּשֶׁהוּא זָז מִמְּקוֹמוֹ:


**he makes the sea like a seething mixture** Like a mixture of a concoction of spices, which is compounded and mixed, so does he make and mix the sea when he moves from his place.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Job 41:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:24:1)


**אַחֲרָיו יָאִיר נָתִיב.** כְּשֶׁהוּא פּוֹרֵחַ וְשָׁט בַּיָּם, אַחֲרָיו מֵאִיר נָתִיב הַתְּהוֹם; שֶׁלְּפִי שָׁעָה שֶׁהוּא פּוֹרֵחַ, וְאֵין שָׁם מַיִם מֵחֲמַת מְרוּצַת פְּרִיחָתוֹ:


**He makes a path shine after him** When he swims and floats in the sea, he makes the path of the deep shine, for at the time he swims, there is no water because of the speed of his swimming.


###### Rashi on Job 41:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:24:2)


**יַחְשֹׁב תְּהוֹם לְשֵׂיבָה.** תְּהוֹם חָשׁוּב לְפָנָיו בְּחַלָּשׁוּת וְתַשּׁוּת:


**he considers the deep to be hoary** To him, the deep is considered to be weak and feeble.


###### Rashi on Job 41:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:24:3)


**לְשֵׂיבָה.** לְחַלְשׁוּת שֶׁל זִקְנָה וְשֵׂיבָה:


**to be hoary** The weakness of old age and hoariness.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Job 41:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:25:1)


**אֵין עַל עָפָר מָשְׁלוֹ.** מֵ״ם חֲטַף קָמַץ, וּפִתְרוֹנוֹ: מֶמְשַׁלְתּוֹ – שֶׁאֵין בְּרִיָּה מוֹשֶׁלֶת עָלָיו. וְהוּא שֵׁם דָּבָר: מוֹשֵׁל וְשִׁלְטוֹן, וְעַל כֵּן הַמֵּ״ם מְנֻקֶּדֶת בַּחֲטַף קָמַץ:


**On the dust there is none that rules over him** The “mem” [is vowelized] with a “hataf kamatz,” and it means his rule, that no creature rules over him, and it is a noun denoting a ruler. Therefore, the “mem” is vowelized with a “hataf kamatz.”


###### Rashi on Job 41:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:25:2)


**הֶעָשׂוּ.** כְּמוֹ ״הֶעָשׂוּי״ (שמות ג:טז), וְלֹא יָדַעְתִּי לוֹ רֵיעַ. וּפִתְרוֹנוֹ: הֶעָשׂוּי בְּלִי פַּחַד וּמְחִתָּה, שֶׁאֵינוֹ מִתְיָרֵא מִן הַבְּרִיּוֹת; כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בַּסּוּס ״יִשְׂחַק לְפַחַד וְלֹא יֵחָת״ (איוב לט:כב):


**which is made** Heb. העשו, like העשוי [the passive participle]. However, I know of no similar case [in Scripture]. It means one that was made without fear or dismay, that is not afraid of the creatures, as it is stated concerning the horse (above 39:22): “He scorns fear and is not dismayed.”



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Job 41:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:26:1)


**אֶת כָּל גָּבֹהַּ יִרְאֶה.** כָּל הַגְּבוֹהִים שֶׁבָּעוֹלָם הוּא רוֹאֶה תַּחְתָּיו, וְכֵן הוּא מִנְהַג הָעוֹלָם, אֲשֶׁר אוֹתוֹ הָעוֹמֵד לְמַעְלָה רוֹאֶה אֶת הַתַּחְתּוֹן יוֹתֵר מִמַּה שֶּׁרוֹאֶה הַתַּחְתּוֹן, וְאֵין לַתַּחְתּוֹן גְּבוּרָה וְנִצָּחוֹן נֶגֶד הָעֶלְיוֹן. וְלִוְיָתָן זֶה רוֹאֶה אֶת כָּל הַגִּבּוֹרִים, לוֹמַר שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם גְּבוּרָה כְּנֶגְדּוֹ:


**He looks at all high things** All the high things in the world he sees beneath himself, for so is the custom of the world, that whoever stands above sees the one below more than the one below sees [him]. Also, the one below has no power or victory against the one above. Now this leviathan sees all mighty men; that is to say, they have no power against him.


###### Rashi on Job 41:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/41:26:2)


**הוּא מֶלֶךְ עַל כָּל בְּנֵי שָׁחַץ.** מִלָּה כְּפוּלָה: עַל כָּל גַּאֲוָה יִרְאֶה, וְהוּא מֶלֶךְ עַל כָּל בְּנֵי שָׁחַץ – בְּנֵי גַּסּוּת הָרוּחַ שֶׁבָּרָאתִי בְּעוֹלָמִי, שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם כְּנֶגְדּוֹ מֶמְשָׁלָה וּשְׂרָרָה. וְהָאֵיךְ נְשָׂאֲךָ לִבְּךָ לָשֵׂאת רֹאשׁ לְפָנַי, לְהִתְוַכֵּחַ נֶגְדִּי – כִּי הִנְנִי עוֹשֶׂה אֶת כָּל הַנִּפְלָאוֹת הָאֵלֶּה?!


**he is king over all proud beasts** This is a repeated expression. He looks over all proud beasts and he is king over all the proud beasts, over all haughty creatures that I created in My world, who have no rule or dominion against him. Now how did you dare to lift your head before Me, since I perform all these wonders?



## Chapter 42


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Job 42:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:2:1)


**(מענה איוב):** 
**יָדַעְתִּי כִּי כֹל תּוּכָל.** כִּי הַכֹּל תּוּכַל לַעֲשׂוֹת, כַּאֲשֶׁר יָשָׁר בְּעֵינֶיךָ:


**Job’s Reply**
**I knew that You can do everything** That You can do anything that pleases You.


###### Rashi on Job 42:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:2:2)


**וְלֹא יִבָּצֵר מִמְּךָ מְזִמָּה.** כּוֹפֵל מִלָּתוֹ: שֶׁאֵין מְזִמָּה וּמַחֲשָׁבָה נִבְצֶרֶת וְנִסְתֶּרֶת מִמְּךָ, אֲשֶׁר כָּל מַחֲשְׁבוֹתֶיךָ תּוּכַל לִגְמֹר וּלְמַלְּאוֹת; כִּי בְיָדְךָ כֹּחַ וּגְבוּרָה וּבְיָדְךָ לַעֲשׂוֹת כָּל הַטּוֹב בְּעֵינֶיךָ (דברי הימים א כט:יב). וּמִקְרָא זֶה דּוּגְמַת ״וְעַתָּה לֹא יִבָּצֵר מֵהֶם כֹּל אֲשֶׁר יָזְמוּ לַעֲשׂוֹת״ (בראשית יא:ו):


**and no design is restrained from You** He repeats his words, that no design or plan is restrained or withheld from You, that You can complete and fulfill Your plans, for strength and might are in Your hand, and You have the power to do all that pleases You. This verse is similar to (Gen. 11:6), “Now will nothing that they have planned to do be restrained (יבצר) from them?”



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Job 42:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:3:1)


**מִי זֶה מַעְלִים.** אֲשֶׁר הֶעֱלִים וְכִסָּה עֲצָתוֹ וְנִפְלְאוֹתָיו שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא בִּבְלִי דַעַת? יָדַעְתִּי כִּי הַכֹּל בְּיָדְךָ לַעֲשׂוֹת, וְעַל כֵּן **הִגַּדְתִּי** קְצָת מִגְּבוּרוֹתָיו כַּאֲשֶׁר הָיוּ עִם לְבָבִי, כַּאֲשֶׁר **יָדַעְתִּי**:


**Who is this** who concealed and covered the counsel and the wonders of the Holy One, blessed be He, without knowledge? I knew that everything is in Your hand to do, and so I related some of Your mighty deeds as were in my heart; as I knew [them].


###### Rashi on Job 42:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:3:2)


**וְלֹא אָבִין.** אֲבָל לֹא הֵבַנְתִּי כָּל כָּךְ, כַּאֲשֶׁר הוֹדַעְתַּנִי; כִּי כַּמָּה נִפְלָאוֹת נִסְתָּרוֹת וְנֶעֱלָמוֹת מִמֶּנִּי, אֲשֶׁר אֵינֶנִּי יוֹדֵעַ:


**but I did not understand** But I did not understand so much, as You informed me, for many wonders are hidden and concealed from me, which I do not know.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Job 42:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:5:1)


**לְשֵׁמַע אֹזֶן שְׁמַעְתִּיךָ.** כַּמָּה וְכַמָּה פְּעָמִים שָׁמַעְתִּי שִׁמְעֲךָ, **וְעַתָּה עֵינִי** רָאֲתָה אֶת שְׁכִינָתְךָ:


**I have heard of You by the hearing of the ear** Many many times I have heard of You. **and now, my eye has seen You** Your Shechinah.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Job 42:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:6:1)


**עַל כֵּן.** אֲשֶׁר זָכִיתִי לַחֲזוֹת שְׁכִינָתְךָ, מוֹאֵס אֲנִי בְּחַיַּי; וְאֶהְיֶה מִתְנַחֵם, אִלּוּ הָיִיתִי שׁוֹכֵן בַּקֶּבֶר, לָשׁוּב **לְעָפָר וָאֵפֶר** אֲשֶׁר לֻקַּחְתִּי מִשָּׁם:


**Therefore** Since I merited to see Your Shechinah. **I despise** I despise my life. **on dust and ashes** if I were resting in the grave, to return to dust and ashes whence I was taken.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Job 42:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:7:1)


**וּבִשְׁנֵי רֵעֶיךָ.** בִּלְדַּד הַשּׁוּחִי וְצוֹפַר הַנַּעֲמָתִי:


**and your two companions** Bildad the Shuhite and Zophar the Naamathite.


###### Rashi on Job 42:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:7:2)


**כִּי לֹא דִבַּרְתֶּם.** שֶׁהֲרֵי לֹא דִבַּרְתֶּם אֵלַי טַעֲנָה נְכוֹנָה **כְּעַבְדִּי אִיּוֹב**; שֶׁהֲרֵי הוּא לֹא פָּשַׁע בִּי, כִּי אִם עַל אֲשֶׁר אָמַר ״תָּם וְרָשָׁע הוּא מְכַלֶּה״ (איוב ט:כב), וְעַל יְדֵי הַשָּׂטָן הַמְקַטְרֵג אֶת הָעוֹלָם, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״אִם שׁוֹט יָמִית פִּתְאוֹם וְגוֹ׳״ (איוב ט:כג). וְאִם הוֹסִיף לְדַבֵּר – מִפְּנֵי קֹשִׁי יִסּוּרִין אֲשֶׁר כָּבְדוּ וְחָזְקוּ עָלָיו דִּבֵּר. אֲבָל אַתֶּם פְּשַׁעְתֶּם, עַל אֲשֶׁר הִרְשַׁעְתֶּם אוֹתוֹ, לֵאמֹר ״הֲלֹא יִרְאָתְךָ כִּסְלָתֶךָ״ (איוב ד:ו), וְהֶחֱזַקְתֶּם אוֹתוֹ בְּחֶזְקַת רָשָׁע. וּלְבַסּוֹף הֱיִיתֶם מְשֻׁתָּקִים וּמְנֻצָּחִים לְפָנָיו; וְהָיָה לָכֶם לְנַחֲמוֹ, כַּאֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה אֱלִיהוּא. וְלֹא דַי לְאִיּוֹב בְּצָרָתוֹ וְיִסּוּרָיו, כִּי גַם הוֹסַפְתֶּם עַל חַטֹּאתֵיכֶם פֶּשַׁע לְהַקְנִיטוֹ:


**because you did not speak** Because you did not speak to Me with a correct argument as did My servant Job, for he did not rebel against Me except for what he said (above 9:22), “He destroys both the innocent and the wicked,” and through the Adversary who denounces the world, as it is stated (ibid. verse 23): “If the scourge kills suddenly etc.” And if he continued to speak, he spoke because of the severity of the pains that burdened him and overwhelmed him; but you rebelled by condemning him, saying (4: 6), “Behold, your fear was your foolishness,” and you held him to be a wicked man, and at the end when you were silenced and defeated before him, you should have consoled him as Elihu did. Was it not enough for Job with his trouble and his sufferings, that you added rebellion to your sins to provoke him?



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Job 42:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:8:1)


**וְהַעֲלִיתֶם עוֹלָה בַּעַדְכֶם.** לְמַעַן שֶׁתִּתְרַצּוּ וְתִתְפַּיְּסוּ לְפָנַי, וּבִלְבַד אֲשֶׁר עַבְדִּי אִיּוֹב יִתְפַּלֵּל עֲלֵיכֶם אֲשֶׁר אֶסְלַח לְחַטֹּאתֵיכֶם, וְלִפְשָׁעֵיכֶם לֹא אֶזְכֹּר עוֹד:


**and offer up a burnt offering for yourselves** So that you placate and appease Me, provided that My servant Job pray for you that I forgive your sins, and no longer remember your transgressions.


###### Rashi on Job 42:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:8:2)


**כִּי אִם פָּנָיו אֶשָּׂא.** שֶׁהֲרֵי פָנָיו אֶשָּׂא לְקַבֵּל תְּפִלָּתוֹ. הֲרֵי אִם כְּמוֹ אֲשֶׁר, וְכֵן ״עַד אִם כִּלּוּ לִשְׁתּוֹת״ (בראשית כד:יט), ״אִם כֹּפֶר יוּשַׁת עָלָיו״ (שמות כא:ל), וְכֵן הַרְבֵּה:


**for I will favor him** Heb. כי אם, because I will favor him to accept his prayer (thus the word אִם is used for “that”), and likewise (Gen. 24:19), “until (עד אם) they have finished drinking”; (Ex. 21:30), “in which case (אם) an atonement is placed on him,” and many others.


###### Rashi on Job 42:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:8:3)


**כִּי לֹא.** שֶׁהֲרֵי לֹא:


**for...not** Because...not.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Job 42:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:9:1)


**וַיִּשָּׂא ה׳ אֶת פְּנֵי אִיּוֹב.** וַיִּשָּׂא כְּמוֹ ״נָשָׂאתִי פָנֶיךָ גַּם לַדָּבָר הַזֶּה״ (בראשית יט:כא), שֶׁקִּבֵּל תְּפִלָּתוֹ וְהִסְבִּיר לוֹ פָּנִים:


**and the Lord favored Job** Heb. וישא, [lit. and the Lord lifted His countenance] as in (Gen. 19: 21), “I have favored you (נשאתי) also in this respect,” that He accepted his prayer and showed him a bright countenance.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Job 42:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:10:1)


**שָׁב אֶת שְׁבוּת אִיּוֹב בְּהִתְפַּלְלוֹ.** שָׁב נוֹפֵל לוֹמַר בְּעִנְיָן זֶה, וַהֲרֵי הוּא כְּאִלּוּ כְּתִיב וְהֵשִׁיב אֶת שְׁבוּת אִיּוֹב:


**Now the Lord returned Job’s captivity when he prayed** [The word] שָׁב is inappropriate in this matter, and it is as though it were written: “And He returned (נשאתי) Job’s captivity when he prayed.”


###### Rashi on Job 42:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:10:2)


**בְּהִתְפַּלְלוֹ בְּעַד רֵעֵהוּ.** בִּזְכוּת שֶׁהִתְפַּלֵּל עַל כָּל רֵיעַ וְרֵיעַ שֶׁלּוֹ:


**for his friend** In the merit that he prayed for each friend of his.


###### Rashi on Job 42:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:10:3)


**לְמִשְׁנֶה.** עַל אֶחָד שְׁנַיִם (נראה דקאי גם על מה שכתב למעלה שהיה לו לומר להשיב מהר״ז), וְלֹא דִקְדֵּק הַמִּקְרָא בְּמִלָּתוֹ:


**twice as much** Heb. למשנה, for one, two; and Scripture was not exact in its wording.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Job 42:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:11:1)


**לְפָנִים.** כָּל אֲשֶׁר הָיוּ אוֹהֲבָיו וּמַכִּירָיו בְּטֶרֶם הִגִּיעוּ אֵלָיו הַיִּסּוּרִים:


**previous** All who were his friends and acquaintances before the sufferings befell him.


###### Rashi on Job 42:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:11:2)


**וַיָּנֻדוּ לוֹ.** לְשׁוֹן קוֹנְפְּלֵיְינְטָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and they bemoaned him** Complaindre in Old French, to complain.


###### Rashi on Job 42:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:11:3)


**קְשִׂיטָה.** מָעָה:


**one piece of money** a ma’ah.


###### Rashi on Job 42:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:11:4)


**וְאִישׁ.** וְכָל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד (רוצה לומר שלא תאמר שהכונה שיש שנתנו לו קשיטה ויש שנתן לו נזם לכך כתב רש״י ואיש פירושו כל אחד ואחד מהר״ז):


**and each one** and every one of them.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Job 42:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:12:1)


**מֵרֵאשִׁיתוֹ.** יוֹתֵר מֵרֵאשִׁית עָשְׁרוֹ:


**more than his beginning** More than his former wealth.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Job 42:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:13:1)


**שִׁבְעָנָה.** הֵם פַּעֲמַיִם שֶׁבַע, שְׁתֵּי שְׁבִיעִיּוֹת; כְּמוֹ ״שְׁבֻעַיִם״ (ויקרא יב:ה), שֶׁהֵם שְׁתֵּי שָׁבוּעוֹת:


**fourteen** Heb. שבענה. They were twice seven, two sevens, like (Lev. 12:5), שְׁבֻעַיִם which means two weeks.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Job 42:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:14:1)


**וַיִּקְרָא שֵׁם הָאַחַת יְמִימָה.** עַל שֵׁם יָפְיָתָן נִקְרְאוּ שְׁמוֹתֵיהֶן:


**And he named the first Jemimah** They were named according to their beauty.


###### Rashi on Job 42:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:14:2)


**יְמִימָה.** צַחָה וּמְלֻבָּנָה כַּיּוֹם:


**Jemimah** Bright and white as the sun (יום).


###### Rashi on Job 42:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:14:3)


**קְצִיעָה.** רֵיחָהּ נוֹדֵף עָרֵב וּמְבֻשָּׂם, כִּקְצִיעָה שֶׁל בֹּשֶׂם:


**Keziah** She had a fragrant and perfumed scent like the spice, cassia.


###### Rashi on Job 42:14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:14:4)


**קֶרֶן הַפּוּךְ.** עַל שֵׁם הַקֶּרֶן שֶׁנּוֹתְנִין בּוֹ כְּחוֹל וּבוֹרִית, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וְתִקְרְעִי בַפּוּךְ עֵינַיִךְ״ (ירמיהו ד:ל):


**Keren-happuch** Because of the horn in which they put stibium and lixivium, as it is stated (Jer. 4: 30): “that you enlarge your eye with paint (בפוך).”



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Job 42:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:15:1)


**וְלֹא נִמְצָא נָשִׁים יָפוֹת כִּבְנוֹת אִיּוֹב וְגוֹ׳.** וְלֹא נִמְצָא מַעֲשֶׂה שֶׁל נָשִׁים יָפוֹת, כְּמַעֲשֵׂה יֹפִי שֶׁל בְּנוֹת אִיּוֹב; וּמִקְרָא זֶה דּוּגְמַת ״וַיְהִי אֲנָשִׁים אֲשֶׁר הָיוּ טְמֵאִים״ (במדבר ט:ו); וְכֵן הַרְבֵּה:


**Nowhere...were women as beautiful as Job’s daughters to be found** An instance of beautiful women like the phenomenon of the beauty of Job’s daughters was not found. This verse is on the pattern of (Num. 9:6), “But there was [an incident of] men who were unclean.”


###### Rashi on Job 42:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:15:2)


**וַיִּתֵּן לָהֶם אֲבִיהֶם נַחֲלָה בְּתוֹךְ אֲחֵיהֶם.** מִתּוֹךְ חֲשִׁיבוּתָן וְיָפְיָן, נָתַן לָהֶם נַחֲלָה עִם הָאַחִים:


**and their father gave them an inheritance among their brothers** Because of (their esteem and) their beauty, he gave them an inheritance with their brothers.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Job 42:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Job/r/42:17:1)


**וּשְׂבַע יָמִים.** מִן שָׂבֵעַ יֹאמַר שְׂבַע בִּדְבֵקוּת:


**and sated with days** Heb. וּשְׂבַע.From וְשָׂבֵעַ he says וּשְׂבַע, in the construct state.
